# 1 Introduction to Cone Penetration Testing

# 1 圆锥静力触探介绍

## 1.1 Cone Penetration Testing (CPT)

## 1.1 静力触探试验（CPT）

In the cone penetration test (CPT), an instrumented electronic probe (cone penetrometer) is situated at the front end of a string of steel rods that are hydraulically pushed into the ground at a constant rate of 20 mm/s to measure soil response, geostratigraphy, and engineering parameters. A CPT vehicle, anchored rig, or hydraulic ram set are often used to provide the reaction and pushing force. The penetrometer takes three continuous readings with depth: (1) measured cone tip resistance ($q_c$), (2) sleeve friction ($f_s$), and dynamic porewater pressure ($u_2$), as depicted in Figure 1.1. Cone penetration tests (CPT) and piezocone tests (CPTU) are routinely performed to depths in excess of 30 m; regularly to 60 m; and in special cases to depths over 200 m.

在 Cone Penetration Test（静力触探，CPT）中，装有传感器的电子探头（圆锥触探探头）安装在一串钢制探杆的前端，通过液压系统以 20 mm/s 的恒定速率压入土体，以测量土体响应、地质分层和工程参数。通常使用静力触探车、锚固式静力触探机或液压千斤顶来提供反力和贯入力。探头随深度连续读取三个参数：（1）实测锥尖阻力（$q_c$），（2）侧壁摩阻力（$f_s$），和（3）动孔隙水压力（$u_2$），如图 1.1 所示。静力触探试验（CPT）和孔压静力触探试验（CPTU）的常规测试深度通常超过 30 m；普遍可达 60 m；在特殊情况下可超过 200 m。

![image](images/rId8.png)

*图 1.1 静力触探试验（CPT）和孔压静力触探试验（CPTU）的基本装置与测试过程*

The cone penetrometer can be used to deploy additional sensors into the subsurface. These additional sensors are typically attached as modules immediately behind the cone probe. Some of these modules include resistivity electrodes, seismic sensors, passive gamma ray scintillating crystals, environmental monitoring sensors, or even image-capturing electronics to visually identify the in-situ soils.

圆锥触探探头还可用于将附加传感器布设到地下。这些附加传感器通常作为模块直接安装在探头后方。部分模块包括电阻率电极、地震传感器、被动式伽马射线闪烁晶体、环境监测传感器，甚至用于直观识别原位土体的图像采集电子器件。

### 1.1.1 Cone Probe Dimensions

### 1.1.1 触探探头尺寸

The internal components of the penetrometer include load cells and transducers to measure axial force, friction, pressure, and inclination. Data are transmitted up to a field computer at the surface through the hollow center of the rods usually by electrical cable, although wireless systems are available including infrared or audio signals. The outer shell of the penetrometer is configured with hardened steel parts to resist abrasion and wear. The tip design is a 60° conical apex and the sleeve is a cylindrical shell. The conventional standard size penetrometer is 35.7 mm in diameter, giving a 10 cm² cross-sectional area, although a slightly larger 43.7 mm diameter version has become more prevalent, resulting in a 15 cm² cross-sectional area.

触探探头的内部组件包括荷载传感器和变送器，用于测量轴向力、摩擦力、压力和倾角。数据通常通过电缆经探杆的中空内芯传输至地表的现场计算机，也有包括红外或声频信号在内的无线传输系统可供使用。触探探头的外壳采用硬化钢部件，以抵抗磨损。锥尖设计为 60° 圆锥顶角，摩擦套筒为圆柱形外壳。常规标准尺寸触探探头的直径为 35.7 mm，对应的截面积为 10 cm²；而稍大的 43.7 mm 直径版本现已更为普遍，对应的截面积为 15 cm²。

Mini-cones as small as 1 cm² and 5 cm² are available for improved resolution in highly-stratified deposits, varved clay-silts, and shallow soundings, as well as for laboratory research chamber testing. In contrast, large penetrometers up to 40 cm² have been manufactured for use in gravelly soils. Figure 1.2 shows a selection of available penetrometers for field use. Moreover, for special small scale lab testing such as centrifuge modeling, a series of micro-penetrometers have been developed that are as small as 10 mm to 1 mm in diameter (0.08 to 0.0008 cm²).

截面积小至 1 cm² 和 5 cm² 的微型探头可用于提高在高分层沉积物、纹泥质粉土和浅层触探中的分辨率，也可用于室内标定罐研究试验。相比之下，截面积达 40 cm² 的大型触探探头也已制造，用于含砾土中。图 1.2 展示了可用于现场测试的多种触探探头。此外，对于离心机模型试验等特殊小比例室内试验，已开发出直径小至 10 mm 到 1 mm（0.08 到 0.0008 cm²）的系列微型触探探头。

![image](images/rId9.png)

*图 1.2 触探探头系列，包括：(a) 标准 10 cm² 和 15 cm² 尺寸；(b) 标准尺寸与小型探头的对比*

### 1.1.2 Types of Cone Penetrometers

### 1.1.2 触探探头类型

A selection of common penetrometer designs is shown in Figure 1.3. In the basic penetrometer design, the electronic tip-friction cone suffices for profiling deep deposits of clean sand, soil formations and fills with no groundwater, or geomaterials that do not generate excess porewater pressures. In ground conditions that exhibit a porewater response, such as clays, silts, and sands with fines, penetration porewater pressures ($u_m$) should also be measured, as detailed by ASTM standard D 5778 (2020) and ISO standard 22476-1 (2012) for the piezocone penetration test (CPTU). As with many site investigations involving natural soil deposits and formations, the use of CPTU is preferred since the soil types are not known in advance.

常见的触探探头设计如图 1.3 所示。在基本型触探探头设计中，电子锥尖-摩擦探头足以对深层洁净砂土沉积、无地下水的土层和填土，或不产生超孔隙水压力的岩土材料进行剖面探测。在出现孔隙水响应的地层条件下，如黏土、粉土和含细粒砂土，还应测量贯入过程中的孔隙水压力（$u_m$），详见 ASTM D 5778 (2020) 和 ISO 22476-1 (2012) 关于孔压静力触探试验（CPTU）的规定。与许多涉及天然土体沉积和地层的场地勘察一样，由于事先并不清楚土类，因此宜优先采用 CPTU。

![image](images/rId10.png)

*图 1.3 电子型和孔压型触探探头的类型*

### 1.1.3 Unequal End Area Corrections

### 1.1.3 不等端面积修正

Porewater pressure readings can be taken at the apex or mid-face (designated $u_1$), shoulder position (just above the cone tip, or $u_2$), or behind the sleeve ($u_3$), but the standard required position is the shoulder position ($u_2$). This is because the effects of pressures at the joint warrants a systematic correction for the point stress, termed the total cone tip resistance ($q_t$) a.k.a. “corrected cone tip resistance” (Campanella et al. 1982; Lunne et al. 1997; Mayne 2007a):

孔隙水压力的量测位置可以是锥尖顶点或锥面中点（记为 $u_1$）、锥肩位置（锥尖正上方，记为 $u_2$）或摩擦套筒后方（$u_3$），但标准规定的量测位置是锥肩位置（$u_2$）。这是因为接缝处的压力效应要求对探头应力进行系统性修正，修正后的值称为总锥尖阻力（$q_t$），又称”修正锥尖阻力”（Campanella et al. 1982; Lunne et al. 1997; Mayne 2007a）：

$$q_t = q_c + (1 - a_{net}) \cdot u_2 \tag{1.1}$$

where $a_{net}$ is the net area ratio of the cone tip, as determined by calibration in a pressurized triaxial cell (Lunne et al. 1997; Mayne 2007). Preferably, $a_{net} \geq 0.80$ in order to minimize the correction magnitude, however, it should be realized that the available commercial systems in use have a range of $0.35 \leq a_{net} \leq 0.85$, with the result that some CPT systems require a large correction that can affect the reliability and uncertainty of measurements.

式中，$a_{net}$ 为锥尖的净面积比，通过在加压三轴压力室内标定确定（Lunne et al. 1997; Mayne 2007）。宜取 $a_{net} \geq 0.80$ 以使修正量最小，但需要注意的是，现有商用系统的 $a_{net}$ 范围为 $0.35 \leq a_{net} \leq 0.85$，因此部分 CPT 系统需要进行较大幅度的修正，这可能影响测量结果的可靠性和不确定性。

This is a systematic correction to the total cone tip resistance. It is presented here in the introduction because all subsequent use of the cone tip resistance will be in terms of $q_t$. Many published studies using the CPT in sands have continued to use the designation $q_c$ since the correction is small because $q_c$ is much greater than $u_2$. While this correction is small in sands, it is still a systematic correction and therefore more correct to report and use $q_t$.

这是对总锥尖阻力的系统性修正。在此处予以介绍，是因为后续所有涉及锥尖阻力的应用都将采用 $q_t$。许多在砂土中使用 CPT 的已发表研究仍沿用 $q_c$ 这一符号，因为在砂土中修正量很小——$q_c$ 远大于 $u_2$。尽管该修正在砂土中很小，但仍是系统性的，因此采用 $q_t$ 进行报告和使用更为正确。

The sleeve reading should also be corrected to a total sleeve resistance (i.e., $f_s \to f_{st}$), but this requires measurements of both $u_2$ and $u_3$, thus deemed impractical for production testing (Lunne et al. 1997). In that case, the sleeve should be designed with equal end areas top and bottom, in order to minimize the error. Note that some European manufacturers of CPT equipment have adopted an empirical $f_{st}$ correction procedure by assuming an interrelationship between $u_2$ and $u_3$ because their sleeve designs have unequal end areas. However, interrelationships between $u_2$ and $u_3$ are soil type-dependent and thus the "corrected" $f_{st}$ values can be in error, thereby misclassifying soil types and leading to other issues in interpretation.

侧壁摩阻力读数也应修正为总侧壁摩阻力（即 $f_s \to f_{st}$），但这需要同时量测 $u_2$ 和 $u_3$，因此在常规工程勘察中被认为不切实际（Lunne et al. 1997）。在此情况下，摩擦套筒应设计为上下端面积相等，以将误差降至最小。需要注意的是，部分欧洲 CPT 设备制造商因其套筒设计上下端面积不等，采用了一种经验性的 $f_{st}$ 修正方法，通过假设 $u_2$ 与 $u_3$ 之间的相关关系来推算。然而，$u_2$ 与 $u_3$ 之间的相关关系随土类而异，因此"修正后的" $f_{st}$ 值可能存在误差，从而导致土类误判及其他解释问题。

An approximate correction for sleeve friction is given by (Mayne 2007a):

侧壁摩阻力的近似修正公式如下（Mayne 2007a）：

$$f_{st} = f_s - b_{net} \cdot u_2 \tag{1.2}$$

where the $b_{net}$ value is also determined by calibration of the penetrometer in a pressurized triaxial cell. Some European penetrometers have $b_{net}$ values on the order of 0.005 to 0.02, which can be a significant correction in soft to firm clays. With equal areas on top and bottom of the sleeve, a value of $b_{net} = 0$ is ideal from a pragmatic standpoint.

式中，$b_{net}$ 值同样通过在加压三轴压力室内标定触探探头确定。部分欧洲触探探头的 $b_{net}$ 值约为 0.005 到 0.02，在软黏土到可塑黏土中可能需要进行显著修正。当摩擦套筒上下端面积相等时，$b_{net} = 0$ 是实用角度的理想值。

ConeTec uses penetrometers that are designed and manufactured in-house by Adara Systems Ltd. with values of $a_{net} = 0.80$ and $b_{net} = 0.0$, thus optimal for production testing. Therefore, the reported CPTU data are fully corrected.

ConeTec 使用的触探探头由旗下 Adara Systems Ltd. 自主设计和制造，其 $a_{net} = 0.80$、$b_{net} = 0.0$，适合常规工程勘察。因此，所报告的 CPTU 数据均为完全修正后的结果。

## 1.2 CPT Vehicles

## 1.2 CPT载具

A variety of specialized CPT rigs have been designed and built to facilitate the hydraulic pushing of cone penetrometers and other in-situ probes on a routine production basis. For full size capability, a 30-tonne hydraulic capacity system is mounted on a vehicle for field operations.

目前已设计和建造出了各种专业的CPT钻头，以便于在常规生产的基础上对锥形贯入仪和其他原位探测仪进行液压推动。对于全尺寸能力，车辆上配备有一个30吨的液压能力系统，用于现场作业。

Common types of vehicles include small- to medium- to large- trucks providing dead weight reactions on the order of 10 to 30 tonnes. For difficult access, CPT vehicles also include track-mounted rigs, all-terrain trucks, and portable frames. For light-weight rigs, anchoring systems can be provided to increase the total reaction capacity. A selection of cone rigs is shown in Figure 1.4.

常见的载具类型包括小型、中型和大型卡车，其提供的自重反力在10到30吨左右。对于难以进入的地方，CPT载具还包括履带式装置、全地形卡车和便携式框架。对于轻型装置，可以提供锚定系统来增加总反力。图1.4展示了一些锥形钻机的选择。

![image](images/rId13.png)

*Figure 1.4: Various Truck- and Track-Mounted CPT Vehicles for Production In-Situ Testing*

In situations involving overwater soundings, the platforms for CPT hydraulic systems can be barge, ship, jackup platforms, or special pushing frame units lowered overboard that are deployed remotely from the seabed. For mine tailings investigations, special amphibious rigs have been developed to facilitate difficult soft ground access. Also, in areas of remote access or limited headroom, portable hydraulic ram-set systems are available to provide CPT services.

在进行水上探测时，为CPT提供液压系统的平台可以是驳船、船舶、自升式平台或者是特殊的推框单元，可以从船舷上降下并远程部署到海床上。对于尾矿勘查，还开发了特殊的两栖装置，以便于进入困难的软土地面。此外，在偏远地区或空间有限的地方，可提供便携式液压拉杆系统，以提供CPT服务。

Hollow steel rods are typically 35.7 or 43.7 mm in diameter and usually added in 1-m increments. A pair of hydraulic or mechanical lateral grips grasp the rods during the vertical pushing (and eventual extraction). Routine depths of 30 m for CPT can be achieved in about 1 hour. In exceptional cases, soundings over 200 m have been completed.

空心钢杆的直径通常为35.7或43.7mm，并且通常以1m的增量添加。在垂直推进（以及最终提升）过程中，由一对液压或机械横向夹持装置夹住钢杆。CPT的常规深度可以在大约1小时内达到30m。在特殊情况下，也完成过200米以上的测深。

While a conventional drill rig can be employed for the purpose of CPTs, they are usually limited to soft ground conditions because of low dead-weight capacity, poor rate control during pushing, and slow rod handling operations due to a direct threaded rod connection with the kelly bar, rather than the mechanical or hydraulic grips used in CPT rigs. Moreover, many CPT soundings by drill rigs have terminated early due to inadequate capacity for reaction.

虽然传统的钻机可以用于进行CPT测试，但由于其自重能力低、在推进过程中控制速度差、以及由于使用凯利杆与直接螺纹杆连接而导致钢管搬运操作缓慢，通常仅限于软土地质条件。相比之下，CPT装置使用了机械或液压夹持装置，使得CPT测试更为灵活。此外，由于反作用力不足，许多使用钻机进行的CPT测试都被迫提前终止。

## 1.3 CPT Soundings

## 1.3 CPT读数

Results from the channels recorded during a piezocone sounding are plotted with depth, as illustrated from the CPTU in Virginia shown in Figure 1.5. In this case, the leftmost graph shows the profile of the corrected cone tip resistance (qt) versus depth, the middle graph presents the measured sleeve friction resistance (fs) versus depth, and the rightmost graph gives the penetration porewater pressures at the shoulder ($u_2$) versus depth. In the original metric system, units were given in kilograms per square centimeter (kg/cm2) or bars (1 bar = 1.02 kg/cm2), whereas in the System International (SI), kiloPascals (kPa) or MegaPascals (MPa) are preferred (1 bar = 100 kPa). In North America, it is still common to use English Imperial units for reporting the measured stresses and pressures (i.e., tons per square foot, or tsf). Also, it can be convenient to provide the results in terms of dimensionless values by dividing the pressures or stresses by atmospheric pressure (\sigmaatm). As a reasonable approximation, unit conversions can be expressed: 1 \sigmaatm ≈ 1 bar = 100 kPa ≈ 1 tsf ≈ 1 kg/cm2. Note: Exact conversions for stress and/or pressure: 1 \sigmaatm = 101.3 kPa = 1.058 tsf = 1.033 kg/cm2. Also note: 1 bar = 100 kPa. For units of length: 1 meter = 3.28 feet.

在孔压静力触探过程中记录的通道结果按深度绘制，如图1.5所示，展示了弗吉尼亚州的CPTU数据。在这种情况下，最左边的图表显示了修正锥尖阻力（qt）随深度的变化情况，中间的图表展示了测得的套筒摩阻力（fs）随深度的变化，而最右边的图表则给出了锥肩部处的贯入孔隙水压力（$u_2$）随深度的变化。在原始的公制系统中，单位通常以千克每平方厘米（kg/cm2）或巴（1 bar = 1.02 kg/cm2）表示，而在国际单位制（SI）中，更倾向于使用千帕（kPa）或兆帕（MPa）（1 bar = 100 kPa）。在北美，报告测量的应力和压力仍然普遍使用英制单位（例如，吨每平方英尺，即tsf）。此外，通过将压力或应力除以大气压力（\sigmaatm），可以方便地提供无量纲值的结果。作为合理的近似，单位换算可以表达为：1 \sigmaatm ≈ 1 bar = 100 kPa ≈ 1 tsf ≈ 1 kg/cm2。注意：应力和/或压力的精确换算为：1 \sigmaatm = 101.3 kPa = 1.058 tsf = 1.033 kg/cm2。另请注意：1 bar = 100 kPa。长度单位为：1 meter = 3.28 feet。

It is also common to report the penetration porewater pressures in terms of equivalent height of water (ℎw). In these cases, the equivalent ℎw= $u_2$/\gammaw, where \gammaw = unit weight of water ( \gammaw = 9.81 kN/m3 = 62.4 pcf).

通常也会用水的等效高度（ℎw）来表示贯入孔隙水压力。在这些情况下，ℎw = $u_2$/\gammaw，其中 \gammaw = 水的单位重量（\gammaw = 9.81 kN/m3 = 62.4 pcf）。

![image](images/rId14.png)

*Figure 1.5: Representative piezocone sounding in marine sediments at southeast Virginia*

## 1.4 Pore Pressure Dissipation (PPD) Tests

## 1.4 孔隙水压力消散（PPD）试验

The measured penetration porewater pressures reflect the prevailing soil drainage conditions surrounding the probe. Usually in loose to firm permeable clean sands, a drained response occurs ($u_2$ ≈ $u_0$), whereas in saturated clay soils having low permeability, an undrained condition arises. The latter results in a temporary elevated response of high excess porewater pressures caused locally by the insertion of the penetrometer. Similar manifestations occur with the installation of driven piling foundations and/or other in-situ probes into saturated fine-grained soils. If the penetration process is halted, the excess porewater pressures (\Deltau) will decay with time as they seek equilibrium. The monitoring of these readings with time is termed a dissipation test.

测量的贯入孔隙水压力反映了探头周围土壤排水条件的主导情况。通常在松散至坚实的透水纯砂中，会发生排水响应（$u_2$≈$u_0$），而在低透水性饱和粘土土壤中则出现不排水条件。在后一种土壤条件下，探头插入会引起局部的临时过高的超孔隙水压力响应。在将贯入桩基础和/或其他原位探测器安装到饱和细粒土壤中时，会出现类似的表现。如果停止贯入过程，超孔隙水压力（\Deltau）将随时间衰减，直至平衡。监测这些读数随时间的变化被称为消散试验。

In most soft clays and silts, the porewater pressures will decrease monotonically with time, with a maximum value occurring during penetration ($u_2$), then dropping and eventually reaching an equilibrium porewater pressure ($u_0$). Since the rate of dissipations slow with time, they are often alternatively plotted on logarithmic time scales or square root time plots to show the full response records (Styler et al. 2019). As such, it is not convenient or practical to wait for 100% dissipation, therefore many dissipation tests are taken only to 50% completion, whereby regular CPT operations are resumed and the penetrometer pushed at 20 mm/s. From a CPTU conducted near the Mississippi River in Memphis, TN, Figure 1.6 shows a measured monotonic dissipation record at a depth of 17.1 m.

在大多数软黏土和淤泥中，孔隙水压力会随时间单调下降，贯入过程中达到最大值（$u_2$），然后逐渐降低并最终达到平衡孔隙水压力（$u_0$）。由于消散速率随时间变慢，通常会选择在对数时间尺度或平方根时间图上绘制来显示完整的响应记录（Styler等，2019）。因此，等待孔隙水压力完全消散并不方便或实际可行，因此许多消散试验仅进行到50%的完成度，之后恢复常规的圆锥静力触探测试操作，以20 mm/s的速度推动探头。来自田纳西州孟菲斯市密西西比河附近的孔压静力触探测试（CPTU）显示，图1.6展示了在深度17.1米处测得的单调消散记录。

![image](images/rId15.png)

*Figure 1.6: Example piezo-dissipation results from Mud Island, Tennessee, including: (a) Monotonic pore pressure response at 17.1 m, and (b) Dilatory behavior at 7.1 m*

For the shoulder filter position, dilatory porewater response can also be observed, whereby the pressures initially increase up to a peak value followed subsequently by decay with time. Dilatory behavior is primarily encountered in overconsolidated clays, silts, and dense dilative sandy soils. Figure 1.6 shows an example dilatory dissipation curve at a depth of 7.1 meters.

对于圆锥肩部透水石处，还可以观察到扩散孔隙水响应，其中压力最初上升至峰值，随后随时间衰减。扩张行为主要出现在超固结黏土、淤泥和密实的膨胀砂土中。图1.6展示了在深度7.1米处的一个扩张消散曲线示例。

## 1.5 Stratigraphic Profiling

## 1.5 地层剖面

Because of its ability to record continuous resistances with depth, the cone penetrometer is unrivaled in its ability to detect changes in subsurface strata and delineate soil layering, as well as also identify weak zones, thin lenses, and anomalous ground conditions. Not only is this evident for one channel of readings, but for all three simultaneous penetrometer recordings obtained using a piezocone

由于其能够记录随深度连续阻力，锥形贯入仪在探测地下岩层变化、描绘土层分层以及识别薄弱地区、薄晶体和异常地质条件方面无与伦比。这种优势不仅体现在单桥的读数上，同时也适用于使用孔压静力触探同时获取的三个读数。

The detail in piezcone profiling can be seen in the CPT records from New Orleans presented in Figure 1.7. The data acquisition system captures readings at approximately 1 to 2 seconds, therefore resolution in the profiling capabilities is generally in the 1 cm to 5 cm range. Moreover, Figure 1.7 shows the reliability and repeatability of CPT soundings from the results of two adjacent soundings. These were made by two separate cone rigs, crews, and penetrometer systems where the tests were made some three months apart. Other than minor differences due to localized sand content and silt lenses and silty sand pockets, both soundings show remarkable agreement in the cone tip resistance (qt), sleeve friction (fs), and penetration porewater pressure ($u_2$) readings with depth.

孔压静力触探探测的详细信息可以在图1.7中呈现的新奥尔良的圆锥静力触探（CPT）记录中看到。数据采集系统以大约1到2秒的频率捕获读数，因此在探测能力方面，分辨率通常在1厘米到5厘米的范围内。此外，图1.7为来自两个相邻探测的结果，显示了CPT探测的可靠性和重复性。这两次测试由两台不同的圆锥静力触探设备、两组不同的工作人员和贯入仪系统进行，测试时间相隔约三个月。除了由于局部沙含量、淤泥晶体和泥质沙孔袋引起的轻微差异外，两个探测在深度上显示出锥尖端阻力（qt）、套筒摩擦力（fs）和贯入孔隙水压力（$u_2$）读数方面显著的一致性。

![image](images/rId16.png)

*Figure 1.7: Profiling and repeatability of CPTU readings with depth using two separate rigs*

## 1.6 CPT Soil Identification by Rules of Thumb

## 1.6 利用圆锥静力触探进行土分类的经验法则

Some simple rules-of-thumb can be used to identify coarse-grained soils (sands) versus fine-grained soils (clays and silts) by visual examination of the cone readings. For reference, the following benchmark values are established: (a) cone resistance $q_t$ = 50 atm; (b) friction ratio $R_f = 100 \cdot f_s/q_t = 1\%$, and (c) $u_2 = u_0$. For illustration, a CPTU example from Biloxi Mississippi is shown in Figure 1.8. For the cone tip resistance presented in Figure 1.8a, a reference value $q_t$ = 50 atm is drawn, whereby measured $q_t$ > 50 atm imply sands and $q_t$ < 50 atm suggests clays. At this site, it can be seen that the upper 7 meters of soils contain alternating layers and lenses of sand and clay. A thick clay layer extends from 7 to 15.5 m that is underlain by a sand layer from 15.5 m to 20 m, below which indicates clay until the sounding termination depth at 25 m.

通过观察锥尖读数，可以使用一些简单的经验法则来区分粗粒土（砂）和细粒土（粘土和淤泥）。为参考起见，建立了以下基准值：(a) 锥尖阻力 qt = 50 atm；(b) 摩擦比 Rf = 100∙fs/qt = 1%；以及 (c) 孔隙水压力 $u_2$ = $u_0$。示例如图1.8所示，展示了来自密西西比州比洛克西的CPTU数据。在图1.8a中呈现的锥尖阻力中，绘制了参考值 qt = 50 atm，测得的 qt > 50 atm 表明为砂土，qt < 50 atm 则为粘土。在该现场，可以看到土壤的上部7米包含交替的砂晶体和粘土层。从7米到15.5米存在一层厚厚的粘土层，其下方是从15.5米到20米的砂层，再往下直到探测终止深度25米的位置，都是粘土。

![image](images/rId17.png)

*Figure 1.8: Piezocone record illustrating visual soil identification method and rules of thumb*

For the friction sleeve reading, it is convenient to plot the friction ratio, $R_f = 100 \cdot f_s/q_t$ (%). As such, clean sands are identified by $R_f < 1\%$, whereas fine-grained insensitive silts and clays often exhibit $R_f > 1\%$. Using this criterion, a similar geostratigraphy can be ascertained at the site from the $R_f$ profile. Note, however, for sensitive to quick clays, the friction ratio is small and often approaches zero. Thus, the $R_f$ criterion should not be solely used for soil type evaluation but in combination with the $q_t$ and $u_2$ readings. Additionally, when the friction ratio exceeds about 8% it is an indication of organic soils. In Figure 1.8b, the rule of thumb suggests an organic clayey layer from depths of about 1 to 3 m.

对于摩擦套筒读数，便于绘制摩擦比例，Rf = 100 · fs/qt (%)。因此，通过Rf的值可以识别纯砂，其Rf < 1%，而细粒不敏感的淤泥和粘土通常显示出Rf > 1%。利用这一标准，可以通过Rf剖面确定现场的类似地层构造。然而，请注意，对于敏感的快速粘土，摩擦比很小，通常接近零。因此，Rf标准不应单独用于土壤类型评估，而是与qt和$u_2$读数结合使用。此外，当摩擦比例超过约8%时，这表明土壤中存在有机质。在图1.8b中，经验法则表明在深度约1到3米处存在有机粘土层。

Lastly, for the porewater pressure channel, it is advantageous to plot the hydrostatic porewater pressure line as a reference:

最后，对于孔隙水压力通道，绘制静水压孔隙水压力线作为参考是有益的：

$$u_0 = h_w \cdot \gamma_w \tag{1.3}$$

where $h_w$ = height of the water (depth less groundwater table) and $\gamma_w$ = unit weight of water (freshwater: $\gamma_w$ = 9.81 kN/m³ = 62.4 pcf). In Figure 1.8c, the groundwater lies 5 m deep and a dashed blue line is shown to represent hydrostatic conditions. Above the groundwater table, the ambient $u_0$ is often taken equal to zero in clean sands; however, in clays and fine-grained soil materials, $u_0$ can be negative due to capillarity effects depending upon the degree of saturation, recent rainfall, soil permeability, and other site features.

其中，ℎw = 水的高度（深度减去地下水位）而 \gammaw = 水的单位重量（淡水：\gammaw = 9.81 kN/m³ = 62.4 pcf）。在图1.8c中，地下水位深处5米，虚线蓝线表示静水压情况。在地下水位以上，环境中的u₀在纯砂中通常被认为等于零；然而，在粘土和细粒土壤材料中，由于毛细作用的影响，u₀可以是负的，这取决于饱和度、最近的降雨、土壤渗透性和其他场地特征。

In clean “hourglass” sands, the measured porewater pressures are often close to hydrostatic ($u_2 \approx u_0$). In Figure 1.8, the sand layers can be identified by this simple rule. However, if the sands are very dense, dilatancy may result in $u_2$ readings less than $u_0$. Below the groundwater table, intact clays can be found by examining where $u_2 \gg u_0$. As a general guide, the ratio of $u_2/u_0$ increases with clay compactness, such that for soft clays $u_2/u_0 \approx 2$; firm clays $u_2/u_0 \approx 4$; stiff clays $u_2/u_0 \approx 8$; and hard clays: $u_2/u_0 \approx> 15$. Referencing Figure 1.8c, the thick clay layer from 7 to 15.5 m can be easily discerned in this manner. The lower clay layer at depths below 20 m is also evident. Of additional mention is the fine detail in soil profiling collected by CPTU, specifically the $q_t$ and $u_2$ readings at a depth of 20.2 m in Figure 1.8 implicate a thin sandy lense.

在纯净的“沙漏”砂土中，测得的孔隙水压力通常接近静水压（$u_2$≈$u_0$）。在图1.8中，可以通过这个简单规则识别沙层。然而，如果砂土非常密实，膨胀性可能导致$u_2$读数小于$u_0$。在地下水位以下，通过检查$u_2$ ≫ $u_0$的位置可以找到完整的粘土层。作为一般的指导，$u_2$/$u_0$的比率随着粘土的紧密程度而增加，例如软粘土$u_2$/$u_0$≈2；坚硬粘土$u_2$/$u_0$≈4；硬粘土$u_2$/$u_0$≈8；非常坚硬的粘土$u_2$/$u_0$≈> 15。参考图1.8c，可以通过这种方式轻松辨别从7到15.5米处的厚粘土层。在20米以下深度还可见到更低比率的粘土层。值得额外提及的是CPTU得到的土壤剖面的细节，特别是在图1.8中20.2米深处的qt和$u_2$读数暗示了一个薄砂层。

Of final note, the magnitude of porewater pressures in stiff fissured overconsolidated soils are often less than hydrostatic ($u_2 < u_0$), in fact, can often be negative and may reach values as low as $u_2 \approx -100$ kPa (Mayne et al. 1990).

最后需要注意的是，在坚硬的裂缝多、超固结土壤中，孔隙水压力的大小通常小于静水压（$u_2$ < $u_0$），事实上，它们通常可以是负数，且可能达到$u_2$≈-100 kPa（Mayne 等, 1990）。

## 1.7 Seismic Cone Penetration Testing (SCPTU)

## 1.7 地震波孔压静力触探试验

Of particular value in routine site exploration is the seismic piezocone test (SCPTU) as it is a hybrid procedure that combines cone penetrometer recordings (ASTM D5778) with geophysical downhole shear wave velocity measurements (ASTM D7400) into one sounding (Figure 1.9).

在常规现场勘探中特别有价值的是地震波孔压静力触探试验（SCPTU），因为它是一种混合测量方法，将锥形贯入读数（ASTM D5778）和地球物理孔内剪波速度测量（ASTM D7400）合并为一次探测（见图1.9）。

During the penetration portion of the SCPTU, continuous records of $q_t$, $f_s$, and $u_2$ are collected over a 1-m interval, whereby during the temporary halt for the next rod addition, a geophysical downhole test (DST) is performed. This involves an impulse-type surface source generator to create a shear wavelet that is monitored by one or more geophones or accelerometers located within the penetrometer.

在地震波孔压静力触探（SCPTU）的贯入阶段，连续记录了每1米间隔的qt、fs和$u_2$数据，在短暂停顿以添加下一个钻杆时，则进行地球物理孔内测试（DST）。这包括使用脉冲型表面源发生器产生一个剪切波，由贯入仪内的一个或多个地震仪或加速度计监测。

For the DST, the shear wave is a vertically-propagating and horizontally-polarized mode ($V_{S,VH}$ type) with the length of the surface source oriented in the same parallel plane as the geophone axis. A single horizontal geophone can be used (pseudo-interval method), or else two geophones at different elevations (true-interval). In addition, biaxial sets of geophones at the same elevation can be used to account for orientation alignments due to rotation or off-parallel setups. Triaxial geophone arrays provide a third (vertical) component that can collect information on compression wave arrivals (P-waves).

对于地球物理孔内测试（DST），剪切波是一种垂直传播和水平极化的模式（VS,VH类型），表面源的长度与地震仪轴线平行的平面内方向一致。可以使用单个水平地震仪（伪间隔法），或者在不同高程使用两个地震仪（真间隔法）。此外，同一高程的双轴地震仪组也可以用来考虑由于旋转或非平行设置而引起的方向对齐。三轴地震仪阵列提供第三个（垂直）分量，可以收集关于压缩波到达（P波）的信息。

The original setup for SCPTU simply employed a sledgehammer and crossbeam for the source (Campanella et al. 1986). Pairs of left- and right-strikes were required at each depth to ascertain the arrival of the shear wave, using either the first arrival, where the two signals diverge, or a procedure termed first crossover, at the point where the two signals cross after the initial main wavelet. A set of paired left- and right-strikes at each one-meter depth are presented in Figure 1.10. Here, the SCPTU was performed to a final test depth of 43 m in Charleston, SC. The left wavelets are shown as red lines and the right wavelets as blue lines. The downhole shear wave velocity is determined for each vertical depth interval from measured arrival times obtained at two successive depths (Campanella 1994):

最初的SCPTU简单地使用了一把铁锤和十字梁作为震源（Campanella等人，1986年）。在每个深度，需要进行左右两次敲击，以确定剪切波的到达时间，可以使用第一到达时间，即两个信号分开的点，或称为首次交叉的程序，在初始主波群之后两个信号交叉的点。图1.10展示了每个1米深度处一组左右两次敲击。在此示例中，SCPTU在南卡罗来纳州查尔斯顿市进行，最终测试深度达到了43米。左侧波形显示为红线，右侧波形显示为蓝线。从在两个连续深度测得的到达时间中，可以确定每个垂直深度间隔的孔内剪切波速度（Campanella，1994年）：

$$V_S = \frac{R_2 - R_1}{t_2 - t_1} \tag{1.4}$$

where $R$ = hypotenuse distance = $\sqrt{z^2 + x^2}$ at each depth, $z$ = depth, $t$ = characteristic time at depth $z$, and $x$ = perpendicular offset distance from the horizontal source to the axis of the vertical downhole test. Note that the distance $x$ should be kept to less than 1 m so that waves are $V_{S,VH}$ type. Moreover, if $x$ > 1 m, it will be necessary to consider the correction of time arrivals for the interval time method analysis (Hallal & Cox 2019; Stolte & Cox 2020).

在每个深度处，其中 R = 斜边距离 = √(z² + x²)，其中 z = 深度，t = 在深度 z 处的特征时间，x = 水平震源到垂直孔内测试轴线的垂直偏移距离。请注意，距离 x 应保持小于1米，以确保波形为VsVH类型。此外，如果 x > 1 米，则需要考虑时间到达的修正，以进行间隔时间方法分析（Hallal & Cox，2019年；Stolte & Cox，2020年）。

![image](images/rId20.png)

*Figure 1.9: Setup and Procedure for Conducting Seismic Piezocone Test (SCPTU) using the Downhole Test (DST) for evaluation of shear wave velocity (Vs)*

![image](images/rId21.png)

*Figure 1.10: Paired Wavelets from Left- and Right-strikes at one-meter Depth Intervals During Downhole Testing by Seismic Cone in Charleston, SC*

The interpretation of Vs from downhole testing can be made using: (a) first arrival time of shear waves (when the left and right strikes first diverge), (b) first crossover, (c) cross-correlation, and/or (d) frequency domain methods (Liao & Mayne 2006; Ku et al. 2013a, 2013b). These can be applied to pseudo-interval and true-interval DST methods. In the cross-correlation method, each successive wavelet is matched to the prior event to determine the incremental time difference over the depth interval. The cross-correlation method for post-processing of shear waves can be handled via Excel spreadsheets, Matlab, or special geophysics software packages.

从孔内测试中解译Vs可以使用以下方法：(a) 剪切波的第一到达时间（左右敲击首次分离时），(b) 第一次交叉，(c) 交叉相关，以及/或者(d) 频域方法（Liao & Mayne，2006年；Ku等，2013a, 2013b）。这些方法可以应用于伪间隔和真实间隔的动态响应测试方法。在交叉相关方法中，每个连续的波形与前一个事件匹配，以确定深度间隔内的增量时间差异。用于剪切波后处理的交叉相关方法可以通过Excel电子表格、Matlab或特殊的地球物理软件包来处理。

Commercial automatic hydraulic hammers have been in industry use for about 20 years. These devices use an internal hydraulic cylinder to strike two plates in both directions and generate reverse polarity waves. They are fast, efficient, and create reproducible seismic waves.

商业自动液压锤已在工业中使用约20年。这些设备使用内部液压缸在两个方向上敲击两个板，并生成反向极性波。它们速度快、高效，并且能够产生可复制的地震波。

Improvements in the recorded signals, reliability, and quality of the derived shear wave profiles are attained by use of autoseis units because of their repeatability and consistency (Mayne and McGillivray 2008). Autoseis units have been developed on the basis of electrical, electromechanical, pneumatic, and hydraulic force impact (Figure 1.11). For shallow profiling of shear waves at depths < 30 m (100 feet), a portable electric autoseis unit will suffice. For deeper soundings, a heavy-duty hydraulic autoseis may be preferred. An alternate approach is to use stacking, whereby several successive strikes are averaged to increase the signal to noise ratio and improve the wavelet qualities.

通过使用自动地震单元，改进了记录信号、可靠性和所得剪切波剖面的质量，这是由于它们的重复性和一致性（Mayne和McGillivray，2008年）。自动地震单元是基于电力、电机电动、气动和液压冲击力发展而来的（见图1.11）。对于深度小于30米（100英尺）的浅层剖面剪切波，便携式电动自动地震单元就足够了。对于更深的探测，可能更倾向于使用重型液压自动地震单元。另一种方法是使用叠加技术，通过对几次连续冲击进行平均，以增加信噪比并改善波形质量。

![image](images/rId22.png)

*Figure 1.11: Selection of Electric, Electromechanical, and Hydraulic Type Autoseis Generators for use in Downhole Shear Wave Measurements*

The combined results of the alternating field procedure of cone penetrometer testing (CPTU) and geophysical downhole testing (DST) at one-meter depth intervals form a hybrid method termed seismic piezocone testing (SCPTU). An illustrative example of a SCPTU sounding from Hartford, CT is presented in Figure 1.12 showing all four readings with depth: $q_t$, $f_s$, $u_2$, and $V_s$.

在孔压静力触探（CPTU）和地球物理孔内测试（DST）交替领域过程的结合结果，以1米深度间隔形成了一种名为地震波孔压静力触探（SCPTU）的杂交方法。图1.12展示了来自康涅狄格州哈特福德的一个SCPTU探测示例，显示了深度为qt、fs、$u_2$和Vs的所有四个读数。

![image](images/rId23.png)

*Figure 1.12: Seismic Piezocone Test (SCPTU) from Hartford, Connecticut*

An example of SCPTU results taken for the Pitt River Bridge in British Columbia is presented as Figure 1.13 (Tara 2012). These results show the exceptional depths on the order of 96 m (315 feet) that can be achieved using seismic piezocone testing. This offers significant advantages over routine methods that rely on drilling, sampling, casing, and borehole geophysics that are approximately ten times more costly in terms of field time and budget expenses.

以不列颠哥伦比亚省的皮特河大桥为例，图1.13（Tara 2012）展示了进行地震波孔压静力触探（SCPTU）所得到的结果。这些结果显示了可以通过地震波孔压静力触探达到的卓越深度，大约为96米（315英尺）。相比于依赖钻探、取样、套管和地球物理测井学等的常规方法，地震波孔压静力触探在现场时间和预算费用方面约为这些方法的十分之一，这带来了显著的优势。

![image](images/rId24.png)

*Figure 1.13: Results from 95-m deep SCPTU at Pitt River Bridge, British Columbia*

## 1.8 Site Characterization

## 1.8 场地特征

Geotechnical site investigations encompass the entire suite of all in-situ tests, sampling, laboratory tests, geophysical measurements, and field observations made on a site. A site investigation is simply a set of measurements or observations in time and space.

岩土工程现场勘察涵盖了在现场进行的所有原位试验、取样、实验室测试、地球物理测量以及现场观察的全套内容。现场勘察简单来说就是对某一地点在时间和空间上进行的一系列测量或观察。

Geocharacterization combines the site investigation with geological inference and engineering judgment and interpretation in order to create geotechnical models for engineering analysis and design. These models may include 2D and 3D cross-sections that bridge the divide between the sparse results from the geotechnical site investigation. Detailed site characterization is highly dependent on local experience and judgment.

地质特征描述结合了现场勘察、地质推断以及工程判断和解释，旨在创建用于工程分析和设计的岩土模型。这些模型可能包括二维和三维剖面图，以弥补岩土工程现场勘察结果的不足。详细的现场特征描述在很大程度上依赖于当地的经验和判断。

The most important part of the site characterization is to define the geometry of the problem. This geometry is the spatial location and extent of various soil layers. The continuous depth profiles from cone penetration testing are ideal for delineating the location and thickness of various soil layers.

场地特征描述中最重要的部分是定义问题的几何形状。这种几何形状指的是各种土层的空间位置和范围。圆锥静力触探试验得出的连续深度剖面非常适合描绘各种土层的位置和厚度。

A common concern is an inadequate site investigation. The site investigation plan details the numbers, locations, and target depths of the field testing and sampling program. The sufficiency of a site investigation clearly depends on the project and geology. A preliminary investigation may only consist of a few soundings and depending upon the complexity of the stratigraphy, soil layering, strata inclination, and degree of homogeneity and/or heterogeneity of the site, the final geotechnical program may need to consider a good number of soundings that are supplemented with drilling, soil borings, sampling, and laboratory testing as well as geophysics in order to provide adequate information for analysis and design.

常见的问题是现场勘察不足。现场勘察计划详细描述了现场测试和取样计划的数量、位置和目标深度。现场勘察的充分性显然取决于项目和地质条件。初步勘察可能只包括几个CPT探测，根据地层复杂性、土层分层情况、地层倾斜度以及现场均匀性或异质性程度，最终的岩土工程方案可能需要考虑大量CPT探测，并配合钻探、岩土取样、实验室测试以及地球物理测试，以提供足够的信息进行分析和设计。

## 1.9 Objectives

## 1.9 目的

The objective of this guide is to be a reference for interpreting CPT results. The Reader should be familiar with the basic principles of soil mechanics, such as covered by Holtz et al. (2011).

本指南的目标是作为解译圆锥静力触探（CPT）结果的参考。读者应该熟悉土力学的基本原理，如Holtz等人（2011年）所覆盖的内容。

Chapter 1 has shown how the geostratigraphy of a site can be determined directly with a few simple rules-of-thumb from CPT logs. Only once the geometry of the problem is defined do these soil properties have meaning in the geotechnical analysis and design. Further analysis of the CPT results requires a characterization of unit weights, overburden calculations, equilibrium porewater pressures, and effective stress profile, as covered in Chapter 2. Soil behavior type charts can then be used to infer the soil types based on how they respond to an advancing cone probe, as covered in Chapter 3. The stress history of soils is reflected by its preconsolidation, or yield stress, which affects soil response and many aspects of other dependent geoparameters as outlined in Chapter 4.

第一章已经展示了如何通过几个简单的经验法则从圆锥静力触探试验（CPT）记录中直接确定现场的地层地质情况。只有一旦问题的几何形状被定义，这些土壤性质才在岩土工程分析和设计中具有意义。进一步分析CPT结果需要对重度、过载计算、平衡孔隙水压力和有效应力剖面进行表征，这些内容在第二章中有所涵盖。土壤行为类型图表可以根据土壤在锥形探头推进时的响应来推断土壤类型，这部分内容在第三章中有所介绍。土壤的应力历史通过其先期固结或屈服应力来反映，这影响了土壤的响应以及其他依赖地质参数的多个方面，如第四章所述。

The effective friction angle is a fundamental property of the soil and its importance to CPT interpretation is covered in Chapter 5. For clays, the undrained shear strength (Chapter 6) is a total stress parameter that is needed for bearing capacity of shallow foundations and axial, lateral, and moment capacity of deep foundations, as well as used in slope stability, excavations, and embankment analyses. Chapter 7 details a discussion of ground stiffness, including soil modulus that is highly nonlinear starting from the nondestructive region to intermediate strains to large strains. The analysis of porewater pressure dissipation curves is presented in Chapter 8 to ascertain the in-situ values of flow parameters, including the coefficient of consolidation (cv) and soil permeability (k).

第五章涵盖了有效摩擦角，这是土壤的一个基本性质，对圆锥静力触探试验（CPT）解译的重要性进行了讨论。对于粘土，不排水抗剪强度（第六章）是一个总应力参数，用于浅基础的承载能力，以及深基础的轴向、侧向和弯矩承载能力，同时也用于边坡稳定性、挖掘和路堤分析。第七章详细讨论了地基刚度，包括从非破坏区到中等应变到大应变的高度非线性土壤模量。第八章介绍了孔隙水压力消散曲线的分析，以确定原位流参数的值，包括固结系数（cv）和土壤渗透性（k）。

Of increasing importance to society, the phenomena of soil liquefaction, both flow (or static) liquefaction that can occur in earth embankment dams, tailings, and fly ash impoundments and cyclic liquefaction caused by earthquakes, as evaluated using CPT, are detailed in Chapter 9.

第九章详细讨论了对社会日益重要的土壤液化现象，包括在土堤坝、尾矿、粉煤灰堆放场等地发生的流态（或静态）液化，以及地震引起的循环液化，这些通过圆锥静力触探试验（CPT）进行评估。

This guide concludes with a brief description of special advanced cone testing topics (Chapter 10). Additional CPT procedures (e.g., variable rate testing; P-wave measurements) and cone modules (e.g., gamma, resistivity) have special applications where they might provide more information and additional soil properties that cannot be obtained with the standard cone penetration test.

该指南在第十章中简要描述了特殊的改进锥形探针测试。额外的CPT程序（例如，可变速率测试；P波测量）和锥模块（例如，伽马射线，电阻率）在特定应用中具有特殊的应用，它们可以提供更多信息和额外的土壤特性，这些特性无法通过标准的圆锥静力触探获取。

# 2 Soil Unit Weight and Overburden Stresses

# 2 土的重度和上覆层应力

The behavior of soils depends upon the effective stresses in the ground. As stated by the first principle of soil mechanics, the effective stress is equal to the total overburden stress minus the porewater pressure. The total overburden is the accumulation of soil unit weight times soil layer thickness and is caused by gravitation forces acting on the soil particles, while the porewater pressure is a buoyancy phenomenon. As such, the unit weight of soils is needed in order to calculate total and effective overburden stresses with depth.

土壤的行为取决于地面下的有效应力。正如土力学的第一原理所述，有效应力等于总上覆层应力减去孔隙水压力。总上覆层是土的重度乘以土层厚度，由重力作用在土壤颗粒上引起，而孔隙水压力则是一种浮力现象。因此，需要土的重度来计算深度上的总上覆层应力和有效上覆层应力。

It is routine practice to estimate total vertical stress profiles for interpreting CPT logs. This is accomplished by either assuming, measuring, or estimating total unit weights for the soil layers.

解释圆锥静力触探（CPT）记录的常规做法是估算总垂直应力剖面。这通常通过假设、测量或估算土层的总重度来实现。

## 2.1 Total Soil Unit Weight ($\gamma_t$)

## 2.1 土的总重度($\gamma_t$)

Soil unit weights ($\gamma_t$) are best obtained by securing “undisturbed” samples (e.g., thin-walled Shelby tubes; piston samples; Laval samples) from various depths. The ratio of the weight of the sample over a known volume of soil provide the total unit weight: $\gamma_t = W/V$. The profile of soil unit weight with depth is needed in the calculation of overburden stresses, as well as in the conversion of shear wave velocity to small-strain shear modulus. The unit weight relates to the more fundamental mass density ($\rho_t$) through Equation 2.1.

土的重度（$\gamma_t$）最好通过从不同深度获取”未扰动”的样品（例如薄壁Shelby管；活塞样品；Laval样品）来获得。样品的重量与已知土壤体积的比率提供了总重度：$\gamma_t = W/V$。土壤重度随深度的剖面在计算上覆层应力时是必需的，也在将剪切波速度转换为小应变剪切模量时需要用到。重度与更基本的质量密度（$\rho_t$）之间的关系由方程式2.1给出。

$$\gamma_t = \rho_t \cdot g_a \tag{2.1}$$

where $g_a$ = gravitational constant (= 9.8 m/s² = 32.2 ft/s²).

Soil identity relationships provide information about the air-water-solids composition of the soil.

One primary soil identity expression is:

在这里，$g_a$=重力常数（= 9.8 m/s² = 32.2 ft/s²）。

土壤特性关系提供了有关土壤中空气、水和固体组成的信息。

一个主要的土壤特性表达式是：

$$G_s \cdot w_n = S \cdot e_0 \tag{2.2}$$

where $G_s$ is the specific gravity of solids (for “normal” soils: $G_s$ = 2.70 ± 0.1), $w_n$ is the natural (gravimetric) water content (= $W_w/W_s$ which can vary from zero in dry soils to 1000% in highly plastic montmorillonite), $W_w$ = weight of water, $W_s$ = weight of solids, S is the degree of saturation (= $V_w/V_v$ ranges from zero in dry soil to 100% in fully saturated soils), and $e_0$ is the initial void ratio (= $V_v/V_s$), where $V_w$ = volume of water, $V_s$ = volume of solids, and $V_v$ = volume of voids.

The total unit weight can be calculated in terms of these variables using:

其中 $G_s$ 是固体的比重（对于”正常”土壤：$G_s$ = 2.70 ± 0.1），$w_n$ 是自然（重量法）含水量（= $W_w/W_s$，在干燥土壤中可以为零，在高塑性蒙脱石中可以达到1000%），$W_w$ 是水的重量，$W_s$ 是固体的重量，S 是饱和度（= $V_w/V_v$，在干燥土壤中为零，在完全饱和的土壤中为100%），$e_0$是初始孔隙比（= $V_v/V_s$），其中$V_w$是水的体积，$V_s$ 是固体的体积，$V_v$是孔隙的体积。

可以利用这些变量来计算总重度：

$$\gamma_t = \left(\frac{1+w_n}{1+e_0}\right) \cdot G_s \cdot \gamma_w \tag{2.3}$$

where $\gamma_w$ is the unit weight of water ($\gamma_w$ = 9.8 kN/m3 = 62.4 pcf for freshwater; and $\gamma_w$ ≈ 10.0 kN/m3 = 64.0 pcf for salt water). Depending upon the water content and degree of saturation, two boundary cases are commonly taken in soil mechanics: (a) completely dry soil (with $w_n$ = 0); and (b) fully-saturated soil with S = 1 (and then: $e_0$ = Gs ∙ $w_n$). This gives:

$\gamma_w$ 是水的重度（对于淡水，$\gamma_w$ = 9.8 kN/m³ = 62.4 pcf；对于盐水，$\gamma_w$≈10.0 kN/m³ = 64.0 pcf）。根据土力学的要求，根据含水量和饱和度通常考虑两种边界情况：(a) 完全干燥的土壤（$w_n$ = 0）；以及 (b) 饱和的土壤，饱和度 S = 1（此时：$e_0$ = Gs ∙ $w_n$）。

$$\gamma_{dry} = \frac{G_s \cdot \gamma_w}{1+e_0} \tag{2.4}$$

$$\gamma_{sat} = \left(\frac{G_s+e_0}{1+e_0}\right) \cdot \gamma_w \tag{2.5}$$

An extreme case would be a geomaterial with zero porosity ($e_0$ = 0) corresponding to solid rock:

一个极端情况是具有零孔隙率（$e_0$ = 0）的岩石：

$$\gamma_{rock} = G_s \cdot \gamma_w \tag{2.6}$$

Thus the hierarchy for assignment of unit weights would be $\gamma_{dry}$ ≤ $\gamma_t$ ≤ $\gamma_{sat}$ ≤ $\gamma_{rock}$.

For soils above the groundwater table, a dry unit weight would apply for no capillarity (i.e., clean sands), while if full capillarity exists (e.g., clays), then a saturated unit weight would be appropriate. If the soil is partially saturated, the total unit weight will depend upon the ambient degree of saturation, likely a value that changes with the weather, humidity, and temperature.

因此，重度的分配层次将是 $\gamma_{dry}$ ≤ $\gamma_t$ ≤ $\gamma_{sat}$ ≤ $\gamma_{rock}$。

对于地下水位以上的土壤，干重度适用于无毛细作用的情况（例如，纯净沙土），而如果存在完全的毛细作用（例如，黏土），则饱和重度更为合适。如果土壤部分饱和，总重度将取决于环境的饱和度，这通常是一个随着天气、湿度和温度变化的值。

For most soils below the water table, it is often assumed that the total unit weight is equal to the saturated unit weight. In some cases, calculations involve the effective unit weight (γ′ = $\gamma_{sat}$ − $\gamma_w$), also referred to as the buoyant unit weight or submerged unit weight.

As these unit weights are in terms of void ratio, degree of saturation, and specific gravity, the direct relationships between $\gamma_{dry}$, $\gamma_{total}$, and $\gamma_{sat}$ from equation (2.3) for a specific gravity of solids Gs = 2.70 are shown in Figure 2.1.

对于大多数地下水位以下的土壤，通常假设总重度等于饱和重度。在某些情况下，计算涉及有效重度（γ′= $\gamma_{sat}$ − $\gamma_w$），也称为浮重度或浸没重度。

由于这些重度涉及到孔隙比、饱和度和固体比重，方程式（2.3）中对于固体比重 Gs = 2.70 的 $\gamma_{dry}$, $\gamma_t$otal 和 $\gamma_{sat}$ 之间的直接关系如图2.1所示。

![image](images/rId30.png)

*Figure 2.1: Interrelationship between soil unit weight, void ratio, and saturation*

For immediate results, direct in-situ methods for ascertaining soil unit weights are available using gamma ray density penetrometers (Sully and Eschesuria 1988). These probes find use on critical and demanding projects. However, the utilization of these probes adds appreciable burdens in terms of time, costs, environmental risks, and/or safety during deployment on regular day-to-day projects. Therefore, it is of interest to have indirect CPTU methods for assessing unit weights of geomaterials from the basic penetrometer readings ($q_t$, $f_s$, and $u_2$) as well as shear wave velocity ($V_s$).

为了即时获得结果，可以利用伽马射线密度贯入仪（Sully和Eschesuria，1988年）的直接原位方法来确定土壤重度。这些探测器广泛应用于关键和高要求的项目中。然而，这些探测器的使用在日常项目中会增加相当大的时间、成本、环境风险和/或安全负担。因此，从基本探头读数（$q_t$、$f_s$和$u_2$）以及剪切波速度（$V_s$）出发，间接的圆锥静力触探测试单元重量（CPTU）方法对于评估地质材料的重度是非常有趣的。

### 2.1.1 Unit Weight Estimated from Soil Behavior Type

### 2.1.1 依据土壤表现类型估计重度

Total unit weights can be estimated based on soil behavior type classifications or local knowledge of the soil type. Table 2.1 details typical unit weights for different materials tested with the CPT.

The unit weights from Lunne et al. (1997) are based on a 12-zone non-normalized soil behavior type chart (Robertson et al. 1986) that is applicable to shallow soils.

总重度可以根据土壤行为类型分类或对土壤类型的当地了解进行估算。表2.1详细列出了使用CPT测试的不同材料的典型重度。

Lunne等人（1997年）提供的重度是基于一个12区非标准化的土壤行为类型图表（Robertson等人，1986年），适用于浅层土壤。

| Geomaterial | Total unit weight (kN/m³) | Reference |
|-------------|--------------------------|-----------|
| (Zone 1) Sensitive Fines | 17.5 | Lunne et al. (1997)* |
| (Zone 2) Organics | 12.5 | " |
| (Zone 3) Clay | 17.5 | " |
| (Zone 4) Silty Clay to Clay | 18.0 | " |
| (Zone 5) Clayey Silt to Silty Clay | 18.0 | " |
| (Zone 6) Sandy Silt to Clayey Silt | 18.0 | " |
| (Zone 7) Silty Sand to Sandy Silt | 18.5 | " |
| (Zone 8) Sand to Silty Sand | 19.0 | " |
| (Zone 9) Sand | 19.5 | " |
| (Zone 10) Gravelly Sand to Sand | 20.0 | " |
| (Zone 11) Very Stiff Fine Grained | 20.5 | " |
| (Zone 12) Stiff Sand to Clayey Sand | 19.0 | " |
| Fluid Tailings | 9.8 to 14.0 | Styler et al. (2018b) |
| Iron Tailings | 17.8 to 18.9 | Hu et al. (2017) |

### 2.1.2 Unit Weight Estimated from CPT Measurements

### 2.1.2 根据CPT测量估计重度

As CPT provides three readings ($q_t$, $f_s$, $u_2$) for each sounding, it would be convenient to use one or more values to estimate $\gamma_t$. For instance, in clays, Larsson and Mulabdić (1991) developed charts for estimating unit weights of different fine-grained soil types of Scandinavia in terms of net cone resistance ($q_{net} = q_t - \sigma_{v0}$) and porewater parameter $B_q = (u_2 - u_0)/(q_t - \sigma_{v0})$. For granular soils, Młynarek, et al. (2005) showed a family of dry density curves in terms of $q_c$ and $\sigma'_{v0}$ based on CPTU data taken in alluvial and fluvioglacial sands of Poland.

Using data from 18 soils, Robertson and Cabal (2010) present a correlation for $\gamma_t$ in terms of $q_t$ and friction ratio ($R_f$(%) = 100 $\cdot$ $f_s/q_t$) shown in Figure 2.2 and given by:

由于CPT提供每次探测的三个读数（$q_t$、$f_s$、$u_2$），因此使用一个或多个值来估计$\gamma_t$会非常方便。例如，在粘土中，Larsson和Mulabdić（1991年）开发了根据净锥尖阻力（$q_{net} = q_t - \sigma_{v0}$）和孔隙水参数$B_q = (u_2 - u_0)/(q_t - \sigma_{v0})$估算斯堪的纳维亚不同细粒土类型重度的图表。对于颗粒土壤，Młynarek等人（2005年）基于波兰冲积和冰碛沙的CPTU数据展示了一组干密度曲线，用$q_c$和$\sigma'_{v0}$表示。

Robertson和Cabal（2010年）利用18种土壤的数据，根据$q_t$和摩擦比率（$R_f$(%) = 100 $\cdot$ $f_s/q_t$）在图2.2中展示了$\gamma_t$的相关性，并给出了以下表达式：

$$\gamma_t = \gamma_w \left(0.27 \log_{10} R_f + 0.36 \log_{10}\left(\frac{q_t}{\sigma_{atm}}\right) + 1.236\right) \tag{2.7}$$

![image](images/rId33.png)

*Figure 2.2: Direct unit weight estimation from cone resistance and friction ratio (after Robertson and Cabal 2010)*

In a broader study using data from 44 onshore sites comprised of clays, silts, and sands, a set of regression analyses produced the relationship in Figure 2.3 (Mayne et al. 2010):

在一项涵盖44个陆地站点的广泛研究中，包括粘土、淤泥和砂质土的数据，进行了一组回归分析，得出了图2.3中的关系（Mayne等人，2010年）。

$$\gamma_t = 1.81 \gamma_w \left(\frac{q_t - \sigma_{v0}}{\sigma_{atm}}\right)^{0.017} \left(\frac{\sigma'_{v0}}{\sigma_{atm}}\right)^{0.05} \left(\frac{f_s}{\sigma_{atm}}\right)^{0.073} (B_q + 1)^{0.16} \tag{2.8}$$

![image](images/rId35.png)

*Figure 2.3: Unit weight relationship from multiple regression of CPTU data at 44 sites (after Mayne and Peuchen 2012). Note: excludes data from diatomaceous and/or organic soils*

Further research using additional data from offshore sites (Mayne and Peuchen 2012) and onshore locations (Mayne 2014) found that the sleeve friction alone provided a quick estimate of soil unit weight, as presented in Figure 2.4 ($N$ = 83 sites; $n$ = 1009):

进一步的研究利用近海场地（Mayne and Peuchen 2012）和陆地场地（Mayne 2014）的额外数据发现，仅侧壁摩擦力就可以提供土壤重度的快速估算，如图2.4所示（$N$ = 83个场地；$n$ = 1009）：

$$\gamma_t = \gamma_w \cdot [1.22 + 0.345 \cdot \log_{10}(100 \cdot (f_s/\sigma_{atm}) + 0.010)] \tag{2.9}$$

![image](images/rId36.png)

*Figure 2.4: Total soil unit weight versus sleeve friction (Rix et al. 2019). Note: not applicable to organic clays, peats, or diatomaceous soils*

![image](images/rId37.png)

*Figure 2.5: Normalized soil unit weight vs effective cone resistance for 115 different sites*

In the latest review of available compiled data, a total dataset of n = 1229 from 115 different soils has been used to quantify the soil unit weight from three expressions using the CPTU, including eqn (2.7), (2.9), and a new one shown in Figure 2.5 using effective cone resistance: $q_E$ = $q_t$ - $u_2$.

Table 2.2 presents the findings. The highest value of coefficient of determination ($r^2$) and lowest value of SEY is found from the average of all three methods.

在最新的对可用编译数据的审查中，使用了总计n = 1229的数据集，来自115种不同土壤，通过使用CPTU量化土壤重度，包括方程（2.7）、（2.9）和一个新的方程，图2.5显示使用有效锥尖阻力：$q_E$ = $q_t$ - $u_2$。

表2.2呈现了研究结果。通过所有三种方法的平均值获得了最高的决定系数（r2）和最低的SEY值。

**Table 2.2: Expressions for Estimating Soil Unit Weight of Sands, Silts, & Clays from CPTU**

| Reference | Expression | Eqn. No. | Statistics |
|-----------|-----------|----------|------------|
| Robertson & Cabal (2010) | $\gamma_t/\gamma_w \approx 1.776 + 0.27 \log_{10}(f_s/\sigma_{atm}) + 0.09 \log_{10}(q_t/\sigma_{atm})$ | (2.10)** | $r^2$ = 0.609; SEY = 0.217 |
| Mayne (2014) | $\gamma_t/\gamma_w \approx 1.22 + 0.345 \cdot \log_{10}(100 \cdot f_s/\sigma_{atm} + 0.010)$ | (2.11) | $r^2$ = 0.570; SEY = 0.158 |
| This Manual (Figure 2.5) | $\gamma_t/\gamma_w \approx 1.54 + 0.254 \cdot \log_{10}(q_E/\sigma_{atm})$ | (2.12) | $r^2$ = 0.622; SEY = 0.228 |
| Recommended Average ($\gamma_t/\gamma_w$) from all 3 eqns | — | (2.13) | $r^2$ = 0.631; SEY = 0.148 |

*Note: Not applicable to organic soils, diatomaceous earth, or cemented geomaterials*

**Note: Equation (2.10) provides same values of unit weight as (2.7) by separation of $R_f$ terms.*

Of further mention, Machine Learning (ML) has also been used to estimate the soil unit weight from CPT readings. Entezari, et al. (2021) applied ML on the aforementioned large database of soils (n = 1229) and obtained an $r^2$ = 0.86 in the measured vs. predicted statistical analyses.

For soft clays, approximate estimates of soil unit weight can be obtained from the slope of cone resistance ($q_t$) versus depth (z), defined as the parameter $m_q = \Delta q_t/\Delta z$ (Mayne et al. 2011, 2012).

For 34 soft clays (19 offshore and 15 onshore), the following trend was found:

另外需要提到的是，机器学习（ML）也已被用于从CPT读数中估算土壤重度。Entezari等人（2021年）在上述大型土壤数据库（n = 1229）上应用了机器学习，并在测量与预测的统计分析中获得了r2 = 0.86。

对于软粘土，可以通过锥阻力（$q_t$）与深度（z）的斜率来获取土壤单位重量的近似估计，定义为参数$m_q$ = ∆$q_t$ / ∆z（Mayne等人，2011年，2012年）。

对于34种软粘土（19种近海和15种陆地），发现了以下趋势：

$$\gamma_t/\gamma_w \approx 1 + 0.125 \cdot (m_q/\gamma_w) \tag{2.14}$$

where $m_q$ and $\gamma_w$ are in the same units (e.g., kN/m³). Soft clays can be identified when the ratio ($m_q/\gamma_w$) < 8. When the ratio ($m_q/\gamma_w$) > 8, then the presence of stiff to hard clays, including calcareous and/or fissured clays may be assumed and a different procedure is recommended in estimating the soil unit weight (Mayne 2014).

其中，$m_q$和$\gamma_w$的单位相同（例如，kN/m³）。软粘土可以通过比率（$m_q$/$\gamma_w$）< 8 进行识别。当比率（$m_q$/$\gamma_w$）> 8时，则可以假定存在硬性到坚硬性粘土，包括钙质或/和有裂缝的粘土，并建议使用不同的方法来估算土壤重度（Mayne，2014年）。

For soft clays, a similar approach can be adopted by defining the parameter: $m_{\Delta u} = \Delta u_2/\Delta z$, which can be utilized to identify unit weight trends for three categories of soft clay type: (a) regular or normal soft clays; (b) organic soft clays, including peats; and (c) sensitive soft clays, as presented in Figure 2.6.

对于软粘土，可以采用类似的方法，通过定义参数mΔu = Δ$u_2$/Δz来确定三类软粘土的重度趋势：（a）常规或普通软粘土；（b）有机软粘土，包括泥炭；以及（c）敏感软粘土，如图2.6所示。

$$\text{Soft regular clays: } \gamma_t/\gamma_w = 1.0 + 0.322 \cdot (m_{\Delta u}/\gamma_w) \tag{2.15}$$

$$\text{Soft organic clays: } \gamma_t/\gamma_w = 1.0 + 0.286 \cdot (m_{\Delta u}/\gamma_w) \tag{2.16}$$

$$\text{Soft sensitive clays: } \gamma_t/\gamma_w = 1.0 + 0.213 \cdot (m_{\Delta u}/\gamma_w) \tag{2.17}$$

![image](images/rId41.png)

*Figure 2.6: Unit weight trends with slope parameter $m_{\Delta u}$ for soft clays, including regular soft clays, organic soft clays & peats, and sensitive soft clays with slope parameter $m_{\Delta u}$*

### 2.1.3 Unit Weight Estimated from $V_s$

### 2.1.3 根据剪切波速$V_s$估计重度

When SCPTU results are available, the unit weight can be estimated from the shear wave velocity ($V_s$ in m/s) and depth (z in meters):

当SCPTU结果可用时，可以通过剪切波速度（$V_s$，单位为m/s）和深度（z，单位为米）来估算重度：

$$\gamma_t \text{ (kN/m}^3\text{)} = 8.31 \log_{10} V_S \text{ (m/s)} - 1.61 \log_{10} z \text{ (m)} \tag{2.18}$$

This relationship is shown in Figure 2.7 and applies to a wide range (n = 1018) of particulate geomaterials (sands, silts, and clays) that are not cemented or bonded. The expression is not applicable to saprolites, rocks, cemented calcareous or carbonate soils, or special geomaterials such as diatomaceous earth.

这种关系显示在图2.7中，并适用于一大范围（n = 1018）未胶结或粘结的颗粒地质材料（沙、淤泥和粘土）。这个表达式不适用于腐殖质岩、岩石、胶结的钙质或碳酸盐土壤，或者特殊的地质材料如硅藻土。

![image](images/rId43.png)

*Figure 2.7: Relationship for unit weight in terms of shear wave velocity and depth for uncemented geomaterials (modified after Mayne 2001)*

A more fundamental trend for unit weight is derived in terms of $V_s$ (m/s) and σ′v0 (in kPa) since the latter term also includes the effect of groundwater conditions. This relationship is shown in Figure 2.8 and the regression for $\gamma_t$ is expressed:

基于剪切波速度（$V_s$，单位为m/s）和有效应力（σ′v0，单位为kPa）的重度更为基本的趋势得出，因为后者还包括地下水条件的影响。这种关系显示在图2.8中，重度$\gamma_t$的回归表达式为：

$$\gamma_t \text{ (kN/m}^3\text{)} = 4.17 \ln V_{s1} - 4.03 \tag{2.19}$$

where Vs1 = $V_s$/(σ′v0/σatm) 0.25 is the effective stress-normalized shear wave velocity. Since an assessment of unit weight is required in order to calculate σ′v0, then an iterative procedure between the profiles of Vs1 and $\gamma_t$ will be needed.

其中，Vs1 = $V_s$/(σ′v0/σatm) 0.25 是有效应力标准化的剪切波速度。由于需要评估单位重量以计算σ′v0，则需要在Vs1和$\gamma_t$的剖面之间进行迭代过程。

![image](images/rId45.png)

*Figure 2.8: Soil unit weight with stress-normalized shear wave velocity (Mayne 2007b)*

### 2.2 Estimating $V_s$ from CPT profiles

### 2.2 根据CPT剖面估算剪切波速$V_s$

If shear wave velocity measurements are not available, it is possible to estimate the $V_s$ profile from the CPT data, although this will incur additional uncertainties. Many initial correlations were developed for a particular soil type and often for a specific geologic setting. For instance, Baldi et al. (1989) found a relationship for clean sands in Italy shown in Figure 2.9 and given by:

如果没有剪切波速度测量数据，可以尝试从CPT数据估算$V_s$剖面，尽管这样可能会引入额外的不确定性。许多最初的相关性是针对特定的土壤类型，通常是针对特定的地质环境设置开发的。例如，Baldi等人（1989年）在意大利发现了一种关系，适用于纯净沙土，如图2.9所示，其表达式为：

$$V_s \, (\text{m/s}) = 277 \left( q_t \, (\text{MPa}) \right)^{0.13} \left( \sigma'_{v0} \, (\text{MPa}) \right)^{0.27} \tag{2.20}$$

For granular soils, a more recent relationship developed from SCPTU data at 105 sand sites in eastern Canada has been found (Perret et al. 2016). Here, sandy soils with $I_{C,RW}$ < 2.6 and $B_q$ < 0.1 are applicable and the shear wave velocity can be estimated from:

对于颗粒状土壤，最近从加拿大东部105个沙土场地的SCPTU数据中发展出的一种关系已被发现（Perret等人，2016年）。在这里，适用于具有$I_{C,RW}$ < 2.6和$B_q$ < 0.1的沙土，剪切波速度可以从以下方程估算得出：

$$V_s \, (\text{m/s}) = 124.7 \left( \frac{q_t}{\sigma_{atm}} \right)^{0.135} \left( \frac{f_s}{\sigma_{atm}} \right)^{0.014} \left( \frac{\sigma'_{v0}}{\sigma_{atm}} \right)^{0.169} \tag{2.21}$$

On the other hand, for clays, Mayne and Rix (1995) compiled data from 31 different clay sites and found an overall trend shown by Figure 2.10 that can be expressed:

另一方面，对于黏土，Mayne和Rix（1995年）汇编了来自31个不同黏土场地的数据，并发现了图2.10所示的总体趋势，可以表达为：

$$V_s \, (\text{m/s}) = 31.4 \left( \frac{q_t}{\sigma_{atm}} \right)^{0.627} \tag{2.22a}$$

$$V_s \, (\text{m/s}) = 1.75 \cdot \left[ q_t \, (\text{kPa}) \right]^{0.627} \tag{2.22b}$$

If the void ratio ($e_0$) of the clay is known (or alternatively, the water content), then a stronger regression equation that relates $V_s$ to $q_t$ and $e_0$ for intact clays (Mayne & Rix 1995):

如果已知黏土的孔隙比（$e_0$）（或者作为替代，水含量），那么对于完整黏土，可以使用一个更强的回归方程将$V_s$与$q_t$和$e_0$联系起来（Mayne和Rix，1995年）：

$$V_s \, (\text{m/s}) = 9.44 \left( \frac{100 \cdot q_t}{\sigma_{atm}} \right)^{0.435} \cdot (e_0)^{-0.532} \tag{2.23}$$

![Figure 2.9](images/rId50.png)

*Figure 2.9: Empirical relationship for $V_s$ in clean sands from CPT (after Baldi et al. 1989)*

![Figure 2.10](images/rId51.png)

*Figure 2.10: Empirical relationship for $V_s$ in clays from CPT (after Mayne and Rix 1995)*

L'Heureux & Long (2017) provide a review of 22 $V_s$ correlative trends with CPT for clays. For the sensitive clays of Norway, they found the following statistical expression (n = 101; r2 = 0.89):

L'Heureux和Long（2017年）对22种与CPT相关的黏土剪切波速度趋势进行了回顾。对于挪威的敏感黏土，他们找到了以下统计表达式（n = 101; r2 = 0.89）：

$$V_s \, (\text{m/s}) = 71.7 \, (q_{net})^{0.09} \left( \frac{\sigma'_{v0}}{w_n} \right)^{0.33} \tag{2.24}$$

where $q_{net}$ and $\sigma'_{v0}$ are in kPa and $w_n$ = water content (%).

For a variety of different soil types (clays, silts, sands, and mixed soils), Hegazy and Mayne (1995) expressed the shear wave velocity in terms of the cone tip resistance ($q_t$ in kPa) and friction ratio ($R_f$ = 100 ∙ $f_s$/$q_t$ in percent):

对于各种不同的土壤类型（黏土、淤泥、沙土和混合土壤），Hegazy和Mayne（1995年）用锥尖阻力（$q_t$，单位为kPa）和摩擦比（$R_f$ = 100 ∙ $f_s$/$q_t$，单位为百分比）来表达剪切波速度：

$$V_s \, (\text{m/s}) = \left( 10.1 \cdot \log_{10} q_t - 11.4 \right)^{1.67} \cdot (R_f)^{0.3} \tag{2.25}$$

Note that this method does not apply to calcareous soils, diatomaceous mudstone, muskeg, or peats.

The effect of time (or age) has been shown to influence CPT correlations. A study of shear wave velocity data in South Carolina found the following relationship for CPTs in a variety of different soil types (Andrus et al. 2007):

请注意，这种方法不适用于钙质土壤、硅藻泥岩、沼泽地或泥煤。已经证明时间（或年份）的影响会影响CPT的相关性。南卡罗来纳州的剪切波速度数据研究发现，对于各种不同土壤类型的CPT，存在以下关系（Andrus等人，2007年）：

$$V_s \, (\text{m/s}) = 2.62 \, (q_t)^{0.395} \, (I_{C,RW})^{0.912} \, (z)^{0.124} \, (ASF) \tag{2.26}$$

where $q_t$ is in kPa, $I_{C,RW}$ = CPT material index, depth z (m), and ASF is an age scaling factor for the formation (SF = 0.92 for Holocene deposits and 1.12 for Pleistocene soils). The CPT material index $I_c$ is used to determine soil type and discussed in more detail in Section 3. It ranges from about 1 to 2 in clean sands, 2 to 3 in silts, and generally is about 3 or greater in clays.

In a separate study, Robertson (2009b) recommended the evaluation of $V_s$ from CPT net cone resistance and CPT material index:

在这里，$q_t$以kPa为单位，$I_{C,RW}$表示CPT材料指数，深度z以米为单位，ASF是形成世代的年份缩放因子（对全新世沉积物为0.92，对更新世土壤为1.12）。CPT材料指数$I_c$用于确定土壤类型，详细讨论见第3节。在洁净砂中约为1至2，在淤泥中为2至3，而在黏土中通常为3或更大。

在另一项研究中，Robertson（2009b年）建议根据CPT净锥尖阻力和CPT材料指数评估$V_s$：

$$V_s \, (\text{m/s}) = \left( 101.68 + 0.5 \cdot I_{C,RW} \cdot \frac{q_t - \sigma_{v0}}{\sigma_{atm}} \right)^{0.5} \tag{2.27}$$

A recent review of shear wave velocity correlations conducted by the Pacific Earthquake Engineering Research (PEER) Center recommended to use the average value from the three aforementioned methods, specifically eqns (2.25), (2.26), and (2.27), as documented by Wair et al. (2012).

最近由太平洋地震工程研究中心（PEER Center）进行的剪切波速度相关性评估建议使用从三种先前提到的方法（具体为方程式（2.25）、（2.26）和（2.27））中求得的平均值，详见Wair等人（2012年）的文献记录。

## 2.3 Unit Weight in Man-Made Deposits

## 2.3 人造沉积物的重度

### 2.3.1 Fluid Tailings

### 2.3.1 流体尾矿

CPT soundings are frequently used to characterize tailings storage facilities (TSF). These facilities contain tailings in fluid-states with suspended soil particles, recent sedimentary deposits undergoing self-weight consolidation, and soil-like tailings with behavior controlled by the effective stress. Styler et al. (2018) presented the interpretation of CPT results in oil sand tailings facilities showing typical measurements obtained in recycled water, fluid tailings, beach tailings, and into natural ground. In fluid tailings, the cone tip resistance will increase with the measured pore pressure. The effective tip resistance, $q_t$−$u_2$, will be close to zero. The friction sleeve measurements will also be nearly zero.

CPT探测经常用于表征尾矿库（TSF）。这些设施包含悬浮土壤颗粒的液态尾矿，经自重固结的近期沉积物堆积物，以及受有效应力控制的类土壤尾矿。Styler等人（2018年）展示了在油砂尾矿库中解释CPT结果的方法，展示了在再循环水、流体尾矿、海滩尾矿和天然地面中获得的典型测量值。在流体尾矿中，锥尖阻力会随测得的孔隙水压力增加而增加。有效锥尖阻力，$q_t$ − $u_2$，接近于零。摩擦套筒的测量值也几乎为零。

The measured pore pressures will equal the total stress in fluid tailings. The weight of the suspended soil particles is carried by the pore fluid; which increases the unit weight of the pore fluid. Piezometer readings have been used in laboratory tests on sedimentation (Sills 1998) to calculate the total stress and in-situ void ratios. These readings were also used in 10 m high standpipe tests on oil sand tailings (Jeeravipoolvarn et al. 2009). Since the CPT $u_2$ measurements equal total stress, the slope of $u_2$ versus depth equals the total unit weight of fluid tailings.

测得的孔隙水压力将等于流体尾矿中的总应力。悬浮土壤颗粒的重量由孔隙流体支撑，这会增加孔隙流体的容重。水压计读数已在沉积物的实验室测试中（Sills，1998年）用于计算总应力和原位孔隙比。这些读数还在油砂尾矿的10米高立管试验中使用（Jeeravipoolvarn等人，2009年）。由于CPT $u_2$ 测量等于总应力，因此$u_2$与深度的斜率等于流体尾矿的总重度。

Pore pressure dissipation tests can be performed in fluid tailings. These show that the $u_2$ channel does not dissipate to a hydraulic profile of 9.8 $kN/m^3$ from the pond-surface. The $u_2$ channel does not dissipate at all because there are no excess pore pressures from shear or cavity expansion to accommodate the cone probe.

Therefore, the total unit weight, $\gamma_t$, is simply the slope of the measured pore water pressure versus depth in fluid tailings with negligible effective stress.

在流体尾矿中可以进行孔隙水压力消散测试。这些测试表明，这些表明，$u_2$通道不会从池面消散为9.8 $kN/m^3$的水力剖面。$u_2$ 通道根本不会消散，因为没有来自剪切或小孔扩张的多余孔隙水压力来容纳锥形探针。

因此，在具有微小有效应力的流体尾矿中，总重度 $\gamma_t$ 简单地是测得的孔隙水压力与深度之间关系的斜率。

### 2.3.2 Metal Mine Tailings

### 2.3.2 金属尾矿

Most natural soils have specific gravities that range from $G_s$ ≈ 2.6 to 2.8. The total unit weights calculated with $G_s$ are fairly insensitive to specific gravity in natural soils.

Metal mine tailings can have heavier specific gravities, values above $G_s$ > 3. Consequently, unit weights estimated from SBT zones may be underestimated by over 20% for these deposits. Unit weights from $V_s$ or CPT measurements may still be applicable, but should be confirmed by other means.

大多数天然土壤的比重（$G_s$）通常在约2.6到2.8之间。使用比重计算的总重度在天然土壤中对比重不太敏感。

金属矿尾矿的比重可能更重，比如$G_s$ > 3的数值。因此，从SBT区域估算的单位重量可能会低估这些沉积物超过20%。来自$V_s$或CPT测量的重度可能仍然适用，但应通过其他方法进行确认。

## 2.4 Overburden Stress and Porewater Pressure

## 2.4 上覆层应力和孔隙水压力

### 2.4.1 Total Overburden Stress

### 2.4.1 总上覆应力

The total vertical (overburden) stress profile is $\sigma_{v0}$ which is the accumulation of unit weight times layer thickness with depth, equal to the integral:

总垂直（覆盖层）应力剖面为$\sigma_{v0}$，它是单位重量乘以深度的层厚积分累积，等于以下积分：

$$\sigma_{vo} = \int \gamma_t \, dz \tag{2.28}$$

For practical calculations using a spreadsheet, $\sigma_{vo}$ is often approximated by:

用电子表格进行实际计算时，$\sigma_{vo}$通常通过以下近似值来计算：

$$\sigma_{vo} = \sum_{i=1}^{n} (\gamma_{ti} \cdot \Delta z_i) \tag{2.29}$$

### 2.4.2 Equilibrium Porewater Pressure

### 2.4.2 平衡孔隙水压力

Geotechnical soil behavior depends on effective stress. Effective stress is the total stress minus porewater pressure. A porewater pressure profile is required to calculate effective stress. The first principle of soil mechanics states that:

岩土工程土壤行为依赖于有效应力。有效应力是总应力减去孔隙水压力。计算有效应力需要孔隙水压力剖面。土力学的第一原理陈述了：

$$\sigma' = \sigma - u \tag{2.30}$$

The equilibrium pore pressure profile can be hydrostatic if there is no ground water flow. If there is ground water flow, due to ongoing soil consolidation or because of hydraulic gradients across the site, then a non-hydrostatic pore pressure profile will need to be characterized.

The depth to the ground water table can be measured in open soil borings, however, this may be affected by the drilling process. Also, borehole closure may be required in certain states or provinces for reasons of safety and/or protection of groundwater aquifers. In other cases, open boreholes can cave due to instability, thus affecting the measure of water levels.

如果没有地下水流动，平衡孔隙水压力剖面可以是静水压的。如果存在地下水流动，可能是由于土壤持续固结或者由于场地上的水力梯度，那么就需要表征非静水压的孔隙水压力剖面。

地下水位深度可以通过开放式土壤钻孔测量，但这可能会受到钻探过程的影响。此外，出于安全或保护地下水含水层的考虑，某些州或省可能要求进行钻孔封闭。在其他情况下，开放式钻孔可能因不稳定而发生塌陷，从而影响水位的测量。

For projects having groundwater as an important role in construction and performance, the installation of observation wells and/or piezometers may be prudent. Piezometers can be installed by conventional drilling and grouting methods, although the newer push-in piezometers are becoming more frequent and popular because of their expedient and economic installation.

For test elevations beneath the groundwater table, the equilbrium porewater pressure can also be ascertained by the use of dissipation testing. In these cases, a full dissipation to 100% degree of consolidation may be warranted to determine the equilibrium value. Additional details on dissipation testing are discussed later in Section 8. A hydrostatic pore pressure profile can be characterized by a single pore pressure dissipation (PPD) performed below the ground water table. A non-hydrostatic pore pressure profile requires multiple PPD tests run to equilibrium over the full depth of the CPT sounding.

对于地下水在施工和性能中起重要作用的项目，安装观测井和/或水压力计可能是明智的选择。虽然水压力计可以通过传统的钻孔和灌浆方法安装，但新型的推入式水压力计因其快捷和经济的安装而越来越受欢迎。

对于地下水位下的测试标高，可以通过耗散测试来确定平衡孔隙水压力。在这些情况下，可能需要进行完全耗散到100%固结度以确定平衡值。有关耗散测试的详细信息将在第8节后讨论。可以通过单一的孔隙水压力耗散（PPD）测试表征静水压孔隙水压力剖面，该测试在地下水位下进行。非静水压孔隙水压力剖面则需要进行多个PPD测试，并在CPT探测的整个深度上达到平衡。

### 2.4.3 Groundwater Table Depth

### 2.4.3 地下水位深度

In unconfined aquifers, the equilibrium pore pressure profile is simply:

在无约束含水层中，平衡孔隙水压力剖面可以简单地描述为：

$$u_0 = h_w \cdot \gamma_w \tag{2.31}$$

where $h_w$ is the height to the ground water table (GWT). That is, $h_w$ = z - $z_w$, where z = current depth and $z_w$ is the GWT depth. Above the GWT, it is common to take $u_0$ = 0, however in cases of capillarity, negative porewater pressures may occur.

If the soil rate of dissipation exceeds the rate of pore pressure generation, then the measured $u_2$ profile will equal the equilibrium pore pressure profile during penetration. This is seen in free draining soils such as sands and gravels.

其中 $h_w$ 是地下水位（GWT）的高度。也就是说，$h_w$ = z - $z_w$，其中 z 是当前深度，$z_w$ 是地下水位深度。在地下水位之上，通常假设 $u_0$ = 0，然而在毛细作用的情况下，可能会出现负孔隙水压力。

如果土壤的耗散速率超过孔隙水压力生成速率，那么测量得到的 $u_2$ 剖面将等于贯入过程中的平衡孔隙水压力剖面。这种情况在排水良好的土壤中，如沙子和砾石中常见。

### 2.4.4 Non-Hydrostatic Water Table

Artesian conditions and drawdown are also common cases where non-hydrostatic water tables are found. Ground water flow, due to soil consolidation or variable hydraulic gradients across a site, results in non-hydrostatic equilibrium pore pressure profiles. An accurate equilibrium pore pressure profile is necessary to calculate effective stress and properly interpret soil properties from the CPTU measurements.

Several case studies can be referred to for non-hydrostatic groundwater conditions. For instance, Johns and Murray (2018) demonstrated the consequences of an incorrect equilibrium pore pressure profile for CPTs performed in a mine tailings dike. Tanaka and Sakagami (1989) report on a CPTU soundings performed in underconsolidated soft clays caused by a recently constructed man-made island, or reclaimed land, within a bay area. A pore pressure profile less than hydrostatic was found at the Tiller-Flotten national test site in Norway due to drawdown with drainage to the east of the site (L'Heureux et al. 2019).

阿特斯井条件和降水也是非静水压水位常见情况。由于土壤固结或场地上不均匀的水力梯度导致地下水流，结果是非静水压平衡孔隙水压力剖面。准确的平衡孔隙水压力剖面对于计算有效应力并正确解释从CPTU测量中得到的土壤性质是必要的。

可以参考几个非静水压地下水条件的案例研究。例如，Johns和Murray（2018）展示了在矿渣堤坝中进行CPT测试时不正确的平衡孔隙水压力剖面的后果。Tanaka和Sakagami（1989）报告了在一个人工建造的岛屿或被填海地区进行的CPTU探测，该区域内存在未固结的软黏土。在挪威的Tiller-Flotten国家测试场，由于场地东部排水引起的水位降低，发现了低于静水压的孔隙水压力剖面（L'Heureux等，2019年）。

### 2.4.5 Effective Vertical Stress

The effective vertical stress is simply the total vertical stress minus the equilibrium porewater pressure:

有效垂直应力简单地等于总垂直应力减去平衡孔隙水压力：

$$\sigma'_{vo} = \sigma_{vo} - u_0 \tag{2.32}$$

For normal cases of hydrostatic equilibrium in flat ground in natural soils, the common cases for calculating $u_0$ are as follows with z = depth and $z_w$ = depth of the groundwater table (GWT):

Above GWT and dry soil (no capillarity): $u_0 = 0$  $\tag{2.33a}$

Below GWT and saturated soil: $u_0 = \gamma_w \cdot (z - z_w)$  $\tag{2.33b}$

Above GWT and full capillarity: $u_0 = \gamma_w \cdot (z - z_w)$  $\tag{2.33c}$

It is critical to have an accurate evaluation of effective vertical stress in order to interpret soil properties from CPT soundings. An underestimated effective stress will result in over-estimated soil densities and OCRs. This could also result in a misclassification of the soil behavior type.

在自然土壤的平坦地面上，正常情况下的静水压平衡，用于计算 $u_0$ 的常见情况如下，其中 z 表示深度，$z_w$ 表示地下水位（GWT）深度：

Conversely, an overestimated effective stress will have the opposite effects on the interpreted results.

In practice, the interpretation of $\sigma_{v0}$, $u_0$, and $\sigma'_{v0}$ is routine and straight forward. However, mistakes can be made if programs and spreadsheet formulas are used without checking the results. These mistakes could originate from CPTs performed in materials with atypical unit weights. They could occur if there is a drill-out through a surface layer and an incorrect unit weight is used for the overburden. They can occur if a hydrostatic porewater pressure profile is assumed when in fact the site has groundwater flow, drawdown, or artesian conditions.

相反，过高估计的有效应力将对解释结果产生相反的影响。

在实践中，解译 $\sigma_{v0}$、$u_0$ 和 $\sigma'_{v0}$ 是常规且直接的。然而，如果在不检查结果的情况下使用程序和电子表格公式，可能会出现错误。这些错误可能源自在具有非典型重度的材料中进行的CPT测试。如果通过表层进行钻探而用于覆盖层的重度不正确也可能发生这些错误。如果假设了静水压孔隙水压力剖面，而实际上场地存在地下水流、降水或自流条件，这些错误也可能发生。

## 2.5 Horizontal Stress and K0

## 2.5 水平应力和K0

The lateral earth stress coefficient, $K$, is the ratio between horizontal effective stress and vertical effective stress: $K = \sigma’_h / \sigma’_v$. When the vertical and horizontal stresses correspond to the principal stress directions, the lateral earth stress coefficient is known as $K_0$, the lateral at-rest stress coefficient. Furthermore, $K_0$ applies to one-dimensional vertical loading under conditions of zero lateral strain ($\epsilon_h = 0$). This relates to field conditions during the formation of marine sediment in the oceans, as well as the imposed conditions during 1-d consolidation tests in the soils laboratory. The at-rest lateral stress coefficient is defined by Equation 2.34:

侧向土压力系数 ( K ) 是水平有效应力与垂直有效应力之比：K = h’/v’。当垂直和水平应力对应于主应力方向时，侧向土压力系数被称为 K0 ，即静止侧向土压力系数。此外， K0  适用于零侧向应变条件(𝜖ℎ = 0)下的一维垂直加载。这与海洋沉积物在海洋中形成时的现场条件，以及在土壤实验室进行的一维固结试验中所施加的条件相关。静止侧向土压力系数由方程式2.34定义。

$$K_0 = \frac{\sigma'_{h0}}{\sigma'_{v0}} \tag{2.34}$$

$K_0$ is a state parameter of the soil and is related to overconsolidation ratio, OCR. When an unconfined soil is loaded vertically, it will expanded laterally. When this expansion is restricted – by a steel ring in a laboratory apparatus or by adjacent soil elements in the field under the same increase in vertical stress – then effective horizontal stresses develop to maintain zero lateral strain. The value of $K_0$ in overconsolidated soil is higher than $K_0$ in normally consolidated soil.

K0是土壤的状态参数，与超固结比（OCR）相关。当无约束的土壤在垂直加载时，它会在侧向上膨胀。当这种膨胀受到限制时——在垂直应力相同增加的情况下，由实验室装置中通过钢圈或现场的相邻土体元素限制——有效的水平应力会发展出来，以保持零侧向应变。超固结土中的K0 值高于正常固结土壤中的K0值。

Figure 2.11 is a plot of $\sigma'_{v0}$ versus $\sigma'_{h0}$ for a soil element under zero lateral strain. This soil element is consolidated by increasing the vertical stress. Horizontal stresses develop due to the zero-lateral strain boundary condition, resulting in a proportional increase in $\sigma'_{h0}$ during normal consolidation. This proportional increase in $\sigma'_{h0}$ is equal to $(K_0)_{NC}$ on the normal consolidation line. The soil element is then unloaded by removing vertical effective stress. The horizontal stresses are not relieved at the same increment as during normal consolidation. As the vertical stress is unloaded, the OCR and $K_0$ of the soil both increase. At some point, $\sigma'_{v0}$ will equal $\sigma'_{h0}$ and $K_0$ equals 1. Continued unloading of vertical stress will further increase OCR and increase $K_0$ beyond 1. Over-consolidated soils can have higher $\sigma'_{h0}$ than $\sigma'_{v0}$. Reloading the soil decreases OCR and $K_0$, but the horizontal stress lags behind the increase in $\sigma'_{v0}$. Figure 2.11 shows that $K_0$ is highly related to OCR, but it also shows that $K_0$ depends on whether the soil is being unloaded or reloaded. The same soil can have the same OCR, but different $K_0$.

图2.11是一个绘制了在零侧向应变条件下土体元素的\sigma′v0对\sigma′ℎ0的图示。这个土体元素通过增加垂直应力进行固结。由于零侧向应变的边界条件，水平应力发展出来，在正常固结期间导致\sigma′ℎ0成比例增加。这个成比例增加的\sigma′ℎ0等于正常固结线上的K0NC。接着，土体元素通过移除垂直有效应力进行卸载。水平应力的减缓并不像在正常固结期间那样按照相同的增量进行。随着垂直应力的卸载，土壤的超固结比OCR和K0都会增加。在某些点上，\sigma′v0将等于\sigma′ℎ0，而K0等于1。继续卸载垂直应力将进一步增加OCR，并使K0超过1。超固结土的\sigma′ℎ0可能高于\sigma′v0。重新加载土壤会降低OCR和K0，但水平应力的增加滞后于\sigma′v0的增加。图2.11显示了K0与OCR高度相关，但也显示了K0取决于土壤是在卸载还是重新加载状态。同一土壤可以具有相同的OCR，但不同的K0。

![image](images/rId63.png)

*Figure 2.11: Stress path for normal consolidation (blue), unloading (red), and reloading (green) in σ'v and σ'h space (modified from Sully 1991)*

In field programs, the value of $K_0$ can be assessed directly using in-situ tests, including: (a) lift-off pressure ($P_0 = \sigma_{h0}$) from self-boring pressuremeter tests (SBPMT); (b) the final equilibrium stress ($\sigma_{h0}$ from push-in spade cells, also known as Glötzl cells or total stress cells (TSC)); (c) the closure pressure from hydraulic fracture (HF) tests; and (d) paired sets of directional and polarized shear wave velocities, such as crosshole (CHT) and downhole (DST) geophysics surveys (Ku and Mayne 2015). Indirect measures of in-situ $K_0$ can be made by use of the flat dilatometer test (DMT) and Iowa stepped blade (ISB). Several laboratory approaches have also been devised to ascertain the magnitude of $K_0$, including triaxial stress-path testing with local strain measurements, instrumented consolidometers with lateral stress sensors, and suction measurements on high-quality specimens.

在现场项目中，可以直接使用原位测试评估K0的值，包括：(a) 自钻压力计试验(SBPMT)中的起钻压力(P0 = \sigmaℎ0)；(b) 最终平衡应力（来自推入式铲形传感器，也称为Glötzl传感器或总应力传感器（TSC））；(c) 水力压裂（HF）试验中的封闭压力；以及(d) 成对的定向剪切波速和极化剪切波速，如井间（CHT）和井下（DST）地球物理调查（Ku和Mayne，2015）。间接测量原位K0的方法包括使用扁铲侧胀试验（DMT）和爱荷华式阶梯铲（ISB）。此外，还有几种实验室方法用于确定K0的大小，包括具有局部应变测量的三轴应力路径测试、带有侧向应力传感器的固结计以及高质量试样上的吸力测量。

Towards a pragmatic solution, laboratory data from 171 different soils (clays, silts, sands, and gravels) that were subjected to either triaxial and/or instrumented consolidometer tests were compiled to investigate trends (Mayne and Kulhawy 1982). Laboratory data for $K_0$ measurements on 43 clays are summarized in Figure 2.12. Similar findings are available for virgin loading and unloading of silts and sands in laboratory $K_0$ tests.

为寻求务实解决方案，Mayne和Kulhawy（1982）收集了对171种不同土壤（粘土、淤泥、砂土和砾石）进行了三轴试验和/或装有传感器的固结计试验的实验室数据，以研究其趋势。图2.12总结了对43种粘土进行的K0测量的实验室数据。类似的发现也适用于实验室中对淤泥和砂土进行的原始加载和卸载的K0测试。

For loading-unloading of uncemented sands and non-structured clays of low to medium sensitivity, the following power law expression was offered:

关于低至中敏感性未胶结砂土和非结构化粘土的加载-卸载情况，提供了以下的幂律表达式：

$$K_0 = (1 - \sin \phi') \cdot OCR^{\sin \phi'} \tag{2.35}$$

where $\phi'$ = effective friction angle and OCR = overconsolidation ratio. For highly-structured soils, higher values of $K_0$ can be realized where the exponent term is related to the clay sensitivity (Hamouche et al. 1995).

Confirmation of equation (2.35) is also found in Figure 2.13 that shows field $K_0$ data from total stress cell (TSC) measurements and self-boring pressuremeter tests (SBPMT) in clays.

其中，𝜙′表示有效摩擦角，OCR表示超固结比。对于高度结构化的土壤，在指数项与粘土敏感性相关的情况下，可以实现更高的K0值（Hamouche等，1995年）。

方程（2.35）的确认也可以在图2.13中找到，该图展示了粘土中来自总应力单元（TSC）测量和自钻压力计测试（SBPMT）的现场K0数据。

![image](images/rId65.png)

*Figure 2.12: Summary of laboratory K0 data on 43 clays showing effects of OCR during virgin loading and unloading*

![image](images/rId66.png)

*Figure 2.13: Lateral stress coefficient K0 from in-situ field measurements by TSC and SBPMT versus OCR for 19 clays (Mayne 2020)*

For clean unaged and uncemented quartz and silica sands, available data are compiled in Figure 2.14. These results include $K_0$ data from large calibration chamber tests, small laboratory triaxial and oedometer test series, as well as field pressuremeter tests (PMT) from Po River Sand (Italy), Stockholm (Sweden), Holmen (Norway), and Thanet Sand (UK).

对于未老化和未胶结的纯净石英和硅砂，已有的数据编制在图2.14中。这些结果包括来自大型标定罐试验、小型实验室三轴和压缩仪试验系列的K0数据，以及来自意大利波河沙、瑞典斯德哥尔摩、挪威霍尔门和英国坦尼特沙的现场旁压试验（PMT）数据。

![image](images/rId67.png)

*Figure 2.14: Lateral stress coefficient K0 versus OCR from field and lab tests on sands with OCRlimit applied*

For Equation 2.35, a maximum value for $K_0$ during unloading can be established by the passive stress coefficient ($K_P$), which is given for the simple Rankine case by:

对于方程式2.35，在卸载过程中，可以通过被动应力系数KP来确定K0的最大值，简单的朗肯情况下，KP应如下计算：

$$K_P = \frac{1 + \sin \phi}{1 - \sin \phi} = \tan^2(45° + \phi/2) \tag{2.36}$$

The $K_P$ limit is shown in Figure 2.14 for the $K_0$ versus OCR relationships for sands. As such, a limiting OCR can be calculated:

在图2.14中显示了受KP限制的砂土的K0与 OCR 关系。因此，可以计算出一个限制性的 OCR 值：

$$OCR_{limit} = \left[\frac{1 + \sin \phi}{(1 - \sin \phi)^2}\right]^{1/\sin \phi} \tag{2.37}$$

The OCR represents the stress history of the soil. It is a state variable. Notably, CPT measurements can be used to evaluate OCR, as covered in Section 4.4. For the more complex case of virgin loading, unloading, and reloading of soil, Schmidt (1983) found:

OCR代表土壤的应力历史，是一个状态变量。值得注意的是，圆锥静力触探（CPT）测试可以用来评估OCR，详细内容见第4.4节。对于土壤的原始加载、卸载和重新加载的更复杂情况，Schmidt（1983年）发现：

$$K_0 = \frac{1 - \sin\phi'}{OCR_{max} - 1} \cdot \left[ OCR_{max} - OCR + (OCR - 1) \cdot OCR_{max}^{\sin\phi'} \right] \tag{2.38}$$

where $OCR_{max}$ = maximum experienced value of OCR during the unloading phase.

其中，$OCR_{max}$ = 卸载阶段经历的最大OCR值。

# 3 Stratigraphy and Soil Type

# 3 地层及土壤类型

Natural soils and rocks are complex geomaterials having diverse compositions, mineralogies, and varied geologic origins that have been subjected to long exposure to various environmental, seasonal, climatic, and thermal conditions over many thousands to millions of years. Soils are formed by many different processes, many of them from the action of water forming sediment (marine, lacustrine, fluvial, alluvial, deltaic, estuarine), but also produced by wind (dune, aeolian, loess), ice (glacial), and by in-place disintegration and deterioration of the underlying parent bedrock that creates particles (residuum, saprolite, laterites, andosols). Their variety is extensive and limitless (Figure 3.1).

自然土壤和岩石是复杂的地质材料，具有多样的组成、矿物组合和不同的地质起源，经过数千到数百万年的长期暴露于各种环境、季节、气候和热力条件之下。土壤形成于许多不同的过程中，其中许多是由水的作用形成沉积物（海洋、湖泊、河流、冲积、三角洲、河口），同时也可以由风（沙丘、风化土、黄土）、冰（冰川）以及地下母岩的原位风华和侵蚀形成颗粒（残积岩、腐殖土、红壤、安山岩）。它们的种类丰富而多样（见图3.1）。

Composition of soil particles can include basic minerals (quartz, feldspar, mica, kaolin, etc.) or bio-geo origins (calcareous, carbonates, peats). Their behavior is complicated by aspects involving stress history, anisotropy, drainage, and nonlinear stress-strain-strength response under loading, with additional facets such as strain rate, ageing, cementation, fissuring, and other difficult-to-quantify nuances.

土壤颗粒的组成可以包括基本矿物（如石英、长石、云母、高岭土等）或生物地质起源物质（如钙质、碳酸盐、泥炭）。它们的行为受到多种因素的复杂影响，包括应力历史、各向异性、排水情况以及在加载下的非线性应力-应变-强度响应，还包括应变速率、老化、胶结、裂隙化以及其他难以量化的微妙细节。

![image](images/rId71.png)

*Figure 3.1: Immense variety and wide diversity of geomaterials in the environment*

## 3.1 Initial Conditions

## 3.1 初始条件

A rather large number of geotechnical parameters have been defined by the profession in an effort to represent soil conditions and behavior. In the grouping of Initial Conditions shown by Figure 3.2, there are two major categories of parameters that reflect: (1) Indices which help quantify the compositional makeup and components of the soil particles; and (2) State parameters that relate to the packing arrangement and current state of stress of the assemblage of particles. Certainly, a realization of the soil index parameters (first group) requires sampling, laboratory testing, and knowledge of the local geology and terrain formation. With regard to field testing, the identification of soil indices can be aided in part by use of the Vision Cone Penetration Test (VisCPT), as detailed by Hryciw et al. (1998) and Hryciw and Shin (2004).
