在尝试表征土壤条件和行为方面，地质工程界已经定义了相当多的地质技术参数。在图3.2所示的初始条件分组中，有两大类参数可以反映：（1）帮助量化土壤颗粒的组成和成分的指标；以及（2）涉及到颗粒组成的排列方式和当前应力状态的状态参数。显然，对土壤指标参数（第一组）的理解需要采样、实验室测试以及对当地地质和地形形成的了解。在现场测试方面，使用视界圆锥静力触探试验（VisCPT）可以部分辅助识别土壤指标，如Hryciw等人（1998年）和Hryciw与Shin（2004年）详细描述的内容。

![image](images/rId72.png)

*Figure 3.2: List of soil parameters to quantify initial soil conditions: (a) particle indices; and (b) in-situ state (modified after Mayne et al. 2009)*

## 3.2 Soil Behavior Type (SBT)

## 3.2 土壤行为类型

The CPT was first developed as a profiling tool to identify the thickness of soft surface deposits of clays and organic “veen” soils (Barentsen 1936). The conical shaped probe was pushed by hand through the bottom of a borehole until refusal on an underlying stronger layer. The low cone tip resistance above this stronger layer was used to infer the soil type. Therefore, the very first application of cone penetration testing was to profile soil layers based on their observed cone behavior. This is an efficient and economical alternative to characterizing soil types by collecting soil samples and measuring their intrinsic properties in a laboratory.

Soil classification in the laboratory is most often done using sets of nested sieves for coarse particles (sands and gravels) and hydrometric analyses for finer soil particles (silts and clays). Several systems are used in North America including the: (a) Unified Soil Classification System (USCS) as documented by ASTM D-2487 and D-2488; (b) United States Dept. of Agriculture (USDA) that uses a triangular plot of percentages of sand, silt, and clay; (c) American Association of State Highway and Transportation Officials (AASHTO) related to interstate roadways; and other systems. Details on the USCS and AASHTO are given by Rix et al. (2019).

圆锥静力触探试验（CPT）最初被开发为一种分层工具，用于确定软表层沉积物（如黏土和有机“veen”土壤）的厚度（Barentsen，1936年）。锥形探头被手推穿过钻孔，直至在下方较强层前停止。在较强层下方，较低的锥尖阻力被用来推断土壤类型。因此，圆锥静力触探试验的最初应用是基于观测到的锥的行为来分析土层的结构。这是一种有效且经济的替代方法，可以避免在实验室内通过采集土样和测量其固有性质来表征土壤类型。

实验室中的土壤分类通常使用一组嵌套筛网来处理粗颗粒（如沙和砾石），并使用水力分析来处理较细的土壤颗粒（如淤泥和黏土）。在北美，使用几种系统进行土壤分类，包括：（a）统一土壤分类系统（USCS），由ASTM D-2487和D-2488记录；（b）美国农业部（USDA）使用三角形图表来表示沙、淤泥和黏土的百分比；（c）美国州际公路和交通官员协会（AASHTO）与州际公路相关联；以及其他系统。关于USCS和AASHTO的详细信息由Rix等人（2019年）提供。

The CPT soil behavior type and laboratory soil classification can be in disagreement. A non-plastic rock flour may behave like a silt, but the particle size may classify as a clay or a sand. A clayey-gravel mixture may behave like a clay if the gravel particles are suspended in the clay matrix, but it may classify in the laboratory as a gravel. If the soil is not being excavated as a borrow material, then in practice it may be more relevant to know how the soil behaves in-situ rather than the actual laboratory classification.

To facilitate the identification of soil types indirectly by CPT, a good number of empirical soil behavior type (SBT) charts have been developed (e.g., Douglas and Olson 1981; Kulhawy and Mayne 1990; Lunne et al. 1997; Fellenius and Eslami 2000; Robertson 2009b; Schneider et al. 2008; 2012). In this section, several of the more popular methods for assessing soil type from CPT are reviewed.

CPT土壤行为类型与实验室土壤分类可能存在不一致。非塑性岩粉可能表现为淤泥，但其颗粒大小可能根据实验室分类而被归类为黏土或砂。如果含有砾石颗粒的黏土砾石混合物中，砾石颗粒悬浮在黏土基质中，则其可能表现为黏土，但在实验室中可能被归类为砾石。如果土壤并非作为借料开挖，则在实际操作中，了解土壤的原位行为可能比实际的实验室分类更为重要。

为了通过CPT间接地推进土壤类型的识别，已经有许多经验性土壤行为类型（SBT）图表（例如，Douglas和Olson，1981年；Kulhawy和Mayne，1990年；Lunne等，1997年；Fellenius和Eslami，2000年；Robertson，2009b年；Schneider等，2008年；2012年）。本节将回顾几种根据CPT的评估土壤类型的较为主流的方法。

### 3.2.1 qt versus Rf

### 3.2.1 qt与Rf关系

The friction sleeve was added to the cone probe by Begemann (1953) to create a tool for pile foundation design. Begemann (1965) observed that the soil type could be inferred from the ratio of friction resistance to cone tip resistance, termed friction ratio (%): $R_f = 100 \cdot f_s / q_t$. Ratios above 3% were inferred to be clayey soils. Ratios below 1% were inferred to be sandy soils.

Begemann（1953年）为了创建桩基础设计工具，将摩擦套筒加到了锥形探头上。Begemann（1965年）观察到，可以通过摩擦阻力与锥尖阻力的比值来推断土壤类型，称为摩擦比（%）：Rf = 100 ∙ fs/qt。摩擦比大于3%被推断为黏土土壤，小于1%则被推断为砂土土壤。

Robertson et al. (1986) presented a 12-zone SBT system that compares the cone tip resistance ($q_t$) and friction ratio ($R_f$), as seen in Figure 3.3. This chart was based on preceding CPT research by other practitioners and experience gained using the CPT in known natural soil deposits. The primary advantage of this chart is that it can be used with almost raw CPT data, the only calculation required being the corrected cone tip resistance $q_t$. The shortcomings of this chart are exposed in deep CPTs greater than 20 m. As such, the net tip resistance, $q_{net} = q_t - \sigma_{v0}$, is a more valid parameter for SBT classifications as well as for bearing capacity analyses. Deep soundings see an increase in effective stress with depth; which increases the tip resistance. The shortcomings were addressed in a normalized SBTn chart proposed by Robertson (1990, 1991) which is discussed later in this section.

Robertson等人（1986年）提出了一个包括12个区域的SBT系统，比较了锥尖阻力（qt）和摩擦比（Rf），如图3.3所示。这张图表是基于其他从业者之前的CPT研究和在已知自然土壤沉积中使用CPT获得的经验。这张图表的主要优点在于几乎可以使用原始CPT数据，唯一需要计算的是修正后的锥尖阻力qt。然而，这张图表在超过20米的深层CPT中显现出缺点。因此，净尖端阻力qnet=qt−\sigmav0是SBT分类以及承载力分析中更有效的参数。随着深度增加，深层探测中有效应力增加，从而增加了尖端阻力。这些缺点在Robertson（1990年，1991年）提出的归一化SBTn图表中得到了解决，这将在本节后面讨论。

![image](images/rId73.png)

*Figure 3.3: CPT soil behavioral charts for 12 classification zones using cone resistance (qt) and friction ratio (Rf= 100 * fs/qt) from Robertson et al. (1986)*

### 3.2.2 Q versus Fr

### 3.2.2 Q与Fr的关系

In order to account for depth effects and overburden stress on the readings, stress-normalized CPT parameters have been defined as follows (Lunne et al. 1997):

为了考虑深度效应和地层应力对读数的影响，已经定义了应力归一化的CPT参数如下（Lunne等人，1997年）：

$$Q = \frac{q_t - \sigma_{v0}}{\sigma'_{v0}} \tag{3.1}$$

$$F_r (\%) = \frac{100 f_s}{q_t - \sigma_{v0}} \tag{3.2}$$

Note that $Q$ is also now designated as $Q_t$ and $Q_{t1}$, depending upon research institution or agency. These variables were used to develop a 9-zone SBT chart (Robertson 1990), as presented in Figure 3.4. In this system, basic “vanilla” clay is zone 3 while “hourglass” sands are found in zone 6.

While the definition of $Q$ in (3.1) works well in soft clays, the results of CPT in sands shows that $q_{net}$ is more aligned with the square root of $\sigma'_{v0}$, especially as the sounding increases with depth. This resulted in an upgrade of $Q$ to $Q_{tn}$ where the effective stress has a variable exponent: $Q_{tn} = q_{net}/(\sigma'_{v0})^n$ (in units of bars) with “n” ranging from 1 (clays) to 0.75 (silts) and 0.5 (sands). In dimensionless terms, $Q_{tn} = (q_{net}/\sigma_{atm})/(\sigma'_{v0}/\sigma_{atm})^n$ for any units (Robertson 2009b).

注意，现在Q也被称为Qt和Qt1，具体取决于研究所或机构。这些变量被用来制定了一个9区SBT图表（Robertson 1990），如图3.4所示。在这个系统中，基础的“vanilla”黏土位于第3区，而“hourglass”砂土则位于第6区。

尽管在软黏土中Q的定义（式3.1）效果良好，但CPT在砂土中的结果显示，随着探测深度的增加，qnet与有效应力vo'的平方根更加一致。这导致Q被升级为Qtn，其中有效应力具有可变指数：Qtn = qnet/(vo')n（单位为巴），其中“n”从1（黏土）到0.75（淤泥）和0.5（砂土）不等。在无量纲的术语中，Qtn = (qnet/atm)/(σvo'/σatm) n，适用于任何单位（Robertson 2009b）。

![image](images/rId75.png)

*Figure 3.4: CPT soil behavioral type classification charts with 9-zones and normalized $Q_{t1}$ versus $F_r$ (after Robertson 1990, 1991; Lunne et al. 1997; Robertson 2009b)*

Using the normalized $Q$ and $F$ parameters, Schneider et al. (2012) developed a 4-zone SBT chart as shown in Figure 3.5. The soil boundaries in this chart were developed analytically and evaluated with field data. The soil boundaries are hyperbolic and intersect the $Q_{t1}$ and $F_r$ axes at theoretically calculated values based on the soil friction angle, rigidity index, and an equivalent normally-consolidated penetration resistance.

Schneider等人（2012年）利用归一化的Q和F参数，开发了一个四区SBT图表，如图3.5所示。该图表中的土壤边界是通过分析方法开发并通过现场数据评估的。这些土壤边界是双曲线的，并在理论计算的基础上与Qt1和Fr轴相交，这些计算基于土壤摩擦角、刚度指数和等效常规固结贯入阻力。

![image](images/rId76.png)

*Figure 3.5: SBT chart with hyperbolic boundaries developed by Schneider et al. (2012)*

### 3.2.3 Qtn versus Fr

### 3.2.3 Qtn和Fr的关系

As noted in the last section, the stress normalization for the net cone tip resistance was upgraded to:

正如在上一节中所指出的，净锥尖阻力的应力归一化已经升级为：

$$Q_{tn} = \frac{(q_{net}/\sigma_{atm})}{(\sigma'_{v0}/\sigma_{atm})^n} \tag{3.3}$$

or if units of bars are used, it is simply: $Q_{tn} = q_{net}/(\sigma'_{v0})^n$. As seen in Figure 3.6, the CPT material index, $I_{c,RW}$, can be defined as the radius of a circle centered at ($Q_{tn}$ = 2951; $F_r$ = 0.06%):

或者如果使用的单位是巴，那么简单地表示为：Qtn = qnet/(vo')n。如图3.6所示，CPT材料指数IcRW可以定义为以(Qtn = 2951; Fr = 0.06%)为中心的圆的半径：

$$I_c = \sqrt{(3.47 - \log Q_{tn})^2 + (1.22 + \log F_r)^2} \tag{3.4}$$

where the exponent n is initially chosen as 1.0 and then found from (Robertson 2009):

指数n最初选择为1.0，然后从（Robertson 2009）中找到：

$$n = 0.381 \cdot I_c + 0.05 \cdot (\sigma'_{v0}/\sigma_{atm}) - 0.15 \leq 1.0 \tag{3.5}$$

and therefore requires iteration. Usually only two or three iterations are required. So, for soil zones 2 through 7, only the CPT Index $I_{c,RW}$ is needed to identify these soil classifications. A value of $I_{c,RW}$ = 2.6 serves as the demarcation for separating drained behavior ($I_{c,RW}$ < 2.6) from undrained behavior ($I_{c,RW}$ > 2.6).

因此，这需要迭代。通常只需要两到三次迭代。所以对于土壤区域2到7，只需要CPT指数IcRW来识别这些土壤分类。当IcRW = 2.6时，用作区分排水行为（IcRW < 2.6）和非排水行为（IcRW > 2.6）的界线。

![image](images/rId80.png)

*Figure 3.6: CPT classification index $I_{c,RW}$ applied to $Q_{tn}$-$F_R$ chart for 9-soil behavioral types*

An illustrative example from a piezocone sounding conducted in Biloxi, MS is presented in Figure 3.7 with six side-by-side plots with depth: (a) cone resistance, (b) sleeve friction, (c) friction ratio, (d) dynamic porewater pressure, (e) derived SBTn from the 9-zone chart scaled to the specific zone number and colorized soil behavior type, and (f) text wording of soil type.

These automated SBTn classifications should be compared with the soil types per "rules of thumb" for the same sounding that was presented earlier as Figure 1.8. Generally good matches are found with the various sand and clay layers at this site. The colorization of different soil types provides a quick and valuable visualization of the primary soil types so that the user can appreciate the geostratigraphy quickly and efficiently.

图3.7展示了在密西西比州比洛克西进行的一个孔压静力触探测试的示例，包括六个并列的深度图：(a) 锥尖阻力，(b) 套筒摩擦力，(c) 摩擦比，(d) 动态孔隙水压力，(e) 根据缩放到特定区域号和颜色化土壤行为类型的9区图表推导的SBTn，以及(f) 土壤类型的文本描述。

这些自动化的SBTn分类应与之前作为图1.8展示的同样的探测的“经验法则”土壤类型进行比较。通常，这些分类在该场地的各种砂层和粘土层中都匹配良好。不同土壤类型的颜色标记提供了对主要土壤类型的快速和有价值的可视化，使用户能够快速有效地理解地层地质。

Note that an "undefined" zone may also occur in the soil profile from SBT charts. On occasion, the $F_r$ may fall below 0.1% or above 10%, or a reading with $Q_{tn}$ does not lie in the range of 1 to 1000 from Figure 3.6. Thus, in such cases, an "undefined" classification may result. Also, shallow drillout may produce an "undefined" soil type since no CPT data were collected.

请注意，在SBT图表中土壤剖面中可能还会出现“未定义”区域。偶尔，摩擦比Fr可能低于0.1%或高于10%，或者Qtn读数不在图3.6中的1到1000的范围内。因此，在这种情况下可能会出现“未定义”的分类。此外，浅钻可能会产生“未定义”的土壤类型，因为没有收集到CPT数据。

![image](images/rId81.png)

*Figure 3.7: Representative piezocone output using colorized and normalized SBTn from CPTU sounding from Biloxi, MS*

Robertson (2016) extended the chart in Figure 3.6 by combining the work from Schneider et al. (2012) with a dilative-contractive boundary proposed by Robertson (2010b). A version of this updated chart is shown in Figure 3.8 and includes the hyperbolic lines separating sand-like from clay-like behavior with a zone for transitional soils.

Robertson (2016)通过将Schneider等人（2012年）的研究成果与Robertson（2010b年）提出的膨胀-收缩边界相结合，扩展了图3.6中的图表。这一更新后的图表版本显示在图3.8中，其中包括区分砂质行为和粘质行为的双曲线，并包含过渡土壤的区域。

![image](images/rId82.png)

*Figure 3.8: SBT chart proposed by Robertson (2016) based on inferred drainage behavior and contractive (loose) versus dilative (dense) soil states*

### 3.2.4 Q versus Bq

### 3.2.4 Q与Bq的关系

Robertson (1990) developed a companion SBT chart to the $Q$-$F_r$ chart in Figure 3.4 using the pore pressure parameter $B_q$ defined in Equation 3.6:

Robertson（1990年）根据方程式3.6中定义的孔隙压力参数Bq，开发了与图3.4中的Q-Fr图表对应的SBT图表的伴随版本：

$$B_q = \frac{u_2 - u_0}{q_t - \sigma_{v0}} \tag{3.6}$$

The $Q_{t1}$ versus $B_q$ chart is shown in Figure 3.9 and includes the same SBTn zones as in the $Q$-$F_r$ chart. Soils that behave like sands and gravels have high tip resistances and $B_q$ values near zero due to the rapid dissipation of any generated pore pressure. Soils that behave like clays have low tip resistances and can have large amounts of either positive or negative pore pressures.

图3.9展示了Qt1与Bq图表，包括与Q-Fr图表中相同的SBTn区域。表现为砂和砾石的土壤具有高的锥尖阻力和接近零的Bq值，这是由于任何生成的孔隙压力都能迅速消散。表现为粘土的土壤具有低的尖端阻力，可以具有大量正或负的孔隙水压力。

![image](images/rId84.png)

*Figure 3.9: $Q_{t1}$ versus $B_q$ SBT chart for 9-zone SBTn classification (after Robertson 1991)*

### 3.2.5 Q versus $\Delta u / \sigma'_{v0}$

### 3.2.5 Q与$\Delta u / \sigma'_{v0}$的关系

Schneider et al. (2008) developed a chart to improve the identification of drained, partially drained, and undrained CPT behavior in soils. This chart is shown in Figure 3.10 and is presented in terms of $Q$ versus an alternate normalized porewater pressure parameter $U = (u_2 - u_0)/\sigma'_{v0}$ instead of the more common $B_q$. They showed that $B_q$ is not particularly able to distinguish between increasing OCR and increasing coefficients of consolidation. Note that the porewater pressure parameters $U$ and $B_q$ are interrelated via:

Schneider等人（2008年）开发了一张图表，旨在改进对土壤中排水、部分排水和不排水CPT行为的识别。这张图表显示在图3.10中，使用了Q与一种替代的归一化孔隙水压参数U = ($u_2$ − $u_0$)/\sigma′v0，而不是更常见的Bq。他们表明，Bq在区分OCR增加和固结系数增加方面并不特别有效。请注意，孔隙水压参数U和Bq是通过以下关系相关联的：

$$U = \frac{\Delta u_2}{\sigma'_{v0}} = B_q \cdot Q \tag{3.7}$$

As noted earlier in Section 3.2.2, the term $Q$ is also expressed as $Q = Q_t = Q_{t1}$.

正如早在3.2.2节中提到的，Q也可以表示为Q = Qt = Qt1。

It will be shown later in Section 6.3 that the excess porewater pressure $\Delta u = (u_2 - u_0)$ is proportional to the undrained strength of the soil, $s_u$, through an empirical $N_{\Delta u}$ factor. Therefore, $(u_2 - u_0)/\sigma'_{v0}$ is proportional to the undrained strength ratio of the soil ($s_u/\sigma'_{v0}$). In this chart an increase in OCR, which increases the undrained strength ratio, increases both $Q_{t1}$ and $\Delta u/\sigma'_{v0}$. An increase in the soils coefficient of consolidation also increases $Q_{t1}$, but decreases $\Delta u/\sigma'_{v0}$ as excess pore water pressures are dissipated.

之后的第6.3节将会展示，超孔隙水压力Δu = ($u_2$ − $u_0$)与土壤的不排水强度su成正比，通过一个经验性的N\Deltau系数构成关系。因此，($u_2$ − $u_0$)/\sigma′v0与土壤的不排水强度比(su/\sigma′v0)成正比。在这张图表中，OCR的增加会增加土壤的不排水强度比，从而增加Qt1和Δu/\sigma′v0。土壤的固结系数增加也会使Qt1增加，但随着超孔隙水压力的消散Δu/\sigma′v0会减少。

![image](images/rId86.png)

*Figure 3.10: $Q_{t1}$ versus $\Delta u / \sigma'_{v0}$ SBT chart for separating partial drainage and OCR soil behaviors (after Schneider et al. 2008)*

### 3.2.6 Q(1-Bq)+1 versus Fr

### 3.2.6 Q(1-Bq)+1与Fr的关系

Jefferies and Been (2006, 2015) use the combined parameter $Q_{t1} \cdot (1 - B_q) + 1$ plotted versus normalized friction ratio, $F_R$ (%) to evaluate soil type, as presented in Figure 3.11. Note that the combined parameter can be reduced to simply the effective cone resistance ($q_t - u_2$) divided by the effective overburden stress: $Q_{t1} \cdot (1 - B_q) + 1 = (q_t - u_2)/\sigma'_{v0}$.

杰弗里斯和宾（2006，2015）使用组合参数Qt1 ∙ (1 − Bq) + 1与归一化摩擦比率FR（%）绘制图来评估土壤类型，如图3.11所示。请注意，这个组合参数可以简化为有效锥阻力（qt − $u_2$）除以有效过压应力：Qt1 ∙ (1 − Bq) + 1 = (qt − $u_2$)/\sigma′v0。

![image](images/rId87.png)

*Figure 3.11: Soil behavioral type from $Q_{t1} \cdot (1 - B_q) + 1$ versus normalized friction sleeve ($F_R$) and CPT material index, $I_{C,BJ}$ (after Jefferies and Been 2006, 2015)*

### 3.2.7 SBT Index $I_{C,BJ}$

### 3.2.7 土壤行为类型指标IC,BJ

The first use of a CPT material index was made by Jefferies & Davies (1991), where it was quite similar to that presented as equation (3.8). The index reduces the three normalized CPT readings to a single number. Jefferies & Been (2006, 2015) defined Equation 3.8 based on their 6-region SBT chart shown in Figure 3.11. The numbers produced by these indices can be used in empirical CPT relationships that depend on soil-type. They are also easily implemented in spreadsheets; where identifying a zone number can be a more difficult.

杰弗里斯和戴维斯（1991）首次使用了CPT材料指数，该指数与方程式（3.8）相似。该指数将三个归一化的CPT读数简化为一个单一的数值。杰弗里斯和宾（2006，2015）根据他们在图3.11中展示的6区域SBT图表定义了方程式3.8。这些指数产生的数值可以用于依赖于土壤类型的经验性CPT关系中。它们在电子表格中也很容易实现；而识别区域编号可能会更加困难。

$$I_{C,BJ} = \sqrt{\left(3 - \log_{10}\left(Q_{t1}(1 - B_q) + 1\right)\right)^2 + \left(1.5 + 1.3\log_{10}(F_R)\right)^2} \tag{3.8}$$

In essence, $I_{C,BJ}$ is the radius from an imaginary centerpoint and defines the zonal boundaries between different soil types. For example, referencing Figure 3.11, if $I_{C,BJ}$ < 1.25, the interpreted soil classification falls within “gravelly sands”. Corresponding soil behavioral types, zone numbers, and associated ranges of material index for $I_{C,BJ}$ are given in Table 3.1.

实质上，IC,BJ是从一个虚拟中心点定义的半径，用于划分不同土壤类型之间的区域边界。例如，参考图3.11，如果IC,BJ < 1.25，则解释的土壤分类属于”砾石砂土”。表3.1列出了对应的土壤行为类型、区域编号以及IC,BJ的材料指数范围。

**Table 3.1:** *Soil Behavioral Type and Zone Number as defined by CPT Material Index ($I_C$) for Two CPT Classification Systems*

| Soil classification | SBT Zone | $I_{C,BJ}$ (a) | $I_{C,RW}$ (b) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Sensitive fine-grained | 1 | n/a | $Q_{tn}$ < 12exp(−1.4$F_R$) |
| Organic soils | 2 | $I_{C,BJ}$ ≥ 3.22 | $I_{C,RW}$ ≥ 3.60 |
| Clays | 3 | 2.76 ≤ $I_{C,BJ}$ < 3.22 | 2.95 ≤ $I_{C,RW}$ < 3.60 |
| Silty mixtures | 4 | 2.40 ≤ $I_{C,BJ}$ < 2.76 | 2.60 ≤ $I_{C,RW}$ < 2.95 |
| Sandy mixtures | 5 | 1.80 ≤ $I_{C,BJ}$ < 2.40 | 2.05 ≤ $I_{C,RW}$ < 2.60 |
| Sands: clean to silty | 6 | 1.25 ≤ $I_{C,BJ}$ < 1.80 | 1.31 ≤ $I_{C,RW}$ < 2.05 |
| Sands with gravels | 7 | $I_{C,BJ}$ < 1.25 | $I_{C,RW}$ < 1.31 |
| Stiff clayey sands | 8 | n/a | 1.5 < $F_R$ < 4.5% I |
| Stiff fine-grained soils | 9 | n/a | $F_R$ ≥ 4.5% I |

(a) $I_{C,BJ}$ per *Equation 3.8* after Jefferies and Been (2015) and *Figure 3.11*.
(b) $I_{C,RW}$ per *Equation 3.4* after Robertson and Wride (1998) and Robertson (2009b), and using *Figure 3.6*.
(c) Stiff soils Zones 8 and 9 identified when $Q_{tn} \geq \frac{1}{0.006(F_R - 0.9) - 0.0004(F_R - 0.9)^2 - 0.002}$

### 3.2.8 Conflicting SBT profiles

### 3.2.8 权衡SBT图

Various SBT charts have been presented. These charts all compare two CPT derived variables to infer the soil behavior type. In many cases these various charts will be in agreement. When the charts disagree, the applicable answer to the geotechnical design may depend on the geotechnical application. A problem involving consolidation or undrained strength may be better characterized by a SBT chart that incorporates the pore pressure measurement, such as Figure 3.10. A problem involving friction, cyclic liquefaction, or drained strengths may be better characterized by a SBT chart that compares the tip and sleeve friction - such as those provided in Subsection 3.2.1.

各种SBT图表已被提出。这些图表都比较了两个CPT衍生的变量，以推断土壤的行为类型。在许多情况下，这些不同的图表会达成一致。当图表产生分歧时，对岩土工程设计的适用答案可能取决于岩土工程应用的情况。涉及固结或不排水强度的问题可能更适合使用包括孔隙水压力测量的SBT图表，例如图3.10。涉及摩擦、循环液化或排水强度的问题可能更适合使用比较锥尖和套筒摩擦的SBT图表，如3.2.1节中提供的那些。

In some cases, the charts may disagree between each other and with laboratory results near layer boundaries. The tip resistance responds to a larger soil zone than the friction sleeve and pore pressure. The ratios of these different measurements may incorrectly identify very thin layers at the boundary between two larger consistent soil types.

在某些情况下，不同的图表之间可能存在分歧，并且在靠近层边界的地方与实验室结果不一致。锥尖阻力对比摩擦套筒和孔隙压力，能对更大的土壤区域做出相应。这些不同的测量比值可能会错误地识别在两个大的连续的土壤类型边界处的非常薄层。

## 3.3 Apparent Fines Content

## 3.3 表观细粒含量

The fines content (FC) of soils is usually determined on a representative sample by washing particles through a US No. 200 sieve (75 micron = 0.075 mm) size and determining the percentage by weight that passes. As soil samples are not normally taken during CPT, an estimate of the Apparent Fines Content (AFC) may be desired, especially for soil liquefaction analyses.

土壤的细颗粒含量（FC）通常通过将颗粒通过美国200号筛网（75微米=0.075毫米）进行过滤，并确定通过筛网的重量百分比来确定。由于通常在进行CPT时不会取土样，估算表观细颗粒含量（AFC）可能是需要的，特别是对于土壤液化分析来说。

### 3.3.1 AFC from Material Index, Ic

3.3.1 根据材料指数$I_c$获得表观细粒含量

Robertson and Wride (1998) created an empirical relationship to estimate an apparent fines content ($AFC$) from $I_{C,RW}$. Their proposed relationship is given in Equation 3.9.

Robertson and Wride (1998)提出了一个经验关系，用于从ICRW估算表观细颗粒含量（AFC）。他们提出的关系式如方程3.9所示。

$$
I_{C,RW} < 1.26 : \quad AFC = 0\% \tag{3.9a}
$$

$$
1.26 \leq I_{C,RW} \leq 3.5 : \quad AFC\ (\%) = 1.75\left(I_{C,RW}\right)^{3.25} - 3.7 \tag{3.9b}
$$

$$
I_{C,RW} > 3.5 : \quad AFC = 100\% \tag{3.9c}
$$

The apparent fines content calculated from Equation 3.9 reflects how soil compressibility and draining conditions affect the CPT penetration resistance. The measured fines content may include hard quartz particles, soft clays, or some intermediate materials.

In a more recent study that included additional data, Boulanger & Idriss (2014) presented the trend shown in Figure 3.12. This also shows the aforementioned relationship by Robertson & Wride (1998).

根据方程式3.9计算得出的表观细颗粒含量反映了土壤的可压缩性和排水条件如何影响CPT的贯入阻力。测得的细颗粒含量可能包括硬石英颗粒、软黏土或一些中间材料。

在包括额外数据的较新研究中，Boulanger和Idriss（2014年）展示了图3.12中显示的趋势。该图还展示了Robertson和Wride（1998年）提出的前述关系。

![image](images/rId92.png)

*Figure 3.12: Data and trendlines for apparent fines content and CPT material index, $I_c$ (from Boulanger & Idriss 2014)*

For estimating AFC, Boulanger & Idriss (2014) recommended:

为了估算AFC，Boulanger&Idriss（2014）建议：

$$
AFC = 80 \cdot (I_c + C_{FC}) - 137 \quad \text{for } 0\% \leq AFC \leq 100\% \tag{3.10}
$$

where $C_{FC}$ = a fitting parameter that can be adjusted to site-specific laboratory data with range: -0.29 ≤ $C_{FC}$ ≤ + 0.29. A value $C_{FC}$ = 0 is recommended when no lab data are available.

In fact, at least 17 relationships for estimating $AFC$ from $I_c$ have been proposed. Agaiby & Mayne (2020) reviewed these in light of a database of 53 different soil types and found that the apparent fines content could be estimated from $I_c$ using the expression ($r^2$ = 0.822; SEY = 14.34):

其中，CFC是一个拟合参数，可以根据具体现场的实验室数据进行调整，其范围为：-0.29 ≤ CFC ≤ +0.29。建议在没有实验室数据的情况下将CFC设为0。

事实上，至少有17种关系式用于从Ic估算AFC。Agaiby和Mayne（2020年）基于53种不同土壤类型的数据库对这些关系进行了回顾，发现可以使用以下表达式从Ic估算得出表观细颗粒含量（r2 = 0.822; SEY = 14.34）：

$$
AFC\ (\%) = 1.3 \left(I_{cRW}\right)^{3.77} \tag{3.11}
$$

They also checked this relationship with five compiled databases (n = 552) and found good agreement, although it lowered the $r^2$ to 0.761 (SEY = 14.40).

他们还使用五个汇编的数据库（n = 552）检验了这种关系，并发现虽然r2降低至0.761（SEY = 14.40），但结果仍然表明较好的一致性。

### 3.3.2 AFC from CPT Index, IB

### 3.3.2 CPT指数获得AFC

The hyperbolic lines shown in Figure 3.8 for $Q$ vs $F_r$ can be expressed in terms of an alternative CPT index, $I_B$, defined as (Robertson 2016):

图3.8中显示的Q 与 Fr的双曲线关系可以用另一种CPT指标IB来表达，其定义为（Robertson，2016年）：

$$
I_B = \frac{100 \cdot (Q_{tn} + 10)}{Q_{tn} \cdot F_R + 70} \tag{3.12}
$$

Agaiby & Mayne (2020) related the $AFC$ to index $I_B$:

Agaiby&Mayne（2020）将AFC与指数IB建立关系：

$$
AFC = \left(\frac{293}{I_B}\right)^{1.65} \tag{3.13}
$$

Using data (purple diamonds) from 137 seismic sites compiled by Boulanger & Idriss (2014), Figure 3.13 shows the empirical relationship between $AFC$ and $I_B$. Also shown superimposed are data from 93 sands, silts, and clays reported in Agaiby & Mayne (2019).

利用Boulanger和Idriss（2014年）编制的137个地震场地的数据（紫色钻石），图3.13展示了AFC和IB之间的经验关系。图中还叠加显示了Agaiby和Mayne（2019年）报道的93个沙、粉土和黏土的数据。

![image](images/rId97.png)

*Figure 3.13: Observed trend of apparent fines content ($AFC$) and CPT Index, $I_B$*

### 3.4 Drained versus Undrained Response for CPT

## 3.4 CPT的排水与不排水响应

In concept, the drained response of soils occurs when no excess porewater pressures are developed during loading, thus $\Delta u$ = 0, whilst undrained behavior is associated with constant volume, or $\Delta V/V_0$ = 0. In the laboratory, the response can be controlled artificially by the applied rate of loading and the selection of values that permit or do not permit the free flow of water.

For the standard CPT conducted at a rate of 20 mm/s, the assessment of “drained” versus “undrained” behavior of soil is not always clear. Robertson (2009) suggests that the threshold boundary of drained-undrained response occurs at a value of CPT index, $I_{cR’09}$ = 2.60. Following Figure 3.6, the notion of undrained soil behavior can be taken approximately when $I_{cR’09}$ > 2.60, as shown by Figure 3.14. Consequently, for values of $I_{cR’09}$ < 2.60, the soil response may be considered as drained, or partially drained. However, there can be some uncertainty in the threshold value of $I_c$ = 2.60 using the $Q_{tn}$-$F_r$ chart, depending upon other main characteristics of the soil formation, such as permeability, microstructure, and fabric.

在概念上，土壤的排水响应发生在加载过程中没有产生超孔隙水压力时，即Δu = 0，而不排水行为则与恒定体积相关，或者ΔV/V0 = 0。在实验室中，可以通过施加的加载速率和选择允许或不允许水的自由流动来人为控制响应。

对于标准的圆锥静力触探试验，加载速率为20 mm/s时，对土壤的“排水”与“不排水”行为的评估并不总是清晰的。Robertson（2009年）建议，排水-不排水响应的阈值边界发生在CPT指数值IcR’09 = 2.60处。根据图3.6，当IcR’09 > 2.60时，不排水土壤行为的概念可以近似采用，如图3.14所示。因此，对于IcR’09 < 2.60的值，土壤的响应可以被视为排水或部分排水。然而，使用Qtn-Fr图来确定Ic = 2.60的阈值值时可能存在一些不确定性，这取决于土壤形成的其他主要特征，如渗透性、微观结构和结构。

![image](images/rId98.png)

*Figure 3.14. Definition of drained-undrained threshold in $Q_{tn}$-$F_r$ plot according to Robertson (2009)*

Alternate means of defining the drained-undrained threshold may be realized from the $Q$-$B_q$ chart, presented previously as Figure 3.9 (Robertson 1991). Here another CPT material index for defining soil zones has been recommended by Torrez-Cruz (2015). For values of $B_q$ > 0, this index is given by:

之前作为图3.9（Robertson, 1991）呈现的Q-Bq图表中可以定义排水-不排水阈值的替代方法。根据这张图表，Torrez-Cruz（2015年）提出了了另一个用于定义土壤区域的CPT材料指数。对于Bq > 0的值，该指数为：

$$I_{Q-B_q} = Q \cdot 10^{(-1.9 B_q)} \tag{3.14}$$

Figure 3.15 shows that undrained soil response occurs when the value of $I_{Q-B_q}$ < 4.

图3.15显示，当IQ-Bq的值小于4时，出现不排水土壤响应。

![image](images/rId100.png)

*Figure 3.15 Alternate definition of drained-undrained threshold in $Q$-$B_q$ plot using CPT index from Torrez-Cruz (2015)*

Similarly, the soil behavior chart of $Q$-$U$ devised by Schneider et al. (2008) and presented earlier as Figure 3.10 can be used to defined undrained soil response. Recalling that the value of $U = \Delta u_2/\sigma’_{v0}$ or $U = B_q \cdot Q$, Figure 3.16 illustrates that undrained soil behavior can be defined when:

类似地，Schneider等人（2008年）设计的Q-U土壤行为图（之前作为图3.10呈现）可以也用来定义不排水土壤响应。回顾U的定义为U = Δu^2/σvo’或U = Bq · Q，图3.16说明了在以下条件下可以定义不排水土壤行为：

$$U > 1.05 + 0.2 \cdot Q^{0.95} \tag{3.15}$$

For data that fall outside of this region, the soil response may be considered either fully drained, or partially drained, or “transitional”.

对于落在这一区域之外的数据，土壤响应可能被视为完全排水、部分排水或者”过渡性的”。

![image](images/rId102.png)

*Figure 3.16 Alternate definition of drained-undrained threshold in $Q$-$U$ plot using CPT soil behavior zones defined by Schneider et al. (2008)*

# 4 Soil State and Stress History

# 4. 土状态和应力历史

CPT measurements primarily depend on the soil stresses, soil type, and soil state. The soil stresses include the overburden stress, porewater pressure, and vertical and horizontal effective stresses.

CPT测量结果主要取决于于土壤应力、土壤类型和土壤状态。土壤应力包括上覆应力、孔隙水压力以及垂直和水平有效应力。

The soil type includes all of the inherent soil variables - such as particle sizes, compressibility, specific gravity, intrinsic friction angles, and other parameters. The soil state is the packing of the particles, including porosity, relative density, fabric, and stress history. The stress history reflects past loading of the soil in terms of effective stress and includes mechanical loading (erosion, glaciation, excavation), as well as apparent preconsolidation due to ageing, desiccation, groundwater, and other geological and environmental factors.

土壤类型包括所有固有的土壤变量，例如颗粒大小、可压缩性、比重、内摩擦角以及其他参数。土壤状态是指颗粒的填充情况，包括孔隙率、相对密度、结构和应力历史。应力历史反映了土壤在有效应力方面的过去加载情况，包括机械加载（如侵蚀、冰川作用、开挖），以及因老化、干燥、地下水和其他地质和环境因素而导致的明显的先期固结。

## 4.1 Void Ratio

## 4.1 孔隙比

The void ratio ($e_0$) is a classic soil state variable that is used in the soil mechanics laboratory. As void ratio relates to porosity ($n$), water content ($w_n$), unit weight ($\gamma_t$), relative density ($D_R$), and state parameter ($\psi$), there has been significant efforts to use cone penetration tests to help assess in-situ void ratios.

In the laboratory, the in-place void ratio ($e_0$) is found as the ratio of the volume of the voids ($V_v$) to the volume of soil solids ($V_s$):

孔隙比（e0）是经典的土壤状态变量，广泛应用于土壤力学实验室中。由于孔隙比与孔隙度(n)、含水量(wn)、单位重(\gammat)、相对密度(DR)和状态参数(\psi)相关，因此人们花费了巨大的精力，利用圆锥静力触探试验来帮助评估原位孔隙比。

在实验室中，原位孔隙比(e0)定义为孔隙体积(Vv)与土壤固体体积(Vs)之比：

$$e_0 = \frac{V_v}{V_s} \tag{4.1}$$

Note that ranges of void ratio can vary from $e_0 = 0$ as a limit in solid rock without voids up to $e_0 > 10+$ in highly compressible plastic clays, such as the infamous Mexico City clay. Since the total volume ($V_T$) is the sum of the two ($V_T = V_s + V_v$), the void ratio represents the same information as porosity ($n$), which is a parameter more commonly found in the fields of rock mechanics, powder industry, and materials science and engineering. The porosity is defined as the ratio of volume of voids to the total volume:

请注意，孔隙比的范围可以从e0 = 0（固体岩石中没有孔隙的极限情况）到e0 > 10 +或更高（如墨西哥城著名的可压缩塑性粘土）不等。由于总体积(VT)等于固体体积和孔隙体积的总和(VT = Vs + Vv)，孔隙比所表示的信息与孔隙度(n)相同。在岩石力学、粉末工业以及材料科学与工程领域中，更常见的是孔隙度，其定义为孔隙体积与总体积的比率：

$$n = \frac{V_v}{V_T} = \frac{e_0}{1 + e_0} \tag{4.2}$$

The full range of porosity is from 0 to 100%, or expressed as a decimal: $0 \leq n \leq 1$.

孔隙度的完整范围是从 0 到 100%，或以小数形式表示为：0 ≤ n ≤ 1。

## 4.2 Relative Density of Clean Sands

## 4.2 纯净砂土的相对密度

The degree of packing of clean sands is often expressed in terms of relative density ($D_R$), also called the density index ($I_{DR}$). This is done under the empirical observations that different sands at the same relative density will exhibit similar behavior. There is no underlying theory supporting the comparison of relative densities between different sand types. If the maximum possible void ratio (loosest state) is designated $e_{max}$ and the densest state represented by $e_{min}$, then the current void ratio ($e_0$) gives the relative density (expressed as a percentage in North America, but commonly as a decimal in Europe and Asia):

对于纯净砂土的密实程度通常用相对密度(DR)，也称为密度指数(IDR)来表达。这是基于经验观察，相同相对密度的不同砂土将表现出类似的行为。目前没有理论支持不同砂土类型之间相对密度的比较。如果将最大可能的孔隙比（最松状态）标记为emax，最密状态表示为 emin，那么当前的孔隙比(e0)给出了相对密度（在北美以百分比表示，在欧洲和亚洲通常以小数形式表示）：

$$D_R(\%) = I_{DR} = 100 \left(\frac{e_{max} - e_0}{e_{max} - e_{min}}\right) \tag{4.3}$$

ASTM provides separate test procedures for direct measurement of $e_0$, $e_{max}$, and $e_{min}$, which is not practical for quality control testing of sands, such as large controlled fill placement for embankments, earth retaining structures, hydraulically-filled ground, and mine tailings. Moreover, a recent study of 6 sands using 4 different standards to determine these index parameters showed differences in values of relative density (Lunne et al. 2019). As such, compaction control in the field is often verified using nuclear density gages or penetration tests, such as SPT or CPT.

ASTM提供了分别用于直接测量e0,、emax和emin的测试程序，这对于砂土的质量控制测试（例如大型受控填充用于堤坝、土挡结构、水力填充地和矿山尾矿等）并不实用。此外，最近对6种砂进行的一项研究使用了4种不同的标准来确定这些指标参数，结果表明相对密度值的结果存在差异（Lunne等人，2019年）。因此，现场的压实控制通常通过核密度计或压实试验（如SPT或CPT）来验证。

In North America, the use of relative density is restricted to sands with fines content not greater than 15%, whereas in Asia, much higher fines contents are allowed. Of interest, several correlations between the relative density index parameters and particle grain characteristics have been studied by Cubrinovski and Ishihara (2002) and Cho, Dodds, and Santamarina (2006). For instance, there exists a general trend between $e_{min}$ and $e_{max}$, as evidenced from several sand databases compiled in Figure 4.1 which suggests, on average:

在北美，相对密度的使用仅限于细料含量不超过15%的砂土，而在亚洲，允许使用含有更高细料含量的砂土。值得注意的是，Cubrinovski和Ishihara（2002年）以及Cho、Dodds和Santamarina（2006年）对相对密度指数参数与颗粒的颗粒特征之间的多个相关性进行了研究。例如，在图4.1编制的几个砂土数据库中显示了一个普遍趋势，即emin和emax 之间存在一般性关系，一般表现为：

$$e_{min} = 0.571 \cdot (e_{max}) \tag{4.4}$$

The parameters emin and emax are also related to the particle roundness, angularity, uniformity coefficient (UC), and other indices of the sand (Youd 1973; Mayne et al. 2002; Cho et al. 2006).

参数emin和emax也与颗粒的圆度、角度、均匀系数（UC）以及砂土的其他指标相关联（Youd，1973年；Mayne等，2002年；Cho等，2006年）。

![image](images/rId107.png)

*Figure 4.1: Observed trend between minimum and maximum void ratios for clean sands*

### 4.2.1 Calibration Chamber Testing

### 4.2.1 标定罐试验

Natural sands are particularly difficult and expensive to sample in an undisturbed state without risk of altering their in-place void ratio or density. Consequently, early geotechnical studies mostly relied on reconstituted sands for derivation of behavioral aspects. With regard to the use of penetration tests for evaluating the in-situ relative density, a number of research institutions produced CPT data from calibration chamber tests (CCT). These chambers range in size from 0.6 to 2.5 m in diameter (D) and height (H), with typical measurements given by D ≈ 1 m and H ≈ 1 m. Figure 4.2 depicts the general setup and approach to CCT.

天然砂土进行取样而不扰动其原位孔隙比或密度是特别困难且昂贵。因此，早期的岩土工程研究大多依赖于再造砂土来推导其行为特性。关于使用圆锥静力触探试验评估原位相对密度，许多研究机构通过标定罐测试（CCT）获得了圆锥静力触探试验（CPT）数据。这些标定罐的尺寸从直径（D）和高度（H）都在0.6到2.5米不等，典型尺寸通常为 D ≈ 1米，H ≈ 1米。图4.2描述了CCT的一般设置和方法。

![image](images/rId108.png)

*Figure 4.2: Schematic of calibration chamber setup for CPT testing of sands*

In essence, the CCT is a very large triaxial sand specimen with a special sealed entry port at the center to allow insertion of a penetrometer while the chamber is pressurized. A large sample of uniform sand (approximately 1 to 5 tons) is artificially-prepared to a known void ratio (and relative density) with variable applied vertical and lateral stresses, as well as induced degrees of preconsolidation ($1 \leq OCR \leq 15$). The chambers have rigid steel shells on the outside, however, the inner walls are made of flexible rubber membranes that are pressurized to create the effective stress states surrounding the large soil specimens. All aspects of the sand are quantified beforehand (e.g., $D_{50}$, fines content, mineralogy, dry/saturated, etc.).

本质上，标定罐测试（CCT）是一个非常大的三轴砂样品，中心设有特殊密封入口，允许在加压的情况下插入贯入仪。一大块均匀的人工制备的砂（约1到5吨）被制成事先确定的孔隙比（和相对密度），同时施加可变的垂直和横向应力，并引入不同程度的预固结（1 ≤ OCR ≤ 15）。标定罐外部有坚固的钢壳，但内部墙壁由柔性橡胶膜构成，这些膜被加压以创建环绕大型土样品的有效应力状态。砂的各个方面在测试之前都被量化（例如，D50、细料含量、矿物学、干燥/饱和等）。

Different methods of sample preparation have been used, including: pluviation, moist tamping, slurry, compaction, sedimentation, and vibration. Then, after the sand is placed to a desired initial state ($e_0$ and $D_R$), a consolidation stress is applied ($\sigma'_{v0}$, $\sigma'_{h0}$, and OCR). Finally, a CPT is conducted and the measured $q_t$, $f_s$, and/or $u_2$ are recorded. Each CCT takes approximately one week for preparation, while the CPT is completed in about 15 seconds. Several CCT test series are required in order to develop relationships between the CPT readings and pre-push $D_R$ and stress state ($\sigma'_{v0}$, $\sigma'_{h0}$, and OCR).

不同的样品制备方法已被使用，包括：降雨法、湿压法、浆料法、压实法、沉降法和振动法。然后，在砂放置到所需的初始状态（e₀ 和 DR）后，施加固结应力（\sigma'v₀、\sigma'ℎ₀ 和 OCR）。最后，进行CPT测试，并记录测得的 qt、fs 和/或 $u_2$。每次标定罐测试大约需要一周时间来进行准备，而CPT测试则仅需约15秒钟。需要进行多次标定罐测试系列，以建立CPT读数与预设的DR和应力状态（\sigma'v₀、\sigma'ℎ₀ 和 OCR）之间的关系。

Several early findings of CPT in CCT of select quartzitic sands have been reported; i.e. Schmertmann (1978), Baldi et al. (1981), and Robertson and Campanella (1983). These studies showed that correlations of $D_R$ with clean quartz sands depended on the measured cone tip resistance ($q_t = q_c$) and effective vertical overburden stress ($\sigma'_{v0}$), with additional effects caused by more hard-to-quantify factors such as sand compressibility, particle fracture, $K_0$ horizontal stresses, and grain crushing. The relative size of the cone penetrometer and chamber size also influence the measured resistances.

已经报道了在选定石英砂的标定罐测试中的几项早期CPT发现；例如Schmertmann（1978年）、Baldi等（1981年）以及Robertson和Campanella（1983年）。这些研究显示，对于纯净石英砂，DR与测量的锥尖阻力（qt = qc ）和有效垂直过载应力（\sigma′v0）之间存在相关性，另外还受到更难以量化的因素影响，如砂的压缩性、颗粒断裂、K0水平应力和颗粒破碎。圆锥贯入仪相对尺寸和标定罐的尺寸也会影响测量到的阻力大小。

### 4.2.2 Quartz-Silica Sands

### 4.2.2 石英砂

Notably, CPT calibration chamber test results require a correction factor due to boundary effects and yielding of the flexible walls during testing. Since the chambers are not infinitely wide (as in natural sand deposits), the measured $q_c$ values are lower than the true values corresponding to far-field conditions. The aforementioned correlations therefore overestimate the actual $D_R$ in the field.

显然，CPT标定罐试验结果需要校正因子，这是由于测试过程中边界效应和柔性壁的屈服所导致的。由于标定罐不是无限宽的（如天然砂岩堆积中那样），测得的qc值比远场条件下的真实值要低。因此，上述相关性因此会高估实际的场地DR值。

A well-known published correlation based on 5 series of uncorrected CCT on clean quartzitic and silica sands (Jamiolkowski et al. 1985) was aware of this important finding, however their recommendation involved a correction factor applied to the field measured $q_c$ before entering their laboratory correlation for $D_R$ in terms of stress-normalized cone resistance: $q_{c1} = (q_c/\sigma_{atm})/(\sigma'_{v0}/\sigma_{atm})^{0.5}$. Many users are apparently unaware of this correction factor and used the lab curves directly, thereby overestimating the relative density on their projects.

根据1985年Jamiolkowski等人发表的5组未校正的纯净石英砂和硅砂的CCT数据，他们意识到了这一重要发现。然而，他们的建议在根据应力标准化锥尖阻力反映实验室DR相关性之前使用一个相关系数对场地qc进行测量：qc1 = (qc/\sigmaatm)/(\sigma′v0/\sigmaatm)。许多使用者显然不了解这个校正因子，直接使用实验室曲线，从而在项目中高估相对密实度。

The correction for flexible-walled chambers depends upon the type of boundary conditions (BC1, BC2, BC3, or BC4) and relative sizes of the penetrometer and chamber. The correction factor used by Kulhawy & Mayne (1990) for clean sands for the BC1 boundary conditions (general stress state: $\sigma'_{vc}$ and $\sigma'_{hc}$) was developed as a function of the penetrometer diameter ($d$), chamber diameter ($D$), and relative density of the sand. The correction factor was developed based on statistical analyses of 6 sands that had been tested by different size cones and/or different size chambers at various $D_R$. Houlsby & Yu (1990) derived correction factors based on large strain cavity expansion analyses that were checked with CCT data obtained at Oxford University. Salgado et al. (1998) used numerical finite element simulations to develop correction curves for CPTs in CCT.

对于柔性筒壁的修正取决于边界条件的类型（BC1、BC2、BC3或BC4）以及贯入仪和标定罐的相对尺寸。Kulhawy & Mayne（1990）针对纯净砂土在BC1边界条件（一般应力状态：σvc'和σhc'）下使用的修正因子是根据贯入仪的直径（d）、标定罐的直径（D）和砂土的相对密实度建立函数而开发的。该修正因子是基于对6种砂土的统计分析开发的，这些砂土经过不同尺寸的锥体和/或不同尺寸的标定罐在各种DR下测试。Houlsby & Yu（1990）根据大应变孔隙扩张分析导出了修正因子，这些分析得到了在牛津大学获得的CCT数据的验证。Salgado等人（1998）使用数值有限元模拟开发了CCT中圆锥CPT试验的修正曲线。

A decade and a half later, Jamiolkowski et al. (2001) returned to their original database, corrected the lab $q_c$ values to equivalent field values, and then produced a direct $D_R$-$q_{t1}$ relationship (see Figure 4.3). Note that in clean sands, essentially the measured cone resistance is the total cone resistance ($q_c = q_t$) because the measured porewater pressures are close to hydrostatic and the unequal end area correction is very small compared to typical measured tip resistances in sands.

十五年后，Jamiolkowski等人（2001年）回顾了他们最初的数据库，将实验室测得的qc值校正为等效的现场数值，然后建立了一个直接的DR − qt1关系（见图4.3）。需要注意的是，在纯净的砂土中，基本上测得的锥尖阻力就是总锥尖阻力（qc = qt），因为测得的孔隙水压接近静水压，并且由于不等的端面积校正与典型砂土中测量锥尖端阻力相比非常小。

![image](images/rId109.png)

*Figure 4.3: Relative density from stress-normalized cone tip resistance in clean NC sands where CPT calibration chamber test data have been corrected for limited D/d ratios (modified after Jamiolkowski, LoPresti, and Manassero 2001)*

The derived relationship for relative density in terms of stress-normalized cone tip resistance can be expressed:

这个用应力归一化锥尖阻力来表达的相对密度的关系可以表示为：

$$D_R(\%) = 100(0.268 \ln(q_{t1}) - b_x) \tag{4.5}$$

where the term $b_x = 0.675$ applies to the mean correlation for normally-consolidated (NC) clean sands. Jamiolkowski et al. (2001) show three lines corresponding to different sand compressibilities: high ($b_x = 0.525$), medium ($b_x = 0.675$), and low ($b_x = 0.825$). Their guidance suggests that sands of high compressibility include mica sands, calcareous sands, and carbonate sands. Siliceous sands (approximately equal parts of quartz and feldspar) comprise the medium compressibility range. Sands of low compressibility include those of quartz, such as Ottawa sand. For practical use in sands of unknown mineralogy, the recommended general expression is:

在正常固结（NC）纯净砂土中，bx = 0.675 适用于平均相关性。Jamiolkowski等人（2001年）展示了三条曲线，对应不同的砂土可压缩性：高（bx = 0.525）、中（bx = 0.675）和低（bx = 0.825）。他们的建议高可压缩性的砂包括云母砂、钙质砂和碳酸盐砂。硅质砂（大约等量的石英和长石）属于中等可压缩性范围。低可压缩性的砂包括石英砂，例如渥太华砂。对于未知矿物组成的砂土的实际应用，推荐的一般表达式是：

$$D_R(\%) = 100(0.268 \ln(q_{t1}) - 0.675) \tag{4.6}$$

Also shown in Figure 4.3 are data from undisturbed samples of 15 sands compiled by Mayne (2006). Because a complete understanding of boundary effects in CCT is not yet fully realized, the corrections can only be considered approximate at best (Maki et al. 2013). In the statistical approach, the correction factor depends upon the relative sizes of the chamber diameter ($D$) and penetrometer diameter ($d$), such that:

图4.3还显示了Mayne（2006年）编制的15种未扰动样本的数据。由于对标定罐（CCT）中边界影响的完全理解尚未完全实现，因此这些校正只能被认为是近似的（Maki等人，2013年）。在统计方法中，校正因子取决于罐直径（D）和贯入仪直径（d）的相对大小，如下所示：

$$\frac{q_{t,field}}{q_{t,chamber}} = \left(\frac{D/d - 1}{70}\right)^{0.005 D_R} \tag{4.7}$$

Data from 705 CCTs on 26 different sands that were corrected for boundary effects produced the overall mean relationship for NC and OC sands (Kulhawy and Mayne 1990, 1991):

$$D_R(\%) = 100\sqrt{\frac{q_{t1}}{305 \cdot (OCR)^{0.2}}} \tag{4.8}$$

A comparison of the two $D_R$ expressions for NC sands are seen to be in excellent agreement in Figure 4.4 for range of relative densities from 0% to 80% where thereafter, the square root algorithm of Equation 4.8 gives slightly higher $D_R$ values than the log function of Equation 4.6.

在图4.4中，比较了正常固结砂的两个DR表达式，发现在相对密度从0%到80%的范围内，两者表现出极好的一致性；在此范围之后，方程式4.8的平方根算法给出的DR值略高于方程式4.6的对数函数。

![image](images/rId114.png)

*Figure 4.4: Relative density relationships from stress-normalized cone tip resistance in clean NC and OC sands (after Mayne 2014)*

### 4.2.3 Carbonate-Calcareous Sands

### 4.2.3 碳酸盐-钙质砂

Calibration chamber tests have also been used with prepared deposits of calcareous-carbonate sands. Table 4.1 provides a summary of index parameters on 6 carbonate sands that were tested in CPT chambers, including: Quiou Sand (Fioravante et al. 1998); Dogs Bay (Nutt and Houlsby 1991), Ewa (Morioka and Nicholson 2000), Kingfish (Parkin 1991), Kenya (Jamiolkowski and Pepe 2001), and Jeju Sand (Lee et al. 2010). Figure 4.5 presents the trend of $q_{t1}$ versus $D_R$ from the available six series of chamber tests on carbonate sands. The trends for carbonate sands appear to be rather independent of calcite content in the range $42\% < CaCO_3 < 98\%$ and indicate simply that:

标定罐试验还用于预制的钙质-碳酸盐砂堆。表4.1总结了在标定罐试验中测试的6种碳酸盐砂的指标参数，包括：Quiou Sand（Fioravante等，1998年）、Dogs Bay（Nutt和Houlsby，1991年）、Ewa（Morioka和Nicholson，2000年）、Kingfish（Parkin，1991年）、Kenya（Jamiolkowski和Pepe，2001年）和Jeju Sand（Lee等，2010年）。图4.5展示了来自这六个系列碳酸盐砂标定罐试验的qt1与DR之间的趋势。碳酸盐砂的趋势似乎相对独立于碳酸钙含量（42% < CaCO3 < 98%），并且简单表示为：

$$D_R(\%) = 0.87(q_{t1}) \tag{4.9}$$

**Table 4.1:** *Properties of carbonate-calcareous sands tested by CPT in chamber tests*

| | Quiou | Dogs Bay | Ewa | Kingfish | Kenya | Jeju | Remarks / Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Location | France | Ireland | Hawaii | Australia | Africa | S.Korea | |
| $D_{50}$ (mm) | 0.58 | 0.25 | 0.80 | 0.30 | 0.13 | 0.41 | Mean grain size |
| FC (%) | 3.00 | 0.10 | 1.00 | 7.00 | 0 | 1.00 | Fines (< 75 μm) |
| CU | 4.52 | 2.66 | 5.60 | 3.05 | 1.86 | 1.61 | $D_{60}/D_{10}$ |
| $G_S$ | 2.72 | 2.75 | 2.70 | 2.71 | 2.785 | 2.79 | Specific Gravity |
| $e_{max}$ | 1.28 | 1.83 | 1.30 | 1.53 | 1.778 | 1.44 | Maximum void ratio |
| $e_{min}$ | 0.83 | 0.98 | 0.66 | 1.07 | 1.283 | 1.03 | Minimum void ratio |
| $CaCO_3$ (%) | 77 | 87 to 92 | 98 | "high" | 97 | 42.60 | Calcium Carbonate |

*Figure 4.5: Relative density from stress-normalized cone tip resistance in carbonate sands*

The two trends for quartz-silica sands are also shown in Figure 4.5. When $D_R < 30\%$, the measured cone tip resistances are similar for all data sets, suggesting that loose sands behave somewhat comparably during full-displacement type penetration despite their mineralogical differences. However, for $D_R > 30\%$, the trends for quartz-silica sands show significant increases in cone resistance, since the hard particles must be forced aside during penetration. On the other hand, the trends for calcareous-carbonate sands remain similar when $D_R > 30\%$ implying grain breakage, fracturing, and crushing as the particles are pushed closer together as they approach their minimum packing arrangement (i.e., $e_{min}$). Thus, it can be concluded that CPTs in carbonate sands are not particularly sensitive to the in-situ relative density.

图4.5中还显示了石英-硅砂的两种趋势。当DR < 30%时，所有锥尖阻力测量数据相似，表明在全位移类型的贯入过程中，尽管矿物学上存在差异，松散砂的行为在某种程度上是相似的。然而，对于DR > 30%，石英-硅砂的趋势显示出锥尖阻力显著增加，因为在穿透过程中必须强行推开硬颗粒。然而当DR > 30%时，钙质-碳酸盐砂的趋势仍然保持于小于30%时类似，表明颗粒在接近最小堆积排列（即emin）时发生破碎和压碎。因此，可以得出结论，碳酸盐砂中的圆锥静力触探试验对原位相对密度并不特别敏感。

An alternative approach to addressing CPTs in carbonate sands involves the use of a shell correction factor (SCF) which “corrects” the cone resistance of the carbonate sand to a value equivalent to that for a “normal” quartz or silica sand, i.e. SCF = $q_c$ (silica)/$q_c$ (calcareous). Suggested SCF trends with $D_R$ are given by Wehr (2005), Mayne (2014), and Mengé et al. (2016).

在处理碳酸盐砂中的圆锥静力触探试验时，另一种方法是使用壳体校正因子（SCF），该因子将碳酸盐砂的锥尖阻力“校正”为等效于“普通”石英或硅砂的值，即SCF = qc (硅)/qc (碳酸盐)。建议的壳体校正因子随着DR的变化趋势由Wehr (2005), Mayne (2014), 和Mengé等人 (2016) 提供。

### 4.3 State Parameter

### 4.3 状态参数

The state parameter is a variable defined in critical-state-soil-mechanics (CSSM) to characterize the state of the soil. In CSSM, soil behavior depends on the state parameter. The state parameter, $\psi$, is the difference in void ratio between the initial value ($e_0$) and the value at failure ($e_{CSL}$) under the same effective stress, as shown in Figure 4.6 and expressed by:

状态参数是在临界状态土力学（CSSM）中定义的一个变量，用于描述土壤的状态。在临界状态土壤力学中，土壤的行为取决于状态参数。状态参数 ψ 是初始状态（e0）和在相同有效应力下失效状态（eCSL）之间的孔隙比差，如图4.6所示，其表达式为：

$$\psi = e_0 - e_{CSL} \tag{4.10}$$

![image](images/rId118.png)

*Figure 4.6: Definition of State Parameter in Simplified Critical State Soil Mechanics*

This value at failure defines a critical-state-line (CSL) in void ratio versus log mean effective stress space. The state parameter is negative if the soil is dense of critical (called “dilative” or “dilatant”) and is positive if the soil is loose of critical (called “contractive”). Soils at different initial void ratios and different initial effective stress states can have identical state parameters.

失效时的这个值定义了在孔隙比与对数平均有效应力空间中的临界状态线（CSL）。如果土壤是紧密的或者达到了临界状态（称为“膨胀性”或“膨胀”的），状态参数为负；如果土壤是松散的或者未达到临界状态（称为“收缩性”的），状态参数为正。具有不同初始孔隙比和初始有效应力状态的土壤可以具有相同的状态参数。

The CPT method to estimate $\psi$ originated in the analysis of calibration chamber test results presented in Been et al. (1987a; 1987b; 1988, 1989), and Been & Jefferies (1993). This method is discussed later in Chapter 9 as well as alternative approaches.

估计状态参数 ψ 的CPT方法源于Been等人（1987a; 1987b; 1988, 1989）和Been & Jefferies（1993）在标定罐试验结果分析中提出的方法。这种方法稍后在第9章及其替代方法中进行了讨论。

Under the simplified CSSM assumption that the CSL and NCL are parallel in $e - \log_{10}(p’)$ space and defined by the slope $\lambda_{10}$, where $P’ = \frac{1}{3}(\sigma’_1+\sigma’_2+\sigma’_3)$ = mean effective stress. As such, then $\lambda_{10}$ equals the compression index, $C_c$.

根据简化的临界状态土壤力学（CSSM）假设，在孔隙比与对数平均有效应力空间中，临界状态线（CSL）和非临界状态线（NCL）是平行的，并由斜率 λ10 定义，其中 P’ = ⅓(σ1’+σ2’+σ3’) = 平均有效应力。因此，λ10 等于压缩指数 Cc。

## 4.4 Overconsolidation Ratio and Yield Stress Ratio

## 4.4 超固结比和屈服应力比

In traditional soil mechanics concepts concerning one-dimensional consolidation, the soil state is represented by the overconsolidation ratio: $OCR = \sigma’_p/\sigma’_{v0}$, where $\sigma’_p$ = preconsolidation stress.

在传统的土力学概念中，关于一维固结的概念，土壤状态可以用过固结比来表示：OCR = σp’/σvo’，其中σp’ = 先期固结应力。

The effective preconsolidation stress or yield stress of soils is best determined through a series of one-dimensional consolidation tests performed on undisturbed samples taken at different elevations in soil formation. Additional knowledge of the local engineering geology and terrain helps to put the stress history profile in perspective (Locat et al. 2003). Figure 4.7 shows an example consolidation test conducted on a specimen of silty clay taken from a depth of 21.5 feet at a highway embankment site in Evergreen, North Carolina ($w_n = 70.8\%$, LL = 44%, PI = 19%).

土壤的有效预固结应力或屈服应力最好通过对取自不同土层高程的原状样品进行一系列一维固结试验来确定。对当地工程地质和地形的额外了解有助于全面理解应力历史剖面（Locat et al，2003）。图4.7展示了在北卡罗来纳州埃弗格林高速公路路基场地深21.5英尺处取样的粉质粘土试件上进行的一例固结试验（含水量为70.8%，液限为44%，塑限为19%）。

Results are plotted in terms of void ratio ($e$) versus log of effective vertical stress ($\sigma'_v$), with a calculated in-situ effective overburden stress of $\sigma'_{v0} = 0.43$ tsf.

结果以孔隙比（e）与对数垂直有效应力（\sigma′v）作图，计算得到原位的有效上覆应力为 \sigma′v0 = 0.43 tsf。

![image](images/rId119.png)

*Figure 4.7: Results from one-dimensional consolidation tests on clay from Evergreen, NC*

Using the classical construction technique from Casagrande (1936), a most probable magnitude of effective yield stress $\sigma'_p = 0.80$ tsf is determined. Following the orange dashed line, a minimum estimate of 0.65 tsf and maximum estimate of 0.90 tsf are also extracted. Beyond this procedure, some 30 different methods have been developed for the evaluation of $\sigma'_p$ from consolidation test data (Ku and Mayne 2013).

使用Casagrande (1936)的经典建构技术，确定了有效屈服应力的最可能大小为 \sigma′p = 0.80 tsf。根据橙色虚线，还提取了最小估计值为0.65 tsf和最大估计值为0.90 tsf。除此之外，已经开发了大约30种不同的方法，用于从固结试验数据中评估 \sigma′p（Ku和Mayne, 2013）。

While oedometer and consolidometer tests will remain the benchmark for determining stress history profiles, there are often cases when “undisturbed” samples are difficult to procure because of the silty to sandy nature of the the ground, effects of sample disturbance, and stress relief, as well as the expense and necessary time required for laboratory testing. Therefore, it has become of great interest and motivation in seeking CPT-based methods to evaluate stress history profiles of soils. These can be used to complement the laboratory program and fill in gaps between sampling depths and locations.

尽管固结仪和一维固结仪试验仍然是确定应力历史剖面的基本方法，但通常存在难以获取“原状”样品的情况，这受到地面土质从粉质到砂质不等、样品扰动效应和应力释放以及实验室测试所需的费用和时间等因素影响。因此，寻求基于CPT的方法来评估土壤的应力历史剖面变得非常有趣且鼓动人心。这些方法可以用来补充室内实验的不足，并填补采样深度与位置的空白。

The yield stress ($\sigma'_p$) can also be shown relative to the state parameter $\psi$ in simplified critical state soil mechanics, as seen by Figure 4.8.

有效屈服应力（\sigma′p）也可以相对于简化的临界状态土壤力学中的状态参数 ψ 来展示，如图4.8所示。

![image](images/rId120.png)

*Figure 4.8: Yield stress idealization in traditional e-log(σv') graph for soil consolidation*

The term YSR is becoming more prevalent because the traditional OCR is associated to mechanic unloading effects (i.e., erosion, glaciation, excavation), while other geologic and environmental changes can also cause an apparent preconsolidation, including: desiccation, compaction, cyclic loading, repeated wetting-drying, seasonal thermal changes, diagenesis, and other effects (Leroueil & Hight 2003; Jardine et al. 2004).

Another convenient parameter for representation of soil stress history is the over consolidation difference, OCD = ($\sigma'_p - \sigma'_{v0}$), or YSD, also called prestress ($\Delta\sigma'_p$), as detailed by Locat et al. (2003). The advantage of the OCD (or YSD) is that it is constant with depth for soil deposits that have become preconsolidated by erosion, glaciation, and/or excavation, where in contrast, the magnitude of OCR decreases with depth (Mayne 2007b). For those cases, the two parameters are interrelated via:

YSR这个术语变得越来越普遍，因为传统的OCR与力学卸载效应相关（例如，侵蚀、冰川作用、开挖），而其他地质和环境变化也可以导致明显的先期固结，包括：干燥、压实、循环荷载、反复的浸润与干燥、季节性热力变化、成岩作用以及其他影响（Leroueil & Hight，2003年；Jardine等人，2004年）。

另一个用于表示土壤应力历史的便利的参数是超固结差异，OCD = (\sigma′p - \sigma′v0)，或称为YSD，也称为预应力（Δ\sigma′p），如Locat等人（2003年）所述。OCD（或YSD）的优点在于，对于已经通过侵蚀、冰川作用和/或开挖而超固结的土层，它在深度上是恒定的，而OCR的幅度则随深度减小（Mayne，2007年b）。对于这些情况，这两个参数通过以下关系相互关联：

$$OCR = \frac{\sigma'_p}{\sigma'_{v0}} = YSR \tag{4.11}$$

$$OCD = \sigma'_p \cdot (OCR - 1) = YSD \tag{4.12}$$

### 4.4.1 Analytical CPT Model for YSR in Clay

### 4.4.1 粘土中YSR的CPT分析模型

A hybrid analytical model for piezocone penetration in clays was developed by combining Spherical Cavity Expansion (SCE) theory and Critical State Soil Mechanics (CSSM), as detailed by Mayne (1991) and Chen and Mayne (1994). The formulation provided separate evaluations for the OCR in terms of the net cone resistance ($q_{net} = q_t - \sigma_{v0}$) and/or the measured excess porewater pressure ($\Delta u = u_2 - u_0$):

由Mayne (1991)和Chen与Mayne (1994)详细阐述的一种混合解析模型，结合了球形小孔扩张（SCE）理论和临界状态土力学（CSSM），用于研究黏土中孔压静力触探装置的贯入。该公式提供了两种不同的OCR评估方式，包括基于净锥尖阻力（qnet = qt - \sigma′v0）和/或测得的超孔隙水压力（Δu = $u_2$ - $u_0$）：

$$OCR = 2 \cdot \left[ \frac{(q_t - \sigma_{v0})/\sigma’_{v0}}{\frac{2}{3}M \cdot \ln I_r + 1 + \frac{\pi}{2}} \right]^{1/\Lambda} \tag{4.13}$$

$$OCR = 2 \cdot \left[ \frac{(u_2 - u_0)/\sigma’_{v0} - 1}{\frac{2}{3}M \cdot \ln I_r - 1} \right]^{\Lambda} \tag{4.14}$$

where $M = 6 \cdot \sin \phi_{cs} / (3 - \sin \phi_{cs})$ and is the frictional envelope in Cambridge University type $q$-$p’$ space, $q$ = deviator stress, $p’$ = mean effective stress, $I_r = G / S_u$ = the undrained rigidity index, $G$ is the shear modulus, $S_u$ is the undrained shear strength, and $\Lambda$ = the plastic volumetric strain potential. The value of $\Lambda \approx 0.8$ for many insensitive clays and increases to about $\Lambda \approx 1$ for sensitive and quick clays (Mayne 1988; Ladd 1991; Ladd and DeGroot 2003). The magnitude of $\Lambda$ also depends upon shear mode (Kulhawy and Mayne 1990). From CSSM, the theoretical value of $\Lambda = 1 - C_s / C_c$ with $C_s$ = recompression or swelling index, and $C_c$ = virgin compression index.

其中M = 6 ∙ sin ϕcs / (3 − sin ϕcs) 是剑桥大学q-p'空间中的摩擦包络线，q 是偏应力，p' 是平均有效应力，$I_r$ = G / Su 是不排水刚度指数，其中 G 是剪切模量，Su 是不排水抗剪强度，Λ 是塑性体积应变能。对于许多不敏感的黏土，Λ 的值约为0.8，而对于敏感和快速黏土，Λ 增加到约为1（Mayne 1988；Ladd 1991；Ladd 和 DeGroot 2003）。Λ 的大小也取决于剪切模式（Kulhawy 和 Mayne 1990）。根据临界状态土力学，Λ 的理论值为Λ = 1 – Cs / Cc，其中 Cs 是回缩或膨胀指数，Cc 是原始压缩指数。

By combining Equations 4.13 and 4.14, a third estimate of OCR can be formulated in terms of effective cone resistance ($q_e = q_t - u_2$) that removes the reliance on rigidity index ($I_r$):

通过结合方程4.13和4.14，可以基于有效锥尖阻力（qe = qt - $u_2$）制定第三个OCR的估算公式，而不依赖于刚度指数（$I_r$）：

$$OCR = 2 \cdot \left[ \frac{1}{1.95M + 1} \cdot \frac{q_t - u_2}{\sigma'_{v0}} \right]^{\Lambda} \tag{4.15}$$

For soft to firm clays, the shear-component of porewater pressures is small (< 20%) of the total measured porewater pressures, thus Equation 4.14 can be reduced without much error to:

对于软到坚硬黏土来说，剪切成分的孔隙水压力占总测量孔隙水压力的比例很小（< 20%），因此方程4.14可以简化而不会引入太大误差为：

$$OCR \approx 2 \cdot \left[ \frac{(u_2 - u_0)/\sigma'_{v0}}{\frac{2}{3}M \cdot \ln I_r} \right]^{1/\Lambda} \tag{4.16}$$

The above equations can be simplified from their power law formulations to form linear equations for evaluating effective yield stress in intact clays. For the three respective CPTU expressions given by Equations 4.13, 4.16, and 4.15 with an adopted value $\Lambda = 1$, we obtain:

以上方程可以从它们的幂律形式简化为线性方程，用于评估完整黏土的有效屈服应力。对于由方程4.13、4.16和4.15给出的三个相应的CPTU表达式，采用$\Lambda = 1$的值，我们得到：

$$\sigma'_p = \frac{q_t - \sigma_{v0}}{M \left(1 + \frac{1}{3} \ln I_r \right)} \tag{4.17}$$

$$\sigma'_p \approx \frac{u_2 - u_0}{\frac{1}{3} M \cdot \ln I_r} \tag{4.18}$$

$$\sigma'_p = \frac{q_t - u_2}{0.975M + 0.5} \tag{4.19}$$

### 4.4.2 Simplified Expressions for Intact Insensitive and Inorganic Clays

### 4.4.2 简化的完整不敏感无机黏土的表达式

For intact inorganic clays of low sensitivity, these expressions can be further simplified for practical use by adopting characteristic values of $M_c = 1.2$ ($\phi' = 30°$) and $I_r = 100$ (Mayne 2001, 2005):

对于低敏感性的完整无机黏土，这些表达式可以通过采用特征值 Mc = 1.2 （ϕ' = 30°）和 $I_r$ = 100 （Mayne 2001, 2005）进一步简化，以便实际应用：

$$\sigma'_p = 0.33(q_t - \sigma_{v0}) \tag{4.20}$$

$$\sigma'_p = 0.54(u_2 - u_0) \tag{4.21}$$

$$\sigma'_p = 0.60(q_t - u_2) \tag{4.22}$$

These relationships have been studied for a wide variety of clays, including statistical studies involving 206 different sites (Chen and Mayne 1996). Additional data from 22 sites in Canada confirmed a general validity in that region (Demers and Leroueil 2002), as well as validation by extensive testing for the Pisa tower site in Italy (Jamiolkowski and Pepe 2001).

这些关系已经被研究应用于各种的黏土类型，包括涉及206个不同场地的统计研究（Chen和Mayne，1996年）。加拿大22个场地的额外数据证实了该地区的普遍有效性（Demers和Leroueil，2002年），并且在意大利比萨斜塔工地经过了大量测试的验证（Jamiolkowski和Pepe 2001）。

Figure 4.9 shows the general trend for effective yield stress with net cone resistance given by showing reasonableness in intact soft to firm to stiff to hard clays. Fissured clays are seen to lie above the relationship in a special grouping of data. It is thought here that one-dimensional tests (i.e.constrained compression) of fissured clays will result in closure of existing cracks, fissures, and discontinuities, whereas the corresponding same clays tested in-situ by piezocone will in fact open the discontinuities and cracks due to the full-displacement requirement of testing. Thus, a disconnect between one-dimensional and three-dimensional response for this grouping of fissured geomaterials.

图4.9显示了在给定净锥尖阻力下有效屈服应力的一般趋势，展示了软到硬黏土中完整状态的合理性。在一组特殊的数据中，裂隙粘土位于关系线上部。在这里认为，裂隙黏土的一维试验（例如约束压缩试验）将导致现有裂缝、裂隙和不连续性的闭合，而同一类型的黏土通过原位的孔压静力触探试验则实际上会由于试验的全位移要求而使得不连续性和裂缝开放。因此，这些裂隙岩土材料的一维与三维响应之间存在脱离关系。

![image](images/rId127.png)

*Figure 4.9: Evaluation of preconsolidation stress from cone resistance in intact clays with low organic content and low sensitivity*

For intact clays, it is useful to utilize all three equations, as redundancy can be helpful in geotechnical site characterization. If the three methods show consensus, then this helps to validate a “well-behaved” or “vanilla” clay and encourages the use of these relationships. An illustrative application of all three solutions given by Equation 4.20, Equation 4.21, and Equation 4.22 is given in Figure 4.10 for soft clay at the national test site in Bothkennar, UK (Hight et al. 2003). Two soundings are shown with one early series completed in 1992 and the other in 2005 (Powell & Lunne 2005b). The profiles of $\sigma'_p$ and YSR from the three CPTU-estimates compare well with the rather large set of consolidation test results at this site. Three types of consolidation tests were completed: (a) incremental load (IL) oedometer; (b) restricted flow (RF) consolidometer; and (c) constant-rate-of strain (CRS). The soft Bothkennar clay is lightly-overconsolidated (LOC) with $1.3 < YSR_s < 1.8$ at depths greater than 4 m.

对于完整的黏土来说，利用所有三个方程式是有用的，因为冗余有助于岩土工程现场特性的表征。如果三种方法显示一致性，那么这有助于验证“行为良好”或“普通”的黏土，并能支持这些关系的使用。图4.10展示了在英国Bothkennar的国家测试场地上软黏土的示例应用，使用了方程式4.20、4.21和4.22提供的所有三种解决方案（Hight等人，2003年）。显示了两个探测数据，一个是1992年完成的早期系列，另一个是2005年完成的（Powell & Lunne，2005b）。这三个CPTU估计得到的\sigma′p和YSR的剖面与该场地上相当大的一组固结试验结果很好地形成对照。在场地内进行了三种类型的固结试验：（a）增量加载（IL）固结仪；（b）限制流（RF）固结计；和（c）恒定应变速率（CRS）试验。Bothkennar软黏土在深度超过4米时呈轻度超固结状态（LOC），其YSRs在1.3到1.8之间。

Thus for “regular” natural clays that are inorganic and relatively insensitive, the following relationships should apply:

因此，对于无机且相对不敏感的“普通”天然黏土，以下关系应适用：

$$\sigma'_p \approx 0.33 \cdot q_{net} \approx 0.54 \cdot \Delta u_2 \approx 0.60 \cdot q_E \tag{4.23}$$

### 4.4.3 CPTU Screening for Sensitive Clays

### 4.4.3 利用孔压静力触探呈现敏感黏土

If the three methods show disparities, then a closer examination and scrutiny of the lab and/or field data may be warranted, perhaps justification that additional tests and investigation should be conducted. With large amounts of special mineralogy in the soil (i.e., calcite, diatoms, forams, etc.), it may be possible to re-tune these equations for the particular geologic formation attributes (e.g., Mayne 2005).

如果三种方法显示出差异，那么可能需要更仔细地审查和检视实验室和/或现场数据，也许需要进行额外的测试和调查。如果土壤中存在大量特殊的矿物组合（例如方解石、硅藻、有孔虫等），可能需要重新调整这些方程式以适应特定的地质形成属性（例如Mayne，2005年）。

![image](images/rId129.png)

*Figure 4.10: Results of CPTU soundings in 1992 and 2005 and yield stresses in regular soft clay at Bothkennar site, UK (lab and field data from Hight et al. 2003; Powell & Lunne 2005b)*

As an alternative or supplement to the SBT charts for the CPTU identification of organic clays and sensitive or structured clays, equations (4.20), (4.21), and (4.22) can be useful. For instance, CPTU results in the soft sensitive Leda clay at the Canadian Test Site 1 in Gloucester, Ontario are presented in Figure 4.11.

作为识别有机黏土、敏感或结构化黏土的CPTU方法的替代或补充，方程式（4.20）、（4.21）和（4.22）可能会很有用。例如，加拿大安大略省Gloucester的测试场地1中软敏感的Leda黏土的CPTU结果显示在图4.11中。

![image](images/rId130.png)

*Figure 4.11: Results of CPTU sounding in sensitive soft Champlain Sea clay at Gloucester national test site, Ontario (after Agaiby & Mayne 2018)*

In general, it has been found that the signature for sensitive clays is in the following hierarchy (Agaiby & Mayne 2018a; Mayne, Greig, & Agaiby 2018; DiBuö et al. 2019; Mayne & Benoît 2020):

一般而言，已经发现敏感黏土的特征遵循以下层次结构（Agaiby & Mayne 2018a; Mayne, Greig, & Agaiby 2018; DiBuö et al. 2019; Mayne & Benoît 2020）：

$$0.60 \cdot q_E < 0.33 \cdot q_{net} < 0.54 \cdot \Delta u_2 \tag{4.24}$$

In the Gloucester results, it can be seen that sensitive clays are evident in the depth interval from about 7 to over 20 m. A closer examination of the three post-processed CPTU curves show: (a) crustal layer to 2 m; (b) an implied organic layer from 2 to 3.5 m; (c) slightly sensitive clay from 3.5 to 5.5 m; (d) regular clay layer from 5.5 to 6.8 m; and (e) very sensitive to quick clay below 7 m through the remainder of the sounding beyond 20 m.

在Gloucester的结果中，可以看出敏感黏土在约7至20米以上的深度范围内很明显。对三个后处理的CPTU曲线进行更详细的检查显示：（a）从地表到2米处为壳层；（b）表明有机层从2米到3.5米；（c）稍微敏感的黏土层从3.5米到5.5米；（d）普通黏土层从5.5米到6.8米；以及（e）从7米以下到20米深处及深孔剩余部分的非常敏感到快速黏土。

### 4.4.4 CPTU Screening for Organic Clays

### 4.4.4 利用孔压静力触探呈现有机黏土

Using the set of triplet equations for p', the observed signature of organic soils has been observed to form the following hierarchy (Mayne et al. 2019; 2020):

使用三元方程组来计算有效应力（σp'），已观察到有机土壤的特征形成了以下层次结构（Mayne等人，2019年；2020年）：

$$0.54 \cdot \Delta u_2 < 0.33 \cdot q_{net} < 0.60 \cdot q_E \tag{4.25}$$

which surprisingly is the opposite order of that noted previously for sensitive clays.

A representative sounding in soft organic silty clays along the Potomac River in Washington DC are presented in Figure 4.12. The SBTn system puts "organic soils" in Zone 2. However, the SBTn analysis at this site indicates the soils are within zone 3 (clay) and zone 4 (silt mixtures), in contrast to the soil boring logs and lab tests which clearly indicate organic soils (OL and OH) from depths of 1 to 13.5 m. Note that the soft clays in the small region from 13.5 to 15.0 m in Figure 4.12 appear to be "regular" inorganic type soils.

令人惊讶的是，这与先前对敏感黏土的观察顺序相反。

在华盛顿特区波托马克河沿岸对软有机淤泥黏土进行了代表性的探测，如图4.12所示。SBTn系统将“有机土壤”放置在第2区。然而，该地点的SBTn分析表明，土壤位于第3区（黏土）和第4区（淤泥混合物），与土壤钻探记录和实验室测试明确指出的1到13.5米深的有机土壤（OL和OH）相反。注意图4.12中从13.5到15.0米的小区域内的软黏土看起来是“常规”的无机类型土壤。

![image](images/rId133.png)

*Figure 4.12: Results of CPTU in soft organic clays at Bolling AFB, Washington DC*

### 4.4.5 Evaluation of Yield Stress from CPTU in Sensitive Clays

### 4.4.5 CPTU对敏感粘土屈服应力的评价

If the CPTU screening indicates sensitive or structured clay, then a modified SCE-CSSM is available for the evaluation of YSR with depth (Mayne, Greig, and Agaiby 2018). In this case, the former equations 4.13, 4.14, and 4.15 become:

如果CPTU呈现出敏感或结构化黏土，则可以使用修改后的SCE-CSSM方法评估该深度下的YSR（Mayne, Greig和Agaiby，2018年）。在这种情况下，原来的方程4.13、4.14和4.15变为：

$$YSR = 2 \cdot \left[ \frac{Q/M_{c1}}{0.667 \cdot \ln I_R + 1.95} \right]^{1/\Lambda} \tag{4.26}$$

$$YSR = 2 \cdot \left[ \frac{U^* - 1}{0.667 \cdot M_{c2} \cdot \ln I_R - 1} \right]^{1/\Lambda} \tag{4.27}$$

$$YSR = 2 \cdot \left[ \frac{(Q - M_{c1})/(M_{c2} \cdot (U^* - 1))}{(1.95 \cdot M_{c1} + M_{c1})/M_{c2}} \right]^{1/\Lambda} \tag{4.28}$$

where $\Lambda \approx 1$ in sensitive clays, $I_R = G/S_u$ = rigidity index, and $M_c = 6 \cdot \sin \phi'/(3 - \sin \phi')$ = frictional parameter in $q$-$p'$ space. The value of $M_c$ is defined at two points on the effective stress path: (1) $M_{c1}$ at peak strength (i.e., $\phi'_1$ at $q_{max}$) and $M_{c2}$ is the value at maximum obliquity (i.e., $\phi'_2$ at large strains), best obtained from either CIUC or CAUC triaxial tests. Note that the condition: $M_{c1} \leq M_{c2}$ should be met.

其中在敏感黏土中  ≈ 1，$I_R$ = G/su = 刚度指数，而 Mc = 6·sin'/(3-sin') = q-p' 为空间中的摩擦参数。Mc 的值在有效应力路径上的两个点定义如下：（1）在峰值强度时的 Mc1（即 qmax 处的 '1），以及在最大斜率处的 Mc2（即大应变时的 '2），最佳通过CIUC或CAUC三轴试验获得。注意应满足条件：Mc1 ≤ Mc2。

The undrained rigidity index is found from the slope parameter ($a_q$) by plotting $\Delta u/\sigma$ vs. $q_{net}$ Alternatively, aq is found as the slope of (U-1) versus Q. Using the CPTU data from sensitive Leda clay at the Gloucester ON site shown in Figure 4.11, the value of the slope parameter is $a_q = 0.783$, as shown in Figure 4.13. Only data from the sensitive clay portion at depths ranging from 7 to 18 m are used, since the results for depths < 7 m appear to suggest a different clay type. Then, the SCE-CSSM formulation directly provides the assessment of undrained rigidity index:

通过绘制 u vs. qnet 图来找到不排水刚度指数，斜率参数 (aq) 也可以作为 (U-1) vs. Q 的斜率来找到。利用图4.11中显示的格洛斯特安大略州敏感莱达黏土的CPTU数据，斜率参数的值为aq = 0.783，如图4.13所示。仅使用深度在7到18米范围内的敏感黏土部分的数据，因为深度小于7米的结果似乎表明了不同的黏土类型。然后，SCE-CSSM公式直接提供了不排水刚度指数的评估：

$$I_R = \exp\left[\frac{1.5 + 2.925 \cdot M_{c1} \cdot a_q}{M_{c2} - M_{c1} \cdot a_q}\right] \tag{4.29}$$

For Gloucester, assigned values of $\phi’_1 = 25°$ and $\phi’_2 = 39°$ provide corresponding $M_{c1} = 0.98$ and $M_{c2} = 1.59$ which are reasonable and comparable to values from laboratory CIUC and CAUC triaxial tests on undisturbed samples made by Bozozuk (1972) and Landon (2007). These values together with $a_q$ provide a calculated $I_R = 95$. Finally, eqns 4.26, 4.27, and 4.28 can provide three new evaluations of $\sigma’_p$ and YSR with depth at the Gloucester national test site, as presented in Figure 4.14 (Agaiby & Mayne 2018). Clearly, the three interpreted CPTU curves now all agree with each other and provide values that match the profile determined using laboratory consolidation test data.

在格洛斯特地区，假定的值 ’1 = 25° 和 ’2 = 39° 分别提供了对应的 Mc1 = 0.98 和 Mc2 = 1.59，这些值是合理的，并且与由Bozozuk（1972年）和Landon（2007年）进行的原状样品CIUC和CAUC三轴试验结果相符。这些值与斜率参数 aq 一起提供了计算得到的 $I_R$ = 95。最后，方程 4.26、4.27 和 4.28 可以提供在格洛斯特国家试验场深度上的三个新的p’和YSR的评估，如图4.14（Agaiby & Mayne 2018）所示。显然，现在解释的三条CPTU曲线相互一致，并提供与实验室固结试验数据匹配的值。

For regular clays that are insensitive and inorganic, note that the values of $M_c = M_{c1} = M_{c2}$. Therefore eqn (4.29) reduces to simply:

对于常规的不敏感和无机黏土，注意到 Mc 的值为 Mc1 = Mc2。因此，方程 (4.29) 简化为：

$$I_R = \exp\left[\frac{1.5 + 2.925 \cdot M_c \cdot a_q}{M_c(1 - a_q)}\right] \tag{4.30}$$

![image](images/rId137.png)

*Figure 4.13: Determination of slope parameter $a_q$ from CPTU data in sensitive Leda clay at Gloucester ON test site*

![image](images/rId138.png)

*Figure 4.14: Profiles of yield stress and YSR in soft sensitive Leda clay at Gloucester using modified SCE-CSSM solutions*

### 4.4.6 Evaluation of Yield Stress from CPTU in Organic clays

### 4.4.6 根据CPTU的有机黏土屈服应力评估

Once the presence of organic soil and organic clay layers have been identified, the effective yield stress can be estimated from (Mayne, Coop, Springman, Huang, & Zornberg 2009):

一旦确认存在有机土壤和有机粘土层，可以根据（Mayne, Coop, Springman, Huang, & Zornberg 2009）估算有效的屈服应力：

$$\sigma'_p = 0.33 \cdot q_{net}^{0.9} \tag{4.31}$$

For the Bolling AFB data with CPTU in organic clays (prior Figure 4.12), the application of this approach is shown in Figure 4.15 indicating good agreement with the consolidation tests.

对于Bolling空军基地的CPTU数据在有机粘土中（参见之前的图4.12），这种方法的应用如图4.15所示，显示与固结试验的良好一致性。

![image](images/rId140.png)

*Figure 4.15: Profiles in soft organic clay at Bolling Air Base, Washington, DC: (a) net cone resistance; (b) yield stress*

## 4.5 Yield Stress of Sands from CPT

## 4.5 来自于CPT的砂土屈服应力

A statistical review of over 626 calibration chamber tests on 26 different clean silica and quartz sands determined relationships between the applied stress state, including effective vertical stress ($\sigma'_v$), lateral stress ratio ($K_0 = \sigma'_{hc}/\sigma'_{vc}$), and induced OCR, with the measured net cone resistance ($q_{net}$), as shown by Figure 4.16. The direct expression for evaluating the OCR in clean sands is given by (Mayne 2007a; 2007b; 2017):

对26种不同的纯净硅和石英砂进行的626次标定罐试验的统计回顾确定了应力状态之间的关系，包括有效垂直应力（\sigma′v）、侧向应力比（K0 = \sigma′ℎc/\sigma′vc）和推导的OCR，与测得的净锥尖阻力（qnet）的关系，如图4.16所示。评估纯净砂中OCR的直接表达式由（Mayne 2007a; 2007b; 2017）给出：

$$OCR = \left[\frac{0.192 \cdot (q_{net}/\sigma_{atm})^{0.22}}{(1 - \sin \phi) \cdot (\sigma'_{v0}/\sigma_{atm})^{0.31}}\right]^{1/(\sin \phi - 0.27)} \tag{4.32}$$

Taking a characteristic value of friction angle $\phi = 35.5°$ for clean sands, the above reduces to the linear format:

对于纯净砂的摩擦角特征值取为 𝜙 = 35.5°，上述表达式简化为线性格式：

$$\sigma'_p = 0.08 \cdot (q_{net})^{0.7} \cdot (\sigma_{atm})^{0.7} \tag{4.33}$$

Using SI units, the reference stress $\sigma_{atm} \approx 1$ bar $= 100$ kPa, therefore further diminishes to the even simpler expression:

使用国际单位制（SI单位），参考应力 \sigmaatm ≈ 1 bar = 100 kPa，因此进一步简化为更加简单的表达式：

$$\sigma'_p (kPa) \approx 0.32 \cdot (q_{net})^{0.72} \tag{4.34}$$

which bears an uncanny resemblance to the expression for clay given by Equation 4.20. As shown by Figure 4.17, this simplified approach for clean sands compares well with the more rigorous algorithm given by Equation 4.32 for the specified ranges of $\phi$, stress levels, and normalized cone resistance. For high stress levels and high $\phi$ values, the simplified approach will underpredict the yield stresses.

这个表达式与方程式4.20给出的黏土表达式非常相似。如图4.17所示，这种简化方法在纯净砂中与方程式4.32给出的更严格算法在指定的𝜙、应力水平和归一化锥尖阻力范围内有较好的吻合。对于高应力水平和高𝜙值，简化方法会低估屈服应力。

![image](images/rId144.png)

*Figure 4.16: Calibration chamber test data for OCR evaluation in clean sands*

![image](images/rId145.png)

*Figure 4.17: Simplified yield stress approach for clean sands vs. rigorous algorithm*

### 4.5.1 Case Study: Blessington Sand Site, Ireland

### 4.5.1 案例研究：爱尔兰的Blessington沙地

The aforementioned approach can be applied to a case study involving dense OC sands in Ireland reported by Doherty et al. (2012) and Igoe & Gavin (2019). The glacially-derived dense fine sands have an in-place relative density around 100% and mean particle size: $0.10 < D_{50}$ (mm) $< 0.15$ mm. Mineralogies include a predominance of quartz with calcite component, and subsets of feldspar, mica, and kaolinite fractions.

上述方法可以应用于爱尔兰由Doherty等人（2012年）和Igoe & Gavin（2019年）报道的涵盖密集OC砂的案例研究。这些冰川衍生的密集细砂的原位相对密度约为100%，平均粒径为：$0.10 < D_{50}$ (mm) $< 0.15$ mm。其矿物包括以石英为主的成分，含有方解石成分，以及少量的长石、云母和高岭石等亚组分。

Measured cone tip resistances from 4 CPT soundings at the test site are presented in Figure 4.18. Samples of the sand were procured using sonic drilling methods that were later tested in the laboratory consolidometer to define the yield stress ($\sigma'_p$) per Casagrande method. The interpreted profiles of yield stress from the simplified CPT approach are shown in Figure 4.18 along with a comparison to the lab reference values, with good agreement evident.

在测试现场进行的4个CPT中测得的锥尖阻力见图4.18。使用声波钻孔方法获取的沙样后，在实验室使用固结仪进行测试，以定义根据Casagrande方法的屈服应力（\sigma′p）。简化的CPT方法解释的屈服应力剖面如图4.18所示，并与实验室参考值进行了比较，显示出良好的一致性。

![image](images/rId146.png)

*Figure 4.18: Yield stress profiles from CPT and lab consolidation tests in dense OC sands at Blessington, Ireland*

## 4.6 Unified Interpretation of Yield Stress in Soils by CPT

4.6 CPT统一解译土壤的屈服应力

For the general case of evaluating effective yield stress in all soil types, Figure 4.19 provides a compilation of data from a variety of natural formations, including sands, silts, clays, and mixed geomaterials. The expressions for clays, Equation 4.20, and sands, Equation 4.34, can be united to provide the general format:

对于评估所有土壤类型有效屈服应力的一般情况，图4.19汇编了来自多种自然形成物的数据，包括砂土、淤泥、黏土和混合地质材料。方程式4.20，对于黏土的表达式，以及方程式4.34，对于砂土的表达式可以结合起来给出如下的一般形式：

$$\sigma’_p = 0.33 \cdot (q_{net})^{m’} \cdot \left(\frac{\sigma_{atm}}{100}\right)^{1-m’} \tag{4.35}$$

![image](images/rId148.png)

*Figure 4.19: General relationship for yield stress in soils from CPT net cone resistance (modified after Mayne et al. 2009)*

where the exponent $m’$ increases with fines content and decreases with mean grain size.

Specifically, the value of m' ≈ 0.72 in clean quartz sands, 0.8 in silty sands, 0.85 in silts, 0.90 in organic clays, and m' = 1.0 in intact clays of low sensitivity. It may even take on values of 1.1+ in fissured geomaterials.

If only SI units are used (kPa), the form simply becomes:

其中，指数m'随着细料含量的增加而增加，随着平均颗粒大小的减小而减小。

具体来说，在纯净石英砂中，m'约为0.72，在含粉土的砂中为0.8，在粉砂中为0.85，在有机黏土中为0.90，在低敏感度完整黏土中为1.0。在有裂缝的地质材料中，m'甚至可以取1.1以上。

如果仅使用国际单位制（kPa），其形式简化为：

$$\sigma'_p (kPa) = 0.33 \cdot \left[\frac{q_{net}(kPa)}{kPa}\right]^{m'} \tag{4.36a}$$

However, for any units:

而对于任意单位：

$$\sigma’_p = 0.33 \cdot (q_{net})^{m’} \cdot (\sigma_{atm}/100)^{1-m’} \tag{4.36b}$$

The CPT material index $I_{C,RW}$ is logically a means of quantifying the magnitude of exponent $m’$ for general profiling of $\sigma’_p$ in homogeneous soils, heterogeneous deposits, mixed geomaterials, and stratified formations. Figure 4.20 shows the trend between $m’$ and CPT index ($I_c$) given by:

CPT材料指数 IC,RW是用于量化指数 m’ 在均质土壤、非均质沉积物、混合地质材料和分层形成物中分析 \sigma′p 综合轮廓的手段。图4.20显示了 m’ 与 CPT指数 (Ic) 之间的趋势，其表达式如下：

![image](images/rId151.png)

$$m' = 1 - \frac{0.28}{1 + (I_{C,RW}/2.65)^{25}} \tag{4.37}$$

![image](images/rId152.png)

*Figure 4.20: Trend for yield stress exponent ($m'$) with CPT material index ($I_c$)*

Where possible, the interpreted $\sigma'_p$ results should be cross-checked and validated with other information, such as the results from one-dimensional consolidation tests on high-quality undisturbed samples, as well as the geologic stress history. In certain cases, additional results and corroboration may be obtained by running other in-situ tests, such as the flat plate dilatometer test (DMT) and/or vane shear test (VST), as discussed elsewhere (Kulhawy and Mayne 1990; Schnaid 2009).

The exponent m' term has also been related to the fines content (FC), mean grain size (D50), and several definitions of CPT material index, as summarized in Table 4.2 (Agaiby 2018).

在可能的情况下，应当通过交叉检验和验证解释的 \sigma′p 结果，使用其他信息，比如高质量未扰动样本的一维固结试验结果，以及地质应力历史。在某些情况下，可以通过进行其他原位测试，如扁铲侧胀试验（DMT）和/或十字板剪切试验（VST），以获取额外的结果和协作验证，详见其他文献（Kulhawy和Mayne，1990年；Schnaid，2009年）。

指数 m' 项也与细料含量（FC）、平均颗粒大小（D50）以及几种CPT材料指数的定义相关联，如表4.2中总结的（Agaiby，2018年）。

**Table 4.2: Relationships for CPT Yield Stress Exponent $m'$ with Select Geoparameters** (after Agaiby 2018)

| Parameter | Definition | Relationship | Eqn |
|-----------|-----------|--------------|-----|
| $D_{50}$ | Mean grain size (mm) | $m' = 0.72 + \dfrac{0.28}{1 + (16 \cdot D_{50})^5}$ | (4.38) |
| FC | Fines content (% < 0.075 mm) | $m' = 1 - \dfrac{0.28}{1 + (FC/55)^{20}}$ | (4.39) |
| $I_{cRW}$ | Index per Robertson (2009) | $m' = 1 - \dfrac{0.28}{1 + (I_{cRW}/2.65)^{25}}$ | (4.40) |
| $I_{SBT}$ | Index per Robertson (2010) | $m' = 1 - \dfrac{0.28}{1 + (I_{SBT}/2.4)^{20}}$ | (4.41) |
| $I_{cJB}$ | Index per Jefferies & Been (2015) | $m' = 1 - \dfrac{0.28}{1 + (I_{cJB}/2.5)^{30}}$ | (4.42) |
| $I_B$ | Index per Robertson (2016) | $m' = 0.72 + \dfrac{0.28}{1 + (I_B/32)^{8}}$ | (4.43) |
| $I_{cR'09}$ | Index per Robertson (2022) | $m' = 1 - \dfrac{0.28}{1 + (I_{cR'09}/2.6)^{15}}$ | (4.44) |

## 4.7 Evaluation of YSR from $V_s$

4.7 根据Vs估计YSR

If SCPTU results are available, then the shear wave velocity can provide a first-order estimate on the yield stress and YSR. Figure 4.21 shows a direct trend between the yield stress and Vs for clays, using an extended database reported by Mayne, Robertson, & Lunne (1998). For intact clays, the general expression is given by:

如果SCPTU结果可用，则剪切波速度可以提供有关屈服应力和YSR的一级估算。图4.21显示了对黏土屈服应力和Vs之间的直接趋势，使用Mayne、Robertson和Lunne（1998年）报告的扩展数据库。对于完整黏土，一般表达式如下：

![image](images/rId154.png)

$$\sigma'_p \approx \left(\frac{V_s}{100}\right)^{1.5} \cdot \sigma_{atm} \tag{4.45}$$

![image](images/rId155.png)

*Figure 4.21: Relationship between effective yield stress of clays and shear wave velocity*

An illustrative example using eqn (4.45) is presented in Figure 4.22 from a SCPTU conducted along the north shore of Lake Superior for Ministry of Transportation Ontario. Here, the estimated three profiles of yield stress using eqn (4.23) from the piezocone readings compare very well with that from the Vs measurements. Also, the estimated shear wave velocity from eqn (2.22) nicely matches with the actual downhole measured Vs profile. If the latter had not been the case, the implications would be that the clay is not “regular” or “normal”, thus warranting additional testing or study into whether the clay was sensitive, organic, cemented, calcareous, or other more special in its behavior.

图4.22展示了一个使用方程(4.45)的说明性示例，该示例来自于在安大略省交通部北岸进行的SCPTU测试。在这里，使用方程(4.23)从孔压静力触探试验读数估算的三个屈服应力剖面与Vs测量结果非常吻合。此外，从方程(2.22)估算的剪切波速度与实际井下测得的Vs剖面非常匹配。如果不匹配，那么这意味着黏土不是“常规”或“正常”，因此需要额外的测试或研究，以确定黏土是否具有敏感性、有机质、胶结、钙化或其他更特殊的特性。

![image](images/rId156.png)

*Figure 4.22: SCPTU sounding at Lake Superior showing yield stress profile from $V_s$ data*

Similarly, a tentative trend for estimating yield stress can also be found for sands, as presented in Figure 4.23, and expressed:

类似地，关于砂土的屈服应力估算的初步趋势也可以在图4.23中找到，并表达为：

$$\sigma'_p \approx \left(\frac{V_s}{158}\right)^{3.43} \cdot \sigma_{atm} \tag{4.46}$$

where $V_s$ is in units of m/s.

其中$V_s$的单位为m/s。

![image](images/rId158.png)

*Figure 4.23: Preliminary relationship between effective yield stress of sands and $V_s$*

The estimated yield stress of sand should be verified using other means such as a detailed engineering geologic study and evaluation by other in-situ testing such as CPT and/or DMT.

应当通过其他手段验证估计的砂土屈服应力，例如进行详细的工程地质研究，并通过其他原位测试，如CPT和/或DMT进行评估。

# 5 Effective Friction Angle and Drained Strength

# 5 有效摩擦角和排水强度

The strength of soil is used to calculate the ultimate capacity of foundations, the stability of embankments, retaining walls, and slopes, and the achievable depth of an unbraced trench. It is used in assessing the stability of earth dams and heap leach piles, the resistance to static and cyclic liquefaction, and the trafficability of tailings deposits.

A strength failure usually results in very large deformations until the geometry of the geotechnical structure regains equilibrium. This can be a rotational failure under a foundation, a slump of a slope, or a collapse of an excavation.

土壤的强度用于计算基础的极限承载能力，路堤、挡土墙和坡道的稳定性，以及无支撑沟槽的可达深度。它在评估土坝和堆浸堆的稳定性、抗静态和循环液化、以及尾矿堆放的通行性中起到作用。

强度失效通常会导致非常大的变形，直到岩土工程结构的几何形状重新达到平衡。这可能是在基础下的旋转失效，坡道的滑动，或者开挖物的坍塌。

The critical strength of a soil is the large strain shear stress that can be supported. This shear stress is the frictional resistance along a plane of soil particles. It depends on the intrinsic friction angle and the effective normal stress at failure through $\tau = \sigma' \cdot \tan\phi'_{cs}$. The effective stress at failure depends on the initial state of the soil, the stress path, and drainage conditions during shearing.

A peak strength defined with a peak friction angle may be realized prior to failure. This includes the critical stress plus a dilation component due to the initial state of the soil.
