An undrained boundary condition may result in a peak undrained shear strength, which depends on the OCR of the soil.

土壤的临界强度是其可以承受的大应变剪切应力。这种剪切应力是沿着土壤颗粒平面的摩擦阻力。它取决于固有摩擦角和在失效时的有效法向应力，通过公式 $\tau = \sigma' \cdot \tan\phi'_{cs}$ 计算。在剪切发生时，有效应力在失效时取决于土壤的初始状态、应力路径和排水条件。

在失效之前，可能达到由峰值摩擦角定义的峰值强度。这包括临界应力加上由于土壤初始状态引起的膨胀成分。不排水的边界条件可能导致土壤达到峰值不排水剪切强度，这取决于土壤的超固结比（OCR）。

## 5.1 Drained Strength of Soils

## 5.1 土壤的排水强度

One of the most important soil properties required in analysis and design involving geotechnics is the friction angle ($\phi'_{cs}$) which is a fundamental property that controls much of its behavioral response to loading and initial stress state. The effective friction angle is a predominant geoparameter that governs the mechanical strength of soils within an important framework called critical state soil mechanics (CSSM). Details on CSSM are given elsewhere (e.g., Schofield and Wroth 1968; Jefferies and Been 2006, 2015; Idriss and Boulanger 2008; Mayne et al. 2009).

在涉及岩土工程分析和设计中，最重要的土壤性质之一是摩擦角（$\phi'_{cs}$），这是控制土壤在加载和初始应力状态下行为响应的基本性质。有效摩擦角是占主导地位的地质参数，它在一个重要的称为临界状态土壤力学（Critical State Soil Mechanics，CSSM）的框架内控制土壤的力学强度。有关CSSM的详细信息可以在其他地方找到（例如，Schofield和Wroth 1968年；Jefferies和Been 2006年、2015年；Idriss和Boulanger 2008年；Mayne等人2009年）。

The effective stress friction angle of sands (also termed angle of internal friction) represents the strength of the material in stability analyses, footing bearing capacity, pile end-bearing resistance, and side resistance in deep foundations, as well as assessing the coefficient of lateral stress ($K_0$). In terms of the commonly-adopted Mohr-Coulomb strength criterion, the shear strength ($\tau_{max}$) is expressed:

砂土的有效应力摩擦角（也称为内摩擦角）代表了材料在稳定性分析、地基承载能力、桩端承载力以及深基础侧阻力中的强度，同时也用于评估侧向应力系数（$K_0$）。在常用的莫尔-库仑强度准则下，剪切强度（$\tau_{max}$）表达为：

$$\tau = c’ + \sigma’ \tan \phi’ \tag{5.1}$$

where $c’$ = effective cohesion intercept (generally $c’$ = 0 for unbonded geomaterials). In most cases, the normal stress can be taken equal to the effective vertical stress: $\sigma’$ = $\sigma’_{v0}$.

其中，$c’$ = 有效黏聚力截距（通常对于非粘结的地质材料而言，$c’$ = 0）。在大多数情况下，正应力可以等于有效垂直应力：$\sigma’$ = $\sigma’_{v0}$。

### 5.1.1 Critical State Friction Angle, $\phi’_{cs}$

5.1.1 临界状态摩擦角$\phi’_{cs}$

Characteristic values of $\phi'_{cs}$ are on the order of 32° for quartz sands, 33° for silty quartz sands with up to 20% fines content, 34° for siliceous sands (76 approx. half quartz-half feldspar), 39° for calcareous sands, and 40° for feldspathic sands (Bolton 1986; Salgado, Bandini, and Karim 2000; Jamiolkowski and Pepe 2001). The friction angle also depends upon mode of testing (i.e., plane strain, triaxial) and direction of loading (compression, extension).

The critical state friction angle (also termed “fully softened” value) depends on the mineralogy, as noted before, and has been found related to the particle roundness. Figure 5.1 shows the trend for $\phi'_{cs}$ decreasing with roundness for a number of natural and crushed sands (Cho et al. 2006).

![image](images/rId160.png)

*Figure 5.1: Effect of particle roundness on critical-state friction angle of clean sands (modified from Cho et al. 2006)*

### 5.1.2 Peak Friction Angle, $\phi’_{p}$

The peak friction angle ($\phi’_p$) of sands is composed of two components: (1) a basic frictional value (designated $\phi’_{cs}$ for critical state) that is due to particle grain shape, compressibility characteristics and mineralogy; and (2) a dilatancy effect (quantified by $\phi’_{\psi}$ the dilatancy angle) which reflects the relative packing of particles ($e_0$ or $D_R$) and ambient stress level ($\sigma’_{v0}$ or $p’$).

Together, the two components combine to produce a peak friction angle:

$$\phi'_p \approx \phi'_{cs} + \phi'_{\psi} \tag{5.2}$$

For illustration, data from a series of triaxial tests on a quartz sand reported by Koerner (1970) are presented in Figure 5.2. As the relative density of the sand increases, so too the measured peak friction angle increases. After attaining the peak $\phi'_p$, strain softening is observed whereby all sand specimens tend towards their $\phi'_{cs}$, here approaching 33°. Realistic peak values may range from around 32° to 45° for the triaxial compression mode.

![image](images/rId162.png)

*Figure 5.2: Drained triaxial compression tests on quartz sand at different prepared relative densities (data from Koerner 1970)*

The effects of dilatancy on peak friction angle must also consider the effective stress state as well. This is best captured in the simplified framework presented by Bolton (1986). This framework is presented in Figure 5.3 where the dilatancy angle, $\phi'_{\psi}$, is expressed in terms of relative density and effective stress level. Data were compiled from triaxial testing of 17 different sands, primarily on reconstituted specimens (Boulanger and Ziotopoulou 2012). The peak friction angle can be expressed in the general format:

$$\phi'_p \approx \phi'_{cs} + 3\left(D_R\left(Q_x - \ln(p'_f)\right) - R_x\right) \tag{5.3}$$

where $Q_x$ = mineralogical component, $R_x$ = fitting parameter, and $p'_f$ = mean effective stress at failure. For purposes of practicality, the value of $p'_f$ can be taken to be around $2\sigma'_{v0}$ (Kulhawy & Mayne 1990). For quartz sands, recommended values of $Q_x$ = 10, $R_x$ = 1, and $\phi'$ ≈ 32° are given by Bolton (1986). Additional details concerning quartz, silica, glauconitic and calcareous sands, as well as sands with fines content are provided by Jamiolkowski et al. (2001). Note that on the y-axis of Figure 5.3 the term $\phi'_{\text{crit}} = \phi'_{cs}$.

A recent review of data from over 500 triaxial tests conducted by the NGI laboratory on primarily clean quartzitic sands from 38 different sites is presented in Figure 5.4 (Andersen and Schjetne 2013). Here, the effects of $D_R$ and effective confining stress are again seen to control the value of peak friction angle and supporting the dilatancy framework for peak friction angle of Bolton (1986).

![image](images/rId164.png)

*Figure 5.3: Bolton's framework for representing dilatancy effect on peak friction angle of sands (from Boulanger & Ziotopoulou 2012)*

*Figure 5.4: Bolton equation with superimposed data from over 500 triaxial tests on clean quartz sands at 38 sites (data from Andersen and Schjetne 2013)*

### 5.1.3 Drained Friction Angle of Sands from Cone Resistance

For the assessment of $\phi'_p$ of sands from CPT, there are several approaches: (a) use of a dilatancy framework where $q_t$ provides the input $D_R$ for the Bolton expression (b) inverse bearing capacity, such as from cavity expansion or limit plasticity theories (Yu and Mitchell 1998; Mayne 2006; Schnaid 2009); (c) numerical simulation by finite elements, finite differences, and/or discrete elements (e.g., Salgado, Mitchell, and Jamiolkowski 1998; Susila and Hryciw 2003; Lee, Salgado, and Carraro 2004); or (d) direct CPT methods (Lunne et al. 1997; Mayne 2007a; 2007b; Uzielli et al. 2013).

Many early approaches relied on reconstituted samples where small companion triaxial specimens were prepared at similar relative densities and confining stress levels to those of larger calibration chamber tests subjected to CPTs. The procedures for reconstituting specimens are not standardized, however, and include: pluviation, compaction, vibration, sedimentation, moist tamping, and/or slurry methods. Different triaxial results are obtained by the various reconstitution methods. Furthermore, in early studies, CPT data were not yet corrected for boundary conditions from limited size chambers (i.e., D/d ratio).

Towards an improved solution, an elite database was compiled from special expensive undisturbed samples of 13 clean sands, as presented in Figure 5.5 (Mayne 2006). The majority of these sands were initially frozen in-place using one-dimensional freezing technology, then transported to the laboratory for testing. After careful mounting of specimens in triaxial apparatuses with membranes, filter paper, and effective confinement, they were allowed to

![image](images/rId165.png)

*Figure 5.5: Direct CPT assessment of peak $\phi_p$ from undisturbed clean sands dataset (modified after Mayne 2006)*

thaw, and then sheared to failure in compression to derive $\phi'_p$ corresponding to undisturbed intact sands. The sandy sites were also subjected to SPT, CPT, and $V_S$ measurements, as well as other lab and field testing.

The triaxial data from undisturbed sands can be seen to fit nicely with the expression derived by Kulhawy and Mayne (1990) that was developed on the basis of CPT calibration chamber data that were corrected for boundary effects:

$$\phi'_p = 17.6° + 11.0 \log_{10}(q_{t1}) \tag{5.4}$$

where $q_{t1} = (q_t/\sigma_{atm})/(\sigma'_{v0}/\sigma_{atm})^{0.5}$ = stress-normalized cone tip resistance. Also note that the $\sigma_{atm}$ terms can be combined so that $q_{t1} = q_t/(\sigma_{atm} \cdot \sigma'_{v0})^{0.5}$. Data from 4 additional natural sands have been added to the original set: (a) Duncan Dam, BC (Plewes et al. 1993); (b) Hibernia Oil Field Thompson and Long (1989; Taylor, Lewis, and Ingersoll 1993); (c) Milford Dam, KS (Stark et al. 2011); and (d) CREC - Charleston, SC (Esposito III and Andrus 2016). The relationship in Figure 5.6 primarily applies to clean quartz to siliceous sands. For sands of high calcareous content, refer to the methodology suggested by Jamiolkowski et al. (2001).

To quantify the reliability of the aforementioned trend, Bayesian statistical and probabilistic analyses were performed (Uzielli et al. 2013), as shown in Figure 5.6. A power law function was employed that gave essentially the same results as with the following deterministic expression:

$$\phi’_p = 25.0° (q_{t1})^{0.10} \tag{5.5}$$

![image](images/rId168.png)

*Figure 5.6: Probability curves of non-exceedance in CPT evaluation of peak $\phi’$ in sands (after Uzielli et al. 2013)*

Detailed statistical analysis allowed for parallel design curves to be developed that provided probabilities ($P_L$) of non-exceedance, with $P_L$ ranging from 0.5% to 10%.

The excellent agreement between the deterministic power law curve given by Equation 5.4 in logarithmic format and the power law given by Equation 5.5 is evident in Figure 5.7. Here, additional data from three sand sites have been included: (a) McDonald’s Farm, BC (Robertson and Campanella 1983); (b) Stockholm sand (Dahlberg 1974); and (c) dense OC fine sand at Blessington, Ireland (Doherty et al. 2012).

![image](images/rId169.png)

*Figure 5.7: Deterministic and probabilistic algorithms for direct CPT evaluation of peak $\phi’$*

Recent use of Equation 5.4 has employed the updated form for stress-normalized cone resistance, $Q_{tn}$, in lieu of the simpler $q_{t1}$ (e.g., Robertson and Cabal 2015). Using the most recent set of triaxial tests from undisturbed samples of sands taken by freezing, gel-samplers, and mazier tubes, Figure 5.8 presents data from 27 sands to silty sands with $\phi'_p$ as a function of $Q_{tn}$.

For all practical purposes, the substitution works equally as well, resulting in (Uzielli & Mayne 2019):

$$\phi'_p = 17.6° + 11.0 \log_{10}(Q_{tn}) \tag{5.6}$$

![image](images/rId171.png)

*Figure 5.8: Peak friction angle of 27 sands and silty sands versus stress-normalized cone resistance, $Q_{tn}$ (data from Uzielli & Mayne 2019)*

The clustering of data above the line in the range of 50 < $Q_{tn}$ < 100 is believed to be due to the site-specific effects of sand mineralogy and particle angularity-roundness, as discussed earlier for Figure 5.1. Since the original equation was developed primarily for quartzitic and 84 alicaceous sands (Kulhawy and Mayne 1990), the evaluation will be on the low side and underpredict $\phi'_p$ in angular sands and soils with different mineralogies, such as feldspathic sands, crushed sands, and those with a calcite component.

### 5.1.4 Sand Friction Angle with CPT Material Index, Ic

The effective friction angle of sands and silty sands is observed to decrease with the CPT material index, as shown in Figure 5.9, resulting in the trend:

$$\phi'_p = 53° - 6.9 \cdot I_{cRW} \tag{5.7}$$

As noted earlier in Section 3.2.3, drained soil behavior is associated with $I_{cRW}$ < 2.6, therefore eqns (5.6) and (5.7) will apply in this range.

![image](images/rId173.png)

*Figure 5.9: Peak $\phi'$ versus CPT material index for 27 sands and silty sands (data from Uzielli & Mayne 2019)*

For silty sands, Lee, Salgado, and Carraro (2004) developed a numerical CPT simulation program for a variety of sands that have silt fractions added to them with corresponding lab measurements. Normalized curves were derived to relate the silty sand parameters to the simulated cone resistances and then fitted to the Bolton expression. Note that these were developed for soils that were reconstituted in the laboratory.

### 5.1.5 Case Study: Silty Sands at Georgia Tech

A test program on silty sands at the west side of the Georgia Tech campus was established for purposes of drilled shaft loading (Mayne & Harris 1993). Below a 3-m fill layer, the native geomaterials are residual soils derived from the in-place weathering of the parent gneiss and schist bedrock. The site is located within the Appalachian Piedmont geology. Rock was encountered approximately 21 m deep at this site and groundwater was about 16 m below grade.

A series of nine boreholes produced 113 samples. The disintegration of rock over time has formed an overburden consisting of silty sands (SM) with an average 67% fine sand, 24% silt, and 7% clay fraction. Mean grain size at the site is about $D_{50}$ = 0.14 mm.

A number of undisturbed Shelby tubes were procured in a boring at this site and transported to two commercial laboratories (Golder and GeoSyntec) for laboratory testing. Results from 13 consolidated triaxial compression tests determined an overall average $\phi’_p$ = 35.9° and $c’$ = 0 kPa for this soil, as illustrated by Figure 5.10.

A CPT profile at the site is shown in Figure 5.11 with cone resistance, sleeve friction, and index $I_{cRW}$. Application of Equations 5.6 and 5.7 to the CPT results in the natural residual silty sands in this case gave very reasonable agreement with the triaxial test values.

![image](images/rId174.png)

*Figure 5.10: Triaxial tests on natural silty sand from GT campus, Atlanta, GA*

![image](images/rId175.png)

*Figure 5.11: Application of CPT for evaluating friction angle of silty sands in Atlanta*

## 5.2 Effective Friction Angle of Clays from CPTU (NTH Solution)

For soft to firm fine-grained silty and clayey soils exhibiting excess porewater pressures during penetration ($B_q$ > 0.1), a limit plasticity solution for undrained penetration can be implemented towards the evaluation of $\phi’_p$. The theory was developed at the Norwegian Institute of Technology, NTH (Senneset et al. 1988; Senneset et al. 1989). In this approach, a cone resistance number ($N_m$) is defined by:

$$N_m = \frac{N_q - 1}{1 + N_u \cdot B_q} = \frac{q_t - \sigma_{v0}}{\sigma’_{v0} + a’} \tag{5.8}$$

where $a’ = c’ \cdot \cot\phi’ =$ attraction, $c’$ = effective cohesion intercept, $N_q = K_p \exp[(\pi - 2\beta)\tan\phi]$ is the end-bearing factor, $K_p = (1 + \sin\phi’)/(1 - \sin\phi’)$ is the passive stress coefficient, $\beta$ = angle of plastification (-30° < $\beta$ < +30°) which defines the size of the failure zone beneath the tip, $N_u = 6(\tan\phi’)(1 + \tan\phi’)$ is the porewater bearing factor. The full solution allows for an interpretation of a paired set of $c’$ and $\phi’$ for all soil types: sands, silts, clays, and mixed soils. For undrained loading, $\beta$ = 0 (Ouyang & Mayne 2018). A further simplification is taken for the case where $c’$ = 0, therefore the cone resistance number ($N_m$) becomes equivalent to the normalized cone tip resistance, $Q$ = $Q_t$ = $Q_{t1}$, or simply: $N_m = Q = (q_t - \sigma_{v0})/\sigma’_{v0}$.

These terms can be combined to produce a single equation:

$$Q = \frac{\tan^2(45° + \phi’_p/2) \cdot e^{\pi \tan \phi’_p} - 1}{1 + (6 \tan \phi’_p)(1 + \tan \phi’_p) B_q} \tag{5.9}$$

resulting in a family of curves (Figure 5.12) for various values of porewater pressure ratio, $B_q$.

![image](images/rId178.png)

*Figure 5.12: NTH solution chart for evaluating $\phi’_p$ from CPTU readings of Q and $B_q$ (after Senneset et al. 1989)*

### 5.2.1 Case Study: Soft Clay at Sandpoint Idaho

To illustrate the use of the NTH method, results from an 80-m deep CPTU conducted for the Idaho DOT State Route 95 in Sandpoint, Idaho are used (Figure 5.13). Details on the site conditions can be found in (Fellenius et al. 2004). Primarily, the sounding penetrates through a clayey silt to silty clay deposit with sand layers in the upper 10 m, at depths from 50 to 53 m, and 60 to 66 m.

Numerous sand “stringers” and lenses can be seen at other depths. For the post-processing, we are interested in the fine grained clays and silts, thus have chosen some representative points along the profile, as indicated by the open dots.

The procedure for determining the cone resistance number is found as the slope of plotting the net cone tip resistance versus the effective overburden, as illustrated in Figure 5.14a. In this instance, we force the best fit line through the origin (assuming $c’$ = 0) to obtain $N_m = Q$ = 4.2.

Similarly, the porewater parameter $B_q$ is determined as the slope of $\Delta u$ versus net cone resistance (Figure 5.14b), giving $B_q$ = 0.75 for the Sandpoint site.

The attained $Q$ and $B_q$ values are entered into the solution chart in Figure 5.15, finding a representative $\phi’_p$ = 32.5° for the clayey silt. This compares well with extensive series of laboratory CIUC triaxial tests conducted on undisturbed samples taken at the site (Figure 5.16).

![image](images/rId179.png)

*Figure 5.13: Representative CPTU sounding at Sandpoint, Idaho (after Fellenius et al. 2004)*

![image](images/rId180.png)

*Figure 5.14: Determination of (a) cone resistance number Nm from net cone resistance vs. effective overburden, and (b) porewater parameter Bq from Δu vs. net cone resistance at Sandpoint*

![image](images/rId181.png)

*Figure 5.15: NTH solution chart with Sandpoint data point; Figure 5.16: Laboratory CIUC triaxial test results from Sandpoint*

### 5.2.2 Approximate NTH Solution for $\phi’$ in Clays

Since Equation 5.8 necessitates an iterative procedure to evaluate $\phi’_p$ from $Q$ and $B_q$, an approximate NTH algorithm has been devised that allows a line-by-line analysis, easily handled by computer software or spreadsheets (Mayne 2005, 2016):

$$\phi’_p = 29.5° \cdot B_q^{0.121(0.256 + 0.336 B_q + \log Q)} \tag{5.10}$$

that is applicable to soft to firm clays with OCR < 2.5 and for the following ranges of parameters: 20° ≤ $\phi’_p$ ≤ 45° and 0.1 ≤ $B_q$ ≤ 1.0. Figure 5.17 shows the close match between the theoretical and approximate inversion algorithm over the specified value ranges.

![image](images/rId183.png)

*Figure 5.17: Comparison of theoretical NTH solution and approximate algorithm for evaluating $\phi’_p$ from CPTU*

### 5.2.3 Case Study: Soft Chicago Clay at Northwestern University

The national geotechnical experimental site (NGES) at Northwestern University (NWU) in Evanston, Illinois is underlain by a 10 m thick sand layer over 12 m of soft Chicago clay, as well as deeper stiffer layers. A variety of in-situ and laboratory tests have been conducted here to investigate the properties of the clay soils at this site (Finno et al. 2000). Figure 5.18 shows a representative portion of a CPTU sounding in the soft clay layers (Deerfield and Bloggett formations) from 10 to 22 m depths with respective total cone resistance ($q_t$), sleeve friction ($f_s$) and porewater pressures ($u_2$) obtained using the Georgia Tech cone truck (Mayne 2007b). The soft clay under investigation has a mean unit weight of 20 $kN/m^3$, water content of around 20%, and plasticity index PI= 12%.

Series of CAUC triaxial tests (consolidated anisotropically undrained triaxial compression tests) were conducted on undisturbed samples of these soft clay tills (Chung and Finno 1992). The triaxial results are shown in a MIT q-p’ stress space presented as Figure 5.19. An effective stress strength envelope defined by $c’$ = 0 and $\phi_p$ = 28.3° was determined. Figure 5.20 shows the profiles of $Q_{t1}$ and $B_q$ with depth at the site and application of the approximate NTH solution giving a corresponding $\phi_p$ = 28.8° that is comparable with the CAUC tests.

![image](images/rId184.png)

*Figure 5.18: Representative CPTU sounding in soft Chicago clay at NWU NGES site*

*Figure 5.19: MIT q-p’ stress space from CAUC triaxial tests on soft Chicago clay (Chung and Finno 1992)*

![image](images/rId185.png)

*Figure 5.20: Profiles of $Q_{t1}$ and $B_q$ with approximate NTH solution at Chicago site*

### 5.2.4 Case Study: Soft to Firm Clay at Newbury, MA

Boston Blue Clay (BBC) is a glacial marine deposit that exists in the greater Boston area due to deposition from glacial melt water (Landon 2007). At Newbury, Massachusetts, the BBC deposit consists of a 12 m thick clay layer with an overconsolidated crust and low OCRs in soft to firm clay at depth. The BBC at this site has an average water content of 45% and plasticity index PI = 24%. The groundwater table is located 1.7 m below ground level. Summary data from lab and in-situ tests are reported in Landon et al. (2004).

High-quality Sherbrooke block sampling and piston tube sampling were conducted at four depths at the site and trimmed specimens were subjected to laboratory testing to characterize soil parameters (Landon 2007). Beneath the crustal layer, a section of a representative CPTU sounding at the site from depths of 5 to 12 meters is shown in Figure 5.21. The effective peak friction angle from piston and Sherbrooke samples at each of four sampling depths ranges from 35° to 37°, averaging about $\phi_p$ = 36.5°. The corresponding profile of triaxial $\phi_p$ is in good agreement with the approximate NTH evaluation using CPTU data.

![image](images/rId186.png)

*Figure 5.21: CPTU sounding and NTH evaluation at Newbury, MA BBC site*

### 5.2.5 Friction Angle Database in Clays

Based on a study of 17 clays by Diaz-Rodriguez, Leroueil, and Aleman (1992), the effective stress friction angle of natural clays ranges from 17° to 43°. Figure 5.22 shows a summary plot of the peak friction angle of selected clays with their corresponding yield envelopes that are anchored by their friction angles and yield stress ($\sigma'_p$), or preconsolidation stress.

![image](images/rId187.png)

*Figure 5.22: Yield surfaces and effective stress envelopes for selected clays (modified after Diaz-Rodriguez, Leroueil, and Aleman 1992)*

For purposes of validating the NTH solution, a special database was compiled from 105 clay, silty clay, and clayey silt sites that were subjected to CPTU and laboratory triaxial compression tests (Ouyang and Mayne 2018). The majority of the sites were normally-consolidated (NC) to lightly overconsolidated (LOC) with 1 < OCRs < 2.5. Geologic origins of these soils vary from marine, alluvial, estuarine, glacial, and deltaic to lacustrine. The names of these sites are listed in Figure 5.23 with a unique and individual symbol assigned to each location.

![image](images/rId188.png)

*Figure 5.23: Summary listing of 105 clays tested both in laboratory triaxial compression and by field piezocone for validation of NTH solution*

Plasticity characteristics of these fine-grained soils ranged widely from very lean to highly plastic (4% < PI < 113%) with mean PI = 38.9% ± 23.3%. Water contents ranged from 20 to 160% with a mean = 61.6% and S.D. = 31.2%. Triaxial friction angles from triaxial tests ranged from 20.2° to 45.1°, with a mean value $\phi'_p$ = 31.6° ± 4.5°. The NTH solution gave a mean value $\phi'_p$= 32.2° ± 4.4°. Corresponding sensitivities generally were in the range of 2 to 8, indicating low to medium sensitive soils, except a few clays from Canada and Scandinavia that exhibited higher sensitivities.

For these clays, the effective stress friction angle $\phi'_p$ was obtained from laboratory triaxial tests (CIUC, CK0UC, and/or CAUC) performed on undisturbed samples to establish the benchmark value. The triaxial friction angle can be evaluated on the basis of different criteria, including: (a) maximum deviator stress ($q_{max}$); (b) maximum obliquity, M.O. = ($\sigma'_1/\sigma'_3$)max; (c) value taken at large strains (e.g., $\varepsilon$ = 15% axial strain). For the majority of the data, criteria (a) or (b) were used, although for many cases, the actual definition was not provided.

For many insensitive inorganic clays, the above criteria provide comparable values of $\phi'$. For instance, CK0UC triaxial tests on soft Atchafalaya clay gave $\phi'$ = 20.2° for $q_{max}$ and 20.9° for M.O.

(Donaghe & Townsend 1978). For soft Chicago clay, $\phi'$ = 28.3° at M.O. while at large strains, $\phi'$ = 27.6° (Finno & Chung 1992). These are small differences.

Figure 5.24 presents a summary plot for the measured laboratory triaxial test values of $\phi'$ versus the CPTU-determined values of $\phi'$ via the NTH solution. Two sets of statistical measures were made on the data set, including: (a) arithmetic statistics, and (b) regression statistics, as

![image](images/rId189.png)

*Figure 5.24: Triaxial $\phi'$ versus CPTU-evaluated value for 105 soft-firm clays with OCRs < 2.5 (Ouyang and Mayne 2018)*

indicated on the figure. The measured laboratory values of $\phi'_p$ cover the full range from 20.2° to 45.0° and the CPTU-evaluated $\phi'_p$ from 22.9° to 46.8°. From the arithmetic measures, the ratio of measured/evaluated values ranges from 0.88 to 1.16 with an overall mean of 0.98 and standard deviation = 0.06, with corresponding COV (coefficient of variation) = 0.06. From the regression evaluations of lab versus field, the regression slope = 0.98 with a coefficient of determination of $r^2$ = 0.833 and standard error of the Y-estimate of SEY = 1.88. The above statistics general support that the NTH method gives a reasonable evalution of the effective stress friction angle of clays when referenced to the lab triaxial test as the benchmark value.

## 5.3 Friction Angle of OC Intact Clays

For CPTU in overconsolidated intact clays, the same methodology applies, however the cone resistance number must be adjusted for stress history effects. For this, the modified cone resistance number ($N_{mc}$) becomes $Q’$ defined as (Ouyang & Mayne 2019; 2020b):

$$Q’ = \frac{Q}{\text{OCR}^{\Lambda}} \tag{5.11}$$

where OCR = $\sigma’_p/\sigma’_{vo}$ = overconsolidation ratio and $\Lambda$ = plastic volumetric strain ratio. Typical values of $\Lambda$ range from 0.5 to 0.7 for artificially prepared samples of clay (i.e., kaolin and kaolinitic-sand mixtures) to around 0.8 ± 0.1 for inorganic and insensitive clays to as high as 1 for structured and sensitive soils.

For OC clays, the normalized porewater pressure parameter $U = \Delta u_2/\sigma’_{vo}$ (Section 3.2.5) is also affected by stress history and can be similarly adjusted:

$$U’ = \frac{U}{\text{OCR}^{\Lambda}} \tag{5.12}$$

Otherwise, the same NTH solution given by rigorous equation (5.9) or the approximate algorithm (5.10) can be used to evaluate $\phi’$ using $Q’$ and $B_q$. Note also that $U’ = B_q \cdot Q’$.

### 5.3.1 Case Study: CPTU in OC Clay, Anchorage

Results of piezocone tests are utilized to illustrate the application of the modified NTH method for evaluating $\phi’$ in overconsolidated clay at the Port of Anchorage in Alaska. Extensive in-situ and laboratory testing programs have been conducted for the Port of Anchorage Expansion involving use of a SeaCore jack-up platform for conducting soil test borings, laboratory triaxial testing, one-dimensional consolidation, piezocone penetration tests, vane shear, and downhole shear wave velocities to characterize the subsurface profiles (Mayne & Pearce 2005).

Basic mean index parameters on this overconsolidated clay include: natural water content $w_n$ = 20 to 31%, liquid limit LL = 45%, and plasticity index PI = 24%. From the consolidation testing, the mean value of virgin compression index $C_c$ = 0.242 and swelling index $C_s$ = 0.060, giving $\Lambda$ = 1 - $C_s$/$C_c$ = 0.75, which is reasonable for low to medium sensitive clays (Kulhawy & Mayne, 1990).

Figure 5.25 shows the profiles of $q_t$ and $u_2$ from a representative piezocone sounding. Note that depth is measured from the water line and the seafloor begins at 12 m depth. Also a corresponding profile of the overconsolidation ratio (OCR) from the consolidation test data is shown. The OCRs decrease from 9 to 2 with depth in the intervals shown.

![image](images/rId192.png)

*Figure 5.25: Representative profiles at Port of Anchorage, Alaska: (a) CPTU results; (b) overconsolidation ratio from lab consolidation tests (data from Mayne & Pearce 2005)*

A series of 19 lab specimens were subjected to isotropically-consolidated type triaxial compression tests with porewater pressure measurements (CIUC). As shown by the effective stress paths in Figure 5.26, the effective stress friction angle $\phi’$ can be interpreted as $\phi’$ = 28° with $c’$=0.

Following the procedure for determining both the original cone resistance number $N_m = Q$ and the modified cone resistance $N_{mc} = Q’$ along with calculation of the normalized porewater pressure $B_q$, the effective stress friction angle $\phi’$ profiles calculated using the CPTU data are presented in Figure 5.27. It can be observed that the original NTH solution generates an unrealistically high friction angle profile (> 42°) whereas the friction angle interpreted using modified NTH solution gives excellent agreement with the corresponding friction angle determined from the laboratory triaxial compression tests at corresponding elevations, more or less centered about $\phi’$ ≈ 28°.

![image](images/rId193.png)

*Figure 5.26: Summary of CIUC triaxials on overconsolidated clay at Port of Anchorage (data from Mayne & Pearce 2005)*

*Figure 5.27: Profiles at Anchorage: (a) normalized CPTU parameters; (b) interpreted $\phi’$ from triaxial tests and CPTU using modified NTH solution*

## 5.4 Friction Angle of OC Fissured Clays by CPTU

Piezocone tests in fissured overconsolidated clays typically exhibit porewater pressure responses that are close to zero, thus $B_q$ ≈ 0. Thus, the rigorous equation 5.9 can be used for the case $B_q$ = 0 by replacing $Q$ with $Q’ = Q/\text{OCR}$ and solving for $\phi’$ by iteration. Otherwise, an approximate inversion can be made for the case where $B_q$ = 0 which is given by (Ouyang & Mayne 2019):

$$\phi’ \approx 8.18 \cdot \ln(2.13 \cdot Q’) \tag{5.13}$$

### 5.4.1 Case Study: CPTU in Fissured Beaumont Clay

The well-known Beaumont clay of Texas is a desiccated overconsolidated deltaic clay of Pleistocene age. These soils have shrink-swell problems with shallow foundation performance and rather extensive series of discontinuities and cracks. A representative CPTU sounding at a research tests site in Baytown Texas is shown in Figure 5.28 (Stuedlein 2008). The low $u_2$ readings are indicative of fissured OC clays.

Series of CIUC triaxial tests on undisturbed samples from the site indicated an effective stress envelope that can be represented by: $\phi’$ = 24° and $c’$ = 0, as presented in Figure 5.29. A series of one-dimensional consolidation tests were also conducted, showing the $\sigma’_p$ profile given in Figure 5.30(a). If consolidation tests had been unavailable, it can be seen that a quick estimate using the approximate expression $\sigma’_p$ ≈ 0.33$q_{net}$ gives reasonable values for the Baytown site. Using the modified profile of $N_{mc} = Q’$ based on a value $\Lambda$ = 0.7, it is evident that (5.13) provides values of $\phi’$ which are comparable to triaxial compression tests at the Baytown site.

![image](images/rId195.png)

*Figure 5.28: Representative CPTU sounding in fissured OC clay at Baytown, Texas*

![image](images/rId196.png)

*Figure 5.29: Results of CIUC stress paths for fissured OC clay at Baytown*

![image](images/rId197.png)

*Figure 5.30: Profiles in fissured Beaumont clay at Baytown, TX: (a) preconsolidation stress; (b) effective friction angle*

## 5.5 Summary of NTH Solutions for NC and OC Clays

In addition to the 105 soft to firm NC to LOC natural clays shown in Figures 5.23 and 5.24, the NTH solutions have been applied to piezocone tests in laboratory chamber tests, centrifuge deposits of clay, plus a number of OC intact and fissured clays, as well as VRCPTU, or variable rate piezocone tests (Ouyang & Mayne 2020a). Figure 5.31 shows the full set of some 155 clays that have been tested by both triaxial compression tests in comparison to the original and modified NTH solutions for assessing $\phi’$ from CPTU. A similar set of arithmetic and regression statistics are shown on the graph, comparable to the discussion in Section 5.2.5.

![image](images/rId198.png)

*Figure 5.31: Effective friction angle of clays from triaxial compression testing versus value from CPTU using NTH solutions (modified after Ouyang & Mayne 2020a)*

## 5.6 Effective Cohesion Intercept, c’

Herein, the effective cohesion intercept has been taken as zero ($c’$ = 0) since the vast majority of soils considered are NC to LOC with low OCRs. Technically, a value of $c’$ > 0 implies tensile strength which the majority of sands and clays do not have. Notably, $c’$ = 0 is a common assumption for soft to firm clays with OCRs < 2. Nevertheless, some stability analyses involving natural slopes, excavations, and soil nail walls require an assessment of $c’$ for limit equilibrium and/or finite element studies. Also, overconsolidated soils may show an apparent cohesion intercept and friction angle.

Guidance on the selection and magnitude of $c’$ can be found in a few other sources. Mayne & Stewart (1988) review data from CIUC and CAUC triaxial data on 16 different NC to OC clays and conclude that the effective cohesion intercept relates to the effective preconsolidation stress, expressed as the ratio $c’/\sigma’_p$ (Mayne 2016):

$$c’/\sigma’_p \approx 0.03 \tag{5.14}$$

Additional details concerning $c’$ values from backfigured stability analyses of 60 case study slope failures are discussed by Mesri & Abdel-Ghaffar (1993) who found that the ratio of effective cohesion intercept to yield stress fell within the following range: 0.003 < $c’/\sigma’_p$ < 0.11.

Of additional note, a means of extracting $c’$ directly from the CPTU data is also viable from the NTH solution (Senneset et al. 1989; Sandven 1990; Mayne 2016).

## 5.7 Effective Friction Angle(s) of Sensitive Clays

In the case of sensitive and quick clays, an analytical model for YSR presented in Chapter 4 utilizes two definitions of $\phi’$: (1) value at $q_{max}$, designated $\phi’_1$; and (2) value at M.O. = ($\sigma’_1/\sigma’_3$)max, designated $\phi’_2$. Specifically, the stress-strain-strength behavior of sensitive clays shows a peak deviator stress early, with strains on the order of $\varepsilon$ ≈ 1%, while the porewater pressure versus strain curve reaching a maximum value much later on, say $\varepsilon$ ≈ 10% or more. Analogous to the triaxial test, the CPTU can associate the cone resistance ($q_t$) to $\phi’_1$ and the porewater pressure reading ($u_2$) to the $\phi’_2$ value.

To obtain both values of $\phi’$ from CPTU, the following can be recommended: (a) use of the original NTH solution with $Q$ and $B_q$ provides $\phi’_2$; (b) implementation of the modified NTH solution with $Q’$ and $B_q$ gives the value of $\phi’_1$. Case studies that utilize the original and modified NTH solutions for obtaining $\phi’_2$ and $\phi’_1$, respectively, are given for sensitive clay sites in Canada (Agaiby & Mayne 2018a; Mayne, Greig, & Agaiby 2018), Norway (Mayne et al. 2019), Finland (DiBuö et al. 2019), and the USA (Mayne & Benoît 2020).

In fact, the ratio of $\phi’_1/\phi’_2$ is shown to track with slope parameter $a_q = (U-1)/Q$ that is described earlier in Section 4.4.5 (DiBuö et al. 2019). Figure 5.32 shows a summary of data from 8 regular and 14 sensitive natural clay deposits, as well as 4 artificial kaolinitic clays. The data indicate sensitive clays can be identified when $a_q$ > 0.5.

![image](images/rId200.png)

*Figure 5.32: Observed trend for ratio $\phi’_1/\phi’_2$ with slope parameter $a_q$ for 26 clays*

## 5.8 Peak versus Fully-Softened versus Residual Strength

The aforementioned discussions on strength reflect test results on soils where the peak strength or softened strength are relevant. The peak strength can be associated to OC clays and the fully-softened strength related to NC clays. In the case of clays involved in landslides and slope instability problems, the residual strength may be appropriate, especially if the clays have slickensides and fissures (Holtz, et al. 2011).

The residual shear strength can be represented by $\tau_{\text{residual}}$ given by (Lupini et al. 1981; Holtz, Kovacs, & Sheahan 2011):

$$\tau_{\text{residual}} = c’_{\text{residual}} + \sigma’ \cdot \tan\phi’_{\text{residual}} \tag{5.15}$$

Some additional guidance may be found in Stark & Eid (1994, 1997) and Stark & Hussein (2013).

# 6 Undrained Shear Strength

# 6 不排水剪切强度

When loading of soils results in a maintained constant volume of the soil mass ($\Delta V/V_0 = 0$), an undrained shearing mode occurs. This is most often associated with clays because they have low permeability and the rate of loading is usually high, such that water cannot escape the very tiny pores in the clay matrix, at least not immediately. Thus, a time-rate behavior occurs and a consolidation process begins that may take several hours, days, or even years for completion.

当土体的加载过程导致土体的体积保持恒定（$\Delta V/V_0 = 0$）时，便发生了不排水剪切模式。这种情况最常与粘土相关，因为粘土具有低渗透性，并且加载速率通常较高，以至于水无法从粘土基质中极小的孔隙中排出，至少不会立即排出。因此，随时间变化的行为会发生，随之而来的固结过程可能需要数小时、数天甚至数年才能完成。

In the soils laboratory, the triaxial apparatus is common and the resulting stress-strain-strength response is depicted in Figure 6.1. In terms of deviator stress ($\sigma_1 - \sigma_3$) versus axial strain, the undrained shear strength ($s_u$ or $c_u$) is conventionally defined as one-half the deviator stress at peak. However, other definitions are also found in use, such as maximum obliquity, $(\sigma_1'/\sigma_3')_{\max}$ (Abdulhadi, Germaine, and Whittle 2012), as well as large-strains, fully-softened behavior, and sometimes taken at a set specified strain, such as 15%.

在土工实验室中，常用的设备是三轴试验仪，其产生的应力-应变-强度响应如图6.1所示。以偏应力（$\sigma_1 - \sigma_3$）与轴向应变为指标，不排水剪切强度（$s_u$或$c_u$）通常定义为峰值偏应力的一半。然而，也存在其他的定义方式，例如最大倾斜度（$(\sigma_1'/\sigma_3')_{\max}$）（Abdulhadi, Germaine, 和Whittle 2012），以及大应变、完全软化行为，有时也采用在特定应变水平下，如15%的应变时的剪切强度。

![image](images/rId202.png)

Figure 6.1: Conventional laboratory definition of undrained shear strength at peak stress: (a) triaxial compression; (b) simple shear

图6.1：在峰值应力下常规实验室定义的不排水剪切强度：(a) 三轴压缩；(b) 简单剪切

Figure 6.1 also shows the corresponding definition of $s_u$ at peak value for the simple shear mode. In this case, direct simple shear (DSS) produces curves of shear stress ($\tau$) versus shear strain ($\gamma_s$). The maximum value of shear stress ($\tau_{\max}$) is termed the shear strength. If the test is conducted at constant volume, then the measured value is the undrained shear strength ($\tau_{\max} = s_u = c_u$).

图6.1还显示了简单剪切模式下，在峰值条件下的剪切强度定义。在这种情况下，直接简单剪切（DSS）产生的剪应力（$\tau$）与剪应变（$\gamma_s$）曲线。剪应力的最大值（$\tau_{\max}$）被称为剪切强度。如果测试在恒定体积下进行，那么测得的数值即为不排水剪切强度（$\tau_{\max} = s_u = c_u$）。

Note that if volume change is permitted, however, the $\tau_{\max}$ will represent a different condition. For instance, if no excess porewater pressures occur ($\Delta u = 0$), then the resulting shear strength would be the drained shear strength ($\tau_{\max} = \tau_d$). Intermediate shear strengths between fully undrained and fully drained are also possible.

需要注意的是，如果允许体积变化，那么$\tau_{\max}$将代表不同的条件。例如，如果没有产生超孔隙水压力（$\Delta u = 0$），那么所得的剪切强度将是排水剪切强度（$\tau_{\max} = \tau_d$）。在完全不排水和完全排水之间还可能存在中间的剪切强度。

## 6.1 Mode of Shearing and Strength Anisotropy

## 6.1 剪切模式与强度各向异性

The undrained shear strength is not a unique value for a given soil deposit, but depends upon the type of test mode, direction of loading, strain rate, initial stress state, and other factors (Kulhawy and Mayne 1990; Karlsrud et al. 2005). This creates difficulties when trying to assess a representative $s_u$ in clays, as the user will already have selected a bias towards his or her choice of reference test for $s_u$, such as triaxial or vane shear or unconfined compression.

For any clay, there will actually be a family of $s_u$ profiles. For example, various sets of undrained shear strengths measurements for the well-documented Bothkennar national test site in the UK will be used to illustrate the dilemma. Bothkennar is underlain by up to 30 m of soft clay and has been subjected to all types of in-situ and laboratory tests by a large number of universities, testing firms, and research organizations (Hight et al. 2003). Figure 6.2 shows results from laboratory series of test, including: triaxial compression ($CK_0$UC), direct simple shear (DSS), and triaxial extension tests ($CK_0$UE) on undisturbed samples (Hight et al. 2003), as well as in-situ pressuremeter tests (SBPMT) and field vane shear tests (FV). Also shown is the backcalculated $s_u$ value from a 2.2-m square footing that was load tested to failure at the site and analysed via limit plasticity solutions for bearing capacity. At any given depth, these $s_u$ values range up to a factor of six, depending upon the particular test mode under consideration. The differences are not due to site variability but occur because of the soil behavioral aspects of $s_u$ and its nuances.

对于给定的土层，不排水剪切强度并不是一个唯一的值，而是取决于测试模式、加载方向、应变速率、初始应力状态及其他因素（Kulhawy 和 Mayne 1990；Karlsrud 等 2005）。这给评估粘土中的代表性不排水剪切强度带来了困难，因为用户在选择不排水剪切强度参考测试时，例如三轴剪切、扭剪或无侧限压缩试验，已经存在某种偏见。实际上，对于任何粘土，都存在一系列的su剖面。举例来说，英国著名的Bothkennar国家测试场的各种不排水剪切强度测量数据被用来说明这一难题。Bothkennar底下有30米厚的软粘土层，已经被许多大学、测试公司和研究机构进行了各种原位和实验室测试（Hight 等 2003）。图6.2展示了实验室系列测试的结果，包括对原状样品的三轴压缩（CKoUC）、直接简单剪切（DSS）和三轴延伸试验（CKoUE）（Hight 等 2003），以及原位压入试验（SBPMT）和现场扭剪试验（FV）。图中还展示了通过对在现场进行破坏的2.2米方形基础进行荷载试验后，使用极限塑性理论对承载力分析反演得到的su值。在任一深度，这些su值因所考虑的特定测试模式的不同可相差多达六倍。这些差异并非由于场地的可变性，而是由于su的土体行为特性及其细微差别所致。

![image](images/rId203.png)

Figure 6.2: Various profiles of undrained shear strengths from different laboratory and field methods in Bothkennar soft clay, UK

图6.2：英国Bothkennar软粘土中不同实验室和现场测试方法得到的各种不排水剪切强度剖面

Similar wide ranges and variances in $s_u$ profiles can be observed for other clays, such as the well-known Boston Blue Clay (see Table 6.1). Here, the listed values of undrained shear strengths are reported in terms of the normalized strength ratio: $S = (s_u/\sigma'_{v0})_{NC}$ which correspond to normally-consolidated conditions (OCR = 1). Table 6.1 shows results varying from a low value of 0.14 for unconfined compression (UC) testing to a high value of $S$ = 0.42 for pressuremeter tests (PMT) on the same clay. This non-uniqueness of $s_u$ must be addressed if a clear and consistent framework is to be adopted by the geotechnical community in practice.

类似地，在其他粘土中也可以观察到$s_u$剖面的宽范围和变异性，例如著名的波士顿蓝粘土（见表6.1）。这里列出的不排水剪切强度值以归一化强度比率表示：$S = (s_u/\sigma'_{v0})_{NC}$，适用于正常固结条件（OCR = 1）。表6.1显示，在同一粘土上，未固结无侧限压缩（UC）测试的最低值为0.14，而压入试验（PMT）的最高值为0.42。如果岩土工程界在实际操作中要采用清晰且一致的框架，那么就必须解决这种不唯一性问题。

Table 6.1: Undrained strength ratios (S) for normally-consolidated Boston Blue Clay (data from Ladd et al. 1980; Ladd 1991; Whittle et al. 1994)

表6.1：正常固结波士顿蓝粘土的不排水强度比率（S）（数据来源：Ladd 等 1980；Ladd 1991；Whittle 等 1994）

![image](images/rId204.png)

**Table 6.1:** *Undrained strength ratios ($S$) for normally-consolidated Boston Blue Clay (data from Ladd et al. 1980; Ladd 1991; Whittle et al. 1994)*

| Shearing Mode | $S$ = ($s_u$ / $\sigma'_{v0}$)$_{NC}$ |
|---|---|
| Self-boring pressuremeter test (SBPMT) | 0.42 |
| Plane strain compression (PSC) | 0.34 |
| Triaxial compression ($CK_0$UC) | 0.33 |
| Unconsolidated Undrained (UU) | 0.275 |
| Field vane shear test (VST) | 0.21 |
| Direct simple shear (DSS) | 0.20 |
| Plane strain extension (PSE) | 0.19 |
| Triaxial extension ($CK_0$UE) | 0.16 |
| Unconfined compression (UC) | 0.14 |

## 6.2 Undrained Strength from Stress History

## 6.2 应力历史中的不排水强度

One means to evaluating $s_u$ in clays by piezocone involves the utilization of stress history (i.e., OCR or YSR) of the clay deposit to profile a family of undrained strength ratios:

通过孔压静力触探（piezocone）评估粘土中的不排水剪切强度的一种方法是利用粘土沉积物的应力历史（即OCR或YSR）来描绘不排水强度比率家族

$$\left(\frac{s_u}{\sigma'_{v0}}\right) = S \cdot OCR^m \tag{6.1}$$

where the coefficient $S$ and exponent $m$ can be found using either the MIT SHANSEP (stress history and normalized soil engineering parameters) approach, or CSSM (critical state soil mechanics).

其中，系数$S$和指数$m$可以通过MIT的SHANSEP（应力历史和归一化土工参数）方法或临界状态土力学（CSSM）方法得到。

In the SHANSEP approach (Ladd 1991; Ladd and DeGroot 2003), the value of $S = (s_u/\sigma'_{v0})_{NC}$ is experimentally found in the laboratory by extensive testing for large projects, with companion series of plane strain compression (PSC), simple shear (SS), and plane strain extension (PSE) tests on the soils at varied OCRs for embankments and long aligned structures, else series of triaxial compression (TC), simple shear (SS), and triaxial extension (TE) tests for axi-symmetric type loadings (e.g., footings, mats). Otherwise, on small projects, the values of $S$ are estimated from empirical relationships. The exponent $m$ can be determined experimentally and has been generally found to be on the order of 0.8 ± 0.1. The value of $S$ has been slightly related to plasticity index of the clay and Figure 6.3 shows three modes: TC, DSS, and TE.

在SHANSEP方法中（Ladd 1991；Ladd 和 DeGroot 2003），$S = (s_u/\sigma'_{v0})_{NC}$值通过对大项目进行大量的实验室测试获得，在这些测试中，对不同OCR条件下的土体进行平面应变压缩（PSC）、简单剪切（SS）和平面应变延伸（PSE）试验，以适应堤坝和长线性结构的需求，或者进行三轴压缩（TC）、简单剪切（SS）和三轴延伸（TE）试验，以适应轴对称加载类型（如基础、垫层）的需求。对于小型项目，$S$值则通过经验关系估算。指数$m$可以通过实验确定，通常发现其值约为0.8 ± 0.1。$S$值与粘土的塑性指数稍有相关性，图6.3显示了三种模式：TC、DSS和TE的经验$S$值。

![image](images/rId206.png)

Figure 6.3: Empirical values of $S$ for triaxial compression, simple shear, and triaxial extension (after Jamiolkowski et al. 1985; Ladd 1991)

图6.3：三轴压缩、简单剪切和三轴延伸的$S$值经验数据（来源：Jamiolkowski 等 1985；Ladd 1991）

A more fundamental avenue is afforded through critical-state soil mechanics (CSSM), however, this considers only stress-induced anisotropy effects and does not include the influences of inherent or fabric type anisotropy. In this case, a constitutive soil model provides the hierarchy of the strength modes (i.e., $S$ values), such as presented for the Wroth-Prevost formulation given in Figure 6.4. Note that the equations for "$S$" modes are given in Kulhawy & Mayne (1990). Thus, a rational evaluation of $s_u$ by piezocone tests in clay is accomplished in a two-step procedure: (a) evaluation of OCR profile using the aforementioned relationships between yield stress $\sigma'_p$ and ($q_t - \sigma_{v0}$), $\Delta u_2$, and/or ($q_t - u_2$); (b) use of Equation 6.1 and selected $S$ values to obtain a complementary suite of $s_u$ profiles.

通过临界状态土力学（CSSM）提供了更为基础的方法，然而，该方法仅考虑应力引起的各向异性效应，并未包含固有或结构类型的各向异性影响。在这种情况下，土体本构模型提供了强度模式的层次结构（即S值），如图6.4所示的Wroth-Prevost公式。需要注意的是，“S”模式的方程在Kulhawy和Mayne（1990）中有所给出。因此，通过孔压静力触探评估粘土中的不排水剪切强度可以通过以下两个步骤完成：（a）利用前述的屈服应力σ'p与(ge-ovo)、Δ$u_2$和/或(qe-$u_2$)之间的关系评估OCR剖面；（b）使用公式6.1和选定的S值来获得一套su剖面。

![image](images/rId207.png)

Figure 6.4: Evaluation of normally-consolidated strength ratios ($S$) of clays for different modes using Wroth-Prevost constitutive soil model (after Kulhawy and Mayne 1990)

图6.4：使用Wroth-Prevost本构模型对不同模式下正常固结粘土的强度比S的评估（来源：Kulhawy 和 Mayne 1990）

In CSSM, the exponent $m$ is derived from theoretical considerations and thus, $m = \Lambda \approx 1 - C_S/C_C$ where $C_S$ = swelling index and $C_C$ = compression index, however, the operational value is assigned more so on the basis of strength data (Mayne 1980):

在CSSM中，指数 m通过理论推导得到，因此，m = \Lambda ≈ 1 - CS/CC，其中CS为膨胀指数，CC为压缩指数，然而，操作值更多地基于强度数据来确定（Mayne 1980）：

$$\Lambda = \frac{\Delta \log(s_u/\sigma'_{v0})}{\Delta \log(OCR)} \tag{6.2}$$

Often, $\Lambda$ is taken to be 0.8 for many inorganic and insensitive “regular” clays (e.g., Kulhawy and Mayne 1990; Karlsrud and Hernandez-Martinez 2013) while $\Lambda \approx 1$ will be appropriate for structured and highly sensitive to quick clays.

通常，$\Lambda$值为0.8适用于许多无机且不敏感的”常规”粘土（例如，Kulhawy和Mayne 1990；Karlsrud和Hernandez-Martinez 2013），而$\Lambda \approx 1$适用于结构性强且高度敏感的软粘土。

The effect of OCR on the normalized undrained strength is essentially similar for all modes, as illustrated by Figure 6.5. Here data from a plastic clay from offshore New Jersey (named AGS for "Atlantic Generating Station") were obtained from laboratory tests using 5 different modes: PSC, TC, DSS, PSE, and TE, with very similar m (i.e., \Lambda) values, ranging from 0.72 to 0.86, and averaging 0.80 ± 0.06 (Koutsoftas & Ladd 1985).

正如图6.5所示，对于所有模式，OCR对归一化不排水强度的影响基本相似。这里展示了来自新泽西州海岸外一塑性粘土（称为AGS，即”大西洋发电站”）的数据，这些数据来自使用五种不同模式进行的实验室测试：PSC、TC、DSS、PSE和TE，其$m$值非常接近，即0.72到0.86，平均为0.80 ± 0.06（Koutsoftas 和 Ladd 1985）。

![image](images/rId209.png)

Figure 6.5: Effect of OCR on normalized strength of offshore AGS clay tested under five modes (data from Koutsoftas and Ladd 1985)

图6.5：五种模式下的OCR对AGS海上粘土归一化强度的影响（数据来源：Koutsoftas 和 Ladd 1985）

In the event that consolidation data or triaxial results are unavailable for a particular clay deposit, Table 6.2 provides a recommended range of exponent values for $m = \Lambda$ to use for several situations and clay types (Ladd & DeGroot 2003; Ouyang & Mayne 2019).

如果对于某一特定的粘土沉积物缺乏固结数据或三轴试验结果，表6.2提供了若干情况和粘土类型的推荐指数$m = \Lambda$值范围（Ladd & DeGroot 2003；Ouyang & Mayne 2019）。

Table 6.2. Recommended values of exponent term m = \Lambda for selected clay soils

表6.2：选定粘土土壤的指数项m = \Lambda的推荐值

![image](images/rId210.png)

The CSSM and SHANSEP procedures allow for a distinct and separate evaluation of each of the various strength modes, therefore helping to sort out the confusion and scatter normally found in $s_u$ plots. The role of the in-situ testing therefore becomes focused on the assessment of the stress history profile, uniquely defined in terms of the effective preconsolidation stress ($\sigma'_p$).

CSSM和SHANSEP程序允许对各类强度模式进行明确且独立的评估，因此有助于解决在$s_u$图中常见的混乱和分散情况。因此，原位测试的作用重点在于评估应力历史剖面，这通常通过有效的前期固结应力（$\sigma'_p$）来定义。

If the geotechnical problem has not yet been fully established, then the simple shear (SS) mode is probably a wise value to choose. The SS represents a middle representative value amongst the compression, shear, and extension modes. In the SS, pure shear is applied to the specimens, whereas in several available commercial systems, a direct simple shear (DSS) mode is a close approximation but does not provide the complementary shear stresses on top, bottom, and sides. For OCR=1, the corresponding $S_{DSS} = (s_u/\sigma'_{v0})_{NC[SS]} = \frac{1}{2}\sin\phi$ (Wroth 1984). Combining terms, the general expression for the undrained shear strength corresponding to the DSS and SS modes for intact clays over a range of overconsolidation ratios is given by (Wroth and Houlsby 1985):

如果岩土问题尚未完全确定，那么选择简单剪切（SS）模式可能是一个明智的选择。SS代表了压缩、剪切和延伸模式中的一个中间代表值。在SS中，纯剪切施加于试样，而在一些现有的商业系统中，直接简单剪切（DSS）模式是一个接近的近似，但未提供顶部、底部和侧面的补充剪应力。对于OCR=1，对应的SDSS = (su/\sigma′v0)NC[SS] = ½sin𝜙 (Wroth 1984)。结合各项参数，针对一系列超固结比范围内的原状粘土，DSS和SS模式下的不排水剪切强度的一般表达式由Wroth 和 Houlsby（1985）给出：

$$\left(\frac{s_u}{\sigma'_{v0}}\right)_{OC[SS]} = \frac{1}{2}(\sin\phi) \cdot OCR^\Lambda \tag{6.3}$$

as presented in Figure 6.6. If uncertain about the input parameters, characteristic values may be adopted: $\phi$ = 28° and $\Lambda$ = 0.80. This would give (Ladd and DeGroot 2003):

如图6.6所示。如果对输入参数不确定，可以采用特征值：𝜙 = 28° 和 \Lambda = 0.80。这将得出（Ladd 和 DeGroot 2003）：

$$\left(\frac{s_u}{\sigma'_{v0}}\right)_{OC[SS]} = 0.22 \cdot OCR^\Lambda \tag{6.4}$$

![image](images/rId213.png)

Figure 6.6: Measured and calculated undrained strength ratio with OCR for simple shear mode on normally-consolidated to overconsolidated clays (Mayne 2007a)

图6.6：正常固结至超固结粘土中，简单剪切模式下测量和计算的随OCR变化的不排水强度比率（Mayne 2007a）

In the case of soft lightly-overconsolidated (LOC) to normally-consolidated (NC) clays and clayey silts where OCRs < 2, the expression can deconvolute to the following simple form:

对于软的轻微超固结（LOC）至正常固结（NC）粘土和含粘土质粉土，OCR < 2时，该表达式可以简化为以下简单形式：

$$s_u \approx 0.22 \cdot \sigma'_p \tag{6.5}$$

which was surprisingly developed independently by Mesri (1989) by re-analysis of the backcalculated database of case history failures of embankments, footings, and excavations on soft clays that were tested using field vanes.

这一简化形式由Mesri（1989）独立发展，基于对使用现场扭剪试验测试软粘土的堤坝、基础和开挖失败的案例历史数据库的重新分析。

![image](images/rId215.png)

Figure 6.7: Recommended hierarchy of methods using stress history to evaluate $s_{uDSS}$ in clays

图6.7：利用应力历史评估粘土中$s_{uDSS}$的推荐层次方法

In summary, Figure 6.7 shows the hierarchy approach to a consistent interpretation of a representative "average" undrained shear strength for clays and clayey silts, more or less consistent with the simple shear mode (Mayne 2008).

总之，图6.7显示了在与简单剪切模式大致一致的情况下，对粘土和含粘土质粉土的代表性“平均”不排水剪切强度进行一致性解释的层次方法（Mayne 2008）。

For fissured clays, the calculated undrained strengths should be reduced to one-half of those for intact geomaterials because of the additional planes of weakness offered by the presence of cracks, joints, and discontinuities in the clay matrix. For the triaxial compression mode, CSSM provides the following evaluation of the undrained shear strength ratio:

对于裂隙粘土，由于裂缝、接缝和不连续面在粘土基质中提供了额外的薄弱面，计算的不排水强度应减少至完整地质材料的一半。在三轴压缩模式下，CSSM提供了以下不排水剪切强度比率的评估：

$$\frac{s_{uc}}{\sigma'_{v0}} = \frac{M_c}{2} \cdot \left(\frac{OCR}{2}\right)^{\Lambda} \tag{6.6}$$

Figure 6.8 shows a summary of CIUC data on 58 different clays tested over a wide range of OCRs, more or less confirming the validity of CSSM in a general assessment of undrained shear strength. Note that organic soils generally show higher values of $\sigma'$ and $s_u/\sigma'_{v0}$ than inorganic clays (Edil & Wang 2000). Furthermore, sensitive and structured clays will often show brittle behavior and significant strain softening, if properly sampled, and thus strong consideration and prudent judgment should be utilized when analyzing and designing in these types of geomaterials.

图6.8总结了对58种不同粘土在宽范围OCR下进行的CIUC数据，更多地确认了CSSM在不排水剪切强度评估中的普遍有效性。需要注意的是，有机土壤通常表现出比无机粘土更高的$\sigma'$和$s_u/\sigma'_{v0}$值（Edil & Wang 2000）。此外，敏感的和结构性粘土在适当取样时通常表现出脆性行为和显著的应变软化，因此在分析和设计这类地质材料时应慎重考虑和明智判断。

![image](images/rId217.png)

Figure 6.8: Undrained shear strength summary of triaxial compression results on 58 clays

图6.8：58种粘土三轴压缩试验的不排水剪切强度汇总

## 6.3 Undrained CPT Penetration

## 6.3 不排水CPT贯入

As the piezocone penetrometer collects at least three readings with depth, the potential exists to utilize three parameters: (a) net cone resistance $q_{net}$;  (b) excess porewater pressure, $\Delta u$;  and effective cone resistance, $q_E$;  towards the assessment of peak $s_u$ in clays, while $f_s$ provides an estimate of the remoulded strength.  Figure 6.9 depicts the approach for this purpose.

由于孔压静力触探仪（piezocone）在深度上至少采集三个读数，因此可以利用三个参数：(a) 净圆锥阻力 $q_{net}$；(b) 超孔隙水压力 $\Delta u$；以及有效圆锥阻力 $q_E$；来评估粘土中的峰值不排水剪切强度 $s_u$，同时，摩阻力 $f_s$ 可以估算重塑强度。图6.9展示了为此目的而采用的方法。

![image](images/rId218.png)

Figure 6.9: Direct assessment of $s_u$ in clays from piezocone readings

图6.9：通过孔压静力触探读数直接评估粘土中的 $s_u$

Common practice is to directly evaluate the in-situ undrained shear strength ($s_u = c_u$) from the net cone resistance ($q_{net}$) which comes from an inverted bearing capacity form:

通常的做法是直接从净圆锥阻力（$q_{net}$）评估原位不排水剪切强度（$s_u = c_u$），其形式源于倒置的承载力公式：

$$s_u = \frac{q_t - \sigma_{v0}}{N_{kt}} \tag{6.7}$$

where $q_t$ = total (corrected) cone tip resistance (Lunne et al. 1997), $\sigma_{v0}$ = total vertical overburden stress, and $N_{kt}$ is a bearing factor that depends upon the theory (e.g., limit plasticity, cavity expansion) or numerical simulation method (e.g., strain path method, finite elements), as discussed by Konrad and Law (1987), Yu and Mitchell (1998), Lu et al. (2004), and others. In practice, either an assumed value for $N_{kt}$ is adopted for evaluation of $s_u$, or else a fitted value is obtained by referencing the results to a benchmark tests, such as field vane ($s_{uv}$) or laboratory shear test, such as triaxial compression ($s_{uc}$), simple shear ($s_{ud}$), or extension ($s_{ue}$). Ranges of values for $N_{kt}$ in soft intact clays are generally taken to be between 10 and 20, yet are certainly mode-dependent (Lunne et al. 1997). For CPTU soundings in soft to firm offshore clays, Lunne et al. (2005) recommend a value $N_{kt} \approx 12$ for $s_{uc}$. A study of piezocone data on 3 onshore and 11 offshore clays by Low et al. (2010) found the range: $8.6 \leq N_{kt} \leq 15.3$, with a mean value of $N_{kt} = 11.9$ for triaxial compression mode. Similarly, a larger study of 51 soft to firm intact clays found a mean value of $N_{kt} \approx 11.8$ for $s_{uc}$ corresponding to the CAUC triaxial mode (Mayne et al. 2015). For 17 Norwegian clays, Karlsrud et al. (2005) found that $N_{kt}$ increases with OCR and PI of the clay and is also influenced by sensitivity.

其中 $q_t$ = 总（修正后）圆锥尖端阻力（Lunne 等 1997），$\sigma_{v0}$ = 总竖向上覆应力，$N_{kt}$ 是一个承载系数，取决于理论（如极限塑性、空腔膨胀）或数值模拟方法（如应变路径法、有限元法），如Konrad和Law（1987）、Yu和Mitchell（1998）、Lu等（2004）及其他学者所讨论的那样。在实际操作中，$N_{kt}$ 的取值要么是假设的，要么通过参考基准测试的结果（如现场扭剪试验 $s_{uv}$ 或实验室剪切试验，如三轴压缩 $s_{uc}$、简单剪切 $s_{ud}$ 或延伸剪切 $s_{ue}$）来得到拟合值。对于软粘土，$N_{kt}$ 的取值范围通常在10到20之间，但这些值显然依赖于模式（Lunne等 1997）。对于软至中密海上粘土的CPTU探测，Lunne等（2005）推荐 $s_{uc}$ 的取值为 $N_{kt} \approx 12$。Low等（2010）对3种陆上和11种海上粘土的孔压静力触探数据研究发现，三轴压缩模式下 $N_{kt}$ 的范围为8.6到15.3，平均值为 $N_{kt} = 11.9$。类似地，Mayne等（2015）对51种软至中密原状粘土的更大研究发现，CAUC三轴模式对应的 $s_{uc}$ 平均值为 $N_{kt} \approx 11.8$。对于17种挪威粘土，Karlsrud等（2005）发现 $N_{kt}$ 随着OCR和粘土的塑性指数（PI）增加，并且还受灵敏度的影响。

For other shearing modes, other operational values of $N_k$ must be used. For instance, Low et al. (2010) found a mean $N_{kt} = 13.6$ for the lab average strength ($s_{uAve}$) from triaxial compression, simple shear, and triaxial extension (range: $10.6 < N_{kt} \leq 17.4$), which is close to a simple shear mode ($s_{uSS}$). For calibration with the field vane ($s_{uv}$), they determined $N_{kt}$ averages 13.3 with a range: $10.8 \leq N_{kt} \leq 19.9$. Several research programs concluded that $N_{kt}$ varies inversely with porewater parameter $B_q$. The reported trends are summarized in Figure 6.10 and correspond to intact clays. In fissured overconsolidated clays, the characteristic porewater pressure response gives $B_q$ that are zero or negative values. For these geomaterials, N factors have been reported in the higher range of 20 to 30, and somewhat dependent upon the degree of fissuring (Powell and Quarterman 1988; Powell and Lunne 2005a).

对于其他剪切模式，需要使用其他的操作性 $N_k$ 值。例如，Low等（2010）发现三轴压缩、简单剪切和三轴延伸的实验室平均强度（$s_{uAve}$）的平均值 $N_{kt} \approx 13.6$（范围：$10.6 < N_{kt} \leq 17.4$），这接近于简单剪切模式（$s_{uSS}$）。对于校准现场扭剪（$s_{uv}$），他们确定了 $N_{kt}$ 的平均值为13.3，范围为：$10.8 \leq N_{kt} \leq 19.9$。若干研究计划得出的结论是，$N_{kt}$ 与孔隙水压力参数 $B_q$ 呈反比。报告的趋势总结在图6.10中，适用于原状粘土。对于裂隙超固结粘土，特征孔隙水压力响应给出了 $B_q$ 为零或负值。对于这些地质材料，N系数的高范围为20到30，并且在一定程度上取决于裂隙程度（Powell和Quarterman 1988；Powell和Lunne 2005a）。

![image](images/rId220.png)

Figure 6.10: Calibrated trends for cone factor $N_{kt}$ with porewater parameter $B_q$

图6.10：圆锥系数 $N_{kt}$ 与孔隙水压力参数 $B_q$ 的校准趋势

Using a consistent $s_{ue}$ reference strength from CAUC tests, a summary of $q_{net}$ data from CPTU tests on clays is presented in Figure 6.11, confirming the decrease of $N_{kt}$ with increase in $B_q$.

利用来自CAUC试验的 $s_{ue}$ 参考强度，图6.11总结了CPTU测试中 $q_{net}$ 数据，证实了随着 $B_q$ 增加，$N_{kt}$ 减少的趋势。

![image](images/rId221.png)

Figure 6.11: Data sets for establishing $N_{kt}$ relationship with porewater parameter $B_q$

图6.11：用于建立 $N_{kt}$ 与孔隙水压力参数 $B_q$ 关系的数据集

With piezocone data, it is also feasible to independently evaluate a profile of $s_u$ entirely from the excess porewater pressure measurements ($\Delta u$). While this may be best handled by a mid-face element (designated $u_1$), the shoulder filter element ($u_2$) reading is usually more common, because $u_2$ is required in the correction of raw $q_c$ to total resistance $q_t$. The expression for undrained strength here is given by:

利用孔压静力触探数据，也可以完全根据超孔隙水压力测量值（$\Delta u$）独立评估 $s_u$ 剖面。虽然这可能最好由中面滤元件（称为 $u_1$）处理，但通常使用肩部滤元件（$u_2$）的读数，因为 $u_2$ 在将原始 $q_c$ 修正为总阻力 $q_t$ 时是必需的。这里不排水强度的表达式为：

$$s_u = \frac{u_2 - u_0}{N_{\Delta u}} \tag{6.8}$$

where $N_{\Delta u}$ porewater bearing factor (Tavenas and Leroueil 1987; Lunne et al. 1997). For the triaxial compression mode, Lunne (2012) recommends a value of $N_{\Delta u} \approx 6$ for preliminary work or initial estimates until calibrated with laboratory tests on undisturbed samples. From their study on soft offshore deposits, Low et al. (2010) indicated a mean value of $N_{\Delta u} \approx 5.9$, while the larger study by Mayne et al. (2015) found a representative $N_{\Delta u} \approx 6.5$ for soft to firm clays. Karlsrud et al. (2005) found that $N_{\Delta u}$ decreases with OCR and increases with PI. Hong et al. (2009) also found an increase of $N_{\Delta u}$ with $B_q$.

其中 $N_{\Delta u}$ 是孔隙水承载系数（Tavenas和Leroueil 1987；Lunne等 1997）。对于三轴压缩模式，Lunne（2012）推荐初步工作或初始估算中使用 $N_{\Delta u} \approx 6$，直到通过对原状样品的实验室测试校准为止。在他们对软海上沉积物的研究中，Low等（2010）指出 $N_{\Delta u}$ 的平均值为5.9，而Mayne等（2015）的更大研究发现，软至中密粘土的代表性 $N_{\Delta u}$ 值为6.5。Karlsrud等（2005）发现 $N_{\Delta u}$ 随着OCR的增加而减少，并且随着PI的增加而增加。Hong等（2009）也发现 $N_{\Delta u}$ 随着 $B_q$ 增加而增加。

Using the CAUC $s_{uc}$ reference strengths, a summary of $\Delta u_2$ data from CPTU tests on clays is presented in Figure 6.12, confirming the increase of $N_{\Delta u}$ with increasing value of $B_q$.

利用CAUC $s_{uc}$ 参考强度，图6.12总结了CPTU测试中 $\Delta u_2$ 数据，证实了 $N_{\Delta u}$ 随着 $B_q$ 值增加而增加的趋势。

![image](images/rId223.png)

Figure 6.12: Data sets for establishing $N_{\Delta u}$ relationship with porewater parameter $B_q$. Note: not applicable to CPTU results where $B_q < 0.2$

图6.12：用于建立 $N_{\Delta u}$ 与孔隙水压力参数 $B_q$ 关系的数据集。注：不适用于 $B_q < 0.2$ 的CPTU结果

Furthermore, a third evaluation is possible using the effective cone resistance ($q_t - u_2$), given by:

此外，还可以使用有效圆锥阻力（$q_t - u_2$）进行第三种评估，其表达式为：

$$s_u = \frac{q_t - u_2}{N_{kE}} \tag{6.9}$$

where $N_{kE}$ = bearing factor averaging about 8.0 in soft-firm NC-LOC clays (Mayne et al. 2015). In the study by Lunne et al. (1985), it was found that $N_{kE}$ decreases with porewater parameter which was also noted by Karlsrud et al. (1996) and Hong et al. (2009). Using the noted four data sets of CAUC $s_{uc}$ reference strengths, a summary of $q_E$ data from CPTU tests on clays is presented in Figure 6.13, confirming the trend of decreasing $N_{kE}$ with increasing porewater pressure parameter $B_q$.

其中 $N_{kE} \approx 8.0$，为软至中密正常固结至轻微超固结粘土中的承载系数（Mayne等 2015）。在Lunne等（1985）的研究中发现，$N_{kE}$ 随着孔隙水参数的增加而减少，Karlsrud等（1996）和Hong等（2009）也注意到了这一点。利用四个CAUC $s_{uc}$ 参考强度数据集，图6.13总结了CPTU测试中的 $q_E$ 数据，证实了随着孔隙水压力参数 $B_q$ 增加，$N_{kE}$ 减少的趋势。

![image](images/rId225.png)

Figure 6.13: Data sets for establishing $N_{kE}$ relationship with porewater parameter $B_q$

图6.13：用于建立 $N_{kE}$ 与孔隙水压力参数 $B_q$ 关系的数据集

The ability to produce three separate profiles of peak undrained shear strength in the clay formation is actually good, since hopefully the three will support each other.  If the individual profiles show consensus, then a higher degree of reliance can be afforded in the design.  If the profiles do not agree, then the results may serve as a warning that a higher level of scrutiny needs to be undertaken by the engineer.  For instance, perhaps issues with the porous filter becoming partially desaturated, or other possibilities, such as the soil formation itself having unusual aspects and thus falling within the domain of "nontextbook geomaterials" (Schnaid 2005).

在粘土层中生成三条不同的不排水剪切强度剖面实际上是有益的，因为这些剖面有望相互支持。如果各个剖面显示出一致性，那么在设计中可以给予更高的信任度。如果这些剖面不一致，则可能预示着需要工程师进行更深入的审查。例如，可能存在多孔滤器部分脱气的问题，或者其他可能性，例如土层本身具有非典型的特性，因此属于“非教材地质材料”的范畴（Schnaid 2005）。

### 6.3.1 Case Study: Sandpoint Idaho

### 6.3.1 案例研究：爱达荷州桑德波因特

Returning to the CPTU sounding in soft silty clay from Sandpoint Idaho, Figure 6.14 shows the results of a series of triaxial compression tests (CIUC type) that were peformed on undisturbed tube samples. Using the aforementioned characteristic value of $B_q \approx 0.75$, estimated values of $N_{kt} = 11$, $N_{\Delta u} = 8$, and $N_{kE} = 5$ were chosen and applied to the $q_{net}$, $\Delta u$, and $q_E$ readings, respectively, with the resulting profiles shown in the figure.

回到爱达荷州桑德波因特的软粉质粘土中的CPTU探测，图6.14显示了一系列在未扰动管状样品上进行的三轴压缩试验（CIUC型）的结果。利用前面提到的 $B_q$ 特征值约为0.75，分别选择了 $N_{kt} = 11$、$N_{\Delta u} = 8$ 和 $N_{kE} = 5$ 的估计值，并将其应用于 $q_{net}$、$\Delta u$ 和 $q_E$ 读数中，所得剖面如图所示。

![image](images/rId226.png)

Figure 6.14: Profiles of undrained shear strength from CPTU in soft silty clay using cone bearing factors estimated from $B_q$ at Sandpoint, Idaho

图6.14：使用从 $B_q$ 估算的圆锥承载系数在爱达荷州桑德波因特的软粉质粘土中的CPTU不排水剪切强度剖面

### 6.3.2 Case Study: Tiller-Flotten Quick Clay, Norway

### 6.3.2 案例研究：挪威Tiller-Flotten流沙

A second example involves highly sensitive clay at the national test site established in Norway at Tiller-Flotten, near Trondheim (L'Heureux et al. 2019). A representative sounding (TIL18) is shown in Figure 6.15 and the post-processing of the CPTU data with eqns (6.7), (6.8), and (6.9) provide three profiles of $s_{uc}$ in the right-hand side. The results are comparable to the CAUC test results on undisturbed samples conducted at the NGI Laboratory.

第二个例子涉及挪威Trondheim附近的Tiller-Flotten国家测试场的高度敏感粘土（L'Heureux等 2019）。图6.15显示了一个代表性探测（TIL18），并通过对CPTU数据进行公式(6.7)、(6.8)和(6.9)的后处理，在右侧提供了三条 $s_{uc}$ 剖面。结果与在NGI实验室对未扰动样品进行的CAUC试验结果相当。

![image](images/rId227.png)

*Figure 6.15: Profiles in quick clay at Tiller-Flotten: (a) $q_t$ and $u_2$; (b) $f_s$; (c) CAUC $s_u$*

## 6.4 Evaluation of $s_u$ from $V_s$

## 6.4 从$V_s$评估$s_u$

If SCPTU results are available, an independent assessment on undrained shear strength can be afforded through empirical correlations between $s_u$ and shear wave velocity. L'Heureux & Long (2017) provide a review of some 15 relationships for $s_u$ and $V_s$. In addition, using CAUC triaxial data obtained from 14 Norwegian clays, they develop the following trendline:

如果有SCPTU结果，通过$s_u$与剪切波速度（$V_s$）之间的经验关系，可以进行独立的不排水剪切强度评估。L'Heureux & Long（2017）对$s_u$和$V_s$的约15种关系进行了综述。此外，使用从14种挪威粘土获得的CAUC三轴数据，他们开发了以下趋势线：

$$s_{uc} \text{ (kPa)} = 0.021 \cdot [V_s \text{ (m/s)}]^{1.52} \tag{6.10}$$

Similar expressions have been derived by Agaiby et al. (2016) and Agaiby & Mayne (2016).

Agaiby等（2016）和Agaiby & Mayne（2016）也得出了类似的表达式。

## 6.5 Relationship for Undrained Strength with Vane Shear Test

## 6.5 使用扭剪试验的关系来评估不排水强度

The vane shear test (VST), or field vane (FV), provides a direct in-situ assessment of undrained shear strength, albeit the calculation is based on limit equilibrium analysis. In some instances, it is desirable to compare the results of VST with CPTU. The aforementioned relationships for evaluating undrained strength from the CPTU are specifically for the triaxial compression mode, $s_{uc}$. Therefore, it is of interest to provide an interrelationship between the vane undrained strength, $s_{uv}$, with that from triaxials tests. A review by Chandler (1988) found that the strength ratio tracked with clay plasticity index (PI in %):

扭剪试验（VST），或现场扭剪（FV），提供了不排水剪切强度的直接原位评估，尽管其计算基于极限平衡分析。在某些情况下，将VST结果与CPTU进行比较是有意义的。前述通过CPTU评估不排水强度的关系特定于三轴压缩模式$s_{uc}$。因此，提供扭剪不排水强度$s_{uv}$与三轴试验结果之间的相互关系是值得关注的。Chandler（1988）的回顾发现，强度比率与粘土的塑性指数（PI，百分比）有关：

$$V_r = \frac{s_{uv}}{s_{uc}} \approx 0.55 + 0.008 \, PI \tag{6.11}$$

The data used to develop this trend is presented in Figure 6.16.

图6.16给出了该趋势

![image](images/rId230.png)

Figure 6.16. Interrelationship of vane and triaxial compression strengths with clay plasticity (modified after Chandler 1988)

图6.16：扭剪和三轴压缩强度与粘土塑性的相互关系（根据Chandler 1988修改）

## 6.6 Remolded Undrained Strength and Clay Sensitivity

## 6.6 重塑的不排水强度和粘土灵敏度

Studies have long suggested that the measured sleeve friction resistance ($f_s$) can be considered as a remolded shear strength ($s_{ur}$) in clays (Gorman, Drnevich, and Hopkins 1975; Lunne et al. 1997; Powell and Lunne 2005a; Robertson 2009b), expressed:

研究表明，测得的套管摩擦阻力（$f_s$）可以视为粘土中的重塑剪切强度（$s_{ur}$）（Gorman, Drnevich, and Hopkins 1975；Lunne等 1997；Powell和Lunne 2005a；Robertson 2009b），其表达式为：

$$s_{ur} \approx f_s \tag{6.12}$$

In Figure 6.17, data have been compiled from a number of sites (N = 15) where reference $s_{ur}$ profiles have been obtained from independent measurements, mostly from field vane tests (FV), however in some cases other reference results were available (e.g., lab fall cone, lab mini-vane, UU). The dataset contains 11 clay deposits of low-medium sensitivity ($S_t$<8) and 5 clays that have high sensitivity ($S_t$ > 8).

在图6.17中，汇编了从多个地点（N=15）获得的数据，其中的参考$s_{ur}$剖面来自独立测量，大多数来自现场扭剪试验（FV），但在某些情况下，其他参考结果也可用（如实验室跌落锥、实验室小型扭剪、UU试验）。数据集包含11个低至中等灵敏度（$S_t$ < 8）的粘土沉积物和5个高灵敏度（$S_t$ > 8）的粘土。

It is evident that eqn (6.11) works reasonably in clays that have low sensitivity, however, when needed the most, i.e. sensitive and quick clay deposits, the sleeve friction is not particularly very good at capturing the remolded undrained shear strength. This might be due to the low range of $f_s$ readings <10 kPa which is near the limit of its resolution in the load cell range, as well as due to porewater pressure effects that act on the sleeve.

显然，公式(6.11)在低灵敏度粘土中表现良好，但在最需要的时候，即在敏感和流沙粘土沉积物中，套管摩擦在捕捉重塑的不排水剪切强度方面表现不佳。这可能是由于$f_s$读数范围<10 kPa，这接近其在荷载传感器范围内的分辨率极限，也可能是由于作用在套管上的孔隙水压力效应所致。

![image](images/rId232.png)

Figure 6.17: Measured remolded shear strengths versus CPT sleeve friction for 16 clays

图6.17：测得的重塑剪切强度与CPT套管摩擦之间的关系（用于16种粘土）

For clays of low sensitivity, the CPTU can provide an approximate measure of clay sensitivity, $S_t$, defined as the ratio of peak to remolded shear shear strengths at the same water content:

对于低灵敏度粘土，CPTU可以提供粘土灵敏度$S_t$的近似值，定义为相同含水量下峰值与重塑剪切强度的比率：

$$S_t = \frac{s_{u,peak}}{s_{ur}} \tag{6.13}$$

where the peak $s_u$ is evaluated based on the aforementioned procedures, such as eqns (6.7),(6.8), and/or (6.9), and the remolded $s_{ur}$ = $f_s$.

峰值$s_u$根据上述程序评估，如公式(6.7)、(6.8)和(6.9)，而重塑的$s_{ur}$ = $f_s$。

For clays of high sensitivity, the above will not be adequate and will significantly underestimate the clay $S_t$ since the $f_s$ reading poorly captures the remolded undrained strength. Using the crude statistical analyses presented in Figure 6.18, on the average, the $f_s$ reading overestimates $s_{ur}$ by a factor of 5 in sensitive clays.

对于高灵敏度粘土，上述方法不足且会严重低估粘土$S_t$，因为$f_s$读数不能很好地捕捉重塑的不排水强度。使用图6.18中所示的粗略统计分析，平均而言，在敏感粘土中，$f_s$读数高估了$s_{ur}$约5倍。

![image](images/rId234.png)

Figure 6.18: Statistical analyses of remolded strength with sleeve friction for 11 insensitive clays and 5 sensitive clays

图6.18：11种低灵敏度粘土和5种敏感粘土的套管摩擦与重塑强度的统计分析

## 6.7 Sample Disturbance of Laboratory Reference Strengths

## 6.7 实验室参考强度样品扰动

One issue that frequently arises with CPT interpretation in clays involves a geoengineer who will try and use their lab test results from "undisturbed" samples to adjust the Nkt factor for the cone data interpretations. This is often problematic for two reasons: (a) lab-measured $s_u$ values can be significantly altered by sample disturbance effects; and (b) lack of a proper matching for the appropriate strength mode, as discussed already.

在粘土中进行CPT解释时，经常出现一个问题，即岩土工程师会尝试使用“未扰动”样品的实验室测试结果来调整圆锥数据解释中的Nkt系数。这通常会因两个原因而出现问题：(a) 实验室测得的$s_u$值可能会因样品扰动效应而显著改变；(b) 缺乏适当强度模式的匹配，如前面已经讨论的那样。

An example of the strong influence of sample disturbance on the measured strength and stiffness is given in Figure 6.19 for Ariake clay reported by Tanaka (2000). Here, six different types and qualities of samplers were used to procure undisturbed samples of the soft clay. Each sample was subsequently tested in the laboratory under the unconfined compression mode. It is easily seen that the Sherbrooke sampler provides the highest (and presumably, best representative) value of undrained shear strength ($s_u$ = 38 kPa) whereas the British ELE sampler has the lowest (and correspondingly, most disturbed) value of strength ($s_u$ = 15.5 kPa). The remainder of samplers provide intermediate values between these two extremes. Note also the severe effects on the flattening of the stress-strain curves, thus causing significant differences in derived Young’s modulus values. That is, higher quality samples generally provide a higher strength and higher modulus, whereas in contrast, poor quality samples have low strengths and low stiffnesses.

一个关于样品扰动对测量强度和刚度的强烈影响的例子，如图6.19所示，是Tanaka（2000）报告的Ariake粘土。在这里，使用了六种不同类型和质量的取样器来获取未扰动的软粘土样品。随后对每个样品在实验室中进行了无侧限压缩试验。可以清楚地看到，Sherbrooke取样器提供了最高（并且可能是最佳代表性）的不排水剪切强度值（$s_u$ = 38 kPa），而British ELE取样器提供了最低（相应地，扰动最严重）的强度值（$s_u$ = 15.5 kPa）。其余取样器提供的值介于这两个极端之间。还应注意，扰动对应力-应变曲线的严重影响，从而导致导出的杨氏模量值存在显著差异。即，质量较高的样品通常提供较高的强度和较高的模量，相比之下，质量较差的样品则具有较低的强度和较低的刚度。

![image](images/rId235.png)

Figure 6.19: Comparison of undrained stress-strain curves for Ariake clay from different quality samples (after Tanaka 2000)

图6.19：不同质量样品的Ariake粘土的不排水应力-应变曲线比较（根据Tanaka 2000）

The degree of sample disturbance for each lab specimen can be assessed using one of two methods: (a) specimen quality designation (SQD); or (b) NGI method. After trimming the specimen, both approaches rely on measuring the change in void ratio ($\Delta e$) required during reconsolidation to recover to the in-situ effective overburden stress $\sigma'_{v0}$. In the SQD method, the vertical strain ($\varepsilon_v$) is used to quantify the sample goodness, where: $\varepsilon_v = \Delta e/(1 + e_0)$. In the NGI method, the ratio of $\Delta e/e_0$ measured during consolidation is utilized. Both methods can be applied to individual specimens from triaxial, consolidation, and direct simple shear tests (Lunne et al. 2006). For example, the sample quality is high if $\Delta e/e_0$ < 0.02 and poor if $\Delta e/e_0$ > 0.14.

每个实验室样品的扰动程度可以通过两种方法之一进行评估：(a) 样品质量等级（SQD）；或(b) NGI方法。修整样品后，这两种方法都依赖于测量在重新固结至原位有效上覆应力$\sigma'_{v0}$期间所需的孔隙比变化（$\Delta e$）。在SQD方法中，使用垂直应变（$\varepsilon_v$）来量化样品质量，其中：$\varepsilon_v = \Delta e / (1 + e_0)$。在NGI方法中，使用在固结期间测量的$\Delta e / e_0$比值。这两种方法都可以应用于三轴、固结和直接简单剪切试验的单个样品（Lunne等 2006）。例如，如果$\Delta e / e_0$ < 0.02，样品质量较高；如果$\Delta e / e_0$ > 0.14，则样品质量较差。

Table 6.3 details the specific criteria for assessing a grade to each specimen.

表6.3详细说明了对每个样品评定等级的具体标准。

Krage et al. (2015) recognized that the recovery of void ratio during sample reconsolidation depends on recompression index of the soil, $C_r$. Consequently, silt soil samples with lower $C_r$ values could mistakenly be classified as high quality using Table 6.3. They recommended normalizing $\Delta e/e_0$ by $C_r$ in order to apply sample quality criteria to any soils.

Krage等（2015）认识到，样品重新固结期间的孔隙比恢复取决于土壤的再压缩指数$C_r$。因此，具有较低$C_r$值的粉土样品可能会错误地被归类为高质量样品。他们建议通过$C_r$对$\Delta e / e_0$进行归一化，以便将样品质量标准应用于任何土壤。

Table 6.3: Quantification of sample disturbance on mechanical soil properties based on specimen volume change to reach $\sigma'_{v0}$ (modified after DeGroot et al. 2005)

表6.3：基于样品体积变化达到$\sigma'_{v0}$的机械土壤性质的样品扰动量化（根据DeGroot等 2005修改）

![image](images/rId236.png)

Notes:
1. Vertical strain during recompression to reach $\sigma'_{vo}$ where $\varepsilon = \Delta e/(1+e_0)$, $\Delta e$ = change in void ratio, and $e_0$ = initial void ratio.
2. For soils with 1 < OCR < 2
3. For soils with 2 < OCR < 4

注释：

1. 垂直应变在重新压缩至$\sigma'_{v0}$期间，其中$\varepsilon = \Delta e / (1 + e_0)$，$\Delta e$ = 孔隙比变化，$e_0$ = 初始孔隙比。

适用于1 < OCR < 2的土壤。

适用于2 < OCR < 4的土壤。

With regards to the aforementioned issues, some good news here is that the measured $\phi'$ is apparently little affected by sample disturbance! An example is shown in Figure 6.20 using data from Lierstranda clay in Norway (Lunne et al. 2006). Three different sampler types were used to obtain undisturbed specimens including: (a) high quality block; (b) medium quality 75-mm tube, and (c) low quality 50-mm tube. Each give a different stress-strain curve, pore pressure response, effective stress path, and corresponding peak $s_{uc}$ depending upon the quality of the sample.

关于上述问题，有一个好消息是，测得的有效摩擦角$\phi’$显然很少受到样品扰动的影响！图6.20使用来自挪威Lierstranda粘土的数据展示了一个例子（Lunne等 2006）。使用了三种不同的取样器类型来获得未扰动的样品，包括：(a) 高质量块状取样；(b) 中等质量75毫米管状取样；以及(c) 低质量50毫米管状取样。每种样品质量不同，导致的应力-应变曲线、孔隙水压力响应、有效应力路径和相应的峰值$s_{uc}$也不同。

However, in the effective q-p’ stress space plots, it is evident that all specimens converge on the same effective stress friction angle ($\phi$ = 34.4°). Similar findings are presented by Karlsrud & Hernandez-Martinez (2013) and DeGroot et al. (2019) for other clays.

在有效q-p’应力空间图中，所有样品都趋向于相同的有效应力摩擦角（$\phi$ = 34.4°）。Karlsrud和Hernandez-Martinez（2013）以及DeGroot等（2019）在其他粘土上的研究也得出了类似的结论。

![image](images/rId237.png)

Figure 6.20: Stress-strain curves and effective stress plots for three different quality specimens tested in CAUC triaxial compression (data from Lunne et al. 2006)

图6.20：在CAUC三轴压缩试验中测试的三种不同质量样品的应力-应变曲线和有效应力图（数据来源：Lunne等 2006）

# 7 Ground Stiffness and Soil Moduli

# 7 地面刚度和土壤模量

A measure of the soil stiffness is needed for deformation analyses, including foundation settlements, pile displacements, and movement of walls and other civil engineering structures.

The stiffness of soils is represented by several types of geoparameters, depending upon the theoretical framework adopted including: (1) one-dimensional consolidation theory and associated compressibility parameters ($C_r$, $C_c$, and $C_s$); (2) elasticity theory in terms of moduli (i.e., E’, G’, B’, D’, $E_u$, $G_u$); (3) subgrade reaction models (e.g., mat foundations or pavement subgrades; (4) spring models; and (5) other empirical methods. The value of stiffness parameters will vary depending upon the level of applied loading, drainage conditions, loading history (monotonic static, dynamic, or cyclic), initial stress state, and induced strain levels.

对土壤刚度的测量对于变形分析是必要的，包括基础沉降、桩位移以及墙体和其他土木工程结构的移动。

土壤的刚度由几种地质参数表示，这些参数取决于采用的理论框架，包括：（1）一维固结理论及其相关的压缩性参数（Cr、Cc 和 Cs）；（2）弹性理论中的模量（即 E'、G'、B'、D'、Eu、Gu）；（3）基床反应模型（例如，垫层基础或路面基床）；（4）弹簧模型；以及（5）其他经验方法。刚度参数的值会根据施加的荷载水平、排水条件、荷载历史（单调静态、动态或循环）、初始应力状态和引起的应变水平有所不同。

## 7.1 Consolidation Theory

## 7.1 固结理论

Foundation settlement is a common situation for many geotechnical studies. Many office buildings are built towards tolerable settlements of less than 25 mm (1 inch), while open structures such as parking garages are able to withstand up to 50 mm (2 inches) of vertical movement. Large bridge structures can sustain up to 75 mm (3 inches) movements, yet earthen embankments may undergo displacements of 100 to 1000 mm during initial undrained loading ($s_{initial}$), drained primary consolidation ($s_{consolidation}$), and long-term secondary compression ($s_{creep}$).

基础沉降是许多岩土工程研究中的常见情况。许多办公楼建造时要求沉降量不超过 25 毫米（1 英寸），而开放结构如停车场可以承受最多 50 毫米（2 英寸）的垂直位移。大型桥梁结构可以承受最多 75 毫米（3 英寸）的位移，但土堤在初始无排水荷载（sinitial）、排水固结（sconsolidation）和长期次级压缩（screep）过程中，可能会经历 100 到 1000 毫米的位移。

In the traditional approach to calculating displacements of shallow foundations and embankment fills, the full consideration of deformations can be expressed (Holtz et al. 2011):

在传统的浅基础和填土物料位移计算方法中，对变形的全面考虑可以表达为（Holtz 等，2011）：

$$s_{total} = s_{initial} + s_{consolidation} + s_{creep} \tag{7.1}$$

For the calculation of drained primary consolidation and long-term creep components, conventional practice is to utilize the results from one-dimensional consolidation tests, specifically: void ratio versus logarithm of effective stress: e – log $\sigma'_v$ curves.
