The derived compressibility parameters include the recompression index ($C_r$), virgin compression index ($C_c$), and swelling or rebound index ($C_s$), as well as evaluation of the preconsolidation or yield stress ($\sigma'_p$). For the long-term creep phase, the coefficient of secondary compression ($C_\alpha$) is also a compressibility parameter.

对于排水初期固结和长期蠕变分量的计算，传统做法是利用一维固结试验的结果，具体来说：孔隙比与有效应力的对数关系：e – log \sigma′v 曲线。由此得到的压缩性参数包括重压缩指数（Cr）、原始压缩指数（Cc）和膨胀或回弹指数（Cs），以及先期固结或屈服应力（\sigma′p）的评估。在长期蠕变阶段，次级压缩系数（C\alpha）也是一个压缩性参数。

The initial or immediate displacements are more complex as the situation depends upon whether the ground is fully saturated or partially saturated. Usually, elastic solutions are employed for calculating initial undrained displacements, e.g., Foott & Ladd (1981). The elastic solutions may be required for flood control structures, filling water tanks, and from the rapid installation of prefabricated structures. In most cases, an undrained distortion (or initial displacement) is only of concern when constructing a facility rather quickly over very soft ground conditions, i.e., embankment on soft clay or offshore gravity platform on clay. In fact, most construction advances slowly and immediate settlements are not normally of concern on firm ground. Thus, only the two components of primary consolidation and creep will occur for most projects. Long term creep is problematic only in the case of soft organic clays, peats, muskeg, and sensitive clays. Thus, the main focus of displacements is usually towards the magnitude of drained settlements due to primary consolidation.

初始或瞬时位移较为复杂，因为情况取决于土壤是完全饱和还是部分饱和。通常，弹性解法用于计算初始无排水位移，例如，Foott 和 Ladd（1981）的研究。弹性解法可能适用于防洪结构、填充水箱以及快速安装预制结构。在大多数情况下，无排水变形（或初始位移）仅在非常软的地基条件下快速建造设施时才值得关注，例如在软粘土上建造堤坝或在粘土上安装海上重力平台。实际上，大多数施工进展缓慢，坚实地基上的瞬时沉降通常不值得担心。因此，大多数项目中主要发生的是初期固结和蠕变两个分量。长期蠕变仅在软有机粘土、泥炭、沼泽土和敏感粘土的情况下才会成为问题。因此，位移的主要关注点通常是由于初期固结引起的排水沉降的幅度。

In the calculation of settlements due to drained primary consolidation, the effective yield stress ($\sigma’_p$) plays an important role since applied loading that causes stresses less than $\sigma’_p$ will often result is small manageable displacements, while loads where stresses > $\sigma’_p$ can cause large movements that may exceed bearing capacity and stability limits. The evaluation of $\sigma’_p$ from CPTU was discussed in Section 4. For additional details, please see Holtz et al. (2011).

在计算由于排水初期固结引起的沉降时，有效屈服应力（p’）起着重要作用，因为施加的荷载如果导致的应力小于p’，通常会导致较小的可控位移，而应力大于p’的荷载则可能导致超过承载能力和稳定性极限的大位移。关于从CPTU评估p’的内容已在第4节讨论。有关更多详细信息，请参见Holtz等（2011）。

## 7.2 Elastic Moduli

## 7.2 弹性模量

The representation of ground stiffness by elastic moduli is common, since these are utilized in simple analytical solutions derived from elasticity theory, as well as serve as input parameters to numerical finite element simulations. In the laboratory, the specific determination of Young’s modulus (E), shear modulus (G), constrained modulus (D’, or also M’), and bulk modulus (K’) are found directly from triaxial compression, simple shear, one-dimensional consolidation, and isotropic consolidation testing, as depicted in Figure 7.1.

通过弹性模量来表示地基刚度是很常见的做法，因为这些模量用于从弹性理论推导出的简单分析解法中，并作为数值有限元模拟的输入参数。在实验室中，杨氏模量（E）、剪切模量（G）、约束模量（D’或M’）和体积模量（K’）的具体测定是通过三轴压缩、简单剪切、一维固结和各向同性固结测试来获得的，如图7.1所示。

![image](images/rId239.png)

*Figure 7.1: Definitions and tests for the four elastic moduli of soils*

Elastic continuum theory allows for interrelationships between the elastic moduli in terms of the Poisson’s ratio ($\nu$), such that:

$$E = 2G(1 + \nu) \tag{7.2}$$

$$D = \frac{E(1-\nu)}{(1+\nu)(1-2\nu)} \tag{7.3}$$

$$K = \frac{E}{3(1-2\nu)} \tag{7.4}$$

The moduli E and G can have both drained values (E’ and G’) as well as undrained values ($E_u$ and $G_u$), depending upon the specific stress path (i.e., drained when $\Delta u$ = 0; undrained when $\Delta V$ = 0).

Note that the constrained modulus (D’) and bulk modulus (K’) take on only a drained value, since strains and void ratios are measured specifically at the end of primary consolidation.

At the drained value of $\nu’$ = 0, the theoretical ratio D’/E’ = 1.0, i.e., same value. Moreover, at a practical value $\nu’$ ≈ 0.2, the ratio D’/E’ = 1.1. Therefore, the terms constrained modulus and drained Young’s modulus are often used somewhat interchangeably.

模量E和G可以有排水值（E’和 G’）以及不排水值（Eu和Gu），具体取决于应力路径（即，当 \Deltau = 0 时为排水；当 \DeltaV = 0 时为未排水）。

注意，约束模量（D’）和体积模量（K’）仅取排水值，因为应变和空隙比是在初期固结结束时特定测量的。

在排水值 \nu′ = 0时，理论上 D’/E’ = 1.0，即相同值。此外，在实际值 \nu′ ≈ 0.2 时，D’/E’ = 1.1。因此，约束模量和排水的杨氏模量常常在使用时有所互换。

In terms of stress-strain curves, there are different definitions for the moduli, particularly Young’s modulus and shear modulus, as depicted in Figure 7.2. These include: (a) initial tangent modulus at small strains, $E_{max}$: (b) secant modulus, $E_{sec}$; (c) tangent modulus, $E_{tan}$; and (d) unload-reload modulus, $E_{ur}$. These moduli can be specified at established levels of strain (say $E_{0.001}$ at corresponding 0.1% strain), or at certain levels of mobilized stress (say $E_{50}$ at 50% of ultimate strength).

在应力-应变曲线中，对于模量（特别是杨氏模量和剪切模量）有不同的定义，如图7.2所示。这些定义包括：（a）小应变下的初始切线模量，Emax；（b）割线模量，Esec；（c）切线模量，Etan；以及（d）卸载-重载模量，Eur。这些模量可以在确定的应变水平下指定（例如，在相应的 0.1% 应变下的 E0.001），或在某些动员应力水平下指定（例如，在极限强度的 50% 下的 E50）。

![image](images/rId241.png)

*Figure 7.2: Conceptual deviator stress versus axial strain for triaxial compression test showing different definitions of elastic Young's modulus*

### 7.2.1 Constrained Modulus from Compressibility Parameters

### 7.2.1 由压缩性参数得到的约束模量

In terms of the compressibility parameters from consolidation testing, the constrained modulus has a tangent definition and can be expressed as functions of the void ratio, effective stress ($\sigma'_{v0}$), and compression indices:

根据固结试验中的压缩性参数，约束模量具有切线定义，可以表示为空隙比、有效应力（\sigma′v0）和压缩系数的函数：

$$D = \frac{1 + e_0}{C_r \ln(10) \sigma'_{v0}} \quad \text{(OC Soils)} \tag{7.5}$$

$$D = \frac{1 + e_0}{C_c \ln(10) \sigma'_{v0}} \quad \text{(NC Soils)} \tag{7.6}$$

### 7.2.2 Constrained Modulus from CPT

### 7.2.2 根据CPT得到的约束模量

For a quick and direct evaluation of the constrained modulus (and drained Young’s modulus) from CPT results, the common approach is expressed in the form:

为了快速直接地从 CPT 结果中评估约束模量（以及排水杨氏模量），常用的方法可以表示为：

$$D’ = \alpha_D(q_t - \sigma_{v0}) \tag{7.7}$$

where D’ corresponds to the current effective stress state (i.e., $\sigma’_{v0}$) and $\alpha_D$ is an empirical scaling factor that has been shown to depend upon soil type, confining stress level, relative density, overconsolidation, and other factors (e.g., Kulhawy and Mayne 1990). Figure 7.3 shows that for wide range of soil types: $\alpha_D$ ≈ 5 is an approximate starting place, excepting soft plastic organic clays and cemented geomaterials. The original database for this compilation relied on laboratory consolidometer data to provide the corresponding D’ for clays and silts, while the consolidation phase of calibration chamber tests supplied many of the values of D’ for clean quartz sands (Mayne 2007b).

To check the general validity of this trend, three case studies were reviewed: (1) a large footing load test (B = 3 m) on sand at Texas A&M University (Briaud 2007); (2) a long-term drained footing load test on clay (B = 2.2 m) conducted by Imperial College (Lehane and Jardine 2003);

其中，D’ 代表当前的有效应力状态（即 \sigma′v0），\alphaD 是一个经验缩放因子，已被证明依赖于土壤类型、围压水平、相对密度、超固结度以及其他因素（例如，Kulhawy 和 Mayne 1990）。图 7.3 显示，对于各种土壤类型，\alphaD ≈ 5 是一个近似的起始值，但软塑性有机粘土和胶结的地质材料除外。该编纂的原始数据库依赖于实验室固结仪数据，以提供粘土和粉土的对应 D’ 值，而标定罐试验的固结阶段则提供了许多干净石英砂的 D’ 值（Mayne 2007b）。

![image](images/rId245.png)

*Figure 7.3: First-order trend between drained constrained modulus and net cone tip resistance in various soils (after Mayne 2007)*

and (3) the Treporti instrumented test embankment on silty soils near Venice (Marchetti et al.

2004). The net cone resistances were determined as the average values within a depth equal to one foundation width deep beneath the applied loading elevation. The equivalent moduli were back-calculated using elastic continuum theory where the drained response of the Texas footing corresponded to a factor of safety FS = 2 and the drained settlements for the Bothkennar footing were at an applied loading level associated with an undrained FS = 1.6. These case studies are also shown in Figure 7.3 with acceptable agreement.

为了检查这一趋势的普遍有效性，审查了三个案例研究：（1）德克萨斯 A&M 大学在砂土上的大基础负载试验（B = 3 米）（Briaud 2007）；（2）帝国学院进行的粘土上的长期排水基础负载试验（B = 2.2 米）（Lehane 和 Jardine 2003）和（3）在威尼斯附近的 Treporti 仪器化试验堤坝上的粉土（Marchetti et al. 2004）。净锥阻力是通过测量施加荷载高度下深度等于一个基础宽度的平均值来确定的。等效模量是通过弹性连续体理论反向计算的，其中德克萨斯基础的排水响应对应于安全系数 FS = 2，而 Bothkennar 基础的排水沉降则与无排水条件下的 FS = 1.6 相关。这些案例研究也在图 7.3 中显示，结果与预期一致。

The flat plate dilatometer test (DMT) provides a modulus termed the dilatometer modulus (ED) that related directly to the Young's modulus of soil (E) via elastic continuum theory (Marchetti et al. 2004). Robertson (2009b) conducted a comparative study of flat dilatometer tests (DMT) and CPT data taken in the same soils and found a similar relationship (E ≈ 5 ∙ qnet), shown in Figure 7.4. Thus a first-order estimate of the soil modulus can be taken as:

扁铲侧胀仪试验（DMT）提供了一个称为扁铲侧胀模量（ED）的参数，该模量通过弹性连续体理论与土壤的杨氏模量（E）直接相关（Marchetti et al. 2004）。Robertson（2009b）对在相同土壤中进行的扁铲侧胀仪试验（DMT）和圆锥静力触探试验（CPT）数据进行了比较研究，并发现了类似的关系（E ≈ 5 ∙ qnet），如图 7.4 所示。因此，土壤模量的一级估计可以表示为：

$$D' \approx 5 \cdot q_{net} \tag{7.8}$$

![image](images/rId247.png)

*Figure 7.4: Normalized Flat dilatometer modulus versus CPT Q for 19 soils (Robertson 2009b)*

Since most undisturbed natural soils are at least lightly-overconsolidated, if not moderately to heavily OC, then the use of a constrained modulus would generally only apply during recompression loading up to the yield stress ($\sigma'_{v0}$ ≤ $\sigma'_p$), thereafter a modulus corresponding to NC conditions would need be applied. This can be evaluated through the dimensionless modulus number: $m_D$ = $\Delta D'$/($\Delta \sigma'_v$)$^{d^*}$ where d* = exponent that varies from 1 in clays to 0.5 in sands, as discussed by Schmertmann (1986); Senneset et al. (1989); and Kulhawy and Mayne (1990). Of additional note, the modulus number has been empirically correlated to water content (Fellenius 2020).

由于大多数未扰动的天然土壤至少是轻微超固结的，如果不是中等到重度超固结的，那么约束模量通常只在重新压缩荷载下适用，直到屈服应力（\sigma′v0 ≤ \sigma′p），之后需要应用对应于常规固结条件的模量。这可以通过无量纲模量数来评估：mD = \DeltaD′/(\Delta\sigma′v) d∗，其中 d* 是一个从粘土的 1 到砂土的 0.5 变化的指数，如 Schmertmann（1986）、Senneset 等（1989）和 Kulhawy 与 Mayne（1990）所讨论。需要额外注意的是，模量数已与含水量进行经验相关（Fellenius 2020）。

### 7.2.3 Small-Strain Shear Modulus

### 7.2.3 小应变剪切模量

The small-strain shear modulus ($G_0 = G_{max}$) is a fundamental stiffness that relates to the initial state of the soil. This stiffness applies to the initial loading for all stress-strain-strength curves, including static, cyclic, and dynamic types of loading, as well as undrained and drained conditions (Burland 1989; Mayne 2001; Leroueil and Hight 2003; Amoroso et al. 2014). The small-strain shear modulus is calculated directly from elasticity theory using the total soil mass density ($\rho_T = \gamma_T/g_a$) and shear wave velocity ($V_s$), where $g_a$ = 9.8 m/s² = gravitational acceleration constant:

小应变剪切模量（$G_0 = G_{max}$）是与土壤初始状态相关的基本刚度。这种刚度适用于所有应力-应变-强度曲线的初始加载，包括静态、循环和动态加载类型，以及不排水和排水条件（Burland 1989；Mayne 2001；Leroueil 和 Hight 2003；Amoroso 等 2014）。小应变剪切模量直接从弹性理论计算，使用土壤总质量密度（$\rho_T = \gamma_T/g_a$）和剪切波速（$V_s$），其中 $g_a$ = 9.8 m/s² 是重力加速度常数。

$$G_{max} = \rho_T \cdot V_s^2 \tag{7.9}$$

The initial stiffness can be expressed in terms of an equivalent small-strain Young’s modulus of soil through elastic theory:

初始刚度可以通过弹性理论以土壤的等效小应变杨氏模量来表示：

$$E_{max} = 2 G_{max} (1 + \nu) \tag{7.10}$$

Preferably, these small-strain moduli should be determined from direct measurements of shear wave velocity, such as in-situ crosshole tests (CHT), downhole tests (DST), and/or surface wave methods (SASW). The seismic piezocone penetration tests (SCPTU) and seismic flat dilatometer test (SDMT) both provide measurements of the DST type, which is an $V_sV_H$ type (vertically-propagating and horizontally-polarized).

最好通过直接测量剪切波速来确定这些小应变模量，例如使用原位交叉孔测试（CHT）、井下测试（DST）和/或表面波方法（SASW）。地震孔压静力触探测试（SCPTU）和地震扁铲侧胀试验（SDMT）都提供了类似于 DST 的测量，这是一种 $V_sV_H$ 类型（垂直传播和水平极化）。

## 7.3 Modulus Reduction Curves

## 7.3 模量减小曲线

The value of small-strain shear modulus $G_{max}$ (and corresponding stiffness, $E_{max}$) applies strictly to the nondestructive range of strains, where $\gamma_s$ < $10^{-4}$ as a decimal (or $\gamma_s$ < $10^{-6}$ in %). For loading levels at strains exceeding a threshold value, the use of modulus reduction curves (RF = $G/G_{max}$) must be implemented. For cyclic loading and dynamic problems in geotechnical engineering, Vucetic and Dobry (1991) present $G/G_{max}$ curves in terms of soil plasticity and logarithm of shear strain. The appropriate value of secant shear modulus is then obtained from:

小应变剪切模量 $G_{max}$（及相应的刚度 $E_{max}$）的值严格适用于非破坏性应变范围，其中 $\gamma_s$ < $10^{-4}$（或 $\gamma_s$ < $10^{-6}$%）。对于应变超过阈值的加载水平，必须使用模量减小曲线（RF = $G/G_{max}$）。对于岩土工程中的循环加载和动态问题，Vucetic 和 Dobry（1991）根据土壤塑性和剪切应变的对数提供了 $G/G_{max}$ 曲线。然后，从以下公式获得适当的割线剪切模量：

$$G = \left( \frac{G}{G_{max}} \right) G_{max} = RF \cdot G_{max} \tag{7.11}$$

A similar procedure can be used for tangent definitions of shear moduli, as discussed by Fahey and Carter (1993).

The ratio ($G/G_{max}$) is a reduction factor (RF) that is applied to the small-strain modulus, depending on current loading conditions, in order to obtain the relevant G for that situation.

The $G/G_{max}$ curves can be presented in terms of logarithm of shear strain ($\gamma_s$), or alternatively in terms of mobilized shear stress ($q/q_{max}$), as discussed by Fahey (1998). The mobilized shear stress can be considered as the reciprocal of the factor of safety ($q/q_{max}$ = 1/FS). In terms of fitting stress-strain data, $G/G_{max}$ versus mobilized stress level ($q/q_{max}$) plots are visually biased towards the intermediate- to large-strain regions of the soil response. In contrast, $G/G_{max}$ versus log $\gamma_s$ curves tend to accentuate the small- to intermediate-strain range.

类似的程序可以用于剪切模量的切线定义，正如 Fahey 和 Carter（1993）所讨论的那样。

比值 ($G/G_{max}$) 是一个减小因子（RF），该因子根据当前的加载条件应用于小应变模量，以获得与该情况相关的G。

$G/G_{max}$ 曲线可以以剪切应变的对数（$\gamma_s$）或以动用剪切应力（$q/q_{max}$）的形式呈现，正如 Fahey（1998）所讨论的。动用剪切应力可以被视为安全系数的倒数（$q/q_{max}$ = 1/FS）。在拟合应力-应变数据时，$G/G_{max}$ 与动用应力水平（$q/q_{max}$）的关系图通常视觉上偏向于表示土壤响应的中到大应变区域。相比之下，$G/G_{max}$ 与对数 $\gamma_s$ 的关系曲线则更能突出小到中等应变范围。

![image](images/rId251.png)

*Figure 7.5: Measured modulus reduction curves from monotonic laboratory shear tests on assorted clays and sands under both drained and undrained loading*

A selection of modulus reduction curves, represented by the ratio ($G/G_{max}$), has been collected from monotonic laboratory shear tests performed on an assorted mix of clayey and sandy materials (Mayne 2005; Mayne 2007a, b). The results are presented in Figure 7.5, where $G = \tau/\gamma_s$ = secant shear modulus. These lab tests include static torsional shear and special triaxial tests with internal local strain measurements, so that the initial reference value of $E_{max}$ or $G_{max}$ has been obtained. Where necessary, an assumed constant value of $\nu$ has been applied with the conversion: $E = 2G(1 + \nu)$ to permit plotting of $E/E_{max}$ versus $q/q_{max}$, where $q = (\sigma_1 - \sigma_3)$ = deviator stress. Undrained tests are shown by solid dots and drained tests are indicated by open symbols. In general, the clays were tested under undrained loading, and the sands were tested under drained shearing conditions (except Kentucky clayey sand). Pisa clay was tested both drained and undrained. Similar trends for the various curves are noted for both undrained and drained tests on both clays and sands.

A number of different mathematical expressions have been adopted to represent the reduction factor, RF (Mayne 2005). One simple algorithm involves a modified hyperbola where the modulus reduction is given by Fahey and Carter (1993) and Fahey (1998):

从对各种粘土质和砂土质混合材料进行的单调实验室剪切测试中收集了一些模量减小曲线，表示为比值 ($G/G_{max}$)（Mayne 2005；Mayne 2007a, b）。结果展示在图 7.5 中，其中 $G = \tau/\gamma_s$ = 切线剪切模量。这些实验室测试包括静态扭剪和具有内部局部应变测量的特殊三轴试验，，以获得初始参考值 $E_{max}$ 或 $G_{max}$。如有必要，可以应用一个假定常数 $\nu$，并通过转换 $E = 2G(1 + \nu)$ 绘制 $E/E_{max}$ 对 $q/q_{max}$ 的图，其中 $q = (\sigma_1 - \sigma_3)$ = 偏差应力。不排水测试由实心点表示，排水测试由空心符号表示。通常，粘土在不排水加载下测试，而砂土在排水剪切条件下测试（除了Kentucky粘土砂）。PISA粘土则在排水和不排水条件下均进行了测试。无论是对粘土还是砂土的不排水和排水测试，曲线显示出相似的趋势。

为表示减小因子 RF（Mayne 2005），采用了多种不同的数学表达式。其中一个简单的算法涉及修改后的双曲线，其中模量减小由 Fahey 和 Carter（1993）以及 Fahey（1998）给出。

$$RF = \frac{G}{G_{max}} = 1 - \left( \frac{q}{q_{max}} \right)^g \tag{7.12}$$

with an exponent value of g ≈ 0.3 ± 0.1 for “well-behaved” soils that are uncemented, insensitive, and/or not highly-structured. Figure 7.5 shows several values for exponent g.

对于“表现良好”的土壤（即未胶结、无敏感性和/或结构上未完全发育的土壤），其指数值 g ≈ 0.3 ± 0.1。图 7.5 显示了几个不同的指数 g 值。

## 7.4 Stress-Strain-Strength Curves

## 7.4 应力-应变-强度曲线

The above approach can be used to generate approximate nonlinear stress-strain-strength curves for all soil types from the results of SCPTU soundings. Specifically for clays under a simple shear mode, the shear stress versus shear strain curves are developed from:

上述方法可用于根据 SCPTU 探测结果生成所有土壤类型的大致非线性应力-应变-强度曲线。特别是对于在简单剪切模式下的粘土，剪切应力与剪切应变曲线是通过以下方式开发的：

$$\tau = G \cdot \gamma_s \tag{7.13}$$

The $\tau_{max}$ value is the undrained shear strength ($\tau_{max} = s_u$) obtained from the CPT resistances and the initial $G_{max}$ is determined from the Vs data. Examples of the evaluated $\tau$ versus $\gamma_s$ responses at various depths for soft Burswood clay are presented in Figure 7.6 in comparison with lab simple shear tests conducted on undisturbed samples (Low et al. 2010).

$\tau_{max}$ 值是从 CPT 阻力获得的不排水剪切强度 ($\tau_{max} = s_u$)，而初始 $G_{max}$ 则是通过 Vs 数据确定的。图 7.6 展示了在不同深度下对软质 Burswood 粘土的 $\tau$ 与 $\gamma_s$ 响应的评估示例，并与对未扰动样本进行的实验室简单剪切测试（Low et al. 2010）进行了比较。

![image](images/rId254.png)

*Figure 7.6: Shear stress versus shear strain curves for Burswood clay*

A similar set of examples can be shown for triaxial tests on sands. A set of undisturbed samples of sands obtained by special freezing process have been reported for the CREC facility in Charleston, SC (Esposito & Andrus 2016). A series of drained triaxial compression tests (CIDC) were performed on trimmed specimens after thawing, as shown in Figure 7.7. Results from SCPTU tests were available and evaluated to determine the strength ($\phi'$) from normalized cone resistance and $E_{max}$ from $V_s$ measurements. The maximum deviator stress, or ($\sigma_1 - \sigma_3$)$_{max}$, from CIDC tests is calculated from the expression in Figure 7.2 for drained tests with $K_c = 1$ for isotropic consolidation. The modulus reduction factor is the same as Equation 7.12, giving:

类似的例子也可以展示在砂土的三轴测试中。报告指出，CREC 设施（位于南卡罗来纳州查尔斯顿）使用特殊的冻结工艺获得了一组未扰动的砂土样本（Esposito & Andrus 2016）。图 7.7 显示了在解冻后对修整样本进行的一系列排水三轴压缩测试 (CIDC)。SCPTU 测试结果被用于评估强度 ($\phi'$)，从归一化的圆锥阻力和 $E_{max}$ 从 $V_s$ 测量值中确定。CIDC 测试中最大偏应力或 ($\sigma_1 - \sigma_3$)$_{max}$ 是根据图 7.2 中的公式计算的，适用于 $K_c = 1$ 的等向压实排水测试。模量减小因子与方程 7.12 相同，结果为：

$$E = E_{max} \left[ 1 - \left( \frac{\sigma_1 - \sigma_3}{(\sigma_1 - \sigma_3)_{max}} \right)^{0.3} \right] \tag{7.14}$$

![image](images/rId256.png)

*Figure 7.7: Undisturbed triaxial tests and SCPTU-derived stress-strain curves for Charleston SC sands: (a) Specimen C2; (b) Specimen D1*

A recent study of laboratory data from 26 clays by Vardanega and Bolton (2013) used a different modified hyperbolic format for the modulus reduction factor (RF). The calibrated expression is shown in Figure 7.8. In this approach, moduli for both static (monotonic) loading and dynamic loading are different because of strain rate effects and can be considered in a unified manner, as given by:

Vardanega 和 Bolton（2013）对 26 种粘土的实验室数据进行的最新研究使用了一种不同的修正双曲线格式来表示模量减小因子（RF）。校准后的表达式如图 7.8 所示。在这种方法中，由于应变率效应，静态（单调）加载和动态加载的模量不同，可以通过统一的方式加以考虑，如下所示：

$$\frac{G}{G_{max}} = \frac{1}{1 + (\gamma_s / \gamma_{ref})^h} \tag{7.15}$$

![image](images/rId258.png)

*Figure 7.8: Normalized modulus reduction curves for 26 clays reported by Vardanega and Bolton (2013)*

where the exponent h = 0.736 (static loading) and h = 0.943 (dynamic loading) and the reference shear strain ($\gamma_{ref}$) was found related to the clay plasticity. Specifically, $\gamma_{ref}$ is determined from:

其中，指数 h=0.736（静态加载）和 h=0.943（动态加载），参考剪切应变 $\gamma_{ref}$ 与粘土的塑性相关。具体地，$\gamma_{ref}$ 是通过以下公式确定的：

$$\gamma_{ref} = J_p \cdot (0.001 \cdot PI) \tag{7.16}$$

where $J_p$ = 2.2 for static loading and $J_p$ = 3.7 for dynamic loading, and PI (%) = plasticity index. The derived modulus reduction curves for dynamic loading conditions are comparable with the well-known Vucetic-Dobry curves (1991) when adjusted for strain rate effects.

其中，$J_p$=2.2对于静态加载，$J_p$=3.7对于动态加载，PI（%）为塑性指数。在调整应变率效应后，动态加载条件下推导出的模量减小曲线与著名的 Vucetic-Dobry 曲线（1991）具有可比性。

## 7.5 Poisson's Ratio

## 7.5 泊松比

Poisson’s ratio ($\nu$) is an elastic parameter and defined as the ratio of lateral strain to axial strain:

泊松比 ($\nu$) 是一个弹性参数，定义为横向应变与轴向应变的比值：

$$\nu = -\epsilon_h / \epsilon_v \tag{7.17}$$

The value for Poisson's ratio for undrained loading at constant volume is $\nu_u$ = 0.5. For simple isotropic elastic theory, the range of values for drained loading is often taken as: 0 ≤ $\nu'$ ≤ 0.5. However, values > 0.5 are possible if dilatancy is considered.

对于恒定体积下的不排水加载，泊松比的值为 $\nu_u$=0.5。对于简单的各向同性弹性理论，排水加载的泊松比值范围通常取为 0≤$\nu'$≤0.5。然而，如果考虑膨胀性，泊松比值大于 0.5 也是可能的。

### 7.5.1 Poisson's Ratio From Lab Tests

### 7.5.1 实验测试获得的泊松比

For drained loading, local strain measurements made directly on soil specimens with special internal high-resolution instrumentation (e.g., Burland 1989; Lehane and Cosgrove 2000) show that the value of drained $\nu'$ is generally lower than those reported from earlier measurements that contained bedding and boundary errors. These on-specimen measurements indicate a range: 0.15 < $\nu'$ < 0.25 for all types of geomaterials (clays, silts, sands, rocks) at working load levels that correspond to relatively small-strains ($\epsilon_s$ < 0.1%). Figure 7.9 shows a collection of proximeter measurements from several sands and two clays. At higher strains, $\nu'$ increases as failure states are approached (Mayne et al. 2009). Thus, a characteristic $\nu'$ ≈ 0.2 can be adopted for drained loading conditions in the elastic range, generally when FS ≥ 2.

对于排水加载，通过使用特殊内部高分辨率仪器（例如，Burland 1989；Lehane 和 Cosgrove 2000）直接在土样上进行的局部应变测量表明，排水泊松比 $\nu'$ 的值通常低于早期测量报告的值，这些早期测量存在层理和边界误差。这些样本测量结果表明，对于所有类型的岩土材料（粘土、粉土、砂土、岩石），在对应于相对小应变（$\epsilon_s$<0.1%）的工作荷载水平下，排水泊松比 $\nu'$ 的范围为 0.15<$\nu'$<0.25。图 7.9 展示了来自几种砂土和两种粘土的接触计测量结果。在较高的应变下，$\nu'$ 随着接近破坏状态而增加（Mayne et al. 2009）。因此，在弹性范围内的排水加载条件下，通常可以采用特征值 $\nu'$≈0.2，一般适用于 FS≥2的情况。

![image](images/rId261.png)

*Figure 7.9: Poisson's ratios measured by local strain transducers for 7 sands and 2 clays (modified after Lehane and Cosgrove 2000)*

### 7.5.2 Poisson's Ratio from Geophysical Tests

### 7.5.2 根据地球物理测试获得的泊松比

Poisson’s ratio can also be obtained from shear wave ($V_s$) and compression wave ($V_p$) velocities, at least for dry soils and perhaps partially-saturated ground.

The small-strain constrained modulus ($M_0$) is obtained as:

泊松比也可以通过剪切波（$V_s$）和压缩波（$V_p$）的速度来获得，至少对于干土和可能部分饱和的土壤是适用的。小应变约束模量（$M_0$）的计算方式如下：

$$M_0 = \rho_t \cdot V_p^2 \tag{7.18}$$

The value of $M_0$ with small-strain shear modulus ($G_0$) from eqn (7.9) can then be used to calculate the Poisson’s ratio from isotropic elasticity theory:

通过公式 (7.9) 计算得到的小应变剪切模量 $G_0$ 的 $M_0$ 值可以用于根据各向同性弹性理论计算泊松比：

$$\nu' = \frac{M_0 - 2 \cdot G_0}{2 \cdot M_0 - 2 \cdot G_0} \tag{7.19}$$

However, it can be noted that once the groundwater table is encountered, the measured Vp data most always reflect the waves travelling through water (a.k.a., Hydro-P), and not the soil skeleton, so that the derived $\nu$ values are close to the undrained case, that is: $\nu$ ≈ 0.5.

然而，需要注意的是，一旦遇到地下水位，测得的 $V_p$ 数据通常反映的是通过水传播的波（即水中波），而不是土壤骨架，因此推导出的泊松比 $\nu$ 值接近于不排水情况，即：$\nu$≈0.5。

## 7.6 Backfigured Drained Moduli from Foundation Performance on Sands

## 7.6 根据砂土地基表现反算排水模量

Schmertmann (1970) is recognized as having introduced CPT into the USA and using the results to help improve calculations of footing settlements on sands. From his work with screw-plate load tests in Florida and mechanical CPT results in fine sands, he recommended:

Schmertmann（1970）被公认为将 CPT 引入美国并利用其结果改进砂土基础沉降计算的人。通过在佛罗里达进行螺旋板载荷测试和细砂中的机械 CPT 结果，他建议：

$$E' = 2 \cdot q_c \tag{7.20}$$

Later efforts using calibration chamber tests on clean sand deposits that were prepared at a range of relative densities and various degrees of overconsolidation found that eqn (7.7) better applied with the aD factor increasing with OCR and decreasing DR (Kulhawy & Mayne 1990):

后续使用标定罐测试在不同相对密度和不同超固结程度的纯净砂层上进行的研究发现，公式 (7.7) 更适用于 aD因子随着 OCR 的增加和 DR 的减少而增加的情况（Kulhawy & Mayne 1990）：

$$\alpha_D \approx 10^{1.09 - 0.0075 \cdot D_R} \tag{7.21}$$

$$\alpha_D \approx 10^{1.78 - 0.0122 \cdot D_R} \tag{7.22}$$

Magnitudes of elastic moduli can be backfigured from the field performance of foundations. For shallow foundations on sands, a database of full scale loading tests has been compiled from 67 footings resting on 14 sands where the load versus displacement responses have been evaluated (Mayne and Woeller 2014). These were supplemented with results from measured settlements of bridge and building foundations from 98 structures (Mayne and Dasenbrock 2018). All sands were subjected to CPT soundings and the measured cone resistances were averaged over a depth of approximately 1.5B beneath the foundation bearing elevations.

弹性模量的大小可以通过基础的现场表现反推得到。对于砂土上的浅基础，已经编制了一个包含67个基础在14种砂土上的全尺度加载测试数据库，其中对荷载与位移响应进行了评估（Mayne 和 Woeller 2014）。这些数据还补充了来自98个结构的桥梁和建筑基础的沉降测量结果（Mayne 和 Dasenbrock 2018）。所有砂土都经过了CPT探测，测得的锥尖阻力在基础承载高度下方约1.5B的深度范围内进行了平均。

Figure 7.10 shows the backcalculated equivalent elastic moduli normalized by the cone resistances as a nonlinear function with the normalized displacements (s/B). The overall trend for 130 foundations is represented by:

图 7.10 显示了以锥尖阻力归一化的反推等效弹性模量，作为归一化位移（s/B）的非线性函数。130个基础的总体趋势由以下公式表示：

$$E = 0.5 (q_{net})(s/B)^{-0.5} \tag{7.23}$$

![image](images/rId266.png)

*Figure 7.10: Elastic moduli backcalculated for 130 footings on sands*

## 7.7 Undrained Modulus from Rigidity Index

## 7.7 从刚度指数获得的不排水模量

The rigidity index ($I_r$) is an input parameter to many geotechnical solutions, including those based in cavity expansion, strain path methods, and finite elements. Specifically, $I_r$ is defined as the ratio of shear modulus to shear strength ($I_R = G/\tau_{max}$). For undrained loading, $\tau_{max}$ = $s_u$, and therefore the undrained shear modulus ($G_u$) can be determined from:

刚度指数 ($I_r$) 是许多岩土工程解答中的输入参数，包括基于小孔扩张、应变路径方法和有限元方法的解答。具体而言，$I_r$ 定义为剪切模量与剪切强度的比率（$I_R = G/\tau_{max}$）。对于不排水荷载，$\tau_{max} = s_u$，因此不排水剪切模量 ($G_u$) 可以通过以下公式确定：

$$G_u = I_R \cdot s_u \tag{7.24}$$

Similarly, the undrained Young’s modulus is obtained from the relationship:

同样，不排水杨氏模量由以下关系式得出：

$$E_u = 2 \cdot G_u \cdot (1+\nu) = 3 \cdot G_u \tag{7.25}$$

On large or critical projects, the magnitude of $I_R$ can be ascertained from the results of laboratory stress-strain curves on high-quality undisturbed samples. Thus, for undrained conditions, results from simple shear or triaxial tests can be used (Foott & Ladd 1981). Figure 7.11 depicts a shear stress versus shear strain curve and shows that the rigidity index can be considered as the reciprocal of a reference shear strain ($\gamma_{ref} = 1/I_R$).

As per the aforementioned Sections 7.3 and 7.4, it is well-recognized that the modulus of soil is highly nonlinear from the nondestructive range ($G_{max}$) to intermediate strains (G) to failure at peak strength ($G_f$). Consequently, there are a number of ways to define a reference value of $I_R$ and associated G. One common value is $G_{50}$ that corresponds to the secant modulus at a factor of safety of FS = 2; that is, a secant shear modulus defined at 50% of its shear strength. Moreover, as many laboratory shear tests intend to reach peak strength at 1% strain in soft to firm clays, the operational rigidity index at failure would be $I_R = 1/\gamma_s = (1/0.01) = 100$, which is often taken as a common default value in practice (e.g., Houlsby & Teh 1988; Teh & Houlsby 1991).

在大型或关键项目中，$I_R$的大小可以通过高质量未扰动样品的实验室应力-应变曲线结果确定。因此，对于不排水条件，可以使用简单剪切或三轴测试的结果（Foott & Ladd 1981）。图 7.11展示了剪切应力与剪切应变曲线，并表明刚度指数可以被视为参考剪切应变的倒数（$\gamma_{ref} = 1/I_R$）。

根据前述的第 7.3 和 7.4 节，土壤的模量在未破坏范围（$G_{max}$）、中间应变（G）到峰值强度下的破坏（$G_f$）是高度非线性的。因此，有多种方法来定义$I_R$的参考值及其相关的G。一个常见的值是$G_{50}$，它对应于安全系数FS = 2时的割线模量；即在剪切强度的 50% 时定义的割线剪切模量。此外，由于许多实验室剪切测试旨在于软到中等粘土中以 1% 应变达到峰值强度，破坏时的操作刚度指数为 $I_R = 1/\gamma_s = (1/0.01) = 100$，这在实际中常被作为一个默认值（例如，Houlsby & Teh 1988；Teh & Houlsby 1991）。

![image](images/rId269.png)

*Figure 7.11: Undrained rigidity index from measured τ versus γs curve defined at peak*

The cone penetrometer is a full-displacement probe. Therefore, since 100% of the soil beneath the axis of the penetrometer is displaced during CPT, it is believed that the operational rigidity index corresponds to that at failure, i.e. $I_R$ = Gf/su where Gf is the shear modulus defined at peak strength, as indicated in Figure 7.11.

For estimating a value of $I_R$ from CAUC triaxial tests, empirical trends have been correlated to OCR and plasticity index (PI) of the clay and presented in graphical form by Keaveny and Mitchell (1986). This relationship can be approximated by the expression:

圆锥贯入仪是一个全位移探头。因此，由于在CPT测试过程中探头轴下方所有的土壤会移动，操作刚度指数被认为是对应于破坏时的值，即 $I_R = G_f/s_u$，其中 $G_f$ 是定义在峰值强度下的剪切模量，如图 7.11 所示。

为了从 CAUC 三轴测试中估计 $I_R$ 的值，Keaveny 和 Mitchell（1986）将经验趋势与粘土的 OCR 和塑性指数（PI）相关联，并以图表形式呈现。这种关系可以通过以下表达式近似：

$$I_R \approx \frac{\exp(0.044(137-PI))}{(1+\ln(1+0.038(OCR-1)^{3.2}))^{0.8}} \tag{7.26}$$

By deriving stress-strain curves from SCPTU results, Krage et al. (2014) posed the relationship:

通过从SCPTU结果中推导应力-应变曲线，Krage等人（2014）提出了以下关系：

$$I_{R50} = 1.811 \frac{G_{max}}{(q_{net})^{0.75} \cdot (\sigma'_{v0})^{0.25}} \tag{7.27}$$

where the input terms are all in the same units.

For regular clays that are inorganic and relatively insensitive, the SCE-CSSM solution (Section 4.4.1) provides the operational value of rigidity index (Mayne 2001; Agaiby & Mayne 2018b):

其中，所有输入项都使用相同的单位。对于无机且相对不敏感的常规粘土，SCE-CSSM 解法（第 4.4.1 节）提供了刚度指数的操作值（Mayne 2001；Agaiby & Mayne 2018b）：

$$I_R = \exp\left[\frac{1.5 + 2.925 \cdot M_c \cdot a_x}{M_c \cdot (1 - a_x)}\right] \tag{7.28}$$

where $M_c$ = (6 · sin$\phi'$)/(3 − sin$\phi'$) and slope $a_x$ is found by plotting $\Delta u_\sigma$ = ($u_2$ - $\sigma_{vo}$) versus net cone resistance, $q_{net}$ = $q_t$ - $\sigma_{vo}$; or alternately, slope $a_x$ is determined as the slope of (U-1) versus normalized cone resistance, Q.

A surrogate expression of the solution uses the form:

其中$M_c$ = (6 · sin$\phi'$)/(3 − sin$\phi'$)而斜率 $a_x$ 通过绘制 $\Delta u_\sigma$=($u_2$−$\sigma_{vo}$)与净锥阻力 $q_{net}$=$q_t$−$\sigma_{vo}$的关系图来确定；或者，斜率 $a_x$ 可以作为 (U-1) 与归一化锥阻力Q的关系图的斜率来确定。

解法的替代表达式使用以下形式：

$$I_R = \exp\left[a_y \left(\frac{1.5}{M_c} + 2.925\right) - 2.925\right] \tag{7.29}$$

where $a_y$ = slope of the plot of net cone resistance, $q_{net}$ = ($q_t$ − $\sigma_{v0}$), versus effective cone resistance, $q_E$ = ($q_t$ − $u_2$). For inorganic clays of low-medium sensitivity, the variation of $I_R$ with $\phi'$ and slope $a_y$ is presented in Figure 7.12.

Note that for sensitive and quick clays, Section 4.4.5 provides an expression for undrained rigidity index comparable to that above for a modified SCE-CSSM solution yet requires two values of frictional parameters: $M_{c1}$ (corresponding to $\phi_1'$ at peak) and $M_{c2}$ (corresponding to $\phi_2'$ at large strains). For sensitive clays, a chart version of the interrelationship of $I_R$, slope parameter $a_q$ = $\Delta$(U-1)/$\Delta$Q, and the two friction angles ($\phi_1'$ and $\phi_2'$) is provided by Mayne & Benoît (2020).

其中 $a_y$ 是净锥尖阻力 $q_{net}$=($q_t$−$\sigma_{vo}$)与有效锥尖阻力 $q_E$=($q_t$−$u_2$)之间关系图的斜率。对于低至中等敏感性的无机粘土，$I_R$ 与 $\phi'$ 和斜率 $a_y$ 的变化如图 7.12 所示。

注意，对于敏感性和快速粘土，第 4.4.5 节提供了一种与上述修改版 SCE-CSSM 解法相比较的不排水刚度指数表达式，但需要两个摩擦参数的值：$M_{c1}$（对应于峰值时的 $\phi_1'$）和 $M_{c2}$（对应于大变形时的 $\phi_2'$）。对于敏感性粘土，Mayne & Benoît（2020）提供了 $I_R$、斜率参数 $a_q$=$\Delta$(U-1)/$\Delta$Q 和两个摩擦角（$\phi_1'$ 和 $\phi_2'$）之间相互关系的图表版本。

![image](images/rId274.png)

*Figure 7.12: Undrained rigidity index ($I_R$) of clays as a function of effective stress friction angle (φ') and slope parameter ay from SCE-CSSM solution*

### 7.7.1 Case Study: Sandpoint

### 7.7.1 案例分析：Sandpoint

The results of a CPTU sounding in soft silty clay at Sandpoint, Idaho were shown previously in Figure 5.13. The soils have overall mean index properties: LL = 45.2 ± 6.3%, PI = 19.9 ± 4.7%, and $w_n$ = 45.2 ± 6.4%. Results of DST by SCPTU indicated: $V_s$ (m/s) = 2.35 z (m) + 151.

For a representative OCR = 1.5 and PI = 20%, Equation 7.26 estimated $I_R$ = 170. Using the corresponding $G_{max}$ from the SCPTU profile together with the CPT readings, Equation 7.27 determined an average $I_r$ = 244. For the 2 values of $I_R$ provided by SCE-CSSM, Equations 7.28 and 7.29 provide estimates of $I_r$ = 193 and 184, respectively, as shown by Figure 7.13.

在爱达荷州Sandpoint进行的软质粉土的CPTU探测结果已在图5.13中展示。土壤的总体均值指标属性为：LL = 45.2 ± 6.3%，PI = 19.9 ± 4.7%，和 $w_n$=45.2±6.4%。SCPTU的DST结果表明：$V_s$(m/s)=2.35z(m)+151。

对于代表性的OCR = 1.5和PI = 20%，方程7.26估算 $I_R$=170。使用SCPTU剖面中的相应 $G_{max}$ 和CPT读数，方程7.27确定了平均 $I_r$=244。对于SCE-CSSM提供的两个 $I_R$ 值，方程7.28和7.29分别估算了 $I_r$=193和184，如图7.13所示。

![image](images/rId275.png)

*Figure 7.13: Evaluation of the slope parameters ax and ay for assessing $I_r$ from SCE-CSSM solution using CPTU data from Sandpoint, Idaho*

# 8 Flow Parameters from Dissipation Testing

# 8 消散测试的流参数

Porewater pressures generated during cone penetration in fine-grained soils are transient and caused by insertion of the probe. Once the penetration process is halted, excess pressures will decay with time and readings will eventually reach the equilibrium value ($u_0$).

Results from porewater pressure measurements taken during a piezocone sounding in soft clay at the Canadian Test Site No. 1 in South Gloucester, Ontario are shown in Figure 8.1. At each rod break, the penetration was stopped and the porewater pressures permitted to dissipate with time. The dissipation traces can be seen as drops in the $u_2$ readings at one-meter depth intervals.

在细颗粒土壤中，圆锥贯入过程中产生的孔隙水压力是暂时的，主要由探头的插入引起。一旦渗透过程停止，多余的压力将随时间衰减，读数最终将达到平衡值 $u_0$。

图8.1展示了在安大略省南格洛斯特加拿大测试站1号进行的软土中，通过孔压静力触探测量的孔隙水压力结果。在每次杆子停止时，贯入停止，孔隙水压力随时间消散。消散过程可以从每米深度间隔的 $u_2$读数的下降中观察到。

During undrained penetration, consolidation does not occur since constant volume is maintained. Thus the $u_2$ penetration reading with depth represents the condition corresponding to a zero degree of consolidation (U = 0%). In due time, the $u_2$ reading will eventually reach $u_0$ corresponding to the completion of primary consolidation (U = 100%). For the dissipations at 21 and 22 m depths, full equilibrium was achieved.

在不排水渗透过程中，由于保持了恒定体积，因此不会发生固结。因此，随着深度增加的 $u_2$渗透读数表示零固结度（U = 0%）的状态。随着时间的推移，$u_2$读数将最终达到 $u_0$，对应于主要固结的完成（U = 100%）。在21米和22米深度的消散过程中，已实现完全平衡。

![image](images/rId276.png)

*Figure 8.1: Porewater pressure profile from CPTU sounding at South Gloucester, Ontario*

By measuring the rate of porewater pressure dissipation, two flow parameters of soil can be assessed: (1) coefficient of permeability (k); and (2) coefficient of consolidation ($c_{vh}$). This requires plots of the measured $u_2$ readings versus time, with the latter presented in either arithmetic, logarithmic, or square root plots. Figure 8.2 shows a total of 18 piezo-dissipation records from Gloucester with $u_2$ plotted versus log time. The dissipations are grouped into three categories, primarily based on their depths to indicate an upper clay, intermediate layer, and lower clay layer.

通过测量孔隙水压力的消散速率，可以评估土壤的两个流动参数：（1）渗透系数 k；（2）固结系数 $c_{vh}$。这需要绘制测得的 $u_2$读数与时间的图表，时间可以以算术、对数或平方根坐标系呈现。图8.2展示了18个来自格洛斯特的孔压消散记录，绘制为$u_2$随对数时间变化图。消散数据被分为三类，主要根据其深度，表示上层粘土、中间层和下层粘土。

![image](images/rId277.png)

*Figure 8.2: Piezo-dissipation records with logarithm time in soft clays at Gloucester site*

For convenience, an established time of 50% degree of consolidation, designated $t_{50}$, is often used in practice to represent a characteristic value from the dissipation data. As noted, the penetration value of the $u_2$ reading represents 0% degree and the equilibrium value ($u_2$ = $u_0$) represents 100% degree of consolidation, therefore $t_{50}$ is the time required to reach the porewater pressure value that is half-way between these two values.

Figure 8.3 illustrates the evaluation of $t_{50}$ from a porewater pressure dissipation record in firm clay at a depth z = 4.2 m in Evergreen, NC. This is a monotonic type response, since the porewater pressures are highest during CPT pushing and less at all times following the halting of penetration. First, the $u_0$ is calculated for the noted groundwater depth of $z_w$ = 0.4 m, assuming unconfined conditions, giving $u_0$ = 37 kPa. The value of $u_2$ during penetration is determined as $u_2$ (t = 0) = 829 kPa. Using the porewater reading corresponding to 50% consolidation(433 kPa) determines the characteristic value $t_{50}$ = 7 minutes for this test.

为了方便起见，通常在实践中使用一个已建立的50%固结度时间，称为 $t_{50}$，来表示消散数据中的特征值。如前所述，$u_2$读数的渗透值表示0%固结度，而平衡值（$u_2$=$u_0$）表示100%固结度，因此 $t_{50}$是达到这两个值之间一半的孔隙水压力值所需的时间。

图8.3展示了如何从位于北卡罗来纳州常青树（Evergreen）深度 z=4.2米的坚硬粘土中的孔隙水压力消散记录中评估$t_{50}$。这是一个单调类型的响应，因为在静力触探（CPT）推进过程中，孔隙水压力最高，而在停止渗透后的所有时间内，孔隙水压力均较低。首先，假设在无约束条件下计算$u_0$，其中地下水深度 zw=0.4米，得到 $u_0$=37 kPa。渗透过程中 $u_2$的值被确定为 $u_2$(t=0)=829kPa。使用对应于50%固结度的孔隙水压力读数（433 kPa）确定此测试的特征值 $t_{50}$=7分钟。

## 8.1 from Dissipation Tests

## 8.1 根据消散实验

The coefficient of consolidation ($c_v$) is a flow parameter that governs the rate of porewater pressure dissipation around pile foundations, shallow foundation settlements, and design of vertical wick drains. From one-dimensional consolidation theory, the coefficient of consolidation is given by:

固结系数（$c_v$）是一个流动参数，决定了桩基础周围的孔隙水压力消散速率、浅基础沉降以及垂直引水排水设计。根据一维固结理论，固结系数由以下公式给出：

$$c_v = \frac{k \cdot D'}{\gamma_w} \tag{8.1}$$

![image](images/rId279.png)

*Figure 8.3: Determination of representative time $t_{50}$ from dissipation at Evergreen, NC*

where k = coefficient of permeability, D’ = constrained modulus, and \gammaw = unit weight of water.

The evaluation of $c_v$ from lab consolidation tests involves the use of a time factor which depends on the degree of consolidation, as well as the drainage path (Holtz et al. 2011).

In laboratory consolidation tests, the coefficient of consolidation is designated $c_v$ as related to the vertical flow of water through the soil specimen. In contrast, in pile design, wick drains, and piezocone evaluations, the term cℎ is used to represent flow in the horizontal direction. Herein, the nomenclature $c_{vh}$ will be used so as to not distinguish any particular direction of flow, since drainage occurs in three dimensions away from the cone tip. Moreover, in many natural marine clay deposits, there is little difference in the permeability anisotropy, i.e., kh ≈ kv (see later section and Table 8.1).

其中 k是渗透系数，D′是约束模量，$\gamma_w$是水的单位重度。

从实验室固结试验中评估固结系数cv涉及使用一个时间因子，该因子依赖于固结度以及排水路径（Holtz 等，2011）。

在实验室固结试验中，固结系数被指定为cv，与水通过土壤样本的垂直流动有关。相反，在桩基础设计、引水排水设计和静力触探评估中，使用术语 ch来表示水平流动。本文中将使用术语$c_{vh}$以避免区分任何特定方向的流动，因为排水是在锥尖的三维方向上进行的。此外，在许多天然海洋粘土沉积物中，渗透各向异性差异很小，即 kh≈kv（参见后续章节和表8.1）。

For the evaluation of $c_{vh}$ from CPTU dissipation tests, there exist multiple interpretative approaches based in theory (cavity expansion, dislocation point), numerical simulations (finite elements, strain path method), and empirical methods. A number of available procedures are discussed by Jamiolkowski et al. (1985) and Burns & Mayne (1998a, 1998b). Herein, two methods will be reviewed:

• Strain Path Method (SPM) for monotonic decay of $\Delta u$ with time (Houlsby & Teh 1988)

• An analytical Spherical Cavity Expansion-Critical State Soil Mechanics (SCE-CSSM) solution for both monotonic and dilatory porewater pressure response (Burns and Mayne 2002).

对于通过CPTU（静力触探测试）耗散试验评估 $c_{vh}$，存在多种基于理论（如小孔扩张、位移点）、数值模拟（如有限元、应变路径法）和经验方法的解释性方法。Jamiolkowski等（1985）和Burns & Mayne（1998a, 1998b）讨论了一些现有的程序。本文将回顾以下两种方法：

•应变路径法（SPM）：用于单调衰减的 $\Delta u$随时间变化（Houlsby & Teh 1988）。

•解析球形孔洞扩展-临界状态土力学（SCE-CSSM） 解：适用于单调和扩张性孔隙水压力响应（Burns and Mayne 2002）。

### 8.1.1 Dissipation Evaluation from Strain Path Method

8.1.1 根据应力路径方法的消散评估

For the strain path method (SPM) solution by Houlsby & Teh (1988), theoretical time factors (T*) for different degrees of monotonic porewater pressure dissipation were evaluated at several key points on a penetrometer (e.g, apex, mid-face, shoulder, upper shaft above sleeve). Using the characteristic dissipation time ($t_{50}$), the value of $c_{vh}$ is calculated from:

$$c_{vh} = \frac{T_{50}^* \cdot (a_c)^2 \cdot \sqrt{I_R}}{t_{50}} \tag{8.2}$$

where the time factors for 50% consolidation are $T_{50}$ ∗ = 0.118 and 0.245, respectively, for type 1 (midface) and type 2 filter elements (shoulder position), $a_c$ = probe radius (= 1.78 cm for a 10-cm2 cone and 2.19 for a 15-cm2 size), and $I_R$ = G/$s_u$ = undrained rigidity index, where G = shear modulus and $s_u$ = undrained shear strength.

The solution for type 2 piezocones at 50% consolidation is depicted graphically in Figure 8.4. If the operational value of rigidity index is unknown, a common default value is taken as $I_r$ = 100.

Prior Section 7.7 provides some guidance on the selection of $I_R$.

对于Houlsby & Teh（1988）提出的应变路径法（SPM）解，理论时间因子（T∗）在几个关键点上（例如：锥尖、锥体中面、肩部、上部杆件以上的套筒）被评估，以适应不同程度的单调孔隙水压力耗散。使用特征耗散时间（$t_{50}$），$c_{vh}$的值通过以下公式计算：

其中，50%固结的时间因子分别为 T50∗=0.118和 0.245，适用于类型1（中面）和类型2（肩部位置）过滤元件，ac为探头半径（对于10 cm²锥体为1.78 cm，对于15 cm²锥体为2.19 cm），$I_R$为刚度指数（G/su），其中 G为剪切模量，su为无排水剪切强度。

图8.4 以图形方式描绘了类型2 piezocones 在50%固结时的解。如果操作中刚度指数的值未知，则通常取默认值 $I_R$=100。

之前的第7.7节提供了选择 $I_R$的一些指导。

![image](images/rId281.png)

*Figure 8.4: Chart solution for evaluating $c_{vh}$ from dissipation tests at 50% consolidation using strain path method (after Houlsby & Teh 1988)*

If desired, degrees of consolidation other than 50% can be handled by this method (e.g., 30%, 80%, etc) as detailed by Teh and Houlsby (1991). In fact, a complete matching of the entire measured porewater pressure dissipation curves can be handled by utilizing algorithms that provide all degrees of consolidation (Mayne 2007b; DeJong and Randolph 2012). As such, the normalized excess porewater pressures ($\Delta u$/$\Delta u_i$) are presented as a function of modified time factor T ∗ in Figure 8.5.

如果需要，本方法可以处理除50%固结之外的固结度（例如30%、80%等），如Teh和Houlsby（1991）所详细说明。实际上，通过利用提供所有固结度的算法，可以完全匹配整个测得的孔隙水压力耗散曲线（Mayne 2007b；DeJong 和 Randolph 2012）。因此，标准化的超额孔隙水压力（$\Delta u$/$\Delta u$i）作为修改后的时间因子 T∗的函数，展示在图8.5中。

![image](images/rId282.png)

*Figure 8.5: Strain path solution for type 2 dissipation at any degree of consolidation (after Teh and Houlsby 1991)*

An algorithm that fits approximately the SPM solution over most of the range of values is:

在大多数值范围内近似拟合SPM解的算法是：

$$\frac{\Delta u}{\Delta u_i} = \frac{1}{1 + (4T^*)^{0.75}} \tag{8.3}$$

where the modified time factor (T*) for any degree of consolidation is expressed:

其中，任何固结程度的修正时间系数（T*）表示为：

$$T^* = \frac{c_{vh} \cdot t}{(a_c)^2 \cdot \sqrt{I_R}} \tag{8.4}$$

### 8.1.2 Dissipation Evaluation from SCE-CSSM Solution

### 8.1.2 根据SCE-CSSM解的消散评估

A hybrid analytical solution for piezocone dissipation has been derived from spherical cavity expansion theory and critical state soil mechanics (SCE-CSSM), as presented by Burns and Mayne (1998a, 2002). This approach can handle both monotonic and dilatory porewater dissipation responses. An example fitting of the SCE-CSSM solution is presented in Figure 8.6 for measured dissipations at a depth of 15.6 m in soft silty clay at Sandpoint, Idaho. This shows a monotonic response and iterative procedure to fit the curve.

一种混合分析解法用于处理孔隙水压力耗散，基于球形小孔扩张理论和临界状态土壤力学（SCE-CSSM），由Burns和Mayne（1998a, 2002）提出。这种方法可以处理单调和缓慢孔隙水耗散响应。图8.6展示了SCE-CSSM解法的一个例子，适用于在爱达荷州Sandpoint深度15.6米处测得的软质粉质粘土中的耗散情况。该图展示了一个单调响应和拟合曲线的迭代过程。

![image](images/rId285.png)

*Figure 8.6: Fitting procedure for obtaining $c_{vh}$ from monotonic type dissipation response in soft silty clay at Sandpoint, Idaho*

Whereas monotonic decays always decrease with time after penetration stops, dilatory response involves an initial increase of porewater pressures to a peak value, followed by a subsequent decrease with time. An example of a dilatory $u_2$ dissipation in Chalco Lake clay in Mexico is presented in Figure 8.7. This makes the determination of $t_{50}$ problematic since it is unclear whether to use the initial $u_2$ value (penetration reading), or the latter peak $u_2$ reading that occurs during dilatory dissipation. (P.S. It turns out neither is correct).

尽管单调衰减在穿透停止后总是随着时间的推移而减少，缓慢响应则涉及孔隙水压力的初始增加至峰值，随后随着时间的推移而减少。图8.7展示了在墨西哥Chalco Lake粘土中缓慢的 $u_2$耗散示例。这使得确定 $t_{50}$变得困难，因为不清楚是应使用初始的 $u_2$值（穿透读数），还是在缓慢耗散过程中出现的峰值 $u_2$读数。（顺便说一下，结果是两者都不正确）。

![image](images/rId286.png)

*Figure 8.7: Dilatory porewater pressure response and fitted curve for Chalco Lake clay, Mexico (Cruz and Mayne 2006)*

Spherical cavity expansion theory states that the large soil mass involved in the dissipation (represented by a ball of diameter D) is related to the size of the intruding penetrometer (diameter d) in the ratio D/d is equal to the cube root of the rigidity index:

球形空洞扩张理论表明，参与耗散的大土壤质量（由直径为D的球体表示）与侵入的贯入仪（直径为d）的尺寸有关，D/d的比等于刚度指数的立方根。

$$\frac{D}{d} = \sqrt[3]{I_r} \tag{8.5}$$

Since the rigorous solution involves a second order partial differential solution that depends on several input parameters ($\phi$, OCR, $I_r$, $\Lambda$, and $c_{vh}$), a set of graphical results have been presented (Burns and Mayne 1998a, 1998b). One set of examples is presented in Figure 8.8 for the case ($\phi'$ = 30°, $\Lambda$ = 0.8, and $I_R$ = 200) over a range of OCRs from 1 to 100, with normalized excess porewater pressures ($\Delta u$/$\Delta u_i$ ) versus a theoretical time factor (T) defined by:

由于严格解涉及一个二阶偏微分方程解，这个解依赖于多个输入参数（$\phi$、OCR、$I_r$、$\Lambda$ 和 $c_{vh}$），因此提供了一组图形结果（Burns 和 Mayne 1998a, 1998b）。图8.8展示了一组示例，情况为（$\phi'$ = 30°，$\Lambda$ = 0.8 和 $I_R$ = 200），在OCR从1到100的范围内，绘制了归一化的超孔隙水压力（$\Delta u$/$\Delta u_i$）与一个理论时间因子（T）的关系。

![image](images/rId288.png)

*Figure 8.8: Normalized porewater pressures vs. time factor from SCE-CSSM solution (after Burns and Mayne 1998a; 1998b; and Mayne 2001)*

where t = time and ac = radius of penetrometer.

其中t=时间，ac=贯入仪的半径。

$$T = \frac{c_{vh} \cdot t}{a_c^2} \tag{8.6}$$

### 8.1.3 Approximate SCE-CSSM Solution

### 8.1.3 SCE-CSSM近似解

An approximate SCE-CSSM method has been developed for general spreadsheet use (Mayne 2001). As seen by Figure 8.7, the approximate approach compares well with the rigorous solution.

The measured excess porewater pressures are the sum of an octahedral component (SCE) plus a shear-induced component (CSSM). For a type 2 piezocone, the $\Delta u_2$ values during penetration can be represented by:

一种用于通用电子表格的近似SCE-CSSM方法已经被开发（Mayne 2001）。如图8.7所示，近似方法与严格解的比较效果很好。

测得的超孔隙水压力是八面体成分（SCE）和剪切诱导成分（CSSM）的总和。对于类型2的孔压锥，在贯入过程中，$\Delta u_2$值可以表示为：

$$\Delta u_2 = (\Delta u_{oct})_i + (\Delta u_{shear})_i \tag{8.7}$$

where the components can be calculated from:

其中分量可以从以下公式计算：

$$(\Delta u_{oct})_i = \left(\frac{2M_c}{3}\right)\left(\frac{OCR}{2}\right)^{\!\Lambda} \ln(I_r)\,\sigma'_{v0} \tag{8.8}$$

$$(\Delta u_{shear})_i = \left(1 - \left(\frac{OCR}{2}\right)^{\!\Lambda}\right)\sigma'_{v0} \tag{8.9}$$

To unify the solutions for a range of $I_r$ values, a modified time factor (T’) for the SCE-CSSM model is introduced as:

为了统一一系列$I_r$值的解，引入了SCE-CSSM模型的修改时间因子（T'），如下所示：

$$T' = \frac{c_{vh} \cdot t}{a_c^2 \cdot I_r^{0.75}} \tag{8.10}$$

where t = elapsed time after penetration is halted.

The two porewater pressure components are dissipated at different rates with time:

其中t=贯入停止后经过的时间。

两个孔隙水压力分量随时间以不同的速率消散：

$$(\Delta u_2)_t = \frac{(\Delta u_{oct})_i}{1 + 50\,T'} + \frac{(\Delta u_{shear})_i}{1 + \left(\dfrac{326}{x_s}\right)^{\!2}\!T'} \tag{8.11}$$

where $x_s$ = thickness of the shear zone in mm (usually 1 mm < $x_s$ < 6 mm). The value of $c_{vh}$ is found by trial-and-error fitting of the analytical curves to the measured dissipation data.

An example fitting is shown in Figure 8.9 for dilatory dissipation data obtained in very hard clay from Taranto, Italy (Pane et al. 1995). Reported lab reference values for this site were in the range of 0.10 to 0.25 mm²/s which are compatible with the piezocone interpretations.

其中 $x_s$ =剪切带的厚度，单位为毫米（通常 1 毫米 < $x_s$ < 6 毫米）。$c_{vh}$ 的值是通过试错法将分析曲线拟合到测得的消散数据来确定的。

图8.9展示了一个拟合示例，该示例使用了来自意大利Taranto的非常坚硬的粘土中的延迟消散数据（Pane 等，1995）。该地点报告的实验室参考值在 0.10 到 0.25 mm²/s 范围内，这与孔压锥的解释结果相符。

### 8.1.4 Simplified SCE-CSSM Solution for Monotonic Dissipations

8.1.4 单调消散的简化SCE-CSSM解

For clays that show a simple monotonic porewater pressure response, the SCE-CSSM solution resolves to a simpler expression for $u_2$ dissipations given by:

对于那些显示出简单单调孔隙水压力响应的粘土，SCE-CSSM 解简化为以下的 $u_2$ 消散的表达式：

$$c_{vh} = \frac{T'_{50} \cdot (a_R)^2 \cdot (I_R)^{0.75}}{t_{50}} \tag{8.12}$$

where the modified time factor at 50% consolidation is $T'_{50}$ = 0.028. This solution can also be used for equivalent monotonic dissipations after adjusting the dilatory response curves.

其中，50%固结时的修正时间因子为 $T'_{50}$ = 0.028。这个解也可以用于等效的单调消散，只需调整延迟响应曲线即可。

![image](images/rId295.png)

*Figure 8.9: Measured and fitted dilatory dissipations from hard Taranto clay at 9.1 m depth using SCE-CSSM solution*

### 8.1.5 Case Study: Sandpoint

### 8.1.5 案例研究：Sandpoint

Figure 8.10 shows the overall summary profile of $c_{vh}$ in soft clay at Sandpoint, Idaho, as determined from standard laboratory consolidation tests. The measured $t_{50}$ values from several series of CPTU dissipation tests are presented in the left portion of Figure 8.10. From Section 7.7.1, the undrained rigidity index from the SCE-CSSM solution gave 187 which is used to evaluate the $c_{vh}$ from the CPTU dissipation data per equation (8.12). The CPTU $c_{vh}$ values appear slightly higher than the lab reference values. Note carefully that the soil profile at Sandpoint contains many enumerable sandy lenses, as well as several sand layers (reference CPTU in prior Figure 5.13). Since the lab consolidation data represent results only from the soft clay matrix, the piezocone interpretations likely reflect the clay layer plus included sand lenses and sand stringers.

图8.10 显示了在爱达荷州 Sandpoint 的软粘土中，经过标准实验室固结测试确定的 $c_{vh}$ 的总体总结概况。图8.10 左侧部分展示了从多个 CPTU 消散测试系列中测得的 $t_{50}$ 值。从第7.7.1节可知，SCE-CSSM 解得出的不排水刚度指数为 187，用于根据方程 (8.12) 评估 CPTU 消散数据中的 $c_{vh}$。CPTU的 $c_{vh}$ 值看起来稍高于实验室参考值。请注意，Sandpoint 的土层包含许多可枚举的砂质透镜体以及几个砂层（参考图5.13中的 CPTU）。由于实验室固结数据仅代表软粘土基质的结果，因此孔压锥的解译可能反映了粘土层以及其中包含的砂质透镜体和砂带。

![image](images/rId296.png)

*Figure 8.10: Summary of dissipation data and interpreted $c_{vh}$ profile at Sandpoint, Idaho*

### 8.1.6 Case Study: Gloucester, Ontario

### 8.1.6 案例研究：安大略省Gloucester

A second example is afforded from the series of dissipations shown in prior Figure 8.1 for the Gloucester test site. The reported $t_{50}$ values for the entire profile are given by McQueen et al. (2016). The derived $I_R$ = 96 from the CPTU sounding (Agaiby & Mayne 2016). Using equation (8.12), Figure 8.11 shows a reasonable comparison of $c_{vh}$ values from laboratory and field measurements at the site.

第二个例子来自之前图8.1中展示的 Gloucester 测试场地的消散系列。McQueen 等人（2016）报告了整个剖面的 $t_{50}$ 值。通过 CPTU 测量得到的 $I_R$ = 96（Agaiby & Mayne 2016）。使用方程 (8.12)，图8.11 显示了实验室和现场测量的 $c_{vh}$ 值之间的合理比较。

![image](images/rId297.png)
