## 8.2 Adjusted Dilatory Dissipation Curves

## 8.2调整后的迟滞消散曲线

For dilatory responses, Sully et al. (1999) recommended several types of empirical adjustments to the measured dissipation records for interpretation. One of these procedures includes the plotting of porewater pressures on a square root time plot to assess a projected initial $u_2$ reading. This appears to be a reasonable approach to converting measured dilatory dissipations to an equivalent monotonic curve, thus allowing the assessment of $t_{50}$ values (e.g. Schneider & Hotstream 2010; Mahmoodzadeh and Randolph 2014).

对于迟滞响应，Sully 等人（1999）推荐了几种经验性调整方法，以解释测得的消散记录。其中一种方法是将孔隙水压力绘制在平方根时间图上，以评估预测的初始 $u_2$ 读数。这似乎是将测得的迟滞消散转换为等效单调曲线的合理方法，从而允许评估 $t_{50}$ 值（例如，Schneider & Hotstream 2010；Mahmoodzadeh 和 Randolph 2014）。

From a theoretical viewpoint, dilatory porewater response occurs because the $\Delta u$ shear-induced components are temporarily negative, but decay rather quickly because they only affect a thin annulus next to the penetrometer. The SCE-CSSM model can therefore be used with a "zero" thickness annulus to find the dissipations solely within the spherical cavity zone. Figure 8.12 shows a calculated family of dilatory dissipations for varying shear thickness zones, suggesting that the square-root of time plot is a valid approach.

从理论角度看，迟滞孔隙水响应发生是因为 $\Delta u$ 剪切引起的分量暂时为负，但由于它们仅影响靠近贯入仪的薄环区域，因此衰减较快。SCE-CSSM 模型则可以使用“零”厚度的环来计算仅在球形空腔区内的消散。图8.12 显示了不同剪切厚度区域的迟滞消散计算结果，表明平方根时间图是一种有效的方法。

![image](images/rId298.png)

*Figure 8.12: Family of theoretical dilatory dissipations for varying shear thickness zones*

The procedure for adjusting the dilatory dissipation curve is to pick a point on the post-peak $u_2$ response and project the data back to a time t = 0 on the square root of time plot. The concept is depicted in Figure 8.13.

Figure 8.14 shows an application of the adjusted dissipation curve procedure resulting in a $t_{50}$ of 14.4 minutes. Using the simplified method of Equation 8.12 with same chosen $I_r$ = 32, the derived $c_{vh}$ = 0.15 mm²/s compares favorably with that using the more involved and rigorous calculations given previously in Figure 8.8 where a value of $c_{vh}$ = 0.10 mm²/s was obtained. Laboratory reference values for the coefficient of consolidation of the hard Taranto clay are found in the range: 0.10 mm²/s ≤ $c_{vh}$ ≤ 0.25 mm²/s (Bruzzi & Battaglio 1987; Burns & Mayne 1998a, 1998b).

调整迟滞消散曲线的过程是选择一个峰后 $u_2$ 响应的点，并将数据投影回时间 t = 0 的平方根时间图上。该概念在图 8.13 中展示。

图 8.14 显示了调整后的消散曲线应用结果，得出的 $t_{50}$ 为 14.4 分钟。使用简化的方程 8.12 方法，并选择相同的 $I_r$ = 32，得出的 $c_{vh}$ = 0.15 mm²/s 与图 8.8 中使用的更复杂且严格的计算结果相符，其中得到的 $c_{vh}$ = 0.10 mm²/s。硬质 Taranto 粘土的固结系数的实验室参考值范围为：0.10 mm²/s ≤ $c_{vh}$ ≤ 0.25 mm²/s（Bruzzi & Battaglio 1987；Burns & Mayne 1998a, 1998b）。

![image](images/rId299.png)

*Figure 8.13: Recommended procedure for obtaining the "true" initial porewater pressure and characteristic $t_{50}$ from normalized $u_2$ readings versus square root time plot*

![image](images/rId300.png)

*Figure 8.14: Adjusted dilatory dissipation curve procedure applied to Taranto clay*

## 8.3 Permeability

## 8.3 渗透性

Soil permeability quantifies the flow rate of water in-situ under differential pore pressures. This is different than the coefficient of consolidation, which balances the soil-deformation against porewater pressure dissipation. Soil permeability (k) is also called hydraulic conductivity or the coefficient of permeability, and is in units of m/s. It is related to the more fundamental intrinsic permeability (K) or absolute permeability, which is given in units of m², by the relationship:

土壤渗透性量化了在差异孔隙压力下水在原位的流动速率。这与固结系数不同，后者平衡土壤变形与孔隙水压力消散。土壤渗透率（k）也称为水力传导系数或渗透系数，单位为 m/s。它与更基本的固有渗透率（K）或绝对渗透率相关，后者的单位为 m²，二者之间的关系为：

$$k = K \left( \frac{\gamma_f}{\mu_f} \right) \tag{8.13}$$

where $\gamma_f$ = unit weight of the fluid and $\mu_f$ = viscosity of the fluid.

其中 $\gamma_f$ = 流体的单位重，$\mu_f$ = 流体的粘度。

### 8.3.1 Soil Permeability Anisotropy

### 8.3.1 土的各向异性渗透性

Adapting the approach by Leroueil et al. (1991) and Leroueil & Jamiolkowski (1991), a guideline to geological situations governing permeability anisotropy is given in Table 8.1.

根据 Leroueil 等（1991）和 Leroueil & Jamiolkowski（1991）的方法，表 8.1 提供了控制渗透性各向异性的地质情况指导。

**Table 8.1: Permeability anisotropy in natural soils (adapted after Leroueil et al. 1991)**

| Nature of the formation | Ratio $k_h / k_v$ |
| ----------------------- | ---------------- |
| Homogeneous clays of marine, alluvial, deltaic, and lacustrine origin | 1 to 1.5 |
| Sedimentary clays with discontinuous lenses and layers, well-developed macrofabric | 2 to 3 |
| Varved clays and silts with continuous permeable layers | 1.5 to 5 |
| Highly stratified soils with interbedded layers of clay, silt, sand, and/or gravel | 2 to 10 |

For the majority of natural clays, the horizontal permeability is only around 10% to 20% higher than the vertical value (Mesri, Lo, and Feng 1994; Leroueil and Hight 2003). A summary of several special series of laboratory series of permeability tests on different natural soft clays is given in Figure 8.15 whereby both standard vertical measurements of hydraulic conductivity ($k_v$) are compared with horizontal values (kℎ) using radial permeameter devices. Generally, the ratio of kℎ/$k_v$ averages about 1.1 for intact clays of marine and water-borne origins.

For varved clays and highly stratified deposits, the ratio of horizontal to vertical permeabilities is higher and may range from 1.5 to 5 or more (Saxena, Hedberg, and Ladd 1978). Very rarely, kℎ/$k_v$ approaches 10 in the case of highly stratified deposits and formations.

对于大多数自然粘土，水平渗透性通常比垂直渗透性高出约 10% 到 20%（Mesri, Lo, 和 Feng 1994；Leroueil 和 Hight 2003）。图 8.15 总结了对不同自然软粘土进行的一系列特殊实验室渗透性测试，其中比较了使用径向渗透仪测得的标准垂直水力导度（$k_v$）与水平值（kℎ）。一般而言，对于完整的海洋和水源粘土，kℎ/$k_v$ 的比值平均约为 1.1。

对于层理粘土和高度分层的沉积物，水平渗透性与垂直渗透性的比值较高，可能在 1.5 到 5 或更高（Saxena, Hedberg, 和 Ladd 1978）。在高度分层的沉积物和构造中，kℎ/$k_v$ 比值极少接近 10。

![image](images/rId303.png)

*Figure 8.15: Comparing horizontal to vertical permeability at different sites*

### 8.3.2 Soil Permeability from Dissipation Tests

### 8.3.2 根据消散试验获得的土壤渗透性

A simple empirical method for estimating the coefficient of soil permeability from piezocone dissipation tests with 10 cm² cone probes was presented by Parez and Fauriel (1988), as shown in Figure 8.16. The various wedge regions for several soil types were obtained from available data on normally consolidated deposits. Taking a line through the centers of these wedges gives an approximate trend:

Parez 和 Fauriel（1988）提出了一种简单的经验方法，通过使用 10 cm² 锥形探头的孔隙水压力消散测试来估算土壤渗透系数，如图 8.16 所示。针对几种土壤类型的不同楔形区域是通过可用的正常固结沉积物数据获得的。将这些楔形区域的中心连成一线，可以得到一个大致的趋势：

$$k \text{(cm/sec)} \approx (251 \cdot t_{50})^{-1.25} \tag{8.14}$$

where the time $t_{50}$ is specified in seconds.

其中时间$t_{50}$以秒为单位。

![image](images/rId305.png)

*Figure 8.16: Empirical trend for hydraulic conductivity versus dissipation time $t_{50}$ for 10-cm² penetrometers (after Parez and Fauriel 1988)*

### 8.3.3 Permeability from Soil Behavior Type

### 8.3.3 根据土壤表现类型的渗透性

Lunne et al. (1997) assigned estimated values and ranges of soil permeability for each of the 9 zones in the normalized soil behavioral charts (SBTn). Using these as a guide, Robertson and Cabal (2010) provided the following algorithms that relate hydraulic conductivity (k) in terms of the CPT material index ($I_c$ ) for soil zones 2 through 7:

Lunne 等人（1997）为标准化土壤表现类型图（SBTn）中的 9 个区域分配了土壤渗透性估计值和范围。以这些数据为指导，Robertson 和 Cabal（2010）提供了以下算法，用于将水力导度（k）与土壤区段 2 到 7 的 CPT 材料指数（$I_c$）关联起来：

$$k \text{(m/s)} = 10^{0.952 - 3.04 I_c} \quad;\quad 1.0 < I_c \leq 3.27 \tag{8.15}$$

$$k \text{(m/s)} = 10^{-4.52 - 1.37 I_c} \quad;\quad 3.27 < I_c \leq 4.0 \tag{8.16}$$

In lieu of two linear expressions, a continuous approximation can be fitted to the selected reference values by:

代替两个线性表达式，可以通过以下方法将连续的近似值拟合到所选的参考值：

$$k \text{(m/s)} \approx (I_{cRW})^{-18} \tag{8.17}$$

as shown in Figure 8.17

如图8.17所示。

![image](images/rId308.png)

*Figure 8.17: Hydraulic conductivity estimated from CPT material index (modified after Robertson and Cabal 2010)*

## 8.4 Case Study: Bothkennar, UK

## 8.4 案例研究：英国Bothkennar

Using the two aforementioned approaches, results from CPTU soundings and dissipation tests were used to provide evaluations of soil permeability (k) at the Bothkennar soft clay research site in Scotland (Nash, Powell, and Lloyd 1992; Nash, Sills, and Davison 1992). Dissipation tests are reported by Jacobs and Coutts (1992) for both $u_1$ and $u_2$ piezocones. Where necessary, equivalent values of $t_{50}$ were made for the $u_2$ position from $u_1$ data based on site-specific correlations. Several independent measurements of permeability were made by laboratory triaxial, oedometer, and flow tests, as well as by field methods (Leroueil et al. 1992).

Overall, reasonable agreement in seen for these measurements in comparison with the two simple CPTU methods discussed: (a) $t_{50}$ value in the Parez & Fauriel (1988) trend; and (b) CPT material index Ic method, as seen in Figure 8.18.

利用上述两种方法，CPTU 测量和消散测试的结果被用于评估苏格兰 Bothkennar 软粘土研究的土壤渗透性（k）（Nash, Powell 和 Lloyd 1992；Nash, Sills 和 Davison 1992）。Jacobs 和 Coutts（1992）报告了孔压锥的$u_1$ 和 $u_2$消散测试结果。如有必要，可以基于特定地点的相关性将 $u_1$ 数据转换为 $u_2$ 位置的等效 $t_{50}$ 值。实验室的三轴试验、沉降仪测试和流动测试，以及现场方法（Leroueil 等 1992）提供了若干独立的渗透性测量值。

总体而言，这些测量值与讨论的两种简单 CPTU 方法相比，表现出较好的一致性：（a）Parez 和 Fauriel（1988）趋势中的 $t_{50}$ 值；（b）CPT 材料指数 $I_c$ 方法，如图 8.18 所示。

![image](images/rId309.png)

*Figure 8.18: Comparison of laboratory and field measurements of hydraulic conductivity in soft clay at Bothkennar test site, Scotland*

# 9 Liquefaction Evaluation by CPT

9 通过CPT进行液化评估

Liquefaction is the phenomenon that occurs when soils lose strength and behave as a liquid. This occurs when loading of the ground increases porewater pressures and reduces the effective stress state in the soil. This manifests in ground surface instabilities that cause the ejection of watery sands, subsidence, sand boils, settlement, tilting, bearing capacity failure, lateral spreading, cracking, and other events. Figure 9.1 shows some examples of the destructive consequences following several recent earthquake events that occurred following soil liquefaction.

液化现象是指土体在失去强度后表现为类似液体的行为。这种现象发生在地基加载导致孔隙水压力增加、土体有效应力减少的情况下。这种现象在地表的不稳定表现为含水砂的喷出、地面下沉、砂沸现象、沉降、倾斜、承载力破坏、侧向扩展、裂缝等现象。图9.1展示了近年来几次地震事件中因土体液化而导致的破坏性后果。

![image](images/rId310.png)

*Figure 9.1: Recent earthquake events exhibiting soil liquefaction and ground damage*

图9.1：展示土体液化及地面损害的近期地震事件

Soil liquefaction most often occurs in loose sandy soils that have a high groundwater condition, although the process can also happen in silty and gravelly soils. Details concerning the background, understanding, and development of liquefaction behavior can be found in Idriss & Boulanger (2008).

土体液化最常发生在松散的砂土中，特别是地下水位较高的地区，尽管这一过程也可能在粉土和砾石土中发生。关于液化行为的背景、理解和发展细节，可以参考Idriss和Boulanger（2008）的研究。

There are two main categories of liquefaction: (a) Flow (or static) liquefaction; and (b) Cyclic liquefaction.

液化主要分为两类：（a）流动（或静态）液化；（b）循环液化。

Flow liquefaction manifests as a rapid and brittle undrained loss of soil strength that occurs in sloping terrain, including earth embankments, mine tailings dams, hydraulic fills, and natural sandy to silty soil deposits. In these cases, the soil is already existing in an unstable state and a triggering mechanism (increased load or overburden, sudden groundwater change, blast event, etc.) results in a landslide, slope instability, or even a catastrophic flow failure, such as the recent Brumadinho tailings event in 2019 that resulted in 270 deaths and widespread environmental contamination in Brazil.

流动液化表现为在坡地上土体强度的快速且脆弱的非排水性丧失，包括土坝、尾矿坝、水力填筑和天然砂质至粉质土体沉积物。在这些情况下，土体本身已处于不稳定状态，一个触发机制（如增加的荷载或上覆重力、突发的地下水位变化、爆炸事件等）会导致滑坡、坡体不稳定，甚至灾难性的流动破坏，例如2019年发生在巴西的布鲁马迪纽尾矿坝事故，导致了270人死亡和广泛的环境污染。

Cyclic liquefaction can occur during medium to large size earthquakes when the ground is subjected to shaking and levels of repeated loading that cause elevated porewater pressures in the soil which reduces the effective stress state. This can happen in flat horizontal ground as well as sloping terrain. Large earthquakes of moment magnitude Mw ≈ 7.5 occurred in 2010 and 2012 in New Zealand that resulted in extensive liquefaction around Christchurch. Typical manifestations of liquefied soils are exhibited by sand boils, flowing sands, subsidence, lateral spread, and bearing capacity failures of buildings. This chapter concerns the use of CPT for evaluating liquefaction potential in soils.

循环液化可能发生在中至大规模的地震期间，当地面受到震动和重复荷载时，会导致土体孔隙水压力升高，进而减小有效应力。这种情况可以发生在平坦的地面上，也可以发生在坡地上。2010年和2012年新西兰发生的矩震级Mw ≈7.5的大地震导致了克赖斯特彻奇周围大面积的液化现象。典型的表现包括建筑物的水平扩展和承载力失效。本章涉及静力触探（CPT）在评估土体液化潜力中的应用。

## 9.1 Flow Liquefaction

9.1 流动液化

Static or flow liquefaction most often occurs in saturated contractive soils on sloping ground and may be triggered under static conditions such as a rise in the phreatic surface or water infiltration following a heavy rainfall. Flow liquefaction may also be triggered by transient earthquake loading.

Soils prone to flow liquefaction are characterized by their “contractive” soil behavior, whereby volumetric strains decrease during shear, in contrast to “dilative” soil response, whereby volumetric strains increase. Moreover, these soils tend to be brittle and exhibit a rapid loss of strength at low strain levels, resulting in rapid and progressive flow failures. The identification of soil conditions susceptible to flow liquefaction can be made on the basis of conventional rotary drilling, high quality sampling, and careful laboratory testing, combined with in-situ testing and geophysical measurements; however, at great cost in terms of time and money (Robertson et al., 2000). This is especially true for sands, silty sands, and silts because undisturbed sampling methods (e.g., freezing, gel sampling) are quite difficult and expensive. As an alternative, the use of in-situ tests offers the expedient and economic assessment of flow liquefaction potential, particularly in mine tailings where screening is often used during all stages of dam construction. Of practical use, the cone penetration test (CPT), especially piezocone testing (CPTU), offers three continuous recordings of soil response with depth: cone tip resistance (qt), sleeve friction (fs), and dynamic porewater pressure (u_2), thus well suited for the identification and screening of flow liquefaction problems. Additional data, including shear and compression wave velocities, can be obtained during seismic cone tests (SCPTu).

静态或流动液化通常发生在饱和的收缩性土体上，且多见于坡地，可能在静态条件下触发，例如潜水面上升或大雨后水的渗入。流动液化也可能由地震的瞬态加载触发。易于发生流动液化的土体以其“收缩性”土体行为为特征，即在剪切过程中体积应变减少，与之相对的是“膨胀性”土体响应，即体积应变增加。此外，这些土体往往表现为脆性，并在低应变水平下快速丧失强度，导致快速且渐进的流动破坏。识别易于流动液化的土体条件可以通过常规旋转钻探、高质量取样和精确的实验室测试结合原位测试和地球物理测量来进行，然而这在时间和成本上代价不菲（Robertson等，2000）。特别是对于砂土、粉砂土而言，原状取样方法（如冷冻取样、凝胶取样）非常困难且昂贵。作为替代，使用原位测试提供了快速且经济的流动液化潜力评估，特别是在尾矿坝中，筛选通常在大坝建设的所有阶段进行。实际上，静力触探（CPT），特别是孔压静力触探（CPTU），提供了随深度连续记录的三个土体响应参数：锥尖阻力（qt）、侧壁摩阻力（fs）和动态孔隙水压力（u_2），因此非常适合于流动液化问题的识别和筛选。在地震锥测试（SCPTu）期间，还可以获取剪切波和压缩波速度等附加数据。

### 9.1.1 State Parameter

9.1.1 状态参数

Current methods for the screening and evaluation of flow liquefaction potential of tailings, hydraulic fills, and natural loose soil deposits rely on an evaluation of the state parameter ($\psi$) defined as (Been & Jefferies 1985):

目前用于筛选和评估尾矿坝、水力填筑和天然松散土体沉积物的流动液化潜力的方法依赖于状态参数（ψ）的评估，该参数定义为（Been & Jefferies 1985）：

$$\psi = e_0 - e_{cs} \tag{9.1}$$

where $e_0$ = initial void ratio and $e_{cs}$ = void ratio at critical state for a constant mean effective stress p’ within the context of critical-state soil mechanics (CSSM), as presented in Figure 9.2.

其中e0为初始孔隙比，ecs为在临界状态下的孔隙比，针对在临界状态土力学（CSSM）上下文中某一常数有效平均应力p'，如图9.2所示。

![image](images/rId311.png)

*Figure 9.2: Representation of critical state parameter $\psi$ in graph of void ratio versus logarithm of mean effective stress*

图9.2：孔隙比与有效平均应力对数的图表中临界状态参数ψ的表示

The critical state line (CSL) is conventionally represented as a straight line in e-lnₑ(p’) plot by slope $\lambda$, or alternatively in a plot of void ratio (e) versus log_1_0(p’) plot by slope $\lambda$_1_0, but also as a curved relationship in a power law format, as detailed by Reid et al. (2020). When the soil behavior shows a decrease in volume, such as loose sands and silts, the contractive response is indicative of possible instability and collapse, thus prone to flow liquefaction. In contrast, dense soils exhibit an increase in volume termed dilatant or dilative response and considered not susceptible to flow liquefaction. As such, a value of $\psi$ = 0 signifies the CSSM threshold for the contractive-dilatant boundary.

临界状态线（CSL）通常在e-p'图中以斜率λ表示为一条直线，或者在孔隙比（e）与log10 (p')图中以斜率λ_10表示，但也可以以幂律格式作为曲线关系，如Reid等（2020）所述。当土体行为表现出体积减小时，例如松散砂土和粉土，收缩性响应表明可能的不稳定和坍塌，因此容易发生流动液化。相比之下，密实土体表现为体积增加，即膨胀性或膨胀响应，被认为不易发生流动液化。因此，ψ=0的值标志着收缩-膨胀边界的CSSM阈值。

### 9.1.2 CPT Parameters

9.1.2 CPT参数

The three readings (qt, fs, u_2) obtained by CPTU are utilized to evaluate soil stratigraphy, soil behavioral types, and a suite of geoparameters that are needed in engineering analyses and design. The measurements are often post-processed into net readings, such as net cone resistance (qnet = qt - $\sigma_{vo}$), excess porewater pressure ($\Delta u$ = u_2 - u_0), and effective cone resistance (qE = qt - u_2), and also utilized in terms of normalized and dimensionless parameters, including: Q = qnet/$\sigma_{vo}'$, U = $\Delta u$/$\sigma_{vo}'$, Bq = $\Delta u$/qnet, and F = 100∙fs/qnet (%), where $\sigma_{vo}$ = total overburden stress, u_0 = in-situ equilibrium porewater pressure, and $\sigma_{vo}'$ = effective vertical stress. Note that the two porewater pressure parameters are related via: Bq = U/Q. Additional details can be found in Lunne, Robertson, & Powell (1997). An updated form of Q (also termed Qt and Qt_1) is the stress-normalized cone tip resistance, Qtn = (qnet/$\sigma_{atm}$)/($\sigma_{vo}'$/$\sigma_{atm}$)ⁿ where the exponent “n” is a variable that ranges from about 0.5 in sands to 0.75 for silts to 1.0 in clays, and has been directly related to the CPT material index, Ic (Robertson 2009b). In addition, the effective cone resistance can be normalized to QE = (qt-u_2)/$\sigma_{vo}'$ which is equivalent to QE = Q·(1-Bq)+1. This is used in the CPT soil classification system by Jefferies & Been (2015).

通过CPTU获得的三个读数（qt,fs,$u_2$）被用于评估土体分层、土体行为类型以及工程分析和设计中所需的一系列地质参数。这些测量结果通常被后处理为净读数，例如净锥阻力（qnet=qt-σvo），超孔隙水压力（Δu=$u_2$-$u_0$），以及有效锥阻力（qE=qt-$u_2$），还被用于规范化和无量纲化参数中，包括：Q=q_net/σ_vo'，U=Δu/σ_vo^'，Bq=Δu/q_net，和F=100·fs/q_net（%），其中σ_vo为总上覆应力，u_0为原位平衡孔隙水压力，σ_vo^'为有效竖向应力。需要注意的是，这两个孔隙水压力参数通过Bq=U/Q相关联。更多细节可以参考Lunne、Robertson和Powell（1997）。更新的Q形式（也称为Qt、Qt和Qr1）为应力规范化锥尖阻力，Qtn=（q_net/σ_atm）/（σ_vo^'/σ_atm）^n，其中指数“n”是一个变量，在砂土中约为0.5，在粉土中约为0.75，在粘土中约为1.0，并且直接与CPT材料指数Ic相关（Robertson 2009b）。此外，有效锥阻力可以规范化为QE=（qe-$u_2$）/σ_vo^'，这相当于Q_E=Q·（1-B_q）+1。这被Jefferies和Been（2015）用于CPT土体分类系统。

## 9.2 CPT Screening Methods for Flow Liquefaction

9.2 通过CPT筛选流动液化的方法

Herein, three methods for the screening of flow liquefaction potential by CPT are considered: (1) $\psi$ = -0.05 criterion (Jefferies & Been 2015); (2) Qtn,cs = 70 threshold, as described later in Section 2.2 (Robertson 2010a); and (3) yield stress ratio at critical-state, or YSRcsl ≈ 2.8 (Mayne & Sharp 2019).

本文考虑了三种通过CPT筛选流动液化潜力的方法：（1）ψ=-0.05标准（Jefferies & Been 2015）；（2）Qtn,cs=70阈值，如第2.2节（Robertson 2010a）中描述的；以及（3）在临界状态下的屈服应力比，或YSRcsl≈2.8（Mayne & Sharp 2019）。

Additional post processing of CPT for flow liquefaction concerns are discussed by Olson & Stark (2002, 2003), and Monfared & Sadrekarimi (2013) but not covered here.

有关流动液化问题的CPT后处理的附加内容在Olson & Stark（2002, 2003）和Monfared & Sadrekarimi（2013）的讨论中有所涉及，但不在本文讨论范围内。

### 9.2.1 State Parameter Approach

9.2.1 状态参数法

Been et al. (1986; 1987a) established a framework for assessing $\psi$ in sands that describes initial state, soil behavior, strength, and compressibility using laboratory testing and a number of intermediate geoparameters which were correlated to normalized CPT parameters (Q, Bq, F) from large scale chamber tests and laboratory triaxial results on reconstituted soil samples. Extensions to the method for silts and clays are described by Been et al. (2012b), Been (2016), and Jefferies & Been (2015). In the stand-alone CPT approach, the slope of the CSL is found either from normalized sleeve friction (Plewes et al. 1992):

Been等人（1986; 1987a）为砂土评估ψ建立了一个框架，该框架通过实验室测试和一系列中间地质参数描述了初始状态、土体行为、强度和压缩性，这些地质参数与大型室内试验和重构土样的三轴试验的规范化CPT参数（Q, Bq, F）进行了相关。该方法对粉土和粘土的扩展描述可以参见Been等人（2012b）、Been（2016）和Jefferies & Been（2015）。在独立的CPT方法中，CSL的斜率可以通过规范化的侧壁摩阻力（Plewes等，1992）找到：

$$\lambda_{10} \approx 0.1 \cdot F \;(\%) \tag{9.2}$$

or alternatively using their definition of CPT material index:

或者通过CPT材料指数定义的：

$$I_c^* = \sqrt{\left\{3 - \log\left[Q \cdot (1 - B_q) + 1\right]\right\}^2 + \left\{1.5 + 1.3 \log(F)\right\}^2} \tag{9.3}$$

which is related to slope of the CSL by the expression (Reid 2015):

这一指数与CSL的斜率通过以下表达式相关（Reid 2015）：

$$\lambda_{10} \approx 1/(34 - 10 \cdot I_c^*) \tag{9.4}$$

The state parameter $\psi$ is found from:

状态参数ψ可通过以下公式得到：

$$\psi = -\frac{1}{m'} \cdot \ln\left[Q_p \cdot (1 - B_q) + 1\right] \tag{9.5}$$

where Qp = (qt-p0)/p0’ = normalized cone resistance in terms of mean stress, p0’ = ⅓·$\sigma_{vo}$’·(1+2∙K0), with K0 = lateral stress coefficient. The terms k’ and m’ are empirical fitting parameters found through the following trends:

其中Qp=(q_t-p_0)/p_0^'为以平均应力表示的规范化锥阻力，po’,，K0为侧向应力系数。k'和m'为通过以下趋势找到的经验拟合参数：

$$m' = 11.9 - 13.3 \cdot \lambda_{10} \tag{9.6}$$

$$k' = M_c \cdot (3 + 0.85/\lambda_{10}) \tag{9.7}$$

where the friction parameter Mc = 6∙sinφ'/(3-sinφ') is found within the context of CSSM. For screening purposes, a value of Mc ≈ 1.20 (or φ' = 30°) is suggested.

其中摩擦参数Mc=6⋅sinϕ^'/(3-sinϕ^')，在CSSM上下文中找到。对于筛选目的，建议使用Mc≈1.20（或ϕ^'=30^∘）的值。

![image](images/rId312.png)

Friction Ratio, FR = fs/(qt - $\sigma_{vo}$)   (%)

Figure 9.3: Screening for contractive-dilative soils and flow liquefaction using state parameter $\psi$ approach by Jefferies & Been (2015)

图9.3：使用Jefferies & Been（2015）状态参数ψ法筛选收缩-膨胀性土体和流动液化

In lieu of the theoretical critical state threshold $\psi$ = 0, a practical value $\psi$ = -0.05 was adopted, thus contractive soils prone to flow liquefaction are identified when $\psi$ > -0.05. The general concept of screening for flow liquefaction via the state parameter approach is shown in Figure 9.3.

代替理论上的临界状态阈值ψ=0，采用了实际值ψ=-0.05，因此当ψ>-0.05时，被认为是易于发生流动液化的收缩性土体。图9.3展示了通过状态参数法筛选流动液化的概念。

### 9.2.2 Normalized Cone Resistance Approach

9.2.2 规范化锥阻力法

In this approach, the soil behavioral type is assessed by the SBT charts which use the Q-F diagrams of Robertson (1990, 1991) and Lunne et al.(1997).In an updated format detailed by Robertson (2009b),the Q is replaced by a modified cone tip resistance: Qtn, that was utilized in section 9.1.2. Specifically, the exponent “n” is found by iteration:

在这种方法中，通过使用Robertson（1990, 1991）和Lunne等人（1997）的Q-F图的SBT图表来评估土体行为类型。在Robertson（2009b）详细描述的更新版本中，Q被替换为修正的锥尖阻力：Qtn，用于9.1.2节。具体地，指数“n”通过迭代找到：

$$n = 0.381 \cdot I_{cRW} + 0.05 \cdot \sigma'_{vo}/\sigma_{atm} - 0.15 \quad \leq \quad 1.0 \tag{9.8}$$

where a modified CPT material index (lcRw) is given by:

其中修正的CPT材料指数（IcRw）由以下公式给出：

$$I_{cRW} = \sqrt{(3.47 - \log Q_{tn})^2 + (1.22 + \log F)^2} \tag{9.9}$$

Robertson (2010) defined regions within CPT soil behavior type charts to identify potential soil layers that may be susceptible to flow liquefaction and cyclic liquefaction, as well as define undrained versus drained response and contractive versus dilative behavior. Robertson (2010a,b) found that normalized cone resistance adjusted for fines content, designated (Qtn,cs), trended over a range of state parameter from 0.0 < $\psi$ < -0.20, such that:

Robertson（2010）在CPT土体行为类型图表中定义了可能易于发生流动液化和循环液化的潜在土层的区域，并定义了排水与不排水响应以及收缩与膨胀行为。Robertson（2010a,b）发现，针对细颗粒含量调整后的规范化锥阻力（Qtn,cs）与状态参数在0.0<ψ<-0.20的范围内变化，如下：

$$\psi = 0.56 - 0.33 \cdot \log_{10}(Q_{tn,cs}) \tag{9.10}$$

where Qtn,cs is the normalized equivalent cone resistance for clean sands. The adjustment factor is found from (Robertson&Wride 1998):

其中Qtn,cs=K_c⋅Q_tn，为清洁砂的规范化等效锥阻力。调整系数由（Robertson&Wride 1998）给出：

$$\text{For } I_c \leq 1.64: \quad K_c = 1 \tag{9.11a}$$

对于(9.11a)

$$\text{For } I_c > 1.64: \quad K_c = 5.581 \cdot I_c^3 - 0.403 \cdot I_c^4 - 21.63 \cdot I_c^2 + 33.75 \cdot I_c - 17.88 \tag{9.11b}$$

For the case $\psi$ = -0.05 at the contractive-dilative (CD) boundary, the associated value of Qtn,cs = 70 (Robertson 2009b; Robertson 2010a, b), as shown in Figure 9.4. Soils that exhibit a value of Qtn,cs < 70 are considered contractive, and thus susceptible to flow liquefaction. Also, the contractive-dilative (CD) boundary line is part of the soil behavior type charts updated by Robertson (2016) and presented earlier as Figure 3.8.

在ψ=-0.05的收缩-膨胀（CD）边界处，相关的Qtn,cs值为70（Robertson 2009b；Robertson 2010a, b），如图9.4所示。当Qtn,cs < 70时，被认为是收缩性土体，因此易于发生流动液化。此外，收缩-膨胀（CD）边界线是Robertson（2016）更新的土体行为类型图表的一部分，前文图3.8中已展示。

![image](images/rId313.png)

Figure 9.4: Screening for contractive-dilative soils and flow liquefaction using normalized cone resistance equivalence for clean sands by Robertson (2010a,b)

### 9.2.3 Yield Stress Ratio from CPT

9.2.3 从CPT推导屈服应力比

The yield stress, $\sigma_p'$ (or preconsolidation) of soils can be evaluated from CPT net resistance and material index, Ic (Mayne 2017; Agaiby & Mayne 2019):

土体的屈服应力（或预固结应力）可以通过CPT净阻力和材料指数Ic来评估（Mayne 2017; Agaiby & Mayne 2019）：

$$\sigma'_p = 0.33 (q_{net})^{m'} \cdot (\sigma_{atm}/100)^{1-m'} \tag{9.12}$$

where m’ is an exponent that depends upon soil type (Mayne et al. 2009), specifically: m’ = 1.0 (intact inorganic clays), 0.9 (organic clays), 0.85 silts, 0.80 (silty sands to sandy silts), 0.72 (clean uncemented quartz-silica sands). The exponent has been related to the CPT material index, IcRW, as shown in Figure 9.5.

其中，m'是一个依赖于土体类型的指数（Mayne et al. 2009），具体值为：m' = 1.0（完整的无机粘土），0.9（有机粘土），0.85（粉土），0.80（粉砂质砂到砂质粉土），0.72（清洁的未固结石英-硅砂）。该指数与CPT材料指数IcRW相关，如图9.5所示。

![image](images/rId314.png)

Figure 9.5: Exponent for evaluating yield stress in soils from CPT material index (after Mayne, Coop, Springman, Huang, & Zornberg 2009)

图9.5：通过CPT材料指数评估土体屈服应力的指数（依据Mayne, Coop, Springman, Huang, & Zornberg 2009）

The normalized form is the yield stress ratio (YSR), or apparent overconsolidation ratio (AOCR):

规范化形式为屈服应力比（YSR），或表观超固结比（AOCR）：

$$YSR = \sigma'_p / \sigma'_{vo} \tag{9.13}$$

Derived in terms of the simple shear mode, Figure 9.6 shows that the value of YSR at the critical state line can be found from (Mayne &Sharp 2019):

在简单剪切模式下，图9.6显示了在临界状态线处YSR的值可以通过以下公式求得（Mayne & Sharp 2019）：

$$YSR_{csl} = \left(2/\cos\phi'\right)^{1/\Lambda} \tag{9.14}$$

![image](images/rId315.png)

Figure 9.6: Screening for contractive-dilative threshold and flow liquefaction from YSR method using simplified critical state soil mechanics (after Mayne & Sharp 2019)

图9.6：使用简化的临界状态土力学通过YSR方法筛选收缩-膨胀临界点和流动液化（依据Mayne & Sharp 2019）

where $\Lambda$ = 0.8 is a CSSM parameter related to the compression and swelling lines. For friction angles between 20° ≤ φ' ≤ 40°, the range gives: 2.6 < YSRCSL < 3.3. Interestingly, Jefferies & Been (2006) discuss the value of OCR at critical state for triaxial compression mode, however their overconsolidation ratio is expressed in terms of mean effective stress, designated R Specifically, R at the CSL has a value of 2 for Modified Cam Clay and 2.7 for the original Cam Clay constitutive soil model. The evaluation of φ' for equation (9.15) is obtained from CPT by sorting drained behavior (Ic ≤ 2.6) typically associated with sands, from undrained response (Ic > 2.6) that is characteristic of clayey soils. Thus, for the case of drained CPT response at a standard rate of push of 20 mm/s, the value Ic ≤ 2.6:

其中Λ=0.8是一个与压缩和膨胀线相关的CSSM参数。对于摩擦角在20°≤ϕ^'≤40°之间，YSR在2.6到3.3之间。值得注意的是，Jefferies & Been（2006）讨论了三轴压缩模式下临界状态的OCR值，然而，他们的超固结比以平均有效应力表达，称为R_p。具体来说，R_p在CSL处的值为2（修正剑桥粘土模型）和2.7（原始剑桥粘土本构模型）。通过对排水行为（I_c ≤ 2.6，通常与砂土相关）与不排水响应（I_c > 2.6，粘土特有）进行分类，可以通过CPT获得ϕ^'的评估值。因此，对于20 mm/s的标准推进速率的排水CPT响应，当I_c ≤ 2.6时：

$$\phi' = 17.6° + 11.0° \cdot \log_{10}(Q_{tn}) \tag{9.15}$$

Undrained penetration occurs when Ic > 2.6, therefore:

当I_c > 2.6时则发生不排水穿透，因此：

For overconsolidated soils, the Q is replaced with Q’ = Q/YSR^$\Lambda$ (Ouyang & Mayne 2019).

对于超固结土体，Q被替换为Q' = Q/YSR'（Ouyang & Mayne 2019）。

## 9.3 Case Histories of Flow Liquefaction

9.3 流动液化的案例研究

The three CPT screening methods for flow liquefaction are applied to four case studies involving a natural loose sandy deposit and three tailings dam deposits. Two of the tailings were very loose and resulted in failures, while the third tailings consisted of dense compacted soils.

将上述三种CPT筛选流动液化的方法应用于四个案例研究，包括一个天然松散砂质沉积物和三个尾矿坝沉积物。两个尾矿坝的土体非常松散，导致了失败，而第三个尾矿坝则由密实的压实土组成。

### 9.3.1 Jamuna Bridge, Bangladesh

9.3.1 孟加拉国贾穆纳大桥

The western slopes of the Jamuna Bridge site experienced over 30 submarine flow slides in very young natural sandy sediments. Details are provided by Yoshimini et al. (1999) who discuss the normally consolidated fine-medium sands which contained 15 to 30% mica content. Mean grain size (D ranged from O.1 to 0.2 mm and percent fines (PF) varied from 2 to 10%. Figure 9.7 shows the mean profiles of qt and fs from 22 CPTs at the site with the corresponding materia index  with depth. Application of the aforementioned CPT screening procedures are presented in Figure 9.8. The state parameter approach of Jefferies & Been (2015) hovers around the threshold value  0.05, thus indicating marginal flow liquefaction potential while the Qan-cs method determines a rather consistent Qan-cs  which is well below the threshold of 70 and therefore strongly contractive and prone to flow liquefaction. The YSR method indicates contractive soils below 3 m, in fact, the soils may have been forced to YSR<1 indicating underconsolidation at depths greater than 16 m, therefore unstable and very susceptible to flow liquefaction.

贾穆纳大桥现场的西部斜坡在非常年轻的天然砂质沉积物中经历了超过30次海底流动滑坡。Yoshimini等人（1999）提供了详细信息，他们讨论了含15%到30%云母含量的正常固结的中细砂。平均粒径（D_50）范围为0.1到0.2毫米，细颗粒含量（PF）从2%到10%不等。图9.7展示了该地22次CPT的平均q_t和f_s剖面及相应的材料指数I_c随深度变化的情况。图9.8展示了上述CPT筛选程序的应用。Jefferies & Been（2015）的状态参数法接近阈值ψ≈-0.05，表明边缘的流动液化潜力；而Qtn-cs方法则显示了一个相当一致的Qtn-cs值为50，远低于70的阈值，因此表现为强收缩性，容易发生流动液化。YSR方法表明在3米以下的土体为收缩性土体，实际上，这些土体在16米深度以上可能已被迫到YSR<1，表明欠固结状态，因此不稳定且非常容易发生流动液化。

![image](images/rId316.png)

图9.7：在贾穆纳大桥天然砂土中经历流动液化的平均CPT剖面（数据来源：Yoshimini等人，1999年）

![image](images/rId317.png)

Figure 9.8: Application of three screening methods for flow liquefaction in natural

loose sands at Jamuna Bridge, Bangladesh

图9.8：在孟加拉国贾穆纳大桥天然松散砂土中应用三种流动液化筛选方法

### 9.3.2 Compacted Tailings, Western Canada

9.3.2 加拿大西部的压实尾矿

A compacted tailings facility in western Canada is used as an example for the quantification of primarily dilative soils (Mayne & Sharp 2019). Results from representative piezocone testing are shown in Figure 9.9 with the profiles of q,and l with depth. In terms of the SBTn system, the index l indicates mainly the presence of sands (zone 6) and gravelly sands (zone 7), except foralimited zone of a sandy mixture (zone 5) at depths of between 10 to 11 m.

加拿大西部的一个压实尾矿设施被用作主要膨胀性土体量化的例子（Mayne & Sharp 2019）。图9.9展示了代表性的孔压静力触探测试结果，q_t、f_s、u_z和I_c随深度变化的剖面。在SBTn系统中，I_c指数主要表明砂（第6区）和砾质砂（第7区）的存在，除了一小段10到11米深度的砂质混合物（第5区）。

Post-processing the CPT data for the Western Canada tailings site is shown in Figure 9.10. All three methods clearly categorize that the majority of the soil profile consists of dilative geomaterials, excepting the thin loose layer encountered at depths of 10 to 11 m which is clearly identified as contractive. This special case study shows the consistency of all three approaches in assessing contractive and dilative soil behavior.

图9.10展示了加拿大西部尾矿场地的CPT数据后处理结果。所有三种方法清楚地表明土体剖面的主要部分由膨胀性地质材料组成，除了10到11米深度的薄层松散层被清楚地识别为收缩性土体。这一特殊案例研究展示了所有三种方法在评估收缩性和膨胀性土体行为上的一致性。

![image](images/rId318.png)

图9.9：加拿大西部压实尾矿砂的代表性CPT剖面（数据来源：Mayne & Sharp 2019）

![image](images/rId319.png)

图9.10：在加拿大西部压实砂尾矿中应用三种收缩-膨胀土体行为和流动液化筛选方法

### 9.3.3 Cadia Tailings Failure, Australia

9.3.3 澳大利亚卡迪亚尾矿坝破坏

A gold tailings facility in the New South Wales area of Australia failed on O9 March 2018 with the release of slurry. Luckily no fatalities or pollution occurred, however, the reconstruction efforts were projected to take approximately 2 years for the restoration of the impoundment facilities. Details concerning the mine operations, geotechnical data, analyses, and causes of the embankment failure are given by Jefferies et al. (2019). Figure 9.11 shows a representative CPTU in the area of failure, with corresponding profiles of qt, ,and Ic with depth. The results indicate the presence of very silty to clayey soil types within the upper 58 m of the sounding. Many sandy lenses or stringers are notable throughout most of the profile. Application of all 3 post-processing approaches for Cadia are presented in Figure 9.12. The J’aB approach shows a consistently highly contractive soil profile with  and the Robertson (2010) method a rather constant profile Q with depth. Moreover, the CPT-evaluated YSR  throughout the depths also indicates the presence of highly contractive soils.

澳大利亚新南威尔士州地区的一个金尾矿设施在2018年3月9日因浆液泄漏而发生了失败。幸运的是没有人员伤亡或污染发生，但重建工作预计需要大约2年的时间来恢复储存设施。Jefferies等人（2019）提供了关于矿山运营、岩土数据、分析以及堤坝失败原因的详细信息。图9.11展示了在失败区域的代表性CPTU剖面，及相应的q_t、f_s、u_2和I_c随深度变化的剖面。结果显示，在探测的上58米内存在非常粉质到粘质的土体类型。大部分剖面中还可见到许多砂质透镜体或砂线。图9.12展示了对卡迪亚尾矿坝的所有三种后处理方法的应用。Jefferies & Been（2015）方法显示了一个始终高度收缩的土体剖面，ψ≈+0.10，而Robertson（2010）方法则表明Qtn-cs值在深度上大致恒定在20。此外，CPT评估的YSR≈1也表明存在高度收缩性土体。

![image](images/rId321.png)

embankment failure at Cadia Valley, Australia

图9.12：在卡迪亚谷尾矿坝失败中应用三种流动液化筛选方法

### 9.3.4 Fundão Tailings Failure, Brazil

9.3.4 巴西Fundão尾矿坝溃坝

On 05 November 2015, the spectacular failure of an iron ore tailings dam just southeast of Belo Horizonte, Brazil resulted in 19 deaths, extensive environmental damage, and widespread contamination (Reid 2019). The dam failure released 44 million m of toxic mine tailings into the Doce River. Full details on the geotechnical aspects of the construction history of the tailings, CPT results, laboratory testing, stability analyses, and forensic studies are reported by Morgenstern et al.(2016).

2015年11月5日，位于巴西贝洛奥里藏特东南方向的一个铁矿尾矿坝发生了严重的坍塌事故，导致19人死亡，造成了广泛的环境破坏和污染（Reid 2019）。尾矿坝的破坏释放了4400万立方米的有毒尾矿进入Doce河。Morgenstern等人（2016）详细报告了尾矿的建造历史、CPT测试结果、实验室测试、稳定性分析以及法医研究的岩土工程方面的内容。

Profiles of q, f, u, and I from CPTU sounding FO2 are presented in Figure 9.13. The three screening approaches for flow liquefaction are shown in Figure 9.14 where the YSR and Qtn,cs methods clearly show the fragile condition of the tailings, yet the  approach barely indicates instability and likely collapse, mostly localized zones of contractive soils in the upper 16 m.

图9.13展示了CPTU探测FO2的q_t、f_s、u_2和I_cRW剖面。图9.14展示了三种流动液化筛选方法的应用结果，YSR和Qtn,cs方法清楚地显示了尾矿的脆弱状态，而ψ方法仅在上部16米的局部收缩性土体区域略微显示出不稳定性，可能会导致崩塌。

![image](images/rId323.png)

from CPTU at Fundão tailings dam, Brazil

### 9.3.5 Addition of New Surcharge Loadings

9.3.5 增加新的附加荷载

The YSR method easily allows the assessment of future conditions and the contractive-dilative state of the tailings deposit due to the placement of new surcharge and fill. With the $\psi$ approach and Qtn-cs method, this is not so straightforward.

YSR方法可以轻松评估因增加新的附加载荷和填充材料而导致的尾矿堆积物的未来状态及其收缩-膨胀状态。相对于ψ方法和Qtn-cs方法，YSR方法的操作更为简单。

The conceptual evaluation of an existing soil fill or tailings embankment is depicted in Figure 9.15 showing the profile of yield stress and current effective overburden stress. As new fill or surcharge is added, the corresponding increase in ’results in a reduction in the YSR profile. For the case shown, the fill is initially dilative throughout the entire thickness of 30 m. However, as additional surcharge is added, the profile becomes contractive in the lower portions. Additional discussion on this issue is given by Mayne et al.(2017) and Mayne & Sharp (2019) 2022). Moreover, Styler et al. (2018) provide several actual case studies involving sand fill at various times after surcharge placement and the associated CPT results at these various stages of loading.

图9.15展示了对现有土填或尾矿堆坝的概念性评估，显示了屈服应力剖面和当前有效上覆应力。随着新的填充或附加载荷的增加，随之增加的σ_vo'导致YSR剖面的下降。在所示案例中，填充材料最初在整个30米厚度范围内表现为膨胀性。然而，随着附加载荷的进一步增加，剖面的下部部分变为收缩性。Mayne等人（2017）和Mayne & Sharp（2019, 2022）对这一问题进行了进一步讨论。此外，Styler等人（2018）提供了几个实际案例研究，涉及附加载荷施加后的不同时期砂填充物的CPT结果。

![image](images/rId324.png)

Figure 9.15: Conceptual changes in YSR due to new surcharge placement on existing tailings dam

图9.15：由于在现有尾矿坝上施加新的附加载荷而导致的YSR概念性变化

The yield stress ratio approach is based on a simple nexus that links an analytical solution for clays based on spherical-cavity expansion theory and critical-state soil mechanics (SCE-CSSM) and statistical results originally obtained from CPT chamber tests on sands (Mayne 2017). Initial calibration of the SCE-CSSM solution for YSR was made for 206 natural clays that had been subjected to laboratory consolidation testing of undisturbed samples and field piezocone tests with data taken at corresponding elevations (Chen &Mayne 1996).Chamber tests from 26 different sands provided over 600+ CPT data points for statistical analyses (Mayne 2001).

YSR方法的基础是一个简单的联系纽带，它将基于球形空腔膨胀理论和临界状态土力学（SCE-CSSM）的粘土解析解与最初从砂土CPT室试验中获得的统计结果联系起来（Mayne 2017）。YSR的SCE-CSSM解的初始校准是针对206个经过实验室固结测试的天然粘土，并通过现场静力触探试验进行校准（Chen & Mayne 1996）。来自26种不同砂土的室试验提供了600多个CPT数据点进行统计分析（Mayne 2001）。

Together these datasets for clay and sands were incorporated to identify a common link in the interpretation that allowed a simplified unified approach that relates yield stress to net cone resistance and soil type (Mayne, Coop, Springman, Huang, & Zornberg 2009). A final step provided a direct relationship between the soil behavior type to the CPT material index (I that was calibrated using a variety of sands, silts, clays, and mixed soil types from 93 natural soil deposits (Agaiby &Mayne 2019)

将这些粘土和砂土的数据集结合起来，确定了解释中允许简化统一方法的共同联系，该方法将屈服应力与净锥阻力和土壤类型相关联（Mayne, Coop, Springman, Huang, & Zornberg 2009）。最后一步提供了土体行为类型与CPT材料指数（I_c）之间的直接关系，该关系通过93个天然土沉积物中的各种砂土、粉土、粘土和混合土类型进行校准（Agaiby & Mayne 2019）。

Thus, the basis of the YSR approach is quite different from the  method of Jefferies& Been (2015) which was formulated from testing of reconstituted sands and tailings. The calibrated findings of the YSR approach are primarily obtained from natural soil deposits tested by in-situ CPT soundings to depths of 30 or 40 m.

因此，YSR方法的基础与Jefferies & Been（2015）提出的ψ方法有很大不同，后者是通过重构砂土和尾矿的测试形成的。YSR方法的校准结果主要是通过深度达30或40米的原位CPT探测天然土沉积物获得的。

Some additional limitations to the YSR approach can be stated. As the initial formulation of the YSR approach focused on clays, the critical state adopted the simple linear e-log form that is commonly associated with consolidation results. The extension of this assumption to sands and silty sands may in fact have limited application over certain stress ranges. Much discussion has arisen in the geotechnical literature over the utilization of a more complex and curved critical state line for coarse-grained soils, as suggested by Figure 9.2. For instance, the reader is directed to the works of Pestana & Whittle (1995), Li & Wang (1998), and Reid et al. (2020) for additional debate and details on this issue.

YSR方法的一些额外限制也可以说明。由于YSR方法的初始公式侧重于粘土，临界状态采用了通常与固结结果相关的简单线性e-log_10(σ_v^')形式。将这一假设扩展到砂土和粉砂质砂可能在某些应力范围内应用有限。在岩土工程文献中，对于粗粒土使用更复杂且曲线的临界状态线的讨论很多，如图9.2所示。例如，读者可参考Pestana & Whittle（1995）、Li & Wang（1998）和Reid等人（2020）的相关工作，以获得更多关于这一问题的讨论和细节。

## 9.4 Cyclic Liquefaction Evaluation By CPT

9.4 通过CPT评估循环液化

### 9.4.1 Seismic Ground Motions

9.4.1 地震地面运动

The evaluation of cyclic liquefaction potential is commonly performed using a simplified cyclic stress-based approach that compares the level of ground shaking against the available soil resistance. The level of ground shaking due to an earthquake is represented by the cyclic stress ratio, CSR. In terms of simple shear mode, the CSR is the ratio of the cyclic shear stress to effective stress. The magnitude of CSR can be obtained directly from numerical software programs (e.g., SHAKE, DEEPSOIL, etc.) or estimated from the following:

循环液化潜力的评估通常使用基于简化的循环应力方法，该方法将地面震动水平与土体的抗液化能力进行比较。地震引起的地面震动水平由循环应力比（CSR）表示。在简单剪切模式下，CSR是循环剪应力与有效应力的比值。CSR的大小可以直接从数值软件程序（如SHAKE, DEEPSOIL等）获得，或者通过以下公式估算：

$$CSR_{7.5} = \frac{\tau_{ave}}{\sigma_{vo}'} = 0.65 \cdot \frac{a_{max}}{g_a} \cdot \frac{\sigma_{vo}}{\sigma_{vo}'} \cdot r_d \cdot \frac{1}{MSF} \tag{9.18}$$

where $a_{max}$ = PGA = peak (horizontal) ground acceleration, ($a_{max}/g_a$) is the normalized peak ground acceleration or PGA divided by the gravity acceleration constant ($g_a$ = 9.8 m/s2), $\sigma_{vo}$ and $\sigma'_{vo}$ are the total and effective vertical overburden stresses, respectively, $r_d$ is a stress reduction coefficient, and MSF = magnitude scaling factor.

其中，a_max为峰值（水平）地面加速度PGA，(a_max/g_a)为归一化的峰值地面加速度或PGA除以重力加速度常数（g_a=9.8 m/s²），σ_vo和σ_vo^'分别为总的和有效的垂直上覆应力，r_d为应力降低系数，MSF为震级缩放因子。

The CSR represents the normalized shear stress ($\tau_{av}$/$\sigma_{vo}'$) induced in the soil by the earthquake event (i.e., the seismic demand) and commonly referenced to a benchmark case with Mw = 7.5 having a 15 s duration. For sloping ground and high overburden stresses (i.e., z > 30 m), added inclusions may include a slope correction factor ($K_\alpha$) and/or overburden correction factor ($K_\sigma$), as discussed by Idriss & Boulanger (2008).

CSR_7.5代表了由地震事件在土体中引起的归一化剪应力（τ_ave/σ_vo^'），通常参考一个矩震级为7.5、持续时间为15秒的基准情况。对于坡地和高上覆应力（即z > 30 m）的情况，还可能包括坡度修正系数（K_α）和/或上覆修正系数（K_σ），如Idriss & Boulanger（2008）所述。

The ability of the soil to resist the triggering of liquefaction is quantified in terms of a cyclic resistance ratio (CRR), as illustrated by Figure 9.16. If the CSR exceeds the CRR, liquefaction can be expected; otherwise, if CSR< CRR, liquefaction is not anticipated. The CRR has been correlated to a number of different in-situ tests including: (a) standard penetration test (SPT),(b) cone penetration tests (CPT),(c) flat dilatometer test (DMT),and(d) shear wave velocity (Vs) measurements. A summary of the standard approaches for SPT, CPT, and Vs are given in Youd et al.(2001).

土体抵抗液化触发的能力通过循环抗剪强度比（CRR）来量化，如图9.16所示。如果CSR超过CRR，则可能发生液化；否则，如果CSR < CRR，则预计不会发生液化。CRR已经与多种不同的原位测试进行了相关，包括：（a）标准贯入试验（SPT），（b）静力触探试验（CPT），（c）平板膨胀仪试验（DMT），和（d）剪切波速度（Vs）测量。Youd等人（2001）总结了SPT、CPT和Vs的标准方法。

![image](images/rId325.png)

Figure 9.16: General concept of cyclic stress-based procedure for cyclic liquefaction

PGA for 10% probability of exceedance in 50 years Shedlock and Tanner (1999)

图9.16：循环应力法评估循环液化的一般概念

![image](images/rId326.png)

Figure 9.17: Seismic map for North and Central America (Shedlock & Tanner 1999)

图9.17：北美和中美的地震图（Shedlock & Tanner 1999）

For preliminary analyses, the PGA can be estimated from seismic maps such as those published by the United States Geological Survey (USGS) and Geological Survey of Canada (GSC).For example, Shedlock& Tanner (1999) present area maps showing the PGA for North and Central America, as shown in Figure 9.17.

对于初步分析，可以从美国地质调查局（USGS）和加拿大地质调查局（GSC）发布的地震图中估算PGA。例如，Shedlock & Tanner（1999）展示了北美和中美洲的PGA区域图，如图9.17所示。

Procedures for determining the CSR and CRR from CPT depend upon the specific method adopted. For instance, Figure 9.18 shows the CSR versus normalized cone resistance with associated CRR curves for four different approaches. The CRR curves are determined by either deterministic or probablistic analyses from data sets developed from prior earthquake events. In Figure 9.18, a total of 140 case studies were compiled from site which had undergone a seismic event from a major earthquake. At 30 of these sites, no evidence of liquefaction was apparent (represented by open square dots). In contrast, at 110 site, major indications of liquefaction were reported, including such features as sand boils, ejecta, ground subsisdence, cracking and/or fissure, building tilting and/or settlement, ground movement, etc. These sites are represented by solid blue circles. The CRR is an attempt to demarcate the two categories of sites, separating “liquefied” sites from “non-liquefied” sites.

从CPT确定CSR和CRR的程序取决于所采用的具体方法。例如，图9.18显示了CSR与规范化锥阻力之间的关系及其关联的CRR曲线，使用了四种不同的方法。CRR曲线是通过从先前地震事件中开发的数据集进行确定性或概率性分析得到的。在图9.18中，汇编了140个地震事件发生后的案例研究。其中30个现场没有出现液化迹象（用空心方形点表示）。相比之下，110个现场报告了液化的主要迹象，包括砂沸、喷射、地面下沉、裂缝和/或裂隙、建筑物倾斜和/或沉降、地面移动等。这些现场用实心蓝色圆点表示。CRR试图划分这两类现场，将“液化”现场与“非液化”现场分开。

![image](images/rId327.png)

Figure 9.18: Cyclic stress ratio (CSR) versus normalized cone resistance showing cyclic resistance ratios (CRR) from 4 different methods. (updated from Robertson 2009c)

图9.18：循环应力比（CSR）与规范化锥阻力显示四种不同方法的循环抗剪强度比（CRR）（根据Robertson 2009c更新）

Herein two CPT methods are presented:(a) NCEER, or National Center for Earthquake Engineering Research (Youd et al. 2001; Robertson &Wride 1998); and (b) UCD, or Univ. California-Davis (Boulanger &Idriss 2014). The NCEER approach was developed from two workshops held by the National Science Foundation (NSF) with a final consensus given by 16 experts in the field. The UCD approach represents an updated assessment using additional new data from major earthquakes.

这里介绍了两种CPT方法：（a）NCEER，即国家地震工程研究中心方法（Youd等人，2001；Robertson & Wride 1998）；和（b）UCD，即加州大学戴维斯分校方法（Boulanger & Idriss 2014）。NCEER方法是由国家科学基金会（NSF）举办的两次研讨会开发的，最终由16位专家达成共识。UCD方法则利用了来自重大地震的额外新数据进行了更新评估。

Of general note for both the NCEER and UCD methods, soils which exhibit a value of Ic ≤ 2.6 are considered susceptible to liquefaction (sands and silty sands), whereas lc>2.6 implicates clays and clayey silts that are not technically liquefiable (Idriss & Boulanger 2008).

一般来说，对于NCEER和UCD方法，表现出I_c ≤ 2.6的土壤被认为容易发生液化（砂土和粉砂质砂），而I_c > 2.6则指示粘土和粉质粘土，在技术上不易发生液化（Idriss & Boulanger 2008）。

### 9.4.2 NCEER Approach

9.4.2 NCEER方法

The NCEER approach for CPT cyclic liquefaction evaluation follows the method of Robertson and Wride (1998). For the calculation of CSR, the stress reduction coefficient (r decreases from 1.0 at the ground surface to a value of 0.5 at 30 m and can be obtained as a function of depth (z, in meters) following the recommendations of Youd et al. (2001):

NCEER方法的CPT循环液化评估遵循Robertson和Wride（1998）的方法。对于CSR的计算，应力降低系数（r_d）从地面表面的1.0减小到30米处的0.5，可以根据深度（z，单位为米）作为函数获得（Youd等人，2001的建议）：

$$r_d(z) = \frac{0.05z + 0.00175z^{1.5}}{0.00621z^{1.5} + 0.00121z^{-2}} \tag{9.19}$$

The profile for r d is depicted in Figure 9.19.

图9.19展示了rd的剖面。

![image](images/rId328.png)

Figure 9.19: Stress reduction factor (r NCEER approach (Youd et al. 2001)

图9.19：NCEER方法的应力降低系数（r_d）（Youd等人，2001）

For the NCEER approach, the magnitude scaling factor (MSF) depends upon the moment magnitude (Mw) of the earthquake. The MSR is presented in Figure 9.20 and expressed:

对于NCEER方法，震级缩放因子（MSF）取决于地震的矩震级（Mw）。MSF如图9.20所示，表达式为：

$$MSF = \left(\frac{7.5}{M_w}\right)^{2.56} \tag{9.20}$$

in order to evaluate the level of ground shaking, or CSR given by equation (9.18).

以评估地面震动水平或CSR，如公式（9.18）所示。

![image](images/rId330.png)

Figure 9.20: Magnitude scaling factors for NCEER methods

图9.20：NCEER方法的震级缩放因子

For each elevation in the CPT sounding, the CSR is compared with the level of ground resistance, represented by the cyclic resistance ratio (CRR) shown in Figure 9.21 that is obtained from the following expressions:

在CPT探测的每个深度处，CSR与地面抗剪强度水平进行比较，后者由图9.21所示的循环抗剪强度比（CRR）表示，计算公式如下：

$$CRR_{7.5} = 0.833 \cdot \left[\frac{(Q_{tn})_{cs}}{1000}\right] + 0.05 \tag{9.21a}$$

$$CRR_{7.5} = 93 \cdot \left[\frac{(Q_{tn})_{cs}}{1000}\right]^3 + 0.08 \tag{9.21b}$$

where Qtn,cs = the equivalent clean sand stress-normalized cone resistance is detailed in Section 9.2.2. When the level of ground shaking exceeds the available in-situ soil resistance, that is: CSR > CRR, then liquefaction is likely. In fact, we can define the factor of safety (FS) against liquefaction as simply:

其中，Qtn,cs是详见第9.2.2节中的清洁砂应力规范化锥阻力的等效值。当地面震动水平超过可用的原位土体抗剪强度时，即CSR > CRR，则可能发生液化。事实上，我们可以简单地定义液化的安全系数（FS）为：

$$FS_{liq} = \frac{CRR_{7.5,\sigma'_v=1\ atm}}{CSR_{7.5,\sigma'_v=1\ atm}} \tag{9.22}$$

![image](images/rId331.png)

Figure 9.21: Cyclic stress ratio (CSR) versus normalized cone resistance Qtn,cs with cyclic resistance ratio (CRR) for evaluating cyclic liquefaction potential from CPT using NCEER method (Robertson & Wride, 1998; Youd et al. 2001)

图9.21：使用NCEER方法通过CPT评估循环液化潜力的循环应力比（CSR）与规范化锥阻力Qtn,cs与循环抗剪强度比（CRR）

![image](images/rId332.jpeg)

Applying statistical evaluations to the dataset, CRR curves of different probabilities of occurrence have been developed from mapping functions (Ku et al. 2012). These have been approximately related to the calculated safety factor FS to estimate the liquefaction probability PL from the NCEER approach that are shown in Figure 9.22 and can be expressed (Ku et al. 2012):

通过对数据集进行统计评估，开发了不同发生概率的CRR曲线，这些曲线与计算得出的安全系数FS相关联，以估算NCEER方法下的液化概率（P_L），如图9.22所示，可表示为：

$$P_L = \frac{1}{1 + \left(\frac{FS}{0.9}\right)^6} \tag{9.23}$$

![image](images/rId333.jpeg)

Normalized Cone Resistance, Qtn-cs

Figure 9.22: Probability CRRs for CPT from NCEER dataset

图9.22：来自NCEER数据集的CPT液化概率CRR曲线

### 9.4.3 UCD Method for Cyclic Liquefaction from CPT

9.4.3 通过CPT进行循环液化评估的UCD方法

Since the NCEER consensus reports were developed over two decades ago, a good number of new case studies from large seismic events worldwide have provided additional data to allow updating of the various cyclic resistance ratios (CRRs), as well as the cyclic stress ratios (CSR), for each of the in-situ test methods. Full details on the updated CPT methods are given by Idriss & Boulanger (2008) and Boulanger & Idriss (2014). The level of ground shaking by the earthquake is represented by the cyclic stress ratio (CSR) and is given by the following expression for flat horizontal ground:

由于NCEER共识报告是在20多年前制定的，近年来，全球范围内的大型地震事件提供了大量新的案例研究数据，从而使各类循环抗剪强度比（CRR）和循环应力比（CSR）得到了更新。关于这些更新后的CPT方法的详细信息可参考Idriss & Boulanger（2008）和Boulanger & Idriss（2014）。地震引起的地面震动水平由循环应力比（CSR）表示，其在平坦水平地面上的表达式为：

$$CSR_{M=7.5,\;\sigma'_{v0}=1\;\text{atm}} = 0.65 \cdot \frac{a_{\max}}{g} \cdot \frac{\sigma_{v0}}{\sigma'_{v0}} \cdot r_d \cdot \frac{1}{MSF} \cdot \frac{1}{K_\sigma} \tag{9.24}$$

where the first three terms have been detailed previously. For the Boulanger and Idriss (2014) approach, the stress reduction factor (rd) is expressed as a function of depth (z) and magnitude (M), as presented in Figure 9.23.

其中前面已经详细介绍了前三个术语。对于Boulanger和Idriss（2014）的方法，应力降低系数（rd）作为深度（z）和震级（M）的函数表示，如图9.23所示。

![image](images/rId334.png)

Note: Depth z in meters and sine terms in radians

注译：深度Z单位为m,sin单位为弧度

![image](images/rId335.jpeg)

图9.23：更新后的UCD方法中用于循环液化的应力降低系数（取自Boulanger & Idriss 2014）

Initially, the CPT material index (Ic) as defined by Robertson (2009a,2009b,2010) can be used to evaluate soil type and provide an estimate of the fines content of the sand, as discussed by Boulanger and Idriss (2014):

首先，根据Robertson（2009a, 2009b, 2010）定义的CPT材料指数（I_c）可以用于评估土体类型，并估算砂土的细粒含量，如Boulanger和Idriss（2014）讨论的那样：

$$FC\;(\%) = 80\,(I_c + C_{FC}) - 137 \quad \text{where } 0\% \le FC \le 100\% \tag{9.25}$$

The term CFC is a site-specific calibration factor where the CPT readings can be calibrated with available borehole logs and lab data. For mine tailings, an initial estimate of CFC ≈ 0 may be used for preliminary efforts. Note also that the driving of split-spoon samples during the SPT has been recognized to increase measured fines contents because of particle crushing, fracture, and cracking. For the UCD method, the stress-normalized cone resistance for clean sands is evaluated as a function of effective overburden stress and relative density:

CFC是一个特定于现场的校准因子，可以使用CPT读数进行校准，初步估计可采用-0.29≤CFC≤+0.29。此外，还应注意，在标准贯入试验（SPT）过程中，驱动分裂勺取样器已被证明会增加测量到的细粒含量，因为会发生颗粒破碎、裂解和裂缝。对于UCD方法，清洁砂的应力规范化锥阻力被评估为有效上覆应力和相对密度的函数：

$$Q_{c1N} = C_{Ne} \cdot q_c / \sigma_{\text{atm}} \tag{9.26}$$

where the stress normalization factor is given by:

其中，应力规范化因子为：

$$C_{Ne} = (\sigma_{\text{atm}} / \sigma'_{v0})^m \le 1.7 \tag{9.27}$$

For general CPT interpretation in soils, the use of qnet = qt-vo is essentially mandatory, where qe =total cone tip resistance (Robertson, 1990; Lunne et al., 1997; Mayne, 2007). However, in clean sands, qt ≈ qc because induced penetration pore water pressures are low ($u_2$ ≈ $u_0$). Also, since total overburden stress is small relative to cone tip resistance (qt >> vo) in clean sands at depths z < 30 m, essentially: qnet ≈ qt ≈ qc. Since liquefaction applies primarily to sands, the updated approach by Boulanger & Idriss (2014) has thus retained qc in its formulations.

对于土体中的CPT解释，使用q_net = q_t - σ_vo基本上是强制性的，其中q_t为总锥尖阻力（Robertson, 1990; Lunne et al., 1997; Mayne, 2007）。然而，在清洁砂中，由于引发的穿透孔隙水压力较低（$u_2$ ≈ $u_0$），q_t ≈ q_c。而且，由于总上覆应力相对于浅层清洁砂中的锥尖阻力较小（在深度z < 30 m时，q_t >> σ_vo），基本上：q_net ≈ q_t ≈ q_c。由于液化主要发生在砂土中，Boulanger & Idriss（2014）在其公式中保留了q_c。

The exponent m varies with relative density (DR) of the sand, such that:

指数m随着砂土的相对密度（DR）变化：

$$m = 0.785 - 0.521 \cdot D_R \tag{9.28}$$

For this approach, the relative density of clean sands is given by:

对于这种方法，清洁砂的相对密度为：

$$D_R\;(\%) = 0.478\,(q_{c1N})^{0.264} - 1.063 \tag{9.29}$$

For clean to silty sands, the measured (or estimated) fines content is used to adjust the normalized cone resistance to that for an equivalent clean sand:

对于清洁至粉砂质砂，测量的（或估计的）细粒含量用于将规范化锥阻力调整为等效的清洁砂：

$$q_{c1N\text{-}cs} = q_{c1N} + \Delta q_{c1N} \tag{9.30}$$

where the fines content correction term is expressed:

其中细粒含量校正项表示为：

$$\Delta q_{c1N} = \left[ 9.7 + \left(\frac{11.9}{q_{c1N}}\right) \cdot \exp(1.63) - 14.6 \right] \cdot \left(\frac{FC}{FC + 9.7}\right)^2 + \left[\frac{15.7 \cdot (FC)^2}{(FC + 9.7 + 14.6)^2}\right] - 2.80 \tag{9.31}$$

![image](images/rId336.jpeg)

$$m = 1.338 - 0.249\,(q_{c1N\text{-}cs})^{0.264} \tag{9.32}$$

然后，使用校正后的规范化CPT阻力计算指数m：

(9.32)

where it is restricted to the range: 0.264 ≤ m ≤ 0.782.

其范围被限制在：0.264 ≤ m ≤ 0.782。

The available strength of the ground to resist an earthquake is represented by the cyclic resistance ratio (CRR) for a Mw = 7.5 and effective overburden stress at v' = 1 atm as shown in Figure 9.24, which is evaluated from:

土体抵抗地震能力由Mw = 7.5且有效上覆应力为1 atm下的循环抗剪强度比（CRR）表示，如图9.24所示，计算公式为：

$$CRR_{M=7.5,\;\sigma'_v=1\;\text{atm}} = \exp\left[\frac{q_{c1N\text{-}cs}^2}{113 + (q_{c1N\text{-}cs})^3} + \frac{q_{c1N\text{-}cs}}{1000 + (q_{c1N\text{-}cs})^4} + 2.80 - \frac{137}{140 + (q_{c1N\text{-}cs})^2 + q_{c1N\text{-}cs}}\right] \tag{9.33}$$

![image](images/rId337.jpeg)

The level of ground shaking represented by the cyclic stress ratio (CSR) given by equation (9.24) depends on the magnitude scaling factor (MSF) and overburden stress factor ($K_\sigma$), both of which are dependent on the normalized cone resistance in the UCD procedure. As such, the MSF is derived from the earthquake magnitude and normalized-corrected penetration resistance:

地面震动水平由公式（9.24）中的循环应力比（CSR）表示，具体取决于震级缩放因子（MSF）和上覆应力因子（Kσ），这两者都依赖于UCD程序中的规范化锥阻力。因此，MSF由地震震级和规范化校正的穿透阻力得出：

$$MSF = 1 + (MSF_{\max} - 1) \cdot [8.64 \cdot \exp(-M/4) - 1.325] \tag{9.34}$$

where the maximum MSF value is imposed:

MSF的最大值为：

$$MSF_{\max} = 1.09 + [(q_{c1N\text{-}cs})/180]^3 \le 2.2 \tag{9.35}$$

Figure 9.24: Cyclic stress ratio (CSR) versus normalized cone resistance q c1N-cs with cyclic resistance ratio (CRR) for evaluating cyclic liquefaction potential from CPT using UDC procedure (Boulanger & Idriss 2014)

图9.24：使用UDC程序通过CPT评估循环液化潜力的循环应力比（CSR）与规范化锥阻力q_{c1N-cs}及循环抗剪强度比（CRR）

![image](images/rId339.jpeg)

The overburden stress factor is also expressed as a function of normalized corrected penetration resistance and level of effective stress (Boulanger and Idriss 2014):

上覆应力因子也表示为规范化校正的穿透阻力和有效应力水平的函数（Boulanger和Idriss 2014）：

$$K_\sigma = 1 - C_\sigma \cdot \ln(\sigma'_{v0}/\sigma_{\text{atm}}) \le 1.1 \tag{9.36}$$

where the parameter $C_\sigma$ is determined from:

其中参数Cσ由以下公式确定：

$$C_\sigma = \frac{1}{37.3 - 8.27 \cdot (q_{c1N\text{-}cs})^{0.264}} \le 0.3 \tag{9.37}$$

Of additional note, in addition to the deterministic approach discussed herein, the UCD procedure also provides a family of curves of probability of liquefaction using the CPT.

此外，除了本文讨论的确定性方法外，UCD程序还提供了一系列利用CPT计算的液化概率曲线。

### 9.4.4 Yield Stress Ratio for Screening of Cyclic Liquefaction

9.4.4 用于循环液化筛选的屈服应力比

The aforementioned procedure for yield stress ratio at critical state (YSRcsl) for delineating contractive versus dilative soil behavior can also serve as a simple screening of ground susceptible to cyclic liquefaction. The concept here is that contractive soils are prone to liquefaction, whereas dilatant soils are not susceptible to liquefaction.

前述用于在临界状态下评估屈服应力比（YSR_csl）以划分收缩性与膨胀性土体行为的程序，也可以作为简单的地面易发生循环液化的筛选方法。这里的概念是，收缩性土体容易发生液化，而膨胀性土体则不易发生液化。

## 9.5 Case Studies Involving Cyclic Liquefaction

9.5 涉及循环液化的案例研究

Three case studies will be presented to show a comparison of the simplified cyclic stress-based approach and YSR at critical state line. For the former, the NCEER method is utlilized.

将介绍三个案例研究，以展示简化的基于循环应力的方法与在临界状态线上的YSR之间的对比。对于前者，采用NCEER方法。

### 9.5.1 Case Study: Felipito Bridges, Mexico

9.5.1 案例研究：墨西哥Felipito桥

The Felipito case study involves the performance of two parallel bridges (one highway and one railroad), as presented by Turner et al. (2014, 2016). Figure 9.25 shows the site location and a photo of both bridges that cross the Colorado River in the northern Baja California peninsula. The site was subjected to the 2010 M 7.2 El Mayor-Cucuapah earthquake which imparted a peak ground acceleration of PGA=0.27 g, resulting in a partial collapse of the railroad bridge which was supported by driven steel pilings. The undamaged highway bridge was situated on drilled shaft foundations. Geotechnical investigations utilized the results of shear wave velocity profiles, SPT borings, and CPTu soundings, supplemented with limited laboratory testing.

Felipito案例研究涉及两个平行桥梁（一个公路桥和一个铁路桥）的性能，如Turner等人（2014, 2016）所述。图9.25显示了该地点的位置以及跨越墨西哥下加利福尼亚半岛北部科罗拉多河的两座桥梁的照片。该地点经历了2010年7.2级El Mayor-Cucapah地震，该地震的峰值地面加速度为PGA = 0.27 g，导致了铁路桥的部分倒塌，该桥由打入的钢桩支撑。未损坏的公路桥位于钻孔桩基础上。岩土工程调查利用了剪切波速度剖面、SPT钻孔和CPTu探测结果，并辅以有限的实验室测试。

A representative CPT-01 sounding was selected for evaluation, as presented in Figure 9.26. Postprocessing of the data included: (a) an evaluation of the effective stress and yield stress profiles (Figure 9.26d); and (b) cyclic liquefaction analyses that compare the level of ground shaking, i.e., the cyclic stress ratio (CSR) with the level of ground support, or cyclic resistance ratio (CRR), as shown in Figure 9.26e. The CSR was adjusted to a reference value for Mw = 7.5 using the NCEER analysis (Robertson & Wride 1998; Youd et al. 2001). This identified five sandy layers having a high likelihood of liquefaction where the CSR > CRR. The results can be further processed to provide a comparison of the normalized values, including: yield stress ratio (YSR = p'/vo') and factor of safety (FS = CRR/CSR), as presented in Figure 9.27.

选定了一个代表性的CPT-01探测进行评估，如图9.26所示。数据的后处理包括：（a）有效应力和屈服应力剖面的评估（图9.26d）；（b）循环液化分析，将地面震动水平，即循环应力比（CSR）与地面支持水平或循环抗剪强度比（CRR）进行比较，如图9.26e所示。CSR使用NCEER分析调整到Mw = 7.5的参考值（Robertson & Wride 1998; Youd et al. 2001）。这确定了五个砂层，CSR > CRR表明这些层具有高液化可能性。结果可以进一步处理，以提供规范化值的比较，包括：屈服应力比（YSR = σ_p'/σ_vo'）和液化安全系数（FS = CRR/CSR），如图9.27所示。

![image](images/rId340.jpeg)

Figure 9.25: Liquefaction case study of Felipito Bridges, Mexico: (a) EQ epicenter and site location; (b) highway and railroad bridges (Turner et al. 2014)

图9.25：墨西哥Felipito桥的液化案例研究：（a）地震震中和地点位置；（b）公路桥和铁路桥（Turner等人，2014）

Notably, when the FS<1, we have a strong likelihood of liquefaction. The five sand layers are found to be more or less corresponding to the same depths as those identified as contractive sands per the threshold YSR approach. Of additional note, a thin sixth sand layer at a depth of 16.2 m is also identified as contractive by the YSR approach whereas only marginally liquefiable via the NCEER analyses.

值得注意的是，当FS < 1时，液化的可能性较大。这五个砂层大致与通过YSR方法确定的收缩性砂层在相同深度处相对应。此外，YSR方法还在16.2米深度处识别出一个薄的第六砂层为收缩性，而NCEER分析则仅显示该层具有边缘液化可能性。

![image](images/rId341.jpeg)

Figure 9.26: Results of CPT-01 at Felipito Bridges site: (a) cone resistance, qt; (b) sleeve friction, fs; (c) pore pressure, $u_2$; (d), effective stresses vo' and p' from CPT; (e) CRR and CSR from NCEER cyclic liquefaction analyses.

图9.26：Felipito桥现场CPT-01的结果：（a）锥阻力，q_t；（b）侧壁摩擦力，f_s；（c）孔隙压力，$u_2$；（d）CPT确定的有效应力σ_vo'和屈服应力σ_p'；（e）NCEER循环液化分析中的CRR和CSR。

![image](images/rId342.jpeg)

Figure 9.27: Post-processing of normalized liquefaction parameters at Felipito Bridges site: (a) cone resistance, qt; (b) CPT material index, Ic-RW; (c) yield stress ratio, YSR; and (d) FS against liquefaction using NCEER procedure

图9.27：Felipito桥现场的规范化液化参数的后处理：（a）锥阻力，q_t；（b）CPT材料指数，Ic-RW；（c）屈服应力比，YSR；（d）使用NCEER程序的液化安全系数FS

### 9.5.2 Case Study: Wildlife Site, California

9.5.2 案例研究：加利福尼亚野生动物研究站

The 1987 Superstition Hills Earthquake (Mw = 6.6) resulted in liquefaction at the Wildlife research site in Imperial Valley of southern California. The Wildlife site has been instrumented with an array of ground motion sensors and porewater pressure piezometers for geotechnical research and has experienced 4 earthquakes in the past four decades. The University of Texas - Austin conducted liquefaction research activities at the facility (Cox 2006) and results from sounding CPT-47 Test C are presented in Figure 9.28. The peak ground acceleration for the 1987 event is taken as PGA = 0.206 and the groundwater table is 1 m deep. It can be seen that there are four critical layers of liquefaction concern with FS < 1 in the right-hand portion of the figure. These same four layers coincide with the values of in-situ YSR from the CPT profile that fall below the YSRcsl threshold and indicate contractive soil behavior.

1987年Superstition Hills地震（Mw = 6.6）导致南加利福尼亚州帝国谷的野生动物研究站发生液化。该站点安装了用于岩土工程研究的地面运动传感器和孔隙水压力压强计，并在过去四十年中经历了4次地震。德克萨斯大学奥斯汀分校在该设施进行了液化研究活动（Cox 2006），并展示了探测CPT-47 Test C的结果，如图9.28所示。1987年事件的峰值地面加速度为PGA = 0.206，地下水位为1米。可以看出，在图的右侧有四个关键的液化层，FS < 1。这四个层与CPT剖面中YSR值低于YSRcsl阈值并表明收缩性土体行为的值相一致。

Also note the two layers at depths of 1.5 to 2.5 m and from 6.8 m to termination depth at 8 m which have Ic > 2.6, therefore not considered "liquefiable" in the technical definition (Idriss & Boulanger 2008)

还请注意，深度为1.5到2.5米和6.8米至终止深度8米的两层土体具有I_c > 2.6，因此在技术定义上不被认为是“液化的”（Idriss & Boulanger 2008）。

![image](images/rId343.png)

Figure 9.28: Profiles at Wildlife research site, CA: (a) cone resistance, (b) index Ic; (c) YSR and threshold; (d) Factor of Safety using NCEER method.

图9.28：加利福尼亚野生动物研究站的剖面图：（a）锥阻力，q_t；（b）材料指数，I_c；（c）YSR及其阈值；（d）使用NCEER方法的安全系数。

### 9.5.3 Case Study: Christchurch, New Zealand

9.5.3 案例研究：新西兰克赖斯特彻奇

A significant number of liquefaction sites became apparent following four main earthquake events in Christchurch, NZ during 2010 and 2011. One example case study is detailed by Green et al. (2014) for Site 11 at a park along the Kaiapoi River that experienced surficial liquefaction at the ground surface during both the Darfield (Mw = 7.1; PGA = 0.231 g) and Christchurch (Mw = 6.2; PGA = 0.181 g) earthquake events. Figure 9.29 presents a representative CPT sounding (CPTKAN-26) from the site. For the Darfield EQ, screening of the critical liquefied layers where the CRR < CSR clearly matches the same layers identified when YSR < YSRcsl.

2010年和2011年克赖斯特彻奇，新西兰发生的四次主要地震事件后，出现了大量的液化现场。Green等人（2014）详细描述了其中一个案例研究，即位于Kaiapoi河沿岸的一处公园，在Darfield地震（Mw = 7.1；PGA = 0.231 g）和克赖斯特彻奇地震（Mw = 6.2；PGA = 0.181 g）事件期间，该地表发生了表面液化现象。图9.29展示了该地点的代表性CPT探测（CPTKAN-26）。对于Darfield地震，筛选出的临界液化层CRR < CSR与YSR < YSRcsl所识别的层一致。

![image](images/rId344.png)

Figure 9.29: Profiles at North Kaiapoi, Christchurch, NZ: (a) cone resistance, (b) index Ic; (c) YSR and threshold; (d) CSR and CRR using NCEER method

图9.29：新西兰克赖斯特彻奇北Kaiapoi的剖面图：（a）锥阻力；（b）材料指数I_c；（c）YSR及其阈值；（d）使用NCEER方法的CSR和CRR。

## 9.6 Post-cyclic Undrained Strength Evaluation

9.6 后循环非排水强度评估

Derivations for assessing the post-cyclic liquefied strength of sands from CPT results have been developed (Olson and Johnson, 2008; Robertson, 2010; Boulanger and Idriss, 2014; Sadrekarimi 2014). This parameter is also called the residual undrained shear strength or liquefied undrained strength and designated su(liquefied) or su(LIQ) or sr, as well as other alternative nomenclature.

已经开发了从CPT结果评估砂土后循环液化强度的方法（Olson和Johnson，2008；Robertson，2010；Boulanger和Idriss，2014；Sadrekarimi，2014）。这个参数也被称为残余非排水抗剪强度或液化后的非排水强度，表示为su（液化）或su（LIQ）或sr，以及其他替代命名。

### 9.6.1 UIUC Method

9.6.1 伊利诺伊大学厄巴纳-香槟分校方法

Research by the geotechnical group at the University of Illinois-Urbana-Champaign (UIUC) compiled data from 33 flow liquefaction case studies worldwide (Olson & Stark 2002). Most of the data on the liquefied soils came from either standard penetration tests (SPT) or relative density evaluations, however, with only 11 case studies directly made by CPT. Therefore, they made some assumptions on converting SPT and DR data to an equivalent normalized cone resistance (qc1) based on fines content and/or grain size characteristics, where:
$$q_{c1} = \left(\frac{q_c}{\sigma'_{vo}/\sigma_{atm}}\right)^{1.8} + 0.8 \tag{9.38}$$

伊利诺伊大学厄巴纳-香槟分校（UIUC）的岩土工程研究团队编制了来自全球33个流动液化案例研究的数据（Olson & Stark 2002）。大多数关于液化土体的数据来自标准贯入试验（SPT）或相对密度评估，然而，只有11个案例研究是直接由CPT进行的。因此，他们假设将SPT和DR数据转换为基于细粒含量和/或粒径特征的等效规范化锥阻力（qc1），其公式如下：

where qc is in units of MPa. From the geometry of the flow liquefaction case studies, Olson &Stark (2002) backcalculated the undrained liquefied strength of the soils (suLIQ) and expressed this in terms of a dimensionless ratio: su(LIQ)/vo' that was correlated to the normalized qc1.
$$s_{u(LIQ)}/\sigma'_{vo} = 0.03 + 0.0143 \cdot q_{c1} \pm 0.03 \quad \text{for } q_{c1} \leq 6.5 \text{ MPa} \tag{9.39}$$

其中q_c的单位为MPa。通过流动液化案例研究的几何形态，Olson & Stark（2002）反算了土体的非排水液化强度（suLIQ），并将其表示为无量纲比值：su（LIQ）/σvo'，该比值与规范化qc1相关。

Figure 9.30 presents the dataset trend that can be expressed:

图9.30：使用UIUC方法从规范化锥阻力计算的液化强度关系（依据Olson & Stark 2002）

![image](images/rId345.png)

Figure 9.30: Relationship for liquefied strength from normalized cone resistance using UIUC method (adapted after Olson & Stark 2002)

### 9.6.2 UCD Approach

9.6.2 加州大学戴维斯分校方法

The University of California at Davis (UCD) method for cyclic liquefaction also provides a means to estimate the post-cyclic undrained (residual) strength. This strength is termed Sr by Idriss & Boulanger (2015) and expressed as an undrained strength ratio, Sr/vo', or su(LIQ)/vo'.

加州大学戴维斯分校（UCD）的方法同样为估算后循环非排水（残余）强度提供了一种手段。这种强度由Idriss & Boulanger（2015）称为Sr，并表示为非排水强度比，Sr/σvo'，或su（LIQ）/σvo'。

The UCD method uses a normalized cone resistance that is adjusted for fines content (FC) per a set of tabulated values (i.e., Table 3) provided by Idriss & Boulanger (2015) that is presented graphically here as Figure 9.31. The normalized cone resistance corrected for FC for residual strength is:
$$q_{c1Ncs-Sr} = q_{c1N} + \Delta q_{c1Ncs-Sr} \tag{9.40}$$

UCD方法使用经过细粒含量（FC）调整的规范化锥阻力，根据Idriss & Boulanger（2015）提供的表格值（如表3）调整，如图9.31所示。用于残余强度的规范化锥阻力校正公式为：

where qc1N is obtained from eqn (9.26) and the adjustment for FC is given by:
$$\Delta q_{c1Ncs-Sr} = -0.006 \cdot FC^2 + 1.195 \cdot FC - 0.651 \tag{9.41}$$

其中，qc1N从公式（9.26）中获得，而FC的调整公式为：

![image](images/rId346.png)

Figure 9.31: Relationship for fines content correction using UCD method

图9.31：使用UCD方法进行细粒含量校正的关系

The evaluation of the residual undrained strength ratio is then given for two cases: (a) where void ratio redistribution is expected to be significant following liquefaction; (b) when the void ratio redistribution will be negligible. Both cases are shown in Figure 9.32 with case studies listed by Idriss & Boulanger (2015). These can be represented by the following equations:
$$s_{r}/\sigma'_{vo}\bigg|_{CASE\,1} = \exp\left[-\left(\frac{q_{c1Ncs-Sr} - 24.5}{61.7}\right)^2 + 4.42 \cdot \tan\phi' - \frac{q_{c1Ncs-Sr}}{106}\right] \leq \cdots \tag{9.42}$$
$$s_{r}/\sigma'_{vo}\bigg|_{CASE\,2} = \frac{1}{2}\exp\left[-9.82 \cdot \frac{s_{r}}{\sigma'_{vo}}\bigg|_{CASE\,1} + \tan\phi'\right] \leq \frac{\sigma'_{vo}}{11.1} \tag{9.43}$$

然后，这个校正后的规范化锥阻力用于两种情况下的残余非排水强度比的评估：（a）预计液化后孔隙比再分布显著；（b）预计液化后孔隙比再分布不显著。Idriss & Boulanger（2015）列出了这两种情况的案例研究，并在图9.32中展示了这些情况。这些可用以下公式表示：

![image](images/rId347.png)

Figure 9.32: Liquefied strength from adjusted and normalized cone resistance using UCD method (adapted from Idriss & Boulanger 2015)

图9.32:使用UCD方法调整和归一化锥阻力得到的液化强度(改编自Idriss和Boulanger, 2015)

### 9.6.3 Robertson Method

9.6.3 Robertson方法

Using an updated approach reported by Robertson (2021), Figure 9.33 shows the relationship for normalized residual undrained strength to effective overburden ratio, su(liq)/vo', with normalized CPT cone tip resistance for an equivalent clean sand that can be expressed:
$$s_{u(liq)}/\sigma'_{vo} \approx 0.0007 \cdot \exp(0.084 \cdot Q_{tn,cs}) \cdot Q_{tn,cs}^{0.3} + 0.0178 \cdot Q_{tn,cs} \tag{9.44}$$

使用Robertson（2021）更新的方法，图9.33显示了归一化残余非排水强度与有效上覆应力比（su(liq)/σ_vo'）和等效清洁砂的归一化CPT锥尖阻力之间的关系，可以表示为：

which applies to soils exhibiting IcRW < 3.0. A recommendation is also given to the use of a minimum value su(liq) = 1 kPa for vo' < 50 kPa (Robertson 2021).

该公式适用于IcRW < 3.0的土体。Robertson（2021）还建议对于σ_vo' < 50 kPa的情况，使用最低值su(liq) = 1 kPa。

For critical projects having severe consequences, the evaluation of the residual undrained strength should be accompanied by other independent means, including field vane tests, ball penetrometer, and/or laboratory strength testing on undisturbed samples.

对于有严重后果的关键项目，残余非排水强度的评估应伴随其他独立的测试方法，包括现场剪切测试、球型渗透仪和/或未扰动样品的实验室强度测试。

![image](images/rId348.png)

Figure 9.33: Recommended undrained strength ratio of liquefied sands from CPT normalized resistance (after Robertson, 2021).

图9.33：推荐的通过CPT归一化阻力评估液化砂的非排水强度比（依据Robertson, 2021）

## 9.7 Ground Deformations from Liquefaction

9.7 液化导致的地面变形

Liquefaction can cause subsidence and settlements in the ground due to volumetric and shear strains. The level of strains has been related to the relative density of sands and intensity of liquefaction. Four cases can be considered, as detailed by Zhang et al. (2004). Figure 9.34 depicts simplified geometries of the ground conditions that are covered.

液化可以导致地面由于体积和剪切应变而产生沉降。应变水平与砂土的相对密度和液化强度有关。Zhang等人（2004）详细讨论了四种情况。图9.34展示了地面条件的简化几何形状。

![image](images/rId349.png)

Figure 9.34: Four simplified cases for level and sloping ground during liquefaction

图9.34：液化期间平坦和斜坡地面的四种简化情况

Vertical settlements for level ground conditions are calculated on the basis of volumetric strains, while lateral deformations are associated with sloped terrain.

对于平坦地面的竖向沉降是基于体积应变计算的，而横向变形则与斜坡地形相关。

### 9.7.1 Volumetric Strains

9.7.1 体积应变

The volumetric strains are determined on the basis of the calculated factor of safety (FS) against liquefaction and the relative density (DR) of the sands, as illustrated by Figure 9.35. The seismic-induced permanent settlements (sz) are calculated from the integration of vol with depth:
$$s_z = \int_0^z \varepsilon_{vol} \, dz \tag{9.45}$$

体积应变基于液化安全系数（FS）和砂土的相对密度（DR）来确定，如图9.35所示。地震引起的永久沉降（s_z）通过对深度进行ε_vol的积分计算：

![image](images/rId350.png)

Figure 9.35: Relationship of volumetric strain (vol) in terms of factor of safety against liquefaction (FS) and sand relative density (DR)

图9.35：体积应变（ε_vol）与液化安全系数（FS）和砂土相对密度（DR）的关系

Algorithms to represent the laboratory results obtained by Ishihara & Yoshimi (1992) on cyclically-loaded sand are given here. Determine the sand relative density from the expression:
$$D_R (\%) = 76 \cdot \log_{10}(Q_{tn-cs}) - 85 \leq 100\% \tag{9.46}$$

这里给出了Ishihara & Yoshimi（1992）对循环加载砂土的实验室结果的算法表示。根据以下表达式确定砂土的相对密度：

where normalized cone resistance for an equivalent clean sand is limited to Qtn-cs < 200. For values above 200, set DR = 100%.

其中，等效清洁砂的归一化锥阻力限制为Qtn-cs < 200。对于高于200的值，将DR设置为100%。

$$m_x = 1.91962 - 0.0072 \cdot D_R \tag{9.47}$$
$$\text{For } D_R \leq 50\%: \quad b_x = 2 \times 10^{-6} \cdot (D_R)^{3.04} \tag{9.48a}$$
$$\text{For } D_R > 50\%: \quad b_x = 0.0112 \cdot D_R - 9 \times 10^{-5} \cdot (D_R)^2 - 0.0769 \tag{9.48b}$$

Calculate limiting factor of safety (FSlimit):
$$\varepsilon_{vol\text{-}Limit} = 18.76 - 3.891 \cdot \ln_e(D_R) \tag{9.49}$$
$$FS_{limit} = 2 - 1/[m_x + b_x/\varepsilon_{vol\text{-}limit}] \tag{9.50}$$

计算极限安全系数（FS_limit）：

Determine volumetric strains:

确定体积应变：

$$\text{If } FS \geq 2: \quad \varepsilon_{vol} = 0 \tag{9.51a}$$
$$\text{If } FS < FS_{limit}: \quad \varepsilon_{vol} = \varepsilon_{vol\text{-}Limit} \tag{9.51b}$$
$$\text{If } FS_{limit} \leq FS < 2: \quad \varepsilon_{vol} = b_x/[1/(2-FS) - m_x] \tag{9.51c}$$

The degree to which the approximate algorithms match with the laboratory data are reasonable, as evident from Figure 9.35.

这些近似算法与实验室数据的一致性如图9.35所示。

### 9.7.2 Case Study from Felipito Bridge, Mexico

9.7.2 墨西哥Felipito桥案例研究

At the prior cyclic liquefaction case study from the Felipito Bridges, Mexico site discussed in Section 9.5.1, vertical ground movements around the highway piers showed some 300 to 500 mm of settlement following liquefaction. Figure 9.36 shows the relative movement near Bent 6 at the site.

在第9.5.1节讨论的墨西哥Felipito桥的循环液化案例研究中，公路桥墩周围的垂直地面移动显示出液化后300至500毫米的沉降。图9.36显示了Bent 6附近的相对位移情况。

![image](images/rId353.png)

Figure 9.36: Photo at Bent 6 of Felipito Highway Bridge showing 30 to 50 cm of vertical displacements following soil liquefaction (from Turner et al. 2014).

图9.36：Felipito公路桥Bent 6处液化后30至50厘米的垂直位移照片（来自Turner等人，2014）

For the analysis of displacements following liquefaction, Figure 9.37 presents the cone resistance data, relative density, volumetric strains, and cumulative vertical displacements at Felipito. Using sounding CPT-01, a displacement of 400 mm compares with the observed range of movements (300 to 500 mm).

对于液化后的位移分析，图9.37展示了Felipito的锥阻力数据、相对密度、体积应变和累积垂直位移。使用探测CPT-01，计算的400毫米位移与观察到的移动范围（300至500毫米）一致。

![image](images/rId354.png)

Figure 9.37: Profiles at Felipito Bridges site, Mexico: (a) normalized cone resistance; (b) DR from CPT; (c) volumetric strains; and (d) cumulative vertical displacements with depth

图9.37：墨西哥Felipito桥现场的剖面图：（a）归一化锥阻力；（b）CPT的DR；（c）体积应变；（d）随深度累积的垂直位移

### 9.7.3 Lateral Displacements

9.7.3 横向位移

In sloping terrain and/or ground with a free-face condition, the occurrence of liquefaction can result in lateral spreading and horizontal movements downhill. The magnitudes of induced lateral displacements (LD) can be estimated from the maximum cyclic shear strains (max) which are integrated with depth to obtain the lateral displacement index (LDI):

在斜坡地形和/或自由面条件下，液化的发生可能导致横向扩展和下坡的水平移动。诱发的横向位移（LD）的大小可以通过最大循环剪应变（γ_max）进行估算，并与深度整合以获得横向位移指数（LDI）：

$$LDI = \int_0^z \gamma_{MAX} \, dz \tag{9.52}$$

The level of cyclic shear strains was studied under laboratory controlled conditions by Ishihara & Yoshimine (1992) who related magnitude of max to the factor of safety and sand relative density.

Ishihara & Yoshimine（1992）在实验室控制条件下研究了循环剪应变的水平，他们将γ_max的大小与安全系数和砂土相对密度相关联。

In addition, a limiting value of cyclic shear strains was established from the work of Seed (1976, 1979) and Housner et al. (1985). The resulting methodology is presented graphically in Figure 9.35 (Zhang et al, 2004; Robertson 2004).

此外，Seed（1976, 1979）和Housner等人（1985）的工作建立了循环剪应变的极限值。最终的方法如图9.35中图示（Zhang等人，2004；Robertson 2004）。

For gently sloping ground, depending upon the slope angle (0.2% < S < 3.5%), the calculated lateral displacements (LD) are obtained from:

对于缓坡地形，根据坡度角（0.2% < S < 3.5%），计算的横向位移（LD）如下：

$$LD/LDI = S + 0.2\,(\%) \tag{9.53}$$

For free-face conditions (Fig. 9.34), the geometry of distance from the CPT sounding location to the face location (L) and the height of the free face (H) are used to obtain the amount of lateral displacements (4 < L/H < 40):

$$LD/LDI = 6(H/L)^{0.8} \tag{9.54}$$

The following algorithms are used to approximate the laboratory results of Figure 9.35.

以下算法用于近似图9.35的实验室结果。

Define the Limiting value of Factor of Safety (LFS):

定义安全系数的极限值（LFS）：

$$LFS = 0.0002 \cdot (D_R)^2 - 0.0322 \cdot D_R + 1.768 \tag{9.55}$$

The limiting cyclic shear strain (LCSS):

循环剪应变的极限值（LCSS）：

$$LCSS = 144 - 3 \cdot D_R + 0.0164 \cdot (D_R)^2 \tag{9.56}$$

Calculate the Maximum Shear Strain (MSS):

计算最大剪应变（MSS）：

$$\text{If } FS < LFS: \quad MSS = LCSS \tag{9.57a}$$
$$\text{If } 1 < FS < 2: \quad MSS = +3.55 \cdot (FS) - 4.7 \tag{9.57b}$$
$$\text{If } FS \geq 2: \quad MSS = 0 \tag{9.57c}$$
$$\text{If } LFS \leq FS \leq 1: \quad MSS = LCSS - (LCSS - 3.5) \cdot (FS - LFS)/(1 - LFS) \tag{9.57d}$$

Reasonable agreement can be seen between the laboratory results and approximation algorithms in Figure 9.38.

图9.38显示了实验室结果与近似算法之间的合理一致性。

![image](images/rId361.png)

Figure 9.38. Relationship for Maximum Shear Strain (MSS = max) in terms of sand relative density (DR) and calculated factor of safety against liquefaction(FS).

图9.38：最大剪应变（MSS = γ_max）与砂土相对密度（DR）和液化安全系数（FS）之间的关系

## 9.8 Cyclic Resistance Using Shear Wave Velocity

9.8 使用剪切波速度评估循环抗剪强度

With SCPTU, the opportunity exists to use the shear wave velocity (Vs) to represent the ground resistance available to withstand cyclic liquefaction. In fact, the NCEER procedures offer a parallel method using the same procedure for CSR as given by equation 9.18 (Youd et al. 2001). Three CRR values are provided that depend upon the fines content (FC) which are presented in Figure 9.39 that can be expressed:

通过SCPTU，使用剪切波速度（Vs）来表示地面对抗循环液化的抗剪强度是可行的。事实上，NCEER程序提供了一种平行的方法，使用与9.18节中相同的CSR公式（Youd等人2001）。根据细粒含量（FC），提供了三个CRR值，如图9.39所示，可表示为：

$$CRR = a_v \cdot \left( \frac{V_{s1}}{100} \right)^2 + b_v \cdot \left( \frac{1}{V_{s1}^* - V_{s1}} - \frac{1}{V_{s1}^*} \right) \tag{9.58}$$

where av = 0.022 and bv = 2.8 are empirical fitting constants, Vs1 = Vs/(vo'/atm) 0.25 = normalized measured shear wave velocity, and Vs1* = 200, 210, and 220 m/s for values of FC = 35%, 20%, and 5%, respectively.

其中，a_v = 0.022和b_v = 2.8是经验拟合常数，Vs1 = Vs / (σ_vo' / σ_atm)^0.25 = 归一化测量剪切波速度，Vs1* = 200, 210和220 m/s，分别对应FC = 35%、20%和5%。

![image](images/rId363.png)

Figure 9.39: Deterministic CRS vs. Vs1 liquefaction curves using NCEER method (after Youd et al. 2001)

图9.39：使用NCEER方法的确定性CRS与Vs1的液化曲线（依据Youd等人2001）

The use of Vs1 also affords a separate and independent assessment of liquefaction potential for comparison to the evaluation made by cone resistance, either using Qtn,cs from NCEER or qc1N-cs from UCD approach.

使用Vs1还可以为液化潜力的独立评估提供一种独立的方法，用于与锥阻力评估进行比较，无论是使用NCEER方法的Qtn,cs还是UCD方法的qc1N-cs。

An updated database to the aforementioned was compiled by Kayen et al. (2013) who added 301 new liquefaction field case histories from China, Japan, Taiwan, Greece, and the USA to the NCEER case studies that formed a data set of 422 seismic records for statistical analyses. While the procedure for obtaining CSR differs because of a more complex rd calculation and the use of a different definition for MSW, the overall results can be generally appreciated by the number of data presented in Figure 9.40.

Kayen等人（2013）编制了上述更新数据库，增加了来自中国、日本、台湾、希腊和美国的301个新的液化现场案例历史，形成了一个包含422个地震记录的数据集，用于统计分析。尽管由于更复杂的rd计算和不同的MSW定义，获得CSR的过程有所不同，但可以通过图9.40中展示的数据来理解总体结果。

![image](images/rId364.png)

Figure 9.40: CSR with Vs1 and family of CRR curves for different probabilities of liquefaction (PL) from updated analyses by Kayen et al. (2013) using 422 seismic case studies

图9.40：Kayen等人（2013）使用422个地震案例研究更新分析的不同液化概率（PL）的CSR与Vs1和CRR曲线家族

# 10 Advanced Penetration Testing

# 10 先进的贯入测试

In addition to the cone penetrometer, piezocone, and seismic cone, specialized penetrometers have been developed to address specific needs, including: resistivity CPTU, vision cones, vibrocones, temperature cones, radio-isotope CPT, dielectric cones, as well as other sensors (Mayne 2007).

除了标准的静力触探仪（CPT）、孔压静力触探仪（CPTU）和地震静力触探仪（SCPTU）外，还开发了多种专门的探测器以满足特定需求，包括：电阻率CPTU、视觉锥、振动锥、温度锥、放射性同位素CPT和介电锥等传感器（Mayne 2007）。

In this section, some details will be given on the following CPT probes in use: (a) resistivity or electrical conductivity CPTU; (b) gamma cones; and (c) full flow penetrometers.
