本节将详细介绍以下几种CPT探针的使用情况：（a）电阻率或电导率CPTU；（b）伽马射线锥；以及（c）全流量贯入仪。

Of additional note, the field program can include modified testing procedures to quantify special nuances of soil behavior including: (a) variable rate testing (and related twitch tests); (b) continuous-interval shear wave velocity measurements; and (c) measurements of compression wave velocities with depth; as well as other methods.

此外，现场测试计划还可以包括修改的测试程序，以量化土体行为的特殊细微差异，包括：（a）变速测试（及相关的“微动”测试）；（b）连续间隔剪切波速度测量；以及（c）随深度变化的压缩波速度测量等方法。

## 10.1 Resistivity Piezocone (RCPTU) or Conductivity Cone

## 10.1 电阻率孔压静力触探（RCPTU）或电导率锥

Resistivity piezocone testing (RCPTU) finds use in geoenvironmental site investigations and coastal explorations. Resistivity ($\rho_b$) and its reciprocal, electrical conductivity ($\kappa = 1/\rho_b$), are a measure of the electrical characteristics of the ground and can be useful in assessing subsurface corrosion potential, soil contamination, and the interface between saltwater and fresh water (Campanella & Weemees, 1990; Pidlisecky et al. 2006). Resistivity profiles can also be interpreted to infer lithographic changes in soil layers and recently found to help identify sensitive and quick clays (Löfroth et al. 2013).

电阻率孔压静力触探测试（RCPTU）用于环境地质勘查和沿海勘探。电阻率（ρb）及其倒数，电导率（κ = 1/ρb），是衡量地层电学特性的指标，对评估地下腐蚀潜力、土壤污染以及盐水和淡水之间的界面非常有用（Campanella & Weemees, 1990; Pidlisecky et al. 2006）。电阻率剖面也可以用于推测土层中的岩性变化，最近发现其有助于识别敏感的和易流动的粘土（Löfroth et al. 2013）。

Resisitivity is measured in ohm-meters (Ω-m) and is a property of the medium (Campanella 2008). Both resistivity and electrical conductivity are direct current (DC) type measurements. Dielectric, or relative permittivity, is a similar type measurement but taken in terms of alternating current (AC).

电阻率以欧姆·米（Ω-m）为单位测量，是介质的固有属性（Campanella 2008）。电阻率和电导率都是直流（DC）类型的测量，介电常数或相对介电常数是类似的测量类型，但它是以交流（AC）的形式进行的。

Similar to surface resistivity surveys, the downhole measurement of resistivity employs paired sets of electrodes, usually in groups of four (either Wenner or Schlumberger arrays), but other configurations are also available. For resistivity piezocone testing (RCPTu), the electrodes can either be included as part of the penetrometer, or alternatively positioned axially in a trailing module behind the cone penetrometer. Figure 10.1 shows several of the available configurations used in RCPTu.

与地表电阻率勘测类似，钻孔中的电阻率测量也使用成对电极组，通常为四个一组（采用Wenner或Schlumberger阵列），但也可以使用其他配置。在电阻率孔压静力触探测试（RCPTU）中，电极可以作为探测器的一部分，或者轴向排列在探头后面的模块中。图10.1显示了RCPTU中使用的几种配置。

A representative resistivity piezocone sounding from the Opelika national geotechnical experimentation site is presented in Figure 10.2. The natural ground consists of residual fine sandy silts that were formed in place by the weathering and disintegration of schistose and gneissic bedrock of the Appalachian Piedmont geology. In general, the observed values of resistivity range between 100 and 400 ohm-m at this site.

图10.2展示了来自Opelika国家岩土实验场的典型电阻率孔压静力触探结果。该地层由风化和分解的片麻岩和片岩基岩形成的残积细砂质粉土组成。通常在该地，观察到的电阻率值范围在100至400欧姆·米之间。

![image](images/rId365.png)

Figure 10.1: Selected resistivity penetrometers and electrical conductivity probes

图10.1：选定的电阻率探测器和电导率探头

![image](images/rId366.png)

Figure 10.2: Illustrative example of RCPTU in residual sandy silt at Opelika, AL

图10.2：阿拉巴马州Opelika残积砂质粉土中的RCPTU示例

Resistivity testing is performed in conjunction with piezocone penetration testing with the addition of a module to determine the electrical resistivity of the soil. The module contains electrodes which are in direct contact with the surrounding soil/water/air matrix during penetration. The module measures the voltage drop between the electrodes during penetration in order to calculate the resistance of the surrounding soil. This resistance primarily depends upon the electrical conductivity of the pore-fluid within the soil.

电阻率测试与孔压静力触探测试相结合，增加了一个测定土壤电阻率的模块。该模块包含电极，在贯入过程中与周围的土壤/水/空气基质直接接触。该模块通过测量贯入过程中电极之间的电压降来计算周围土壤的电阻，该电阻主要取决于土壤孔隙液体的电导率。

The use of a resistivity module for geoenvironmental applications is described by Campanella (2008) where he presents a table of typical bulk-resistivity measurements made in different geoenvironmental conditions. In metal mine tailings with oxidized sulphide leachate, the bulk resistivity was 0.01 to 20 Ω-m, without oxidized sulphide leachate the bulk resistivity increased to 20 to 100 Ω-m. Once calibrated to the electrical properties of a given soil and fluid, the resistivity measurements can be used to calculate the saturation of the soil.

Campanella（2008）描述了电阻率模块在环境地质应用中的使用情况，并展示了在不同环境条件下测得的典型体积电阻率测量值。在含氧化硫化物浸出的金属矿尾矿中，体积电阻率为0.01至20Ω-m，而在无氧化硫化物浸出的情况下，体积电阻率增加到20至100Ω-m。一旦校准到给定土壤和液体的电学特性，电阻率测量值可以用于计算土壤的饱和度。

Figure 10.3 shows an RCPTU profile collected in the Fraser River Delta. This profile includes both the resistivity and the inverse of the resistivity, the conductivity. The resistivity profile shows significant dispersion above the ground water table. This is likely due to heterogeneous degrees of saturation. This dispersion is eliminated below the ground water table. The upper sand layer has a higher resistivity than the lower fine-grained layer. The resistivity profile can be useful for identifying soil layers and may be indicative of soil saturation.

图10.3展示了在弗雷泽河三角洲收集的RCPTU剖面。该剖面包括电阻率和电阻率的倒数——电导率。电阻率剖面显示了地下水位以上显著的离散性，这可能是由于不同的饱和度所致。这种离散性在地下水位以下被消除。上部砂层的电阻率高于下部细粒层。电阻率剖面对识别土层非常有用，并可能指示土壤的饱和度。

![image](images/rId367.png)

Figure 10.3: RCPTU profile in the Fraser River alluvial delta showing the relationship between resistivity and the ground water table and soil type

图10.3：弗雷泽河冲积三角洲中的RCPTU剖面，显示电阻率与地下水位和土壤类型之间的关系

## 10.2 Passive Gamma Piezocone Testing (GCPTU)

## 10.2 被动伽马射线孔压静力触探（GCPTU）

Gamma Cone Penetration Testing (GCPTU) uses a passive-gamma module attached behind the cone probe. The module housing contains a scintillating crystal that glows when it is struck by gamma rays. The gamma-module uses a photo-multiplier-tube and associated internal electronics to monitor this scintillating crystal. The observed gamma-ray incidents are counted up and used to calculate the gamma counts-per-second (cps), which is reported in the GCPTU soundings. Quantitative application of gamma measurements requires repeatability between soundings and equipment.

伽马射线孔压静力触探测试（GCPTU）使用附加在锥探头后面的被动伽马模块。模块外壳包含一个闪烁晶体，当伽马射线击中它时，它会发光。伽马模块使用光电倍增管和相关的内部电子设备来监测闪烁晶体。观察到的伽马射线事件被计数并用于计算GCPTU探测中报告的伽马计数率（cps）。伽马测量的定量应用需要在探测和设备之间具有可重复性。

There are three common Naturally Occurring Radioactive Minerals (NORMs). These are natural radioisotopes of Potassium (40K), Thorium (232Th), and Uranium (238U). These three radioisotopes exist in nature due to exceptionally long half-life times of a similar magnitude to the age of the Earth. Potassium is in illite and feldspar minerals, Thorium is associated with heavy minerals, and Uranium is found in uranium salts and organic shale (Ellis and Singer 2007).

三种常见的天然放射性矿物（NORMs）是钾的天然放射性同位素（40K）、钍（232Th）和铀（238U）。这些放射性同位素在自然界中存在，是因为它们具有极长的半衰期，接近地球的年龄。钾存在于伊利石和长石矿物中，钍与重矿物相关，而铀则存在于铀盐和有机页岩中（Ellis and Singer 2007）。

Gamma piezocone testing has proven particularly useful in Oil Sand Tailings (OST) deposits. The mineralogical constituents of bitumen ore remain in the tailings by-product and gamma profiling can identify different tailings units. The gamma measurements in these tailings are proportional to the total fines content (weight of fines / total weight) due to the potassium in the illite mineral. It is not proportional to the geotechnical fines content (weight of fines / weight of solids). The total fines content is proportional to the in-situ concentration of 40K in OST.

伽马孔压静力触探在油砂尾矿（OST）沉积物中特别有用。沥青矿石的矿物成分残留在尾矿副产品中，伽马剖面可以识别不同的尾矿单元。这些尾矿中的伽马测量与细粒总含量（细粒重量/总重量）成正比，这是由于伊利石矿物中的钾含量所致。

The generated gamma rays from these natural sources will interact with matter. This interaction makes it possible to observe gamma rays in-situ. The propagating gamma rays can re-coil, lose energy, and change direction in a process known as Compton scattering. When the gamma ray energy drop below 100 keV, the ray can be extinguished by photoelectric absorption. Gamma rays with energies higher than 1022 keV and split into two rays of 511 keV in a process known as pair-production. (Berger 1961) presents table of energy absorption coefficients that averages these three matter interaction effects. For a typical density of tailings a generated gamma ray from 40K may travel from 10 to 20 cm. This means that the GCPTU gamma profile responds to the same soil measured by the cone penetration test.

生成的伽马射线与物质相互作用，这种相互作用使得可以在现场观测伽马射线。传播中的伽马射线可能会在康普顿散射过程中反冲、失去能量并改变方向。当伽马射线能量降至100 keV以下时，射线可能会被光电吸收而消失。能量高于1022 keV的伽马射线会在一对产生过程中分裂成两个511 keV的射线（Berger 1961）提供了一张能量吸收系数的表格，平均考虑了这些物质相互作用的效果。对于典型的尾矿密度，来自40K的伽马射线可以传播10到20厘米。这意味着GCPTU伽马剖面反映了与静力触探测试测量的相同土层。

Gamma levels (counts/s) are not material properties and are significantly affected by the design of the probe. To provide a quantitative measure for ongoing assessment, careful and consistent manufacturing of the probe is required. Calibration procedures are also required to maintain assurance that each probe is performing uniformly and in a predictable way.

伽马水平（计数/秒）不是材料的固有属性，受到探头设计的显著影响。为了提供定量评估，必须谨慎且一致地制造探头，同时需要校准程序来确保每个探头的性能一致且可预测。

GCPTU profiles in oil sand tailings (OST) may include recycle water, fluid tailings, soil-like tailings, and natural ground. The typical measurements in these types of materials can be used to identify the tailings units and to estimate OST properties – such as solids content and fines content. Figure 10.4 shows an example GCPTU profile through OST.

在油砂尾矿（OST）中，GCPTU剖面可能包括回收水、流体尾矿、类似土壤的尾矿和天然地层。在这些类型的材料中，典型的测量值可以用于识别尾矿单元，并估算OST的特性——如固体含量和细粒含量。图10.4展示了一个通过OST的GCPTU剖面示例。

![image](images/rId368.png)

Figure 10.4: Representative GCPTU profile in oil sand tailings through recycle water, fluid tailings, and soil-like tailings

图10.4：通过回收水、流体尾矿和类似土壤的尾矿的油砂尾矿中的代表性GCPTU剖面

GCPTU measurements in OST recycle water produce an easily identifiable GCPTU profile. First, the corrected tip resistance, qt, equals the pore pressure $u_2$. The friction sleeve, fs, is nearly zero. The pore pressure channel, $u_2$, increases at approximately 9.8 kPa per meter (i.e., kN/m3 ) in pure water and slightly higher in saline water.

在OST回收水中的GCPTU测量会产生一个容易识别的GCPTU剖面。首先，校正后的锥尖阻力qt等于孔隙压力$u_2$。摩擦套管fs几乎为零。在纯水中，孔隙压力通道$u_2$每米增加约9.8 kPa（即kN/m³），在盐水中略高。

Recycle water has negligible concentrations of natural radioisotopes. The observed passive gamma rates are nearly 0 cps. There can be an increase in gamma rates at the pond surface due to atmospheric radiation. Over a very short depth, the impact of atmospheric radiation is completely shielded by the recycle water.

回收水中天然放射性同位素的浓度可以忽略不计。观察到的被动伽马计数率几乎为0 cps。在池塘表面，由于大气辐射可能会导致伽马计数率的增加，但在很浅的深度内，大气辐射的影响会被回收水完全屏蔽。

Hitting the mudline typically has no discernable effect on qt measurements. The friction sleeve, fs, will still be nearly 0 kPa. The slope of $u_2$ will begin to deviate from 9.8 kN/m3 ; but this change can be nearly imperceptible. Consequently, the standard cone probe results cannot be used to identify the mudline depth with any degree of confidence. However, below the mudline, the tailings contain a concentration of natural radioisotopes. Therefore, the mudline corresponds with a sharp increase in passive gamma rates. The GCPTU can be used to easily identify the mudline depth.

击中泥线通常不会对qt测量产生明显影响。摩擦套管fs仍然几乎为0 kPa。$u_2$的斜率将开始偏离9.8 kN/m³，但这种变化几乎不可察觉。因此，标准锥探头结果无法确定泥线深度。然而，在泥线以下，尾矿中含有一定浓度的天然放射性同位素。因此，泥线对应着被动伽马计数率的急剧增加。GCPTU可以轻松识别泥线深度。

All gamma ray measurement devices have an efficiency factor that reduces the in-situ concentration of gamma rays to a measured gamma rate. This efficiency factor depends on the module shielding, module electronics, and scintillating crystal geometry and type. By sheer coincidence, the ConeTec passive gamma module has an efficiency factor that produces measured gamma rates approximately equal to the total fines content in fluid oil sand tailings.

The mudline definition of 5% solids corresponds to a gamma rate of 5 cps.

所有伽马射线测量设备都有一个效率因子，将原位伽马射线浓度转换为测量的伽马计数率。这个效率因子取决于模块屏蔽、模块电子设备以及闪烁晶体的几何形状和类型。碰巧，ConeTec被动伽马模块的效率因子使得测得的伽马计数率大约等于流体油砂尾矿中的细粒总含量。泥线定义为5%的固体含量，对应的伽马计数率为5 cps。

The froth-treatment process concentrates minerals containing 232Th and 238U radioisotopes that naturally occur in the parent ore body. The passive gamma measurements in fluid froth treatment tailings are not proportional to the total fines content. In froth-treatment tailings we measure passive gamma rates that are an order of magnitude larger than conventional OST tailings. While this removes one application of the GCPTU site investigation tool, it adds another. The GCPTU profiles can be used to easily identify depth-ranges of froth-treatment affected tailings.

泡沫处理过程会浓缩含有天然232Th和238U放射性同位素的矿物，这些同位素自然存在于母矿体中。流体泡沫处理尾矿中的被动伽马测量不与细粒总含量成正比。在泡沫处理尾矿中，测得的被动伽马计数率比常规OST尾矿高出一个数量级。虽然这削弱了GCPTU现场调查工具的一个应用，但却增加了另一个应用。GCPTU剖面可以轻松识别受泡沫处理影响的尾矿深度范围。

Other uses of GCPTU include: (a) fines detection in clays having radioactive emitting elements; and (b) hazardous waste detection in man-made deposits containing hazardous elements.

GCPTU的其他用途包括：（a）检测含有放射性元素的粘土中的细粒；以及（b）在含有危险元素的人造沉积物中检测危险废物。

## 10.3 Continuous Interval SCPTU

## 10.3 连续间隔SCPTU

Employing an autoseis generator, continuous-interval shear wave (CiVs) measurements can be obtained such that the shear wave velocity readings become comparable and as frequent as the cone penetrometer values, on the order of several centimeters (McGillivray & Mayne 2008; Styler & Mayne 2013; Ku, et al. 2013a; 2013b). The continuous-interval seismic piezocone test (CiSCPTU) is the fastest method for collecting a full suite of data in soils.

使用自动地震发生器，可以进行连续间隔剪切波（CiVs）测量，使得剪切波速度的读数可以与锥探测器的数据一样频繁，达到几厘米的间隔（McGillivray & Mayne 2008；Styler & Mayne 2013；Ku等2013a；2013b）。连续间隔地震孔压静力触探测试（CiSCPTU）是获取土壤全面数据的最快方法。

The many recorded wavelets from geophone readings during CiVs taken in Norfolk, Virginia are shown as a summary plot in Figure 10.5. The main arrivals of the shear waves can be seen as a "waterfall" evident in the profile to the final termination depth at 22 m. A visual examination clearly shows a change in soil layering at around 9 m depth. These wavelets can be post-processed using a matching process with MatLab or other software to develop a near-continuous profile of Vs with depth (Ku et al. 2013b).

图10.5展示了在弗吉尼亚州诺福克进行的CiVs测量期间，通过地震仪读取的多个波列的汇总图。剪切波的主要到达时间在剖面中以“瀑布”形式显现，终止深度为22米。视觉检查清楚地显示出大约9米深处土层的变化。可以使用MatLab或其他软件的匹配过程对这些波列进行后处理，以生成随深度变化的近乎连续的Vs剖面（Ku等2013b）。

![image](images/rId369.png)

Figure 10.5: Wavelet recordings from continuous-interval Vs measurements in Norfolk, VA

图10.5：弗吉尼亚州诺福克连续间隔Vs测量的波列记录

The full CiSCPTu sounding from Norfolk, Virginia is shown in Figure 10.6 with profiles of four continuous readings with depth: cone tip resistance (qt), sleeve friction (fs), penetration porewater pressure ($u_2$), and shear wave velocity (Vs). Also shown are results from conventional downhole tests (DST) with paired left- and right-strikes to obtain Vs at one-meter intervals. This offers an economic, expedient, and efficient means to procure information on the geostratigraphy, soil engineering parameters, and geophysical data in a single sounding. It is noteworthy that both the continuous Vs and conventional DST results capture the significant step up in velocity at the Holocene–Yorktown interface at a 9m depth. Specifically, the upper Holocene sediments at this site show Vs values of around 130 to 160 m/s, whereas the lower Miocene deposits exhibit higher Vs between 240 to 340 m/s.

图10.6展示了弗吉尼亚州诺福克的完整CiSCPTU探测结果，其中包括四个随深度变化的连续读数：锥尖阻力（qt）、侧壁摩擦力（fs）、贯入孔隙水压力（$u_2$）和剪切波速度（Vs）。图中还展示了通过左右打击成对获取的一米间隔Vs结果。这为在一次探测中获取地层、土壤工程参数和地球物理数据提供了一种经济、快捷且有效的手段。值得注意的是，连续Vs和传统DST结果都捕捉到了在9米深处的全新世-约克镇界面速度的显著上升。具体来说，该地点的上层全新世沉积物显示出Vs值约为130至160 m/s，而下层中新世沉积物的Vs值在240至340 m/s之间。

![image](images/rId370.png)

Figure 10.6: Results of CiSCPTu sounding at Norfolk, VA: (a) cone tip resistance, (b) sleeve friction, (c) porewater pressure, and (d) shear wave velocity (Ku et al. 2013b)

图10.6：弗吉尼亚州诺福克CiSCPTU探测结果：（a）锥尖阻力，(b) 侧壁摩擦力，(c) 孔隙水压力，(d) 剪切波速度（Ku等2013b）

## 10.4 Compression Wave Measurements

## 10.4 压缩波测量

There are two types of seismic body waves: shear (S-wave) and compression (P-wave).

Compression wave velocities are obtained though down-hole seismic testing in boreholes or during seismic cone penetration tests. Compression wave investigations require different seismic sources, receivers, and data processing than shear wave investigations.

地震体波有两种类型：剪切波（S波）和压缩波（P波）。压缩波速度通过钻孔内的地震测试或在地震锥贯入测试期间获得。压缩波调查需要不同于剪切波调查的地震源、接收器和数据处理方法。

Compression wave velocities depend on the bulk modulus of the soil. The very small strain elastic bulk modulus of the soil is sensitive to full saturation. Compression wave velocity profiling is a site-investigation technique that can be used to identify fully saturated soil zones. Compression wave velocities can be combined with shear wave velocities to calculate the Poisson ratio (Section 7.5.2).

压缩波速度取决于土壤的体积模量。土壤的微应变弹性体积模量对完全饱和状态非常敏感。压缩波速度剖面是一种可以用于识别完全饱和土壤区域的现场调查技术。压缩波速度可以与剪切波速度结合起来计算泊松比（见第7.5.2节）。

It is much more challenging to obtain compression wave velocities than shear wave velocities. It requires more effort and field experience in order to confirm that compression waves are in the observed traces. Downward propagating compression waves can lose significant fractions of their energy at impedance boundaries due to reflections, refraction, and the generation of shear waves at the boundary. These impedance boundaries are simply sharp changes in the VP profile.

获得压缩波速度比获得剪切波速度更具挑战性。需要更多的努力和现场经验来确认观测到的波列中确实存在压缩波。向下传播的压缩波在阻抗边界处会由于反射、折射以及在边界处产生剪切波而失去大量能量。这些阻抗边界通常是Vp剖面中的突然变化。

Compression wave profiling requires the use of different seismic sources than the sources used to generate shear waves. It requires consideration for the offset of the seismic source on the ground surface. Data reduction is also complicated by interference from different seismic wave forms. Data reduction must also resolve high compression wave velocities, which magnifies any errors in the interpretation propagation times.

压缩波剖面要求使用与剪切波不同的地震源，并需要考虑地震源在地表的偏移。数据处理也因不同地震波形的干扰而变得复杂，必须解析高压缩波速度，这会放大传播时间解释中的任何误差。

Compression waves can be generated by vertically striking a metal plate on the ground surface with a sledgehammer or by using geophysical MASW sources. These compression waves are observed in-situ using seismic sensors within the cone probe. Butcher et al. (2005) state that these sensors should be vertically orientated to permit the assessment of compression waves.

压缩波可以通过用大锤垂直敲击地表上的金属板或使用地球物理MASW源生成。这些压缩波通过安装在锥探头内的地震传感器进行现场观测。Butcher等人（2005）指出，这些传感器应垂直布置，以便评估压缩波。

Styler et al. (2016) examined both vertical and horizontal geophones for the compression wave characterization. Figure 10.7 shows the recorded wave traces from the 9 m offset to a seismic

![image](images/rId371.png)

Figure 10.7: Compression wave response on X and Z orientated geophones from a source offset of 9 m at a site in Richmond, BC from Styler et al. (2016)

图10.7：不列颠哥伦比亚省里士满9米偏移震源的X和Z方向地震仪上的压缩波响应波列（来自Styler等人，2016）

testing depth of 40 m in Richmond, BC. The compression wave was observed with both the horizontal X geophone and vertical Z geophone. The Y-geophone, which was not aligned with the wave propagation direction, did not respond to the compression wave.

Styler等人（2016）研究了垂直和水平地震仪对压缩波的特性。图10.7显示了在不列颠哥伦比亚省里士满进行的地震测试中，9米偏移的震源在X和Z方向地震仪上的压缩波响应波列。压缩波在X方向地震仪和Z方向地震仪上均有观测到，而未与波传播方向对齐的Y方向地震仪未对压缩波作出反应。

Laing (1985) observed a wave velocity of 6250 m/s using vertical orientated accelerometers. This was much faster than the anticipated 1400 to 1600 m/s compression wave velocity through fully saturated soil. She concluded that it was a wave traveling through the steel cone rods. Styler et al. (2016) also observed these rod-waves in a test at Kamloops, BC. This test was performed with a source offset of 1.8 m. These rod-waves are identified in Figure 10.8.

Laing（1985）使用垂直布置的加速度计观察到6250 m/s的波速，这远高于通过完全饱和土壤时预计的1400至1600 m/s的压缩波速度。她得出结论，这是一种通过钢锥杆传播的波。Styler等人（2016）在不列颠哥伦比亚省坎卢普斯进行的测试中也观察到了这些杆波。该测试的震源偏移为1.8米。这些杆波如图10.8所示。

![image](images/rId372.png)

Figure 10.8: Compression wave response on a vertical geophone from a source offset of 1.8 m showing a wave through rods between 10 and 15 m from Styler et al. (2016)

图10.8：来自不列颠哥伦比亚省坎卢普斯1.8米偏移震源的垂直地震仪上的压缩波响应波列，显示在10至15米之间的杆波（来自Styler等人，2016）

Gillespie (1990) reported that compression waves have frequencies between 400 and 600 Hz, however, this is found not to be the case in many circumstances. Styler et al. (2016) report on a compression wave testing using X and Z accelerometers in Richmond, BC. These results are presented in Figure 10.9, which shows an easily observed compression wave and the frequency contents of the compression waves were mostly less than 250 Hz.

Gillespie（1990）报告说压缩波的频率在400至600 Hz之间，但在许多情况下并非如此。Styler等人（2016）在不列颠哥伦比亚省里士满进行的X和Z加速度计上的压缩波测试结果显示，压缩波频率大多低于250 Hz，如图10.9所示。

![image](images/rId373.png)

Figure 10.9: Compression wave response from horizontal and vertical accelerometers with a source offset of 3 m from Styler et al. (2016)

图10.9：来自不列颠哥伦比亚省里士满3米偏移震源的水平和垂直加速度计上的压缩波响应波列（来自Styler等人，2016）

Compression wave profiling can be challenging, but it is not insurmountable. It requires more attention and effort in the field data collection. It requires careful examination of the measured traces in order to identify the compression waves from the shear waves, rod-waves, and any reflected or refracted waves in the traces. Compression waves can be obtained to significant depths. Figure 10.10 presents a profile of compression waves observed to depths in excess of 80 m. Full saturation is clearly indicated at the break-point in the compression wave arrivals.

压缩波剖面测量具有挑战性，但并非不可逾越。它需要在现场数据收集中更加注意和投入。需要仔细检查测得的波列，以便从中识别出压缩波、杆波以及任何反射或折射波。压缩波可以在相当大的深度获得。图10.10展示了在超过80米深度处观察到的压缩波剖面。压缩波到达的拐点清晰地表明了完全饱和的状态。

![image](images/rId374.png)

Figure 10.10: Compression waves observed to a depth in excess of 80 m (processed with a 0-200 Hz bandpass filter)

## 10.5 Full-Flow Penetrometers

## 10.5 全流动贯入仪

A number of full-flow penetrometers were introduced to better profile strengths in very soft offshore sediments where su < 10 kPa (200 psf), including plate, T-bar, and ball-type (Randolph 2004), as well as hemi-ball and toroidal versions (Yan et al. 2011). The family of full-flow probes is depicted in Figure 10.11. In essence, these devices are electronic cone penetrometers outfitted with much larger heads having a cross sectional area = 100 cm2 (15.5 in2 ) that replace the standard yet smaller 10-cm2 (1.55 in2 ) 60° angled conical tip. The probe head designs are also intended to be a special shape to facilitate soft soils to flow completely around it. In addition to use in very soft offshore clays, the T-bar and ball penetrometers have found applicability in soft mine tailings and slimes, as well as very soft natural onshore deposits.

为了更好地剖析非常软的海洋沉积物（其不排水抗剪强度su < 10 kPa或200 psf）的强度，开发了多种全流动贯入仪，包括板式、T型杆和球型贯入仪（Randolph 2004），以及半球形和环状贯入仪（Yan et al. 2011）。图10.11展示了全流动探针的系列。这些设备本质上是带有更大头部的电子锥贯入仪，其横截面积为100 cm²（15.5 in²），替代了标准的10 cm²（1.55 in²）60°锥形尖头。探针头的设计还具有特殊形状，以便软土可以完全绕其流动。除了在非常软的海洋黏土中使用外，T型杆和球形贯入仪还被应用于软矿尾矿、泥浆以及非常软的陆地天然沉积物中。

For direct comparison, Figure 10.12 shows the standard cone penetrometer, T-bar, plate, and ball penetrometers. An advantage of full-flow penetrometers includes a higher resolution of the load cell since the force is directed over a larger cross-sectional area and a minimization of the porewater pressure correction for unequal end areas.

图10.12展示了标准的锥贯入仪、T型杆、板式和球形贯入仪的对比。全流动贯入仪的优势包括负载传感器的分辨率更高，因为力作用在更大的横截面上，并且由于不等端面面积的孔隙水压力修正减少，降低了不确定性。

Testing standards for the equipment and procedures for field operation of full-flow penetrometer probes are given by DeJong et al. (2010a).

DeJong等（2010a）提供了全流动贯入仪探针的设备和现场操作的测试标准。

![image](images/rId375.png)

Figure 10.11: Family of full-flow penetrometers for very low strength soils

图10.11：用于非常低强度土壤的全流动贯入仪系列

![image](images/rId376.png)

Figure 10.12: View of T-bar, plate, ball, and cone penetrometers (courtesy Jason DeJong)

图10.12：T型杆、板式、球形和锥贯入仪的视图（由Jason DeJong提供）

### 10.5.1 T-Bar

### 10.5.1 T型杆

Beyond profiling strengths in offshore sediments, the T-bar has found good use in characterizing the in-situ undrained shear strength of very soft soils, including natural sensitive clays, mine tailings, slurries, and slimes (DeJong et al. 2010b). The equation for estimating the peak undrained shear strength of soft clays from the measured resistance of a T-bar penetration test (TBT) takes the form:

除了剖析海洋沉积物的强度外，T型杆还广泛用于表征非常软土壤的原位不排水抗剪强度，包括天然敏感黏土、矿尾矿、泥浆和泥液（DeJong等2010b）。从T型杆贯入测试（TBT）测得的阻力估算软黏土峰值不排水抗剪强度的方程为：

$$s_u = \frac{q_{Tbar}}{N_{Tbar}} \tag{10.1}$$

where the bearing factor NTbar depends on shearing mode. For instance, NTbar can take on a value of 10.5 for triaxial compression (suc), while a value of 12 is more suited to an averaged suAVE (average of triaxial compression, simple shear, and triaxial extension) have been recommended based on a comprehensive testing program (Low et al. 2010). Site specific calibration of the bearing factor for a particular geomaterial under study is certainly warranted (DeJong et al. 2010a).

其中，承载系数NTbar取决于剪切模式。例如，NTbar对于三轴压缩（suc）可取10.5，而对于平均suAVE（综合考虑三轴压缩、简单剪切和三轴延伸）建议值为12（Low et al. 2010）。对于特定地质材料的承载系数进行现场校准是合理的（DeJong等2010a）。

With the CPTU in very soft, normally to lightly overconsolidated sediments, $\sigma_{v0}$ can be a significant proportion of qt. The measured porewater pressures can also be in a similar magnitude as qt. These effects reduce the certainty in determining the proportion of the cone tip resistance due to the soil response. This increases the uncertainty in the estimated values of su and are considered the likely reason for the large scatter in many published Nkt values.

在非常软的、正常固结到轻度超固结的沉积物中，CPTU中的初始垂直应力σv0可能占qt的很大一部分。测量到的孔隙水压力也可能与qt相当。这些因素增加了确定锥尖阻力中土壤响应部分的难度，从而增加了对su估值的不确定性，可能导致许多已发表的Nkt值分布较大。

In an effort to reduce inaccuracies due to these large corrections and to continue to achieve a continuous profile of resistance, full-flow testing with a T-bar tip was first introduced in centrifuge testing (Stewart 1991) and then in the field (Stewart and Randolph 1994). Since its introduction, field testing has been carried out at well-characterized sites in Australia (Chung and Randolph 2004), Norway (Lunne et al. 2005), Ireland (Long and Gudjonsson 2004), and the USA (DeJong et al. 2004). Subsequently the use of the Ball penetrometer, a spherical ball mounted on the end of the push rods (Chung and Randolph 2004; DeJong et al. 2008) has become more common.

为了减少由于这些大修正引起的不准确性，并继续获得连续的阻力剖面，首次在离心试验中引入了T型杆尖端的全流动测试（Stewart 1991），随后在现场应用（Stewart and Randolph 1994）。自引入以来，现场测试已在澳大利亚（Chung and Randolph 2004）、挪威（Lunne等2005）、爱尔兰（Long and Gudjonsson 2004）和美国（DeJong等2004）的已知站点上进行了测试。随后，球形贯入仪（一个安装在推杆末端的球形探针，Chung and Randolph 2004；DeJong等2008）也逐渐普及。

Figure 10.13 shows a comparison of the undrained shear strength profiles in soft clays at a site in North Charleston, SC using both the flat plate dilalometer test (DMT) and T-bar test. Beneath the crustal layer at depths below 2 m, the agreement in the evaluated profile of su is quite good between the two tests.

图10.13展示了在南卡罗来纳州北查尔斯顿的一个站点，通过平板扩散测试（DMT）和T型杆测试获得的软黏土中的不排水抗剪强度剖面的对比。可以看到，在2米以下的表层，两个测试所得的su剖面一致性较好。

![image](images/rId378.png)

Figure 10.13: Comparison of T-bar and DMT strength profiling in soft soil in South Carolina (data from Cargill & Camp 2010)

图10.13：南卡罗来纳州软土中T型杆和DMT强度剖面的比较（数据来自Cargill & Camp 2010）

In addition to measuring peak strengths, a cyclic phase of testing can be performed at selected depths to evaluate the remolded strength, thus determine the sensitivity (St) of soft clay soils.

While the standard rate of penetration for T-bar is identical to the CPT at 20 mm/s (0.8 in/s), variable rate testings can also be applied to investigate both strain rate behavior and drainage conditions (i.e., fully undrained, partially drained, and full drained).

除了测量峰值强度外，还可以在选定深度进行循环测试以评估重塑后的强度，从而确定软黏土的灵敏度（St）。虽然T型杆的标准贯入速率与CPT相同（为20 mm/s或0.8 in/s），但也可以采用可变速率测试，以研究应变率行为和排水条件（即完全不排水、部分排水和完全排水）。

### 10.5.2 Ball Penetrometer

### 10.5.2 球形贯入仪

As the T-bar is non-symmetric about one horizontal axis, recent preference has been drawn towards the fully symmetric Ball penetrometer which is also a type of full-flow probe for very soft soils. The basic principles are similar for both devices.

由于T型杆在一个水平轴上是非对称的，近年来越来越倾向于使用完全对称的球形贯入仪，这也是一种用于非常软土的全流动探针。两个设备的基本原理相似。

The determination of undrained shear strength from the ball penetration test (BPT) is similar to that for the T-bar, except that the bearing factor Nball is used. For suc, a value of Nball = 9.5 is characteristic of soft clays, while a value of 11 found for suAVE (Low et al. 2010). A comparison of the very good agreement between su profiles from ball and VST are shown in Figure 10.14 for very soft soils in Northern Alberta.

从球形贯入测试（BPT）中确定不排水抗剪强度的方法类似于T型杆，但使用了不同的承载系数Nball。对于软黏土，Nball的值为9.5，而suAVE的值为11（Low et al. 2010）。图10.14展示了在阿尔伯塔北部非常软的土壤中，球形贯入仪和VST的su剖面的非常好的一致性。

![image](images/rId379.png)

Figure 10.14: Comparison of T-bar and VST strength profiling in very soft soils

图10.14：T型杆和VST在非常软的土壤中的强度剖面比较

The ball penetrometer can also be subjected to cycles to determine the sensitivity of fine-grained soils, in a manner similar to T-bar testing (Yafrate et al. 2007). In research efforts, the ball penetrometer has been outfitted with a porewater pressure transducer that is connected to a filter element, located either at the frontal apex or various equatorial positions on the sphere, to provide additional information useful in site characterization of the soft ground (Peuchen et al. 2005; Long et al. 2014).

球形贯入仪还可以进行循环测试，以确定细粒土壤的灵敏度，这与T型杆测试相似（Yafrate等2007）。在研究中，球形贯入仪配备了一个孔隙水压力传感器，该传感器连接到位于球体前端或赤道位置的滤元件，以提供有助于软地基现场表征的额外信息（Peuchen等2005；Long等2014）。

The ball penetration resistance is interpreted using empirical coefficients by relating the results to VST or laboratory undrained shear strengths. This is shown in Equation 10.2. Typical values for Nball range from 9 to 12, a much tighter range than the CPT Nkt factors.

球形贯入阻力通过将结果与VST或实验室不排水抗剪强度相关联，使用经验系数进行解释。典型的Nball值范围为9至12，比CPT的Nkt因素范围更窄。

$$N_{ball} = \frac{q_{ball} - \sigma_{v0}}{(s_u)_{fv}} \tag{10.2}$$

The Nkt and Nball factors have been observed to decrease with increasing vane sensitivity (DeJong et al. 2011). This suggests that the progressive failure mechanism that lowers the peak vane strength is also responsible for lowering the penetration resistance, otherwise these factors would increase.

The larger surface area of the ball engages a larger volume of in-situ soil to provide a penetration resistance. It will average out any minor lenses in the deposit. Boulanger and DeJong (2018) reviews thin layer identification with CPT and notes that the penetration resistance is strongly influenced by soils within 10 to 30 cone diameters from the tip. If that same ratio is applied to a 100 cm2 ball (diameter 11.3 cm) then it is averaging the soil response within 1.1 to 3.4 m.

Nk 和 Nball 因子被观察到随着旋剪灵敏度的增加而降低（DeJong et al. 2011）。这表明，降低旋剪峰值强度的渐进破坏机制也会降低贯入阻力，否则这些因子应会增加。球形贯入仪较大的表面积使其能够覆盖更大的原位土体体积，从而提供一个更全面的贯入阻力。这可以平滑土层中的细小透镜状夹层影响。Boulanger 和 DeJong（2018）回顾了使用CPT进行薄层识别的研究，并指出贯入阻力主要受到锥尖直径10到30倍范围内的土体影响。如果将同样的比例应用于直径为11.3 cm、面积为100 cm²的球体，那么它对土壤响应的平均范围为1.1至3.4米。

Therefore, the BPT is ideal for obtaining average undrained strengths for thick layers, it cannot be used to characterize thin layers. The ball penetrometer shown in Figure 10.15 includes a friction sleeve. The friction measurements are in the shadow of the ball and are generally not used in any interpretations as the soil has been significantly affected from soil flowing around the ball. The pore pressure is also measured immediately behind the ball in order to calculate the systematic unequal end-area corrections. These pore pressure measurements should also not be used empirically with CPT based interpretations methods.

因此，球形贯入仪（BPT）非常适合用于厚层土体的平均不排水强度测定，但不适用于表征薄层土体。图10.15中显示的球形贯入仪配备有摩擦套筒。由于土壤在球体周围流动，摩擦测量位于球体的阴影中，通常不用于解释。此外，孔隙压力也在球体后面测量，以计算系统不等端面积修正。这些孔隙压力测量不应与基于CPT的解释方法一起使用。

![image](images/rId381.png)

Figure 10.15: Profile comparing VST results against cyclic ball testing using Nball = 10.5 reported by Schaeffers and Weemees (2012)

图 10.15：VST 结果与球形循环测试结果的对比剖面，使用 Nball = 10.5（由 Schaeffers 和 Weemees 在 2012 年报告）

While the ball penetrometer has lost two measurements to infer soil properties, it gains a measurement through cyclic testing. Cyclic ball penetrometer testing can be performed to remold the in-situ soil. This enables a direct measurement of a remolded strength and a calculation of soil sensitivity. Figure 10.15 shows an undrained vane strength profile calculated from a net ball penetration resistance. This includes results from ball penetration cycles and from remolded vane shear testing.

这种设计虽然减少了推断土壤性质的两个测量值，但通过循环测试获得了额外的测量数据。可以进行循环球形贯入测试以重塑原位土壤，从而直接测量重塑后的强度，并计算土壤的灵敏度。图10.15展示了根据球形贯入阻力计算的不排水旋剪强度剖面，包括了球形贯入循环测试和重塑旋剪测试的结果。

The BPT has a number of advantages as a site-investigation tool. These advantages include:

• Efficient profiling of peak undrained strength

• Efficient profiling of remolded undrained strength through cyclic testing

• Increased confidence in undrained strength results in soft soils due to a reduction in the relative magnitude of systematic corrections

球形贯入仪作为现场调查工具具有以下优势：

高效的峰值不排水强度剖面测定

通过循环测试高效测定重塑不排水强度

由于系统修正相对幅度较小，提高了软土中不排水强度结果的可信度

The BPT is a specialized tool. It cannot be used as widely as the CPT.

The disadvantages of the BPT compared to the CPT are:

• Increased penetration resistance can reach shallow refusal

• Unable to punch through some stronger layers that may overlie weaker soils

• Only applicable to soft soils and requires prior knowledge of the site

• Loss of informative friction and pore water pressure measurements due to lack of empirical experience compared to the CPT

• Inability to characterize thin layers due to large zone of soil reaction

然而，球形贯入仪是一种专用工具，其适用范围不如CPT广泛。与CPT相比，球形贯入仪的缺点包括：

增加的贯入阻力可能导致浅层拒穿

无法穿透覆盖在软土层上的某些较硬土层

仅适用于软土，且需要对场地有先验知识

由于经验较少，损失了有价值的摩擦和孔隙水压力测量

由于土壤反应区较大，无法表征薄层土体

## 10.6 Variable Rate Penetration Testing

## 10.6 可变速率贯入测试

At the standard rate of 20 mm/s, CPTU conditions are considered to represent fully drained behavior in sands ($\Delta u$ = 0), while in contrast, taken to be fully undrained response in clays ($\Delta V$ = 0). In silts and mixed soil types, however, a partial drainage condition can predominate. As such, variable rate cone penetration testing (VRCPT) can be implemented to define regions of drained versus partially-drained versus undrained soil behavior, as outlined by Randolph (2004). The variable rate tests can also be performed with T-bar and/or ball or piezo-ball penetrometers.

在标准的20 mm/s速率下，CPTU测试条件通常被认为在砂土中表现为完全排水行为（Δu = 0），而在黏土中则表现为完全不排水行为（ΔV = 0）。然而，在粉土和混合土层中，部分排水条件可能占主导地位。因此，可以通过可变速率锥贯入测试（VRCPT）来定义土壤的排水、部分排水和不排水行为区域（Randolph 2004）。这种可变速率测试也可以与T型杆、球形或压电球形贯入仪一起进行。

In VRCPT, the entire sounding can be performed using a constant rate of penetration that is compared with the results from a sounding at the standard rate. Figure 10.16 shows results from a series of VRCPTU conducted at different rates in Bassendean silt which has a mean grain size of around 70 microns (Suzuki 2015). Rates of CPTU were applied from 20, 2, 0.2, 0.02, and 0.002 mm/s. For soft soils, as the rate decreases from 20 mm/s, a general trend is to observe the qt increase while the porewater pressure $u_2$ reading decreases.

在VRCPT中，整个探测可以使用恒定的贯入速率进行，并将结果与标准速率下的探测结果进行比较。图10.16展示了在Bassendean粉土中进行的一系列VRCPTU结果，该粉土的平均粒径约为70微米（Suzuki 2015）。CPTU速率从20 mm/s逐渐降低到0.002 mm/s。对于软土，随着速率的降低，通常会观察到qt增加而孔隙水压力$u_2$读数下降的趋势。

Results of VRCPTU can be produced from field testing with standard size cone penetrometers, as well as scaled down tests using either 1-g chamber tests or centrifuge deposits and minipenetrometers (d = 10 mm). Another variant is the "twitch test" whereby just a portion of the vertical field soil profile is chosen and rates of penetration are initially started at the standard rate of 20 mm/s and then stepped down to provide rate data over a limited depth interval (Randolph 2004). Some example results of twitch testing in soft Burswood clay are shown for both piezocone and T-bar, as shown by Figure 10.17. The CPTU twitch tests were varied from 20 mm/s down to as low as 0.02 mm/s while the T-bar tests slowed to a final value of 0.01 mm/s.

VRCPTU的结果可以通过使用标准尺寸的锥贯入仪进行现场测试获得，也可以通过使用1-g试验室测试或离心沉积物和小型贯入仪（d = 10 mm）进行缩放测试获得。另一种变体是“twitch测试”，其中仅选择垂直场地土层的一部分，并将贯入速率从标准速率20 mm/s开始，然后逐步降低，以提供有限深度间隔的速率数据（Randolph 2004）。图10.17展示了在澳大利亚Burswood软黏土中进行的压电锥和T型杆twitch测试的一些示例结果。CPTU twitch测试的速率从20 mm/s逐渐降低到最低0.02 mm/s，而T型杆测试的最终速率降低到0.01 mm/s。

![image](images/rId382.png)

Figure 10.16: Results of VRCPTU in Bassendean silt at penetration rates ranging from 0.002 mm/s to 20 mm/s: (a) net cone resistance; (b) porewater pressure (Suzuki 2015)

图10.16：在Bassendean粉土中进行的VRCPTU结果，贯入速率从0.002 mm/s到20 mm/s。（a）净锥阻力；（b）孔隙水压力（Suzuki 2015）。

![image](images/rId383.png)

Figure 10.17: Results from twitch type VRCPT in soft clay at Burswood, Australia: (a) CPTU rate; (b) CPTU reading of net cone resistance and Bq; (c) T-bar rate; and (d) T-bar qnet (from Randolph 2004)

图10.17：在澳大利亚Burswood软黏土中进行的twitch类型VRCPT结果。（a）CPTU速率；（b）CPTU净锥阻力和Bq读数；（c）T型杆速率；（d）T型杆qnet（Randolph 2004）。

Post-processing of variable rate and twitch testing can show the effect of drainage conditions on the measured CPT, T-bar, and/or ball penetration readings. For example, results from VRCPTU in kaolinitic deposits are shown in Figure 10.18 using a normalized velocity (V') along the x-axis:

可变速率和twitch测试的后处理可以显示排水条件对测得的CPT、T型杆和/或球形贯入读数的影响。例如，图10.18展示了在高岭土沉积物中的VRCPTU结果，x轴上的归一化速度（V'）定义为：

$$V' = \frac{v \cdot d}{c_v} \tag{10.3}$$

where v = actual penetration velocity, d = probe diameter, and cv = coefficient of consolidation.

其中，v 是实际贯入速度，d 是探头直径，cv 是固结系数。

For this geomaterial, it can be seen that the ranges of undrained behavior are exhibited when V' > 30 while fully drained behavior (Bq ≈ 0) is found when V' < 0.3. Consequently, partially drained response is noted to be intermediate and corresponding to: 0.3 < V' < 30. Similar ranges of V' are found for the drained-partially drained-undrained thresholds for other soils (DeJong et al. 2012). In fact, the following equations are used for these ranges (DeJong & Randolph 2012):

对于这种地质材料，可以看到，当 V' > 30 时表现出不排水行为，而当 V' < 0.3 时则表现出完全排水行为（Bq ≈ 0）。因此，部分排水响应被认为是中间状态，对应于 0.3 < V' < 30。其他土壤的排水-部分排水-不排水阈值的 V' 范围也类似（DeJong 等人，2012）。事实上，以下方程用于这些范围（DeJong & Randolph，2012）：

$$\frac{Q}{Q_{\text{ref}}} \approx 1 + \frac{\left( \frac{Q_{\text{drained}}}{Q_{\text{ref}}} - 1 \right)}{1 + \left( \frac{V}{V_{50}} \right)^{c}} \tag{10.4}$$

$$\frac{B_q}{B_{q\text{-ref}}} \approx 1 - \frac{1}{1 + \left( \frac{V}{V_{50}} \right)^{c}} \tag{10.5}$$

where Qref = reference value of normalized Q (here taken as undrained value) and Bq-ref  is similar for the porewater pressure response. The exponent "c" and term V50 are empirical fitting parameters. For an example, the results for VRCPTU in kaolin shown in Figure 10.18 were obtained with: c = 1.0, V50 = 6, Q/Qref = 2.2, and Bq-ref = 0.55.

其中，Qref 是归一化 Q 的参考值（这里取为不排水值），Bq-ref 类似于孔隙水压力响应。指数“c”和参数 V50 是经验拟合参数。以高岭土沉积物中的 VRCPTU 结果为例，如图 10.18 所示，c = 1.0，V50 = 6，Q/Qref = 2.2，Bq-ref = 0.55。

![image](images/rId386.png)

Figure 10.18: Summary results of VRCPTU in kaolin deposit (Schneider 2007)

图 10.18：高岭土沉积物中 VRCPTU 的汇总结果（Schneider 2007）

A generalized approach to ascertain the drainage conditions in silts, mixed soil types, and tailings during CPTU was developed by DeJong et al. (2012). The penetration rate versus cv for various normalized velocities are presented for undrained, partially-drained, and drained conditions in Figure 10.19.

DeJong 等人（2012）开发了一种通用方法，以在 CPTU 测试期间确定粉土、混合土和尾矿中的排水条件。贯入速率与固结系数 cv 的关系图针对不同的归一化速度展示了不排水、部分排水和排水条件。

![image](images/rId387.png)

Figure 10.19: Chart for assessing drainage conditions during penetration in soils (modified after DeJong et al. 2012)

图 10.19：贯入土体过程中评估排水条件的图表（根据 DeJong 等人 2012 年修改）

### 10.6.1 NTH Solution Applied to VRCPTU

### 10.6.1 NTH解法在VRCPTU中的应用

Detailed earlier, the effective stress friction angle ($\phi'$) of normally-consolidated clays can be interpreted from CPTU data in terms of normalized parameters Q and Bq using the NTH solution.

如前所述，正常固结黏土的有效应力摩擦角（'）可以通过CPTU数据中归一化参数Q和Bq利用NTH解法来解释。

The effective stress friction angle is a fundamental soil property and independent of the stress path, therefore undrained and drained tests, as well as partially-drained stress paths will reach the same effective stress envelope.

有效应力摩擦角是一项基本的土壤性质，不依赖于应力路径，因此在不排水和排水试验中，以及在部分排水的应力路径中，最终都会达到相同的有效应力包络线。

It is therefore of interest to show Q and Bq data from VRCPTU on a variety of 20 different soils, including clays, silts, and mixed soil types, as presented in Figure 10.20. Here, contours of $\phi'$ from the NTH solution are presented on the Q-Bq diagram. The VRCPTU data from the various soils follow the contours for individual values of $\phi'$, thereby helping to validate the NTH solution.

因此，有必要展示来自不同土壤类型的VRCPTU数据的Q和Bq，这些土壤类型包括黏土、粉土以及混合土壤，如图10.20所示。在该图中，展示了NTH解法下的'等值线在Q-Bq图上的分布。各种土壤的VRCPTU数据沿着不同'值的等值线分布，从而验证了NTH解法的有效性。

Of final note, VRCPTU has found use in helping to characterize mine tailings and slimes, primarily because they exhibit behavior of intermediate geomaterials (e.g., Dienstmann et al. 2018; DeJong & Green 2020).

最后需要提到的是，VRCPTU还用于帮助表征矿山尾矿和泥浆，主要因为它们表现出中间土工材料的行为特性（如Dienstmann等人，2018年；DeJong & Green，2020年）

![image](images/rId388.png)

Figure 10.20: Data from 20 series of VRCPTU in mixed soil types, clays, and silts confirming NTH solution for $\phi'$ from Q and Bq (after Ouyang & Mayne 2020a)

图10.20：混合土体类型、黏土和粉土中的20组VRCPTU数据，确认了NTH解法通过Q和Bq得出$\phi'$（引自Ouyang & Mayne，2020年）

## 10.7 Machine Learning and Big Data for CPTu Interpretation

## 10.7 机器学习与大数据在CPTu解释中的应用

Machine Learning (ML) is a subfield of Artificial Intelligence (AI) which deals with the development of algorithms that enable computers to perform a specific task, without the need of rule-based programming. ML models acquire information from existing data, allowing the computers to discover predictive rules applicable for future data.

机器学习（ML）是人工智能（AI）的一个子领域，涉及开发算法，使计算机能够执行特定任务，而无需基于规则的编程。ML模型通过现有数据获取信息，使计算机能够发现适用于未来数据的预测规则。

![image](images/rId389.png)

Figure 10.21: A Venn diagram of data science (source: https://towardsdatascience.com/clearlyexplained-how-machine-learning-differs-from-statistical-modeling-967f2c5a9cfd).

图10.21：数据科学的维恩图（来源：https://towardsdatascience.com/clearlyexplained-how-machine-learning-differs-from-statistical-modeling-967f2c5a9cfd）。

ML has become widespread in almost all industries and disciplines to provide valuable datadriven insights for making more informed decisions. Geotechnical engineering is a discipline that frequently uses empirical relationships to estimate soil properties and thus can greatly benefit from ML. Data-driven approaches have gained substantial interest over traditional modelling because not only do they deal better with uncertainties and complexities in geotechnical engineering problems, they also do not require prior assumptions about the fundamental and physical relationships between parameters. ML algorithms can therefore lead to more robust predictive models compared to traditional modelling.

机器学习已经在几乎所有行业和学科中广泛应用，为做出更明智的决策提供了有价值的数据驱动洞察。土木工程是一个经常使用经验关系来估算土壤性质的学科，因此可以大大受益于机器学习。与传统建模相比，数据驱动的方法因其更好地处理不确定性和复杂性而受到广泛关注，且不需要关于参数之间基本和物理关系的先验假设。因此，机器学习算法相比传统建模可以带来更为稳健的预测模型。

ML models are generally data-hungry and need large datasets to train and can continuously adapt as more data become available. In general, as more data are available, the more accurate and robust the prediction becomes. ML can be applied on high dimensional datasets with numerous input and output variables.

机器学习模型通常对数据需求量大，需要大量数据进行训练，并且随着数据的增加，可以持续进行适应。一般来说，数据越多，预测就越准确和稳健。机器学习可以应用于高维数据集，包含多个输入和输出变量。

![image](images/rId391.png)

Figure 10.22: Differences between statistical and ML modelling (source: https://turintech.ai/insights/machine-learning-vs-statistical-modelling-which-one-is-right-foryour-business-problem).

图10.22：统计建模与机器学习建模的差异（来源：https://turintech.ai/insights/machine-learning-vs-statistical-modelling-which-one-is-right-for-your-business-problem）。

With increasing computational power, ML has gained substantial interest in the geotechnical engineering community and has gradually become an alternative solution for geotechnical problems. In recent years, several studies have investigated the use of ML for CPT data interpretations and soil classifications. Example applications of ML for CPT interpretations can be found in Erzin and Ecemis (2016), Reale et al. (2018), Wang et al. (2019), Zhang et al. (2021), Erharter et al. (2021), Rauter & Tschuchnigg (2021), and Entezari et al. (2020, 2021a, 2021b, 2022).

随着计算能力的增加，机器学习在土木工程界引起了广泛关注，并逐渐成为解决土木工程问题的替代方案。近年来，已有多项研究探讨了机器学习在CPT数据解释和土壤分类中的应用。机器学习在CPT解释中的应用实例可以参考Erzin和Ecemis（2016年）、Reale等人（2018年）、Wang等人（2019年）、Zhang等人（2021年）、Erharter等人（2021年）、Rauter & Tschuchnigg（2021年），以及Entezari等人（2020年，2021a，2021b，2022年）。

The availability of large in-situ testing databases, specifically CPTu databases, coupled with ML techniques provide an opportunity to develop site-specific models for better site characterization. ML is also a powerful tool for investigating refinements to existing empirical CPTu relationships. With more accurate in-situ predictions of soil properties, geotechnical analysis using the CPTu can be improved. While various amounts and types of drilling and sampling on certain projects may always be required, ML-based CPTu models can add more accurate meaning to in-situ test profiles while providing a rapid, cost-effective, repeatable alternative; potentially reducing the site investigation’s overall schedule and cost.

通过使用大规模的原位测试数据库，特别是CPTu数据库，结合机器学习技术，可以开发出更适合场地特征的模型，从而优化场地表征。机器学习也是研究改进现有经验CPTu关系的一种强大工具。随着土壤性质的原位预测更加准确，使用CPTu进行的土木分析可以得到改进。虽然在某些项目中始终需要大量的钻探和采样，但基于机器学习的CPTu模型可以为原位测试数据增加更精确的意义，同时提供一种快速、经济、高重复性的替代方案，有可能减少场地调查的整体时间和成本。

### 10.7.1 Estimation of Solids and Fines of Tailings

### 10.7.1 尾矿中固体和细颗粒含量的估算

Styler et al. (2018) developed empirical relationships, called Tailings Behaviour Type (TBT) models, to estimate tailings constituents from in-situ Gamma Cone Penetration Test (GCPTu)

measurements. Entezari et al. (2020) developed a regional TBT model to estimate tailings solids and total fines from in-situ GCPTu using neural networks and a dataset comprised of paired GCPTu-laboratory results (n = 13,000) collected over a ten-year period from a mining area with predominately clay mineral and quartz sand tailings. The input variables used in neural networks included the undrained shear strength (Su), the slope of the dynamic pore water pressure versus depth, the passive gamma response, as well as the latitude, longitude, and the mining property name where the data was collected. Figure 10.23 shows the ternary diagram of the development dataset. The relationships between laboratory measured and TBT predicted solids and total fines content are shown in Figure 10.24. A performance evaluation showed that TBT models can predict the solids and fines content of the studied deposits within 4.0 and 4.9% error (by weight), respectively. An example tailings constituent profile estimated using the developed TBT models is shown in Figure 10.25. The laboratory results are over-plotted to visually assess the performance of the TBT models.

Styler等人（2018年）开发了经验关系模型，称为尾矿行为类型（TBT）模型，用于根据原位伽马锥探测试验（GCPTu）的测量数据估算尾矿的组成。Entezari等人（2020年）利用神经网络开发了区域TBT模型，以估算尾矿中的固体和总细颗粒含量。该模型使用的数据集包括在一个以粘土矿物和石英砂尾矿为主的矿区内，十年间收集的13,000组GCPTu和实验室配对结果。神经网络中使用的输入变量包括不排水剪切强度（Su）、动态孔隙水压力随深度变化的斜率、被动伽马响应，以及数据采集的纬度、经度和矿区名称。图10.23显示了开发数据集的三元图。图10.24显示了实验室测量值与TBT模型预测的固体和总细颗粒含量之间的关系。性能评估显示，TBT模型能够在误差分别为4.0%和4.9%（按重量）的范围内预测所研究沉积物的固体和细颗粒含量。图10.25显示了使用已开发的TBT模型估算的尾矿组成剖面实例，并叠加了实验室结果，以直观评估TBT模型的性能。

![image](images/rId393.png)

图10.24：实验室测量值与TBT模型预测的固体含量（左）和总细颗粒含量（右）之间的关系（Entezari等人，2020年）

![image](images/rId394.png)

Figure 10.25: Example TBT profile in fluid and soil-like tailings (Entezari et al. 2020)

图10.25：液态和类土状尾矿中的TBT剖面实例（Entezari等人，2020年）

In another study, Entezari et al. (2021a) explored the potential of a support vector machine (SVM) algorithm to estimate geotechnical fines of sandy soil-like tailings from GCPTu. They trained various SVM models with different combinations of GCPTu parameters (Table 10.1) and showed that a model trained with qt, fs, $u_2$, friction ratio (Rf), and gamma can predict the geotechnical 75 µm fines content of the studied deposits with 7.8% error. The performance of the developed models was observed to be significantly better than the traditional Ic based approach (Robertson and Wride 1998) in estimating the geotechnical 75 µm fines content of loose sandy tailings (Table 10.1). The relationships between laboratory measured and estimated geotechnical 75 µm fines content from trained SVM model (SVM 1 in Table 10.1) and Ic based method are shown in Figure 10.26. An example profile of geotechnical 75 µm fines content estimated using the developed SVM model (SVM 1 in Table 10.1) are shown in Figure 10.27. The Ic estimated geotechnical fines and the laboratory measured geotechnical 75 µm fines are overplotted in order to compare them with the SVM results.

在另一项研究中，Entezari等人（2021a）探讨了支持向量机（SVM）算法在估算类砂土状尾矿中细颗粒含量的潜力。他们训练了不同组合的GCPTu参数（表10.1）构建的多个SVM模型，结果显示，利用qt、fs、$u_2$、摩擦比（Rf）和伽马值作为输入特征训练的模型可以在误差为7.8%的范围内预测所研究沉积物中的75 µm细颗粒含量。与传统的基于Ic方法（Robertson和Wride，1998年）相比，所开发的模型在估算松散砂质尾矿的75 µm细颗粒含量方面表现出显著优势。图10.26显示了实验室测量值与训练的SVM模型（表10.1中的SVM 1）和基于Ic方法估算的75 µm细颗粒含量之间的关系。图10.27显示了使用开发的SVM模型（表10.1中的SVM 1）估算的75 µm细颗粒含量剖面实例，叠加了实验室结果以便与SVM结果进行比较。

### 10.7.2 Estimation of Soil Unit Weight

### 10.7.2 土体单重度的估算

The potential of a ML approach for the estimation of soil unit weight from CPTu data was investigated by Entezari et al. (2021b). A compiled database (n = 1228) of paired CPTu readings and laboratory measured unit weights from different field test sites with a variety of soil types, geological environments, and stress histories was used to explore and develop a ML algorithm directly estimating unit weights from CPTu data. The random forest (RF) algorithm was employed to calibrate the CPTu readings to the soil unit weights. Various RF models were trained with different combinations of input features (Table 10.2). Results showed that a RF model trained with qt, fs, $u_2$, and z as input features (RF3 in Table 10.2) could estimate unit weight with an error of ±0.93 kN/m3 . The relationships between measured and estimated unit weight using the developed RF model as well as those obtained from Robertson and Cabal (2010) and Mayne et al. (2010) methods are shown in Figure 10.28. A comparison performed between the RF models and some of the existing relationships in literature (Table 10.3) showed that all the random forest models developed in this study outperform the existing CPT relationships to estimate unit weight.

Entezari等人（2021b）探讨了使用机器学习方法从CPTu数据估算土壤单重度的潜力。他们使用了一个汇编的数据库（n = 1228），该数据库包括来自不同测试场地、具有多种土壤类型、地质环境和应力历史的配对CPTu读数和实验室测量的单重度数据，以探索和开发直接从CPTu数据估算单重度的机器学习算法。他们采用随机森林（RF）算法将CPTu读数校准到土壤单重度。训练了使用不同组合的输入特征的各种RF模型（表10.2）。结果显示，使用qt、fs、$u_2$和深度z作为输入特征训练的RF模型（表10.2中的RF3）可以在误差±0.93 kN/m³的范围内估算单重度。图10.28显示了使用开发的RF模型以及Robertson和Cabal（2010年）和Mayne等人（2010年）方法估算的单重度与实验室测量值之间的关系。将RF模型与文献中的一些现有关系进行比较（表10.3）显示，本文开发的所有随机森林模型在估算单重度方面均优于现有的CPT关系。

### 10.7.3 Estimation of Shear Wave Velocity (VS)

### 10.7.3 剪切波速（VS）的估算

The use of ML to predict VS from piezocone penetration test (CPTu) was explored by Entezari et al. (2022). A very large dataset of paired VS-CPTu data (n = 104,500) compiled from SCPTu soundings completed in a wide variety of soil types with various stress histories and geological environments was used to develop ML models to directly estimate VS from CPTu data. Table 10.4 lists the summary statistics of the paired dataset. The dataset was tested with the random forest algorithm to develop the models. The models were trained using four input parameters including qt, $u_2$, fs, and z (depth). Furthermore, the impacts of soil microstructure and cementation on estimated VS results were investigated and separate models were developed for the categories of uncemented and cemented soils. The plot of normalized tip resistance (Qtn) versus small-strain rigidity index (IG) to identify uncemented and cemented soils is shown in Figure 10.29. Results showed that the all-soils model developed using RF algorithm can estimate VS with ±49.5 m/s error (Figure 10.30). The model developed for uncemented soils showed a significant improvement and could predict VS with ±28.2 m/s error (Figure 10.31). The model developed for cemented soils achieved an accuracy of ±54.1 m/s (Figure 10.31). All the developed ML models outperformed the existing relationships tested in this study. A summary of model performances is presented in Table 10.5. An example profile of VS estimated using the random forest models is shown in Figure 10.32.

Entezari等人（2022年）探讨了使用机器学习从静力触探试验（CPTu）预测剪切波速（VS）的可能性。他们使用了一个非常大的VS-CPTu配对数据集（n = 104,500），该数据集来源于各种土壤类型、不同应力历史和地质环境下的SCPTu探测数据，并利用此数据集开发了直接从CPTu数据估算VS的机器学习模型。表10.4列出了配对数据集的统计摘要。该数据集使用随机森林算法进行了模型开发。模型使用了包括qt、$u_2$、fs和深度z在内的四个输入参数。此外，还探讨了土壤微观结构和胶结对估算的VS结果的影响，并为非胶结土和胶结土分别开发了模型。图10.29显示了识别非胶结土和胶结土的归一化端阻力（Qtn）与小应变刚度指数（IG）图。结果表明，使用RF算法开发的全土模型可以在±49.5 m/s的误差范围内估算VS（图10.30）。为非胶结土开发的模型显示出显著的改进，可以在±28.2 m/s的误差范围内预测VS（图10.31）。为胶结土开发的模型达到了±54.1 m/s的精度（图10.31）。所有开发的机器学习模型在本研究中均优于现有的测试关系。表10.5列出了模型性能的总结。图10.32显示了使用随机森林模型估算的VS剖面实例。

Table 10.4: Summary statistics of the VS-CPTu dataset (Entezari et al. 2022)
